Chapter 251: The Hidden Hand in the Dark

Compared to fighters, magicians were undoubtably more grandiose.

There were three main reasons.

First, there were far fewer people with the talent for magic, compared to those for fighting,

Second, magicians had more skills and more ways to apply them compared to fighters.

Third, even if they didn’t have extraordinary personal strength, their knowledge was still enough for them to do many things.

Mo Kong was not a magician; he was only a warlock. However, when he was concealing his identity, even he had a way of restraining his aura,

When he was acting alone, he was very prudent. He didn’t infiltrate Snake Rat Island by swimming in or flying in. Instead, he snuck aboard a ship traveling by, replaced someone he murdered, and used that identity to successfully come ashore.

Now, he was mixing in with the crowd, with his eyes gazing at the doors of the Hunting Guild.

A tall and sturdy human fellow was walking through the doors; it was Shi Qi (Zong Ge).

Zong Ge calmly held a bloodstained sack in his hand.

Fellows of the Hunting Guild passed by, most were ordinary hunters, while a few were cultivators.

They gathered by the side of the front desk and raised their head to look at the missions posted on the magic noticeboard on the ceiling.

Zong Ge’s arrival attracted the attention of many.

His body brimmed with the aura of a silver level fighter; no one could ignore him.

Upon seeing Zong Ge, the receptionist at the front desk blankly stared at him for a moment, then jogged from behind the desk and grinned widely, “Lord Shi Qi, good morning.”

Long Fu and Shi Qi’s arrival had attracted the attention of the island’s upper class.

The evening they had landed, Tan Qiu had invited them to dinner, causing information of what Long Fu, Shi Qi, and others looked like to quickly circulate.

Zong Ge nodded, then threw the sack onto the ground, causing one of the heads to roll out like a ball.

In the guild, someone immediately cried out.

“Ah, that looks like Da Zhao.”

“I heard a group got rid of Dao Zhao’s pirate group, took all of their ships, and used them to beat back Rou Zang. Don’t tell me that really happened?”

Zong Ge opened his mouth, his voice low and deep, “Check them, these are the leaders of Da Zhao’s pirate group.”

The receptionist stiffened for a moment, then resumed his prior composure, “Understood. We will do that now, please wait for us for a moment.”

Upon saying that, he put Da Zhao’s head back into the sack, then carried the sack into the back of the guild.

Zong Ge took this opportunity to move towards the front desk and raise his head to look over the information on the magic board.

Wherever he went, other hunters would automatically step aside.

“That wretched guy!” When Warlock Mu Ban witnessed the scene hidden in the crowd, he couldn’t help but grind his teeth.

The magic panel had many writings and images on it, and every few minutes, it would change.

Zong Ge first saw many bounty postings for pirates.

The Pirate Throne had appeared, causing these pirates to rise up like mad.

He wanted to check how much the bounty for Da Zhao’s pirates was when his gaze suddenly paused, he noticed there was a reward for finding Zhen Jin.

Zhen Jin pretended to be Hei Juan, and after being shipwrecked, became the survivors’ captive on Mystifying Monster Island. Nowadays, he was in a coma and held on the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

He was a formidable negotiation chip, and survivors planned to use him to negotiate with the Bai Zhen clan.

Because of special circumstances, Zhen Jin was the Bai Zhen clan’s only heir, and could not be replaced.

Therefore, after Zhen Jin and Zi Di went missing, the Bai Zhen clan mobilized their entire strength to search for him. Since a missing poster of Zhen Jin appeared on Snake Rat Island, Zong Ge undoubtably knew that it was also posted at other islands and other imperial coastal cities.

Besides pirate bounties and finding Zhen Jin, there were many missions of the same type.

Hunting missions.

Most of the hunting assignments were for tide snakes and smelt rats.

There were Snake Rat Island’s signature magic beasts that lived in its underground caverns, there were many communities of them that lived and had multiplied there for many years. There was no lack of silver level magic beast leaders among them. In the subterranean abyss, someone even noticed traces of gold level magic beasts.

In fact, these missions were the Hunting Guild’s main business venue.

Criminal bounties, finding people, and other tasks were considered affiliated tasks.

Identical bounty orders, search and rescue orders, and other missions could be seen in the Thief Guild, the Mercenary Guild, the Alchemy Guild, and so on.

Snake Rat Island was not big; it only had a Hunting Guild office. If one wanted to see these bounties and other missions, this was the only place they could.

Of course, Snake Rat Island hadn’t been built up and developed for long.

As time passed, this place would grow more prosperous.

The Sheng Ming Empire had invaded the Wilderness Continent, and it began to clearly drive the development and trade of some regions.

Zong Ge waited for a while for the receptionist to come back to him.

“Lord Shi Qi, the head has been verified.”

“This is your bounty.”

Zong Ge took a full pouch of money.

“Are you interested in joining the Hunting Guild? After completing some missions, the guild will give you some special rewards. In light of your strength, what magic beast can be your opponent?”

The receptionist used praise to attract Zong Ge.

Gold level existences usually were stationed in a region and had authority.

Silver levels were the main target for guild recruitment. Especially someone like Zong Ge, someone who could vie with an expert like Rou Zang, he was an extremely valuable person to attract.

But Zong Ge shook his head and expressed his disinterest, then left the Hunting Guild.

Warlock Mo Kong watched him leave and didn’t shadow him.

He twitched his nose, breathing in the stink of blood in the air as he slowly walked to the back of the Hunting Guild office.

In the pile of trash in the corner, he found the sack.

The bloodstained sack no longer had the heads in it.

Wherever Mo Kong touched the sack, bloodstains would melt away and coagulate into blood that flowed into his garments, across his body, and into his nose.

“The victims’ blood, the murderer’s aura, very good, this is a good start.” Mo Kong complacently sneered and left.

Above his head, a group of birds flapped their wings as they streaked across the air.

No one detected anything peculiar about this group of birds, it was the scout bird models.

In the Deep Sea Monster Fish, Zi Di was controlling these birds.

On the walls, a light projection was created from screens showing the point of view of every bird.

Mo Kong did not notice these strange birds while Zi Di hadn’t seen through Mo Kong’s disguise.

There were too many screens, too many images flashing past. Mo Kong’s disguise method was outstanding; it far exceeded these scout birds’ detection capabilities.

The entire bird’s-eye-view of Snake Rate Island was gradually being filled in.

As time passed, both the buildings Tan Qiu had on Snake Rat Island and the military layout present could be seen from a panoramic view.

The island was quite round, however on the northeast corner, a piece of it seemed to have been bitten out.

The nick was a wide crescent moon shaped shoal, both ends of which abruptly rose up. There were obvious traces of manmade explosives visible.

This shoal was Snake Rat Island’s only harbor.

Apart from this, although the island was round, it was all sheer cliffs and precipitous rock faces.

On the surrounding cliffs, large flocks of seabirds danced in the breeze. They nested in the cliffs and caught fish in the sea.

The sea wind was strong, creating waves that constantly struck the cliffs in a spray of seawater.

There wasn’t much vegetation on the island, most places were bare and covered in odd stone crags.

From time to time, natural tunnel entrances would appear on the surface. These cave mouths were all dark green and were very conspicuous from the air. The big ones were nearly fifty meters in diameter, while the smaller ones were at least ten.

These caves all connected to the underground.

Snake Rat Island had a massive subterranean area under it. The most abundant natural resources in it were the two magic beast groups, the smelt rats and tide serpents.

“But the Green Jade Gold Coffin was not teleported there.”

When Zi Di saw that, she quietly sighed to herself.

If it had been teleported there, the survivors would find it considerably troublesome to search for it.

The island inhabitants mostly gathered in three areas. The biggest congregation was the port. Streams of people shuttled through the town, it was bustling. The other two were villages that had people coming and going. One of the village’s industries was lumbering yards, while the other was open air mining.

After conducting a basic scan of Snake Rat Island, Zi Di had the scouts fly back t o town to continue investigating.

The town’s most conspicuous building was undoubtably Tan Qiu’s castle.

Although the castle wasn’t big, it was very new. Even though Zi Di hadn’t studied architecture much, she could tell with a glance that it was a fortification that met military standards, easy to defend and difficult to attack.

The castle occupied a high slope. Beneath the slope, the terrain gradually leveled, it was the upper class district.

In the upper district, the streets were wide and there were several important buildings there.

Outside of the wealthy district, there were the slums. Large and small shacks were packed tightly along with many people, it was squalid.

As the slums spread towards the port, it became prosperous again.

The port had a market; their main product was fresh fish.

Breakfast had already pasted, and people continually streamed through the town.

Zi Di gradually grew absent-minded, when she saw these sights, she sensed a thriving vitality.

An urge to throw herself into this place arose in her heart.

People were social beings.

Mystifying Monster Island was a place of imminent crisis and lurking danger, the Deep Sea Monster Fish was cramped and terribly busy, the boundless ocean made the survivors feel insignificant, and Rou Zang pursuing them put them on edge.

Now, Zi Di finally saw civilization, and saw the society she was oh so familiar with. Even if she was merely a spectator, happiness and tranquility still sprung up in her heart.

A few scout birds landed on the ships.

Some on the sides, others on the window sills.

“As expected, this is a slave ship.” After Zi Di saw the images, she understood what it was depicting.

This small fleet had made port next to the survivors’ five ships, of course they wanted to probe them.

“Slave ships mostly transport cowmen, this kind of beastman makes for good plowing slaves.”

“Hm, I’ve detected a cemetery. In addition to the slaughterhouse, perhaps Cang Xu can gather some resources here too.”

The scout birds displayed their extraordinary practicality as they gained more and more information.

Through the birds’ perspective, Zi Di saw Cang Xu and Zong Ge.

The two were walking out of a store.

It was Snake Rat Island’s biggest store; it belonged to the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance.

The merchant alliance magician stationed there personally said farewell to Zong Ge and Cang Xu.

On the one hand, it was to give face to Long Fu and Shi Qi, on the other hand, Cang Xu had given the store its biggest transaction yet.

Cang Xu sold most of the Soft Heart Stone and purchased many goods in exchange.

“I will reserve the goods you need for the time being, however, I also ask for your understanding. We cannot keep goods we cannot sell.” The merchant alliance magician reminded as they parted.

Zong Ge nodded, his tone tranquil, “I understand.”

The merchant alliance magician smiled back, “Then I look forward to attending you two again!”

Enroute on the return trip, Cang Xu couldn’t help but frown.

Many resources were out of stock, even worse, the survivors didn’t have much money on hand and could not even buy all of the things that were available.

The survivors hadn’t gotten a lot of money from Mystifying Monster Island.

Zong Ge was also somewhat disappointed.

He didn’t find anything that could help his arm injury.

“Perhaps Tan Qiu has the thing I want.”

But if that is so, Tan Qiu will undoubtedly use it to get us to collaborate with him to deal with Rou Zang.

That did not conform with the survivors’ plan.

“It seems healing it can only be put aside for the time being. I’ll wait for another opportunity on another island.”

Zong Ge and Cang Xu sank into deep thought.

For a short time, when Zi Di’s gaze lingered on these two, she too began to mutter to herself.

Of the survivors, these two were the strongest and only second to the dragonman youngster. Although Cang Xu was only at the iron level, he had necromancy and had many ghosts that could menace Zong Ge greatly.

Zong Ge hated necromancy, while Cang Xu was vigilant against Zong Ge. This was what the experience on Mystifying Monster Island had produced.

Anyways, during the prior battle with Rou Zang, Zong Ge had willingly taken blows for Cang Xu when the latter was in danger.

Currently, the two’s relationship had eased up greatly.

This time, because they needed to trade and were facing a silver level magician, Zong Ge and Cang Xu decided to partner up together.

After the trade was completed, the two had a lot of supplies on them, thus they also needed Zong Ge’s strength to safeguard the goods.

The survivors did not know that not long after Cang Xu and Zong Ge departed, the warlock, Mo Kong, also entered the store.

Using his successful disguise, he deliberately expressed pleasant surprise, “Did you say you had a large amount of Soft Heart Stone?”

“How wonderful!”

“Quick, let me examine the goods.”

A moment later, with a Soft Heart Stone in hand, Mo Kong left the store.

“Very good, this time, I have gathered the aura of that old magician.” He sneered in his heart.

Chapter 252: Helping Each Other

The Deep Sea Monster Fish, the alchemy cabin.

In a radiant alchemy formation, the young captain bathing in the light opened his mouth wide and gasped for breath.

He was drenched in sweat, his body already exhausted to its limits due to him continuously squeezing his stamina,

At that very moment, his heart was thumping like a large drum, seemingly causing all of his flesh to throb.

However, success brought him happiness.

“Finally…the change is done.”

With the help of the alchemy formation and yellow crab puppets, he constantly discharged his old battle qi, then cultivated a new battle qi. Besides what was absolutely necessary, he did not rest nor relax during the entire process.

He couldn’t afford to lose a moment and made every second count in order to switch his battle qi.

The obsolete habits were purged away, now even if he wasn’t cultivating, his body would now produce the new battle qi.

Bursting Explosion battle qi!

A radiance reflected against the alchemy cabin’s golden walls as it spread out across the young man’s red dragon scales, he attentively sensed how different this new battle qi felt.

He bowed his head, reflecting as he looked down on his hands.

Or to be more precise, a pair of sturdy and powerful dragon claws.

Despite being tired and aching from head to toe, the dragonman youngster still sensed the new battle qi hidden within his body, it had changed him somewhat.

The new battle qi produced a sensation, as if his back was bent and his elbows were curved, ready to strike with his fists.

He grew restless.

The new Bursting Explosion battle qi erupted with power the White River battle qi could never compare to.

Whoosh.

The dragonman youngster punched again.

The simple and straight punch caused a rush of wind.

The new battle qi was refined by this movement.

The fist, forearm, and upper arm all produced Bursting Explosion battle qi. The closer one boxed to the center, the more battle qi was produced.

The young man then tried to use a bit of battle qi.

Ding!

The fist rapidly accelerated, as if it was being boosted by gunpowder.

The air in front of it was blown apart.

“Excellent, now that I have this battle qi, the next step is to store up on it and constantly train. My actual combat abilities are bound to jump leaps and bounds.” The youngster said to himself.

Although the Life Scorching Dragon Breath was powerful, it took too long to cast. The young man understood that this was a great weakness. It would be difficult to replicate his prior victory over Rou Zang.

“White River battle qi is cultivated through swimming; thus Bursting Explosion battle qi is more convenient to cultivate.”

“It really suits the dragonman form, and when I break through to the gold level in the future, it will be boosted greatly too.”

After sensing how different the new battle qi was from the old, the young man was deeply moved by the Bloodcore once again.

“The Bloodcore is just so practical!”

Generally, fighters selected their battle qi technique based on their bloodlines.

As far as everyone was concerned, bloodlines were innate and could not be altered, therefore they had no choice but to pick a battle qi based on them.

Only battle qi that suited the bloodline could display their full benefits.

Normally, after fighters selected their battle qi, they would never change it. Even if there were many other battle qi that suited them, they wouldn’t ever change theirs.

Take Lan Zao for example, there were many battle qi techniques that were compatible with him, however, he persevered in cultivating the Water Grass battle qi. Because changing one’s battle qi technique was very costly, and after changing it, the battle qi would usually give a similar amount of benefit to the bloodline, it was completely unnecessary to squander resources doing this.

Because of this, battle qi techniques weren’t rigorously kept secret.

Even if a battle qi technique was spread, if one didn’t have a compatible bloodline, they could not cultivate it, or perhaps, expend twice the effort for half the result.

Many people even circulated parts of their battle qi techniques themselves.

Because they could be used to detect others with the same bloodline or perhaps allow other fighters to reveal their talents and be convinced into joining the circulator.

People gathered these battle qi techniques, battle skills, meditation methods, and other such things quite easily.

Of course, secret parts that involved the highest levels of the technique were still kept confidential. For example, some hidden skills like the Bai Zhen Clan’s Piercing Needle Explosion was kept under tight wraps. Another example was that a profound part of a battle qi technique was controlled and treated as a benefit to be distributed to strengthen an organization’s rule.

“Eat something.” Zi Di smiled, a pot of meat in her hands.

Smelt rat meat.

The dragonman youngster was hungry, thus he expressed his thanks, sat down, and began to eat.

He opened his mouth, and with sharp teeth, easily chewed the smelt rat meat.

The smelt rate meat was rare, and as the dragonman youngster chewed, blood dribbled from his mouth.

The dragonman youngster swallowed, and his face lit up happily.

Compared to normal humans, dragonmen had better digestive capabilities. Their sense of taste was also different; they found raw meat tastier.

Quickly, fire attribute magic power appeared in the young man’s body. As he digested, more and more magic power spread throughout his body.

The ghost girl calmly watched the dragonman youngster eat, her warm gaze revealing her love.

For a time, the alchemy cabin filled with a warm aura.

“Perhaps I should learn how to roast magic beast meat.” The girl suddenly said as she looked at her beloved.

The dragonman youngster was surprised, “I think you had better become a magician as soon as possible.”

“I know.” The girl rolled her eyes, “Anyway, you know processing magic beast meat also belongs in the domain of alchemy.”

“A wise chef made meal will have harmonious magic power that is easy to absorb. Even more, they can magnify the consumption value of the food.”

“You can look at these dishes as edible magic medicine with peculiar shapes.”

The dragonman youngster contemplated, “Indeed, we are not on Mystifying Monster Island. Next, we will all need three meals of magic beast meat a day. If we had a magic chef helping us make dishes, we could reduce magic beast meat consumption and increase the value of our food. Over a long period, our fighters will benefit considerably.”

“However, the most important thing now is that you become a magician, then we can use the magic formation to improve your bloodline.”

“Yep. I understand.” The ghost girl sweetly smiled.

The dragonman youngster’s battle qi had successfully changed, next was to make Zi Di a magician again.

This was also part of the plan they had discussed.

The two did not want to sink into a sorry state at the same time, after all, the value of War Merchant’s inheritance was too astonishing.

Wealth moved the mind, like what happened to Jia Sha.

The young man and lady trusted their companions; however they also did not think it was necessary to risk testing their humanity.

Like on Mystifying Monster Island when they helped each other, they helped each other now too.

After Zi Di adjusted her condition, she entered the meditation room.

The dragonman youngster personally guarded the cabin door.

Time passed, and the sun rose from the sea.

The bustling market pier soon became sparse once again.

On the deck of a ship, Zong Ge and Tripleblade were focused on training sailors.

They divided into pairs and fought each other in close combat with swords blunted by thick cotton cloth.

“Ah!” A sailor fell to the ground, causing him to let out a mournful yelp.

Although the sword was wrapped, getting hit by it still hurt. The fallen sailor’s face quickly turned dark purple.

This was a common occurrence.

Zong Ge snorted coldly with annoyance, “Stand up, continue fighting.”

“If you fear pain, when death approaches, what can you take out to resist its coming?”

“Don’t stop!”

“Continue! Continue!

Intimidated by Zong Ge’s might, the sailors could only lower their heads with trembling bodies, clench their teeth, and continue training.

The training persisted for a time longer until Zong Ge’s expression changed.

“Rest!”

A moment later, the training sailors seemed to lose their bones as they slammed limply onto the deck.

Tripleblade, who had been drilling during this time, walked over to Zong Ge, “My Lord, these are the elites we have filtered out from the rest. However, now it seems their physical strength has become a problem. Before, when we were dealing with Rou Zang, we did not conduct a wide scale war. If our entire troop goes into battle, these people…”

When he said that, Tripleblade shook his head.

He was once a mercenary group commander; therefore, he also had the ability to conduct drills and see the problems in these sailors.

Zong Ge shook his head, his voice low, “Stamina can be nurtured; however, they lack killing intent. They have not endured pain and pressure; all of these people will flee in terror on their first battlefield.”

Tripleblade sighed, “Those pirates aren’t bad, however, the fisherfolk are all incompetent.”

Currently, the survivors’ troops mainly consist of two groups.

One was recruited from Da Zhao’s pirate group.

The other was the Stone Egg Island fisherfolk they had saved.

Tripleblade’s tone changed, “Anyways, with enough time, we can improve their situation with constant training.”

“All of these people are good at swimming, with enough drills. They will be qualified naval mariners.”

But Zong Ge replied, “Time is exactly what we lack the most.”

“Good luck has never been on our side.”

“If Rou Zang pursues us, he won’t give us a third chance.”

Zong Ge understood that opportunities on the battlefield were fleeting. He had to value time, grab ahold of every chance, and expend the greatest effort to use them to strengthen himself as best as he could.

“We need people of a certain quality.”

“These people need more talent and abilities, at least enough to be worth the energy it takes to train them.”

“They will be our common members.”

Tripleblade’s eyes shone upon hearing what Zong Ge was implying.

Zong Ge always regarded the survivors’ team as a doomed prospect and determined that the layers of contradictions in it made harmony hard, and no matter if their redemption was successful or not, this team would inevitably collapse.

Zong Ge originally wanted to go to the Wilderness Continent to accomplish his goals.

Now, he was beginning to prepare for that future.

He planned to build his own army.

Besides his individual strength, he was also a skilled battle commander.

When he reached the Wilderness Continent, what else could help him better accomplish his goals than an army?

Tripleblade was also somewhat excited.

Zong Ge was his lifesaving benefactor, however he did not follow him solely because of that.

Zong Ge was a half beastman and suffered discrimination on Mystifying Monster Island. As a goblin, Tripleblade resonated with Zong Ge’s awkward situation.

To some degree, Tripleblade had entrusted his own aspirations to Zong Ge.

He immediately began to calculate what Zong Ge was planning.

“My Lord.” Tripleblade suddenly said, “I have thought of a way.”

He pointed at a few ships nearby.

“Those are slave transport ships.”

“They sailed in yesterday; there are many imprisoned beastmen on board.”

“It is a small transport fleet.”

“Intelligence shows that these ships have a large number of beastmen. Most of them are cowmen, the rest are dogmen. To be more accurate, they are shiba inu-men.”

“We can purchase some slaves to cultivate and train.”

Zong Ge gazed at the slave transport ships with rapt attention, “I also intend to do this. After today’s training is finished, you will ask what their price is.”

Chapter 253: Driving the Price Down

Zong Ge continued to preside over training till night fell.

The trainees were frothing at the mouth, and most of them did not even have the strength to eat their dinner.

The rest weren’t having a great time either.

Zong Ge also trained them during his spare time.

One day later, the sailors were all exhausted.

The scout birds perched on the sides and portholes of the ships recorded the sailors’ private complaints and bewilderments about Zong Ge. Some even called Zong Ge a madman.

The pirates who had been on battlefields and felt the pressure of death, began to have their impression of Zong Ge change dramatically.

They would rather fight on the battlefield than endure this oppressive training torture.

“A group of bandits, all completely useless.” Zong Ge coldly snorted when he found out.

He could now only entrust his hopes more into those beastman slaves.

For that mission, Tripleblade had gone to a village pub.

The pub was indeed bustling at night!

Stone fireplaces were raging hot, and there were candles hanging from ceiling lamps and every corner, lighting the entire place up.

In the bright place, a barman at the counter was busily pouring wine.

On a small stage, a blond and freckled female dancer was flicking her red and white dancing skirt and thumping the floor along with the music.

There were some sitting in dark nooks. Some were whispering to each other, as if they were conspiring something. Others were drinking quietly and watching the stairs that led to the second floor or perhaps leaning on the dagger in their hands.

Hei Fei was in the upstairs room seeking pleasure, as a captain, he naturally had his subordinates guarding him.

Down on the first floor, his first mate was making the loudest noises at his drinking table. His face had an ugly scar from a knife wound, back in the day, a knife nearly cut his face into two.

He drained his cup, rolling his Adam’s apples several times, then suddenly threw the oaken wine cup away.

“You’re so ugly, begone from me!” The first mate bellowed.

The female dancer jumped back in fright; she had just narrowly avoided the wine cup. She was an ordinary person, if the thick wine cup had smashed into her, it would have left her badly bruised.

The band also stopped fearfully.

Everyone else began to look at the first mate.

The first mate emitted an iron level aura, and with disheveled hair and red eyes, he looked back at them.

Customers who weren’t strong enough turned away, none dared to look him in the face.

The barman at the counter did not speak, they were somewhat pale. The tavern owner didn’t show his face. Although some of the strong customers indignantly snorted, they didn’t do anything.

Hei Fei had many people here.

“Hehehe!” The first mate heartily laughed as he emitted his tyrannical aura. He turned to his side and yelled, “Boys, let’s give this group of country bumpkins some real entertainment.”

The sailors around him knew what the first mate’s disposition was like and immediately jogged out.

Very quickly, sailors from the back of the pub crowded together, their hands tugging iron chains.

The iron chains clacked together against the bonds of the living creatures held by them.

They were short, green skinned, had large eyes, sharp noses, and pointed ears.

“Are those goblins?” The first mate frowned, displeased.

A sailor rushed to explain, “My Lord, the captain told us that you cannot kill the beastman, we need to sell them tomorrow.”

The first mate coldly snorted, “Then give me a proper show!”

The sailors promptly hurried to the tavern’s small stage, raised their whips, and flogged the goblins.

The goblins were chained to the stage, but not completely immobile. To dodge the whips, they scattered across the stage, and from time to time, the iron chains yanked them to the floor, accompanied by loud shrieks.

The whips were barbed, and when they touched flesh, it would leave it lacerated, splattering the walls and floor with scarlet blood.

Seeing this, the first mate howled with laughter and glee.

“Ah, now this is a dance. Where is the music? Don’t stop!” He growled.

The musicians began to play at once.

As the music and screams of the goblins intermingled, the first mate laughed.

Some people could not bear to watch as they either looked up or at their wine cups. Others were interested and admired the sight; they too laughed from time to time.

Tripleblade pushed open the tavern door and walked in.

He knew He Fei and his men were here.

A few scouting birds were on the town’s bell tower or roofs and continued to monitor this place.

Almost all of the Captain’s Hei Fei’s chief subordinates were here seeking pleasure.

The night tavern was a good place to relax.

Snake Rat Island hadn’t had much construction on it, besides this tavern, there really wasn’t anywhere else to go.

Tripleblade stared blankly, the first thing he saw was his fellow kin being flogged on a stage.

He was also a goblin.

In an instant, his eyes narrowed, and he unconsciously gripped the scimitar at his waist.

Many people looked at Tripleblade, their gazes wary.

He had used the Deceptive Disguise formation to pretend to be human, however his height could not be disguised. Therefore, in the onlookers’ eyes, he looked like a dwarf.

But Tripleblade was iron level!

The long cultivated temperament of a mercenary commander as well as the scimitar at his waist also told the others that he was not someone to trifle with.

Tripleblade’s complexion sank, then after quickly running his eyes around, he directly walked towards the first mate.

The first mate stopped laughing and stared at Tripleblade.

The people around them slowly stood up from their drinking tables.

After waiting until Tripleblade had walked to him, the first mate said, “Friend, what is it?”

Expressionless, Tripleblade replied, “I am looking for your captain, I want to do business.”

At that moment, the screeching and sobbing of women came from the second floor.

The first mate smiled, “No matter what business it is, we cannot disturb the captain’s spirits. I am the first mate; you can speak to me.”

Tripleblade responded, “I am very interested in your beastmen.”

The first mate nodded, “If you want to buy them, you can go to the market.”

“Tomorrow, we will sell a batch of slaves.”

“Eh?” During Tripleblade’s detailed inquiry, he discovered the slaves the other party wanted to sell were defective goods.

These slaves either had physical discrepancies or injuries. The slave ships were worried these slaves would die enroute, therefore they wanted to sell them first.

These were not people Zong Ge and Tripleblade wanted.

Tripleblade shook his head, “We want robust beastmen.”

“Those aren’t for sale. This place cannot offer a good price.” The first mate also shook his head.

Tripleblade, “What’s the price?”

The first mate informed him of it.

Tripleblade couldn’t help but purse his brows.

“The price is too high.” He gazed into the first mate’s eyes.

The first mate laughed, “Friend, that is the price!”

“The current market price has changed.”

“A large-scale war is about to break out on the Wilderness Continent.”

“In recent times, we are finding it hard to capture beastmen slaves.”

“This price is very reasonable!”

Tripleblade sank into silence.

He knew beastmen slaves had always been in high demand.

Because beastmen had stronger bodies than humans, they were always better miners and farmers.

The beastmen the empire wantonly seized from the Wilderness Continent were not only sold in the empire but also to other continents.

Many imperial citizens who had immigrated to other continents to expand the empire’s territory required cheap laborers.

Tripleblade tried to lower the price.

The first mate shook his head; his refusal was very obvious.

He continued to lower prices.

As a former mercenary commander, he also could negotiate and was used to careful accounting.

But this only resulted in mocking and ridicule.

“Forget it, you can’t afford it.”

“We can sell it for higher elsewhere, why must we sell it to you?”

The sailors around the first mate gazed at him with contempt, and even the first mate exposed an impatient expression.

At that moment, a green figure suddenly jumped off the small stage and ran upstairs to the second floor.

A startled shout rang out, “Oh no!”

“A goblin is escaping!”

“Capture him now!”

One of the whipped goblins somehow managed to struggle free of their chains.

At that moment of life and death, the goblin stimulated all of their strength, catching everyone by surprise, and choosing not running into the street, but instead up to the second floor.

“Damm it!” The first mate was greatly alarmed; his battle qi broke out as he jumped up the stairs and into the second floor.

Ding.

A moment later, there was a dull echo.

The door to the second floor was kicked open.

The goblin was killed by the kick, and its broken body splattered with the splinters of the wooden door onto the stairs and floor.

Captain Hei Fei only had a bed sheet wrapped around him as he wrathfully walked down.

“Why weren’t you watching this wretched thing?!” Hei Fei verbally abused.

“Captain.”

“Boss.”

The first mate and the others straightened their bodies, like cicadas in cold weather.

Hei Fei’s entire body was steeped in a silver level aura, as he looked around, his gaze paused on Tripleblade and then the small stage.

The small stage was covered in bloodstains and had a group of scared goblins huddling together on it.

Hei Fei gloomily looked down at the first mate, and from the gaps between his teeth, a voice squeezed through, “Clean up all of your toys, if something disturbs me again, you will feel my fury well.”

“Understood, boss!” The first mate responded at once.

Hei Fei coldly snorted with satisfaction and slowly returned to the room.

“Hey, stop.” Tripleblade’s voice suddenly cut through the deathly stillness of the tavern.

The people around him slowly widened their eyes and their faces twisted into disbelief.

Hei Fei’s footsteps ceased, and he looked at Tripleblade, his mood vile, “Did you just speak?”

Upon saying that, he glanced at the first mate.

The first mate’s heart jumped as he pounced Tripleblade, “Outrageous!”

Shing.

The sound of a blade drawn was sharp and clear.

Everyone’s eyes seemed to blur as they watched Tripleblade suddenly disappear.

When he reappeared, he was in front of the first mate.

He lifted his scimitar, the blade point touching the first mate’s chin.

The first mate locked in place, he was in the middle of an attacking motion, the dagger in his hand still raised high.

He looked especially embarrassed.

He felt bewildered and fearful, as well as angry and spiteful, for there wasn’t enough time for those feelings to fully dissipate.

Everyone’s mouths dropped, cries of alarm still in their throats when Tripleblade suppressed the first mate.

“Good speed!” On the second floor, Hei Fei’s eyes contracted.

The first mate fumed with rage but didn’t dare to rashly act.

“Friend, where are you from?” Hei Fei said, “You best understand the consequences.”

Tripleblade smiled, “We are from the sea.”

“Our ships are next to your ships.”

“Consequences?”

“I understand those!”

“Damn it!” Upon hearing Tripleblade’s origins, the corners of Hei Fei’s eyes couldn’t help but tremble.

“It’s that group of people!” The first mate’s and others’ hearts sank.

Information about the survivors independently streaked across all of their minds.

Two silver levels, one iron level magician, a gold level cannon, they annexed Dao Zhao’s pirate group and forced Rou Zang to withdraw…twice!

“So, it turns out to be a neighbor, hehehe.” The silent Hei Fei suddenly laughed, “It seems we have been acquainted through fighting.”

He changed his attitude and walked down the stairs.

Upon seeing this, Tripleblade drew back his scimitar, but did not sheath it.

The first mate gagged and immediately took a few steps back, fear restraining him.

“He came to purchase slaves.” The first mate faced Captain Hei Fei.

Hei Fei raised an eyebrow, “We can sell.”

“But the price needs to be appropriate.” He added on.

To be able to obtain slaves and transport them between continents, Hei Fei had to be a highly capable person.

He did not want to offend the survivors, but he also wanted to protect his own benefits.

Tripleblade watched Hei Fei attentively, the other party was a genuine silver level, while he was only an iron level, in addition to the other’s many men, he was not their opponent.

But his manner was more unyielding than before, “Currently, this isn’t a business problem. He just stabbed me.”

Hei Fei’s brows wrinkled further, “But you aren’t injured, instead it’s my man…”

The first mate touched his throat, and felt a trace of blood, causing him to raise his had at once and indicate to everyone, “See! You all see!”

Tripleblade coldly snorted and stared back at Captain Hei Fei, “And you.”

As a silver level, Hei Fei felt offended, and barely restrained his anger, “Friend, don’t push your luck.”

Tripleblade was calm and fearless, he first looked at the dead goblin on the floor.

The unlucky goblin had their entire chest caved in, and its fresh blood was flowing onto the ground. Their eyes were open wide; they had died with unresolved grievances.

Tripleblade’s gaze changed to look at his own chest.

He reached out and wiped his hand across his bloodstained chest.

It did not come back clean, but with a handful of blood.

Tripleblade looked back at Hei Fei, “You see, you splashed blood on me.”

Captain Hei Fei coldly snorted, “What do you want to do about it?”

His kin’s corpse reflecting in his eyes, Tripleblade used a heavy tone of voice as he slowly replied, “Therefore, the price has to be cut.”

Everyone: …...

Chapter 254: Beastmen Rebellion

Tripleblade had left the tavern.

Hei Fei and the others gathered together with ugly expressions.

The first mate indignantly whispered, “Captain, why didn’t we seize that arrogant guy?”

“From start to finish, he never even told us his name! He clearly looks down on us.”

“Not only does he look down on me, he, an insignificant iron level, doesn’t even put you in his eyes!”

“We could have seized him and used him to threaten those guys. Even if we can’t beat them, at worst we could walk away.”

“Da Zhao’s pirate ships cannot compare to the speed of ours!”

“Our ships can still sail!”

“An urgent maintenance from the boatwrights can last us until the next island.”

Hei Fei instantly became gloomy, and he grabbed the first mate’s cheeks, veins popping from the back of his hands as he twisted the first mate’s face.

Hei Fei coldly said, “Do you have a brain?”

“The one who lost face was not me, but you!”

“You were subdued in a single move; as an iron level!”

“Also, what was that stupidity you just said?”

“Why did we come here to rest and reorganize? Because pirates are too rampant nowadays, if we run into pirates with our ships in this state, what do we do?”

“When that moment comes, if you die in battle and have our goods robbed, what’s the use if we aren’t alive?”

“Bo, boss, my mistake, my mistake.” The first mate begged for mercy.

“Humph!” Hei Fei released his wrathful hands.

The first mate stroked his cheek with his own hand, frightened and doubtful.

His check had gone completely numb, and he could no longer feel it.

He even felt that his gums had gone slack.

Captain Hei Fei looked at the tavern door, his eyes narrowed and his voice low, “Who do you think they are?”

“They are arms smuggling criminals!”

The first mate’s and the others’ eyes opened wide, this was hard to believe.

Hei Fei sneered, “Use your pitiful brains to think about it.”

“How can a group of people that appeared from nowhere be able to beat back Rou Zang?”

“They have many alchemy crossbows and firearms on hand; these things are rechargeable and can be used by ordinary people. One or two is fine, but they have hundreds on hand!”

“They even have a gold level cannon! Gold level. Their strongest cultivator is only silver level; how can they use this battlefield treasure?”

“It’s very clear that they want to clandestinely transport these arms to the Wilderness Continent. As a result, their secret base on Stone Egg Island was raided by Da Zhao’s pirate group.”

“Ah, they were exposed, thus they had to act!”

“There is no recently organized mercenary group, it’s hogwash!”

Selling armaments was a serious crime.

Alchemy crossbows and firearms, especially rechargeable types, were all controlled by the empire.

Let alone the gold level cannon that could collapse city walls.

Hei Fei was a slave trafficker, at most he plundered small beastman villages, stole away the populace, and sold them into slavery.

The slave trade was allowed and considered an honorable business.

But arms smuggling was different.

It was a crime!

Arms smugglers were groups of fugitives with a backer.

Without a backer, how could they obtain such arms, how could they sell them?

The water was very deep here, and Hei Fei was unsure of it.

He glanced at the tavern, then sighed, “Move, we’ll sleep on the ship tonight.”

After a pause, he then added, “All of you be on guard.”

After this group of people left, the tavern became lively once again.

The goblins were taken away, the small stage was quickly wiped clean, then the band and female dancer took center stage once again.

The customers watching this production started to converse more energetically.

Disguised as a normal person, the silver level warlock, Mo Kong, had arrived in this place at some unknown time and took the spot where the first mate had been sitting.

“That iron level is also somewhat interesting.”

Outwardly, he was drinking, while in reality, he was converging Tripleblade’s life aura.

Not long after, he left.

“Aura, still need a bit more…”

Night enveloped the town as Mo Kong walked towards the port.

The ships berthed at the port were the survivors’ five and Hei Fei’s three.

Mo Kong gazed at the survivors’ distant ships and noticed the weak precautions onboard. Despite having many sailors keeping watch, due to the painful training during the day, they were dispirited and looked like they wanted to sleep.

Mo Kong hid in a corner and wanted to infiltrate the place when suddenly, he stopped.

He looked at the seabirds perched on top of a ship, then gradually became bewildered.

“Alchemy puppets?”

“Hmph, these people have many types of weapons.”

Mo Kong did not give up, as his gaze shifted towards Hei Fei’s ships.

Hei Fei’s ships also had sailors visible in the wind, they were on deck, the bow, the aft, and on the mast keeping watch.

They were heavily guarded.

Mo Kong carefully observed for a moment, sneered, then began to quietly sneak in.

On the ship, he smoothly found the first mate.

Because of his close quarters fight with Tripleblade, he still had some residual life aura from the latter.

“Too hard to act on…”

Mo Kong felt somewhat awkward.

Because Tripleblade easily defeated the first mate and lost face, Hei Fei punished him by putting him in charge of tonight’s watch.

He was an iron level, and his heart was full of indignance, his bright eyes were completely wary, and he frequently moved.

Although Mo Kong was a silver level warlock, many of his methods were useless. After all, most magics needed motions that would leak his aura.

“I need to keep an eye on time, if this takes too long, that iron level swordsman’s aura will scatter.”

Mo Kong thought for a bit, and a stratagem appeared in his mind.

He quietly left the first mate, and went down some stairs, into the ship. Regardless of whether it was the first mate or the patrolling sailors, none of them noticed a trace of him.

He entered the hold and saw many unconscious beastmen.

These beastmen were in a drugged sleep. Only a part of them were awoken, they were wrapped in iron chains and tightly watched.

When dawn came, these people would be escorted to the market and sold.

Mo Kong glanced at these people, then gave up.

These people were old, weak, or disabled. Some were even close to death, otherwise Hei Fei wouldn’t have been pressed to dump them here.

Mo Kong went further into the depths of the hold and chose his target.

He took out a bottle of perfume and popped the cork.

A gentle puff of smoke quickly dissipated into the hold.

The beastmen captives’ eyes slackened and they quickly became conscious.

Mo Kong quietly faded away and headed towards the other ship holds to spread the fragrance.

These people had been unconscious, but because of the fragrance, their consciousness had been restored.

Hei Fei and the others were very confident in their incapacitating agents, and due to them taking precautions against the survivors, most of the sailors were on the deck.

Like Mo Kong wanted, the beastmen awoke and began to secretly assemble together.

“Our village is already destroyed!”

“Boohoo…father, he’s dead, those devils killed him.”

“Vengeance, we will kill them!”

The beastmen united against a common enemy.

But their leaders were not blinded by hatred.

“We only awoke because of their carelessness. We must grab hold of this golden opportunity; we won’t have a second!”

“We aren’t those demons’ match! I still feel drugged and only have half my strength.”

“We are unarmed and defenseless!”

“Even if we die, we won’t make it easy!”

A beastman elder looked at the younger one, “Young patriarch, although our clan has been ruined, it hasn’t been destroyed.”

“We will stir up a rebellion and give you a chance to escape.”

The young patriarch shook his head with a resolute bearing, “I won’t abandon you; we will die together!”

“No. You cannot die, you must live.” The elder put their hands on the young patriarch.

“Even if we rebel, they won’t kill us.”

“Because we are their merchandise, they will capture us alive and treat us as slaves to sell off.”

“As long as you survive, young patriarch, and gather strength outside, you can return, save us, and allow our clan to emerge once again!”

But the young patriarch did not heed their advice, “I won’t desert you all!”

Just as the elder was about to speak, the sailors above started to yell.

They noticed that people in the holds weren’t responding and began to muster together to rush down.

“Come quickly, the beastmen are waking up!”

The beastman elder sighed, “It’s too late.”

“Charge!”

“We will kill them together.” The young patriarch took the lead and charged first.

The hold immediately exploded with noise.

The beastmen gathered together and grappled with the human sailors

The beastmen were united, and hatred caused their morale to skyrocket in spite of life or death.

Although there were many sailors, in the narrow confines of the ship, they could not display their full forces. Their equipment was also of subpar quality; at best they had leather armor and cutlasses.

During this time, when everyone thronged together and rubbed shoulders to do battle, the situation sank into chaos and deadlock.

“What’s going on?” The first mate arrived at the mouth of the stairs but was in no rush to go down.

A subordinate reported that the beastmen were rebelling.

The first mate stood there in indefinite bewilderment.

“We drugged them all, how did they awaken?”

Slave traffickers were very confident in their drugs, otherwise they wouldn’t have sent out this ship with people on it.

“There is something strange inside!”

The first mate thought of the survivors first.

“Don’t tell me they really want to attack us?”

“Previously, it was obvious they were dissatisfied with our quoted price. If they could snatch our slaves, why should they buy them?”

That group of wretched fugitives!”

Thus, the first mate stood above deck and didn’t dare to go down.

He was scared that the survivors had caused this, if this was the case, then there would be an expert hidden down there, setting up a trap specially built for him to fall into.

“Ask the captain to help!” The first mate instructed an underling.

The underling went and came back with news, the captain had insulted the first mate, and asked why he, a cultivator and his first mate, hadn’t acted yet to suppress the rebellion.

The first mate looked at the flagship and found Hei Fei standing there, coldly watching him, his admonishment very strong.

“Damm it!” The first mate immediately realized that Hei Fei had the same worries as him.

Hei Fei wanted his first made to charge in first and let him trigger whatever traps and schemes there were first.

The first mate had no choice but to charge in.

He was an iron level and immediately suppressed the situation.

Mo Kong quietly withdrew from the chaos and moved with the stream of people to approach the first mate and gently draw Tripleblade’s aura from him.

Despite achieving his original aim, he was hesitant to leave.

Because he noticed the first mate and the young beastman patriarch were deadlocked with each other.

The young beastman patriarch was also iron level, but because of the drugs, he could only display a third of his strength.

However, the first mate was wary of potential traps, most of his mind wasn’t focused on the young patriarch, thus creating this kind of battlefield situation.

“Perhaps, I can accomplish more.” After noticing this spectacle, Mo Kong chose to stay and help the beastmen.

With his hidden magic, the beastman elder suddenly noticed a swelling power emitting from his body.

At that moment, he seemed to have regained the pinnacle of his youthful stamina.

The beastman elder did not help the young patriarch, instead he used all of his strength to activate his diminished battle qi and smash through the hull.

“Young patriarch, this way!” He roared, then charged forth to replace the young patriarch in his duel with the first mate.

“Elder?!” The young patriarch wanted to keep fighting on zealously.

“Go now!” The beastman elder anxiously cried.

“Go, go!” Many of the beast men grabbed the young patriarch and pulled him away.

The young patriarch was already seriously injured and didn’t have much strength left. Despite struggling with all of his might, the other beastmen dragged him away.

“Go, this way!” As a silver level warlock, Mo Kong was an expert at confusing the mind.

Presently, he was hidden in the darkness and was quietly influencing the beastmen’s decisions.

The beastmen, in a panic, chose not to go further inland, but instead rushed towards the survivors’ ships.

Mo Kong wanted to use them to attack the survivors’ defensive perimeter and expose the many cards in their hands.

If it also triggered a conflict between Hei Fei’s group and the survivors, then it would be even better for him!

Chapter 255: Return My Slaves

On one of the survivors’ ships, Zong Ge carefully listened to Tripleblade’s report.

“Hei Fei mainly has oxmen and dogmen on hand, as well as a few goblins.”

“An ordinary adult male ox-man is priced at 8 gold. An ordinary adult male dogman is priced at 7 gold.”

“They have twelve bronze level ox-men, all fighters and priced from 60-90 gold. The bronze level dogmen are also fighters and have similar prices on them.”

“They also have three iron level fighters. One female ox-men, who is disabled and old, and priced at 580 gold. Two dogmen elders priced at 730 gold, while the young dogman patriarch is the most expensive, they want 940 gold.”

Zong Ge was somewhat pleased with what he heard.

Because he wanted the dogmen, as they were cheaper than the ox-men.

But he was still very vexed.

Ordinary adult male dogmen were still 7 gold each!

He had never had more than 5 gold on his person ever.

Zong Ge also didn’t understand the market pricing of slaves, so he asked Tripleblade, “Can it be cheaper?”

Tripleblade did not tell him about the minor conflict inside the tavern, instead he shook his head, “I already pushed down the price to its absolute lowest.”

Zong Ge’s face flashed with agony, “Even if that’s the lowest price, we do not have that much money.”

In fact, he used to have some savings too.

However, during the shipwreck of the Hog’s Kiss, he had lost it.

The survivors were nearly in the same situation as Zong Ge.

There had been almost no gold coins in the spoils they had seized from War Merchant.

“My Lord.” Tripleblade deliberated for a bit, then suggested, “We can make a request to the captain and the others.”

“If we all raise money, we will surely have enough.”

“When that happens, we can look to buy them.”

Zong Ge shook his head, “Were we not clear about their situation? Even if they managed to keep some money through the shipwreck, who would hold onto such superfluous and heavy things when fleeing for their lives?”

Tripleblade continued, “In fact, we have money!”

“We have many alchemy crossbows and firearms, along with countless alchemy recipes. Take one out at random and exchange it for a large amount of money.”

“But now, all of those things are in the captain’s and his people’s hands, we can request a part of it, this is a reasonable request.”

“We have contributed a lot to reach today.”

Zong Ge nodded, “It is only fair!”

“But.” He shook his head again, “You should know that our outward identity is a just established mercenary group, while in reality hinting to others that we are actually arms smuggling criminals.”

“This identity is very useful and has already dispelled the doubts of outsiders and also makes them afraid of our backer, they won’t dare to offend us.”

“As arms smugglers, we cannot sell our weapons as we please, we have a specific purchaser in the Wilderness Continent.”

“If we sell, it will be a flaw.”

“Those recipes are also the same, we cannot wantonly sell them and provoke suspicion.”

“So, it’s that difficult…” Tripleblade frowned and bowed his head in perplexion.

Zong Ge nodded and looked at the ship where Cang Xu was at, “We are not the only ones that are perplexed.”

At that moment, a loud noise suddenly came from outside.

There was a problem.

Zong Ge and Tripleblade glanced at each other, then immediately left the bow house and arrived on the deck.

A sailor on duty ran over to report.

“A suspected slave rebellion?” Zong Ge and Tripleblade gazed at Hei Fe’s ships.

The commotion was getting bigger.

“What in the devil are Hei Fei and his men doing?” Zong Ge and Tripleblade were somewhat puzzled.

According to the gathered intelligence, Hei Fei and his men were veteran slave traffickers, such a mistake shouldn’t have happened.

Especially now, Hei Fei and his officers hadn’t acted immediately, rather they waited to see what the situation was like.

Were these not qualified slave traffickers?

Zong Ge did not know that Hei Fei and his men were actually guarding against the survivors.

Tripleblade thoughtfully said, “It might have something to do with us.”

He briefly described what had transpired.

Zong Ge clapped Tripleblade’s shoulder, “You did well.”

Tripleblade’s anger when he saw his fellow goblins killed was understandable. What pleased Zong Ge even more was that Tripleblade didn’t allow it to influence his emotions, instead he took advantage of it to beat down the price.

Tripleblade’s tough negotiation also made Zong Ge satisfied, it agreed with his temperament.

Tripleblade’s heart was moved when he sensed Zong Ge’s support, and he suggested, “My Lord, it would be for the best if we took advantage of this opportunity…”

Zong Ge firmly shook his head, and he murmured, “We are not thieves.”

While the two were discussing, the hull of a ship suddenly split open, and soon after, the rebelling beastmen poured out.

Zong Ge and Tripleblade did not notice warlock Mo Kong, they only saw a group of frantic beastmen charging at them.

Tripleblade instantly grew nervous, “My Lord, should we chase them away?”

But Zong Ge replied, “Not necessary yet. If we refuse this gift, we won’t give Hei Fei any face. Make the arrangements.

Tripleblade exposed a smile and accepted the order.

The sailors acted at once and shouted loudly at each other. Then they split, with some chasing down the escaping beastmen and the others gathering together to welcome Hei Fei and his men who were in hot pursuit.

“Stand back! Come any closer and we will open fire!” Tripleblade was the first to act.

The first mate stopped at once; he had eaten a loss from Tripleblade already.

Behind Tripleblade was a row of sailors, each of them holding an alchemy crossbow or gun.

The first mate knew Rou Zang’s swordsmen force had been slain by these alchemical arms.

How could they even compare to Rou Zang’s battle squads?”

In the darkness, Mo Kong laughed and quietly changed the effect of his spell.

The young patriarch and his clansmen were bewildered, their wisdom constantly affected by the magic.

The magic influenced them to unconsciously flee towards the survivors’ group.

Now the magic confused them further, causing them to pace back and forth and lose the courage to break out further.

Thus, Captain Hei Fei had no choice but to step forward.

These beastmen were very valuable, especially the young patriarch. Him alone was worth almost a thousand coins, and if they were fortunate, they could sell him for even more!

Hei Fei, with a calm face, interrogated Tripleblade, “Friend, what is the meaning of this?”

“Those are our slaves.”

“Wealth we took great trouble in getting.”

“Return it to us, and you will receive our friendship. The price we discussed previously, we can lower it a bit more.”

Tripleblade coldly snorted and put up his hand to stop the approaching Hei Fei, “Stop first, Captain Hei Fei.”

“Don’t provoke a misunderstanding.”

“A group of beastmen rushed into our resting area, of course we want to get the situation under control. Don’t tell me you wanted these beastmen to attack our ships?”

Hei Fei took a deep breath, then did his best to ease the tension, “You’ve done me a favor, I want to thank you for helping us in intercepting those slaves.”

“The thank you gift will be to your satisfaction.”

“Before that, however, let me first lock up those lowly and irascible slaves again.”

After saying that, Captain Hei Fei waved his hand, indicating to his subordinates to follow.

The first mate was hesitant against so many alchemy crossbows and firearms.

But at that moment, Mo Kong used another magic spell.

He had recently collected a sufficient amount of both Tripleblade’s and the first mate’s auras.

Using these life auras, he successfully applied a curse on the first mate.

The first mate’s mind was affected, causing him to suddenly grow furious and hateful. He glared at Tripleblade and walked forward, “Follow me!”

With him in the lead, and compelled by Captain Hei Fei’s authority, their men struck towards the beastmen.

The beastmen immediately grew restless and circled tightly around the young patriarch.

The young patriarch was both annoyed and furious, he wanted to stand, however his heavy injuries left him struggling and dependent on the support of others.

The first mate’s action pleased Captain Hei Fei.

The disregard for danger these people showed as they dashed in also made the survivors hesitant.

After all, this was not the countryside or the sea, this was a port.

The firearms and crossbows were more deterrents, none of them really wanted to fire them.

If shots were fired, then the conflict that followed would be bloody.

Even though Hei Fei’s fleet was weaker than the survivors’, why would they want to make war? The survivors had more urgent matters.

This hesitation immediately caused a surge in courage in Hei Fei and his men, and their footsteps became swifter.

“That’s it! Hehehe.” The warlock Mo Kong cackled, then cast another spell.

This time, his target was Tripleblade.

He had enough of Tripleblade’s life aura to curse him.

After a few breaths, Tripleblade’s heart welled with rage.

The previous scene of the goblin being killed at the tavern floated in his mind again.

The first mate, a defeated opponent acting so arrogant, was drawing near, he was provoking Tripleblade!

“Stop!”

“First and final warning.”

“Stop now, otherwise we will fire!”

Tripleblade drew out his cutlass and shouted in a stern voice.

The first mate coldly snorted, straightened his chest, and shouted, “Shoot me if you dare!”

Tripleblade’s anger reached its apex, and he was about to give the order to fire.

Ha!

At that moment, a wild sound was heard.

A two handed sword was hurled through the air, it passed over Tripleblade, over the first mate and his men, and was going straight towards Captain Hei Fei.

Hei Fei’s eyes flashed angrily, and in a panic, he took out his cutlass to counter it.

Ding.

With a crisp clang, the cutlass in Hei Fei’s hands ferociously clashed against the two handed sword, creating an eruption of dazzling sparks.

The two handed sword fluttered into the air, then fell, pointed down, and inserted itself into the floor tiles. The silver battle qi emitting around the sword blade gradually extinguished.

Hei Fei had been forced back a few steps, the hand that held the cutlass was slightly trembling.

He was both startled and furious as he looked up at a ship, there stood a tall and sturdy figure.

“Shi Qi!” Hei Fei growled, revealing the identity of the one who threw the sword.

“Hei Fei.” Shi Qi (Zong Ge) standing on the ship was overlooking the scene below, “If you want to make war, I will fight you to the end.”

Hei Fei took a deep breath, “My slaves…”

Zong Ge quickly interrupted him, “All I know is that we are detaining these beastmen. No matter where they are from, even Rou Zang’s sailors are now my captives.”

Hei Fei’s eyes flared open angrily, “You aren’t following the rules. You must know that this is not the sea, this is imperial territory, this is Lord Tan Qiu’s territory!”

Just as his voice came out, the city guard rushed forth from a street corner.

The elite soldiers quickly spread out in an encirclement; their leader was a male silver level fighter.

It was the city guard commander that had been absent from the banquet.

“What is going on here?” The high-ranking officer was solemn.

Zong Ge was somewhat surprised, the city guard had appeared rather quickly.

Originally, after the beastmen had rebelled, Hei Fei was worried this was a plot by the survivors and had asked Tan Qiu for help.

They were staying at the port and had paid the anchorage fee; thus, they should receive Tan Qiu’s protection.

“They are stealing my slaves. Those evildoers!” Captain Hei Fei directly lamented.

“A group of people attacked my ship, I knocked them down, they are now my captives.” Zong Ge calmly replied.

After the city guard commander realized what the situation was, he pursed his brows.

If the survivors had directly attacked Hei Fei’s ships and stole the slaves, the city guards would naturally want to defend justice and help Hei Fei’s people deal with the survivors.

But, as it turned out, the beastmen had rebelled, fled here, and became the survivors’ captives.

From the survivors’ point of view, this was entirely self-defense.

Hei Fei believed that everything was a trick by the survivors. Why did the beastmen run towards the survivors’ ships?”

The words of both sides were reasonable.

Wholly incapable of dealing with this and having an incessant headache, the city guard commander said, “Since neither of you will concede, I ask that when dawn comes, you two will go to his Lordship’s castle and make your case.”

The commander was self-aware and knew that this kind of affair was difficult to deal with, something he was no expert in dealing with.

Since it didn’t concern him, it would be for the best if he handed it over to his immediate superior.

Chapter 256: President Zi Di

The Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Negative energy filled the meditation room.

A ghost girl was floating in the strongest concentration of negative energy,

She had exceptional human beauty, and her spectral appearance also gave off a cold captivation.

The ghostly Zi Di’s eyes were shut tight, and her face exposed her suffering.

It was not easy for an ordinary person to cross the crucial step that elevated them into a magician.

This was ascending one’s life level.

Zi Di had experience. She had become a bronze level magician in life.

But that process was quite natural.

She had the aptitude, and her bloodline assisted her. She had also taken magic potions, and within a few months, she naturally broke through.

This was different, she had compressed months of accumulation into a single night.

To accomplish this step, one first needed to be able to perceive external energy, then be capable of absorbing it and converting it.

The perception of external elements was the most important talent of magicians.

The more talented a magician was, the stronger, wider, and more precise their perception was.

Compared to the first two, the last one was the most crucial.

Magicians converted external energy using meditation.

Even if a normal person successfully meditated, it was very difficult to attain this standard.

But Zi Di was different.

She had abundant experience in meditating. She had been an iron level magician when she was alive, and her meditation method primarily absorbed the fire element.

The fire element was classified as a neutral energy type.

Compared to fire, negative energy was colder and more inharmonious with life.

Zi Di was now a ghost, and negative energy enveloped her entire being.

As she meditated, the negative energy filled her body and gradually changed.

The chilliness grew deeper, as if Zi Di had fallen into a cave of ice.

In her perception, at first, she saw the abundant negative energy as an ashen mist, now it had accumulated into a shroud of darkness that wrapped around her closely.

This was the right place.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish’s meditation room could break down necromantic materials, collect the negative energy, and construct a meditative environment suitable for necromancers.

Meditation rooms were very important to magicians; it could improve their efficiency greatly.

Zi Di gradually felt like she was sinking into the depths of an ocean, she was constantly sinking, constantly falling as everything around her was deathly still.

She seemed to be dissolving into the piercing icy coldness.

As she endured, Zi Di recalled her past.

As an apprentice magician, she studied at the College of Magic.

It had been a difficult time.

She began to truly become independent; however, she also realized the limits of her potential.

Her bloodline was only at the iron level.

However, her boyfriend suddenly awakened his bloodline and transformed into a dragonman, he had exceptional talent as a fire magician!

His progress grew wider, his achievements greater, and more attention was piled onto him.

He grew more dazzling, and the number of female apprentices by his side began to increase.

Those female suitors publicly and privately pressured Zi Di.

“You don’t deserve him!”

“What have you accomplished in your cultivation? Your bloodline has already decided that you’ll only ever be an iron level. However, he can become a silver level, a gold level, and even a legend level!”

“Don’t continue to be a burden on him. The sparrow and the goshawk cannot fly together.”

“There is a kind of love called letting go!”

Zi Di persevered, she vowed to never give up, and she believed her boyfriend loved her.

However, sad news arrived.

The boyfriend’s parents forced him to wed another female apprentice that had outstanding talent.

The dragonman boyfriend comforted Zi Di, “I won’t leave you; I don’t have any feelings for her. You must believe me, I love you.”

“I trust you!” Zi Di never doubted her boyfriend’s heart.

But it was because of this belief that she felt even more pained.

“I don’t deserve to be with him; I am a burden on him!”

“What, what can I do to deserve him?”

“No matter what, as long as I can stand side by side with it, it doesn’t matter what I do!”

Zi Di yelled at the sky.

In the shadowy swamp, the image of her father’s corpse came to mind.

“My princess, my love.” Her dead father handed her a piece of parchment.

The parchment unfolded, and its contents alarmed Zi Di.

Soul Devour spell?

Necromancy?!

She hesitated; she struggled.

But in the end, she reached out her deathly pale hands.

The moment those ten trembling fingers touched the parchment, they shrank back, as if touching a red iron.

“Kill them all, kill them all!” Her father urged, then shouted, “Vengeance, retribution!”

Darkness burst from Zi Di’s eyes, and her face warped as she reached out and viciously grabbed the parchment.

She was just about to assimilate the parchment into herself, when suddenly, she paused.

“Hold on.”

“Wait a minute.”

“This, this is…an illusion!”

An instant later, she opened her eyes to see the meditation room.

The dense negative energy was like a layer of green mist so dark that it was black, it enveloped her, and clinging to her with unseen fingers.

Zi Di’s mind quickly sobered up.

She sensed a magic power pool within her.

She was now a magician.

Meditation was not without its risks.

It needed a serene and clear mind.

Often times, this process would make bare and be overflowed by one’s personal thoughts, desires, and feelings that have been unconsciously accumulated during daily life.

Sometimes, this would create hallucinations.

If Zi Di was still deceived and assimilated with the parchment scroll, it would’ve indicated that she decided to indulge in her desires and allowed her emotions to subsume her reason.

Her mind would grow muddled, she would become a bloodthirsty murderer, and she would become an undead monster that relied on her instincts to act.

Luckily, she was experienced, and in that critical moment, she struggled free from it.

She examined her magic power pool.

It had shrunk into a dot and was hidden in the depths of her head, as if it was resting in a void.

You could say that both existed and didn’t exist.

This was the foundation of a magician.

From now on, Zi Di could absorb external energy through meditation and convert it into her own undead magic power.

“Finally, I’ve become a necromancer.”

Zi Di’s mood was somewhat complex.

She continued to meditate, absorbing and converting the energy within her.

With every part that was converted, she felt lighter.

The negative energy eroded her mind, erased her humanity, and tormented her with ghostly hunger.

After changing it into magic power, this erosion was greatly lessened.

Zi Di truly grasped this power!

After an unknown amount of time, she once more slowly opened her eyes.

This time, she had converted all of the negative energy into necromancy magic power.

The magic power in her pool would spring forth when needed.

The erosion of negative energy still existed.

“Ghosts themselves are based in negative energy.”

“If I completely absorb this negative energy, my life shape will be destroyed!”

Zi Di understood this principle.

Thus, she pursued resurrection.

She opened the meditation room door and saw the dragonman youngster standing guard.

“I succeeded.” The girl smiled calmly.

The dragonman youngster nodded, “I knew you could do it.”

The girl sensed the young man’s trust in her and couldn’t help but recall the hallucination she saw earlier.

Love, selfishness, hatred, and vengeance…

It seemed their love had already covered her father’s animosity.

After confirming Zi Di was well, the dragonman youngster said, “I need to leave the Deep Sea Monster Fish first, there’s been a little trouble.”

“Eh? What happened?”

A short while later.

The port, the flagship’s captain’s room.

The dragonman youngster, Zong Ge, Cang Xu, and Tripleblade gathered together.

The young beastman patriarch faced them, and through clenched teeth, swore, “I request that you rescue my people!”

“I will appreciate your benevolence for my entire life.”

“I will command my clansmen to swear loyalty to you, and to never betray you!”

He was called Kuai Tui, he was the young patriarch of the shiba inu beastman. After Zong Ge exposed his intent to recruit him, he quickly decided to implore the survivors’ leaders to save his clansmen.

The dragonman youngster sent him away, then looked at the others.

Zi Di’s figure also appeared.

“Tell me what you think.” The dragonman said as he looked around.

Zong Ge was the first to speak, he pointed out that the sailors were inadequate, it would be very difficult to raise and change their combat power.

“Recruiting these dogmen is necessary.”

“The loyalty of dogmen is well known, the most loyal of all include earth dogmen, shiba inu dogmen, sled dogmen, sheep dogmen, and pine lion dogmen.”

“Slave and servant armies can also become strong forces.”

“If we help Kuai Tui, and save his clansmen, we will have truly reliable subordinates.”

“Correct.” Tripleblade echoed in agreement, “The Deep Sea Monster Fish should not be exposed, and we need surface identities to conceal ourselves.”

“The pirates are rampant right now, and we cannot avoid naval battles.”

“In the worst case scenario, if we cannot obtain the empire’s understanding, we need to expand our power and be able to rely on it.”

Cang Xu replied, “Indeed, when we crossed blades with Rou Zang, the sailor’s bombardment fell short, their firepower was incapable of threatening the Bath Tub at all.”

“However.” Cang Xu changed the subject, “I believe now is not the time to replenish our troops.”

“We have many sailors, if we also bring on these beastmen, it will overload the ships.”

“Furthermore, our funds right now are in short supply.

“Our top priority is still the construction of an Anti-investigation and Prophecy formation.”

“In fact, purchasing the materials needed is very difficult. The main challenge is capital, in fact, I was in the middle of worrying about this matter.”

After pausing for a moment, Cang Xu finally said, “The most crucial thing is still Tan Qiu’s attitude.”

“This morning, when Zong Ge and Hei Fei made their case, Tan Qiu’s attitude was quite ambiguous.”

“On the one hand, he wants to hire us to deal with Rou Zang. On the other hand, he also covets those beastmen slaves.”

“If Hei Fei reaches a compromise with Tan Qiu, he will sell the beastmen slaves for a low price. This benefit will perhaps make Tan Qiu take Hei Fei’s side, and if that happens, I fear we will have to give Kuai Tui back to them.”

For a time, everyone was silent.

Neither Zong Ge nor Tripleblade refuted those points.

Cang Xu’s words also made sense, and the facts were just as stated.

The survivors had already reached a consensus that they wouldn’t deal with Rou Zang.

The survivors had more important matters to do, and dealing with Rou Zang was very risky.

Using force against Tan Qiu and Hei Fei was an even worse strategy.

Tan Qiu had five or six silver levels, while the survivors only had two. Not even Rou Zang would choose to storm this island. If they did that, Hei Fei would probably wake up with a smile every morning.

The final thing they could do was to leave immediately, take Kuai Tui and his people away from Snake Rat Island, preventing anyone from taking the beastmen back.

This was also inappropriate.

Don’t even mention Rou Zang pursuing them, the survivors haven’t even achieved their main goal yet.

They needed to retrieve the Green Jade Gold Coffin!

This divine artifact had incalculable worth, and the survivors would not let it go.

But currently, there haven’t been any clues leading towards it.

Silence enveloped the captain’s room for a time.

The dragonman youngster racked his brains and felt the perplexity of the current situation.

Kui Tui could increase their strength, of course he wanted him.

However, currently, the beastmen aren’t that important.

They were merely a fuse, nothing more.

Hei Fei wanted them returned; they were his money!

The survivors didn’t want to deal with Rou Zang, they wanted to find the Green Jade Gold Coffin and leave. Kui Tui and his clansmen were dispensable, additional benefits they could do without.

Tan Qiu wanted to gather as many forces as possible together to deal with Rou Zang.

Rou Zang was Snake Rat Island’s greatest threat and was choking it.

To Tan Qiu, Hei Fei and Zong Ge’s conflict was not troublesome to deal with, unlike what the city guard commander believed.

Tan Qiu should’ve been very happy, because he had both Hei Fei and the survivors in his hand.

This matter caused these two parties to beseech him.

Hei Fei, the survivors, Tan Qiu, as well as Rou Zang outside had their strengths, weaknesses, and goals. The contests between them formed a maelstrom.

The young captain already sensed that the maelstrom they were in was beginning to grow deeper.

Zi Di gazed at the price list in her hands and quietly said, “An ordinary adult male oxmen is sold for 8 gold. An ordinary adult dogmen is sold for 7 gold.

“This price is very reasonable and is even a bit lower than the market price.”

Everyone stared blankly.

Zi Di smiled and explained, “A slave’s price has many factors influencing it. For example, their health, their strength, their abilities, and so on.”

“For instance, in some areas, the price of female slaves exceeds that of male slaves. Because owners believe female slaves are more obedient.”

“If a slave has skills like knowing multiple languages, is an architect, is a songstress, a musician, or a dancer, their price will also be higher.”

“Generally, an adult human laborer slave costs about 3 gold. Heavy laborers are priced at 6 gold. Those that can do some management or scholarly tasks will be priced at around 9 gold.”
