“If it is a beastman, because beastmen are far stronger than humans, their market value is likewise higher too. An adult male can do three times the labor of a human, however, they are only priced at 8 gold, and thus, obviously cheaper.”

“Ordinary beastmen slaves are normally used as farmers, miners, builders, shepherds, and other manual labor jobs. Because male oxmen are stronger than dogmen, their selling price is generally greater than dogmen.”

“This kind of pattern is not applicable to cultivators. Because on the whole, cultivator slaves are not used for basic labor.”

“Bronze level fighters are sold at ten times the amount of normal slaves. Iron levels are sold at ten times the amount of bronze level, and silver levels sold at ten times that.”

“Silver level slaves are already rare. They are sold anywhere from 1,000 to 10,000 gold each. A price comparable to gold level weapons or armor.”

“Magicians are more expensive than fighters.”

“Gold level slaves are seldom seen.”

“When slaves are traded, both the seller and the buyer need to pay the empire taxes. The tax varies in every noble’s domain and had been high. Recently, these last few years, because the empire invaded the Wilderness Continent, the empire reduced the slave transaction tax and a great amount of beastmen slaves flooded the market. During peacetime, an ordinary adult male oxmen cost at least 10 gold.”

Zi Di raised the price list in her hands, “On here, the dogmen young patriarch, Kuai Tui, is being sold for 940 gold. He is only an iron level, yet he is priced at almost 1,000 gold. It is evident that his potential is great and is likely to become a silver level in the future.”

Zong Ge was silent for a moment, then said, “Miss Zi Di, thank you for your explanation. But it wasn’t what we wanted to hear.”

But Zi Di laughed, “No, I’m not finished yet.”

“If we only saved Kui Tui’s clansmen, wouldn’t that only be a limited improvement to our military power?”

“Of course, the more dogmen, the better.” Said Zong Ge.

Cang Xu interjected, “Our funds have a large shortfall, we cannot purchase all of the magic materials.”

“Actually, we have money.” Said Zi Di.

Everyone stared at her.

Zi Di then said, “Hei Fei sells slaves, we too have people we can resell.”

“Nowadays, we have too many human sailors, no?”

“You just said that if we add in these dogmen, our ships will be overloaded. That won’t be conducive to a long voyage.”

Everyone had different expressions.

Tripleblade mulled it over, “Strictly speaking, those fisherfolk were saved by us. The pirates from Da Zhao’s pirate group are our captives. We are entirely within our rights to resell them.”

“No!” The dragonman youngster resolutely refused, his expression was very solemn, “Don’t tell me you want to treat those fisherfolk and pirates as slaves to traffic?”

“Of course not.” Zi Di definitively vetoed.

Tripleblade and others were growing increasingly puzzled, confused at what Zi Di was peddling to them.

“Sometimes, the demands of the market can change, and even be created out of nothing.” Zi Di declared her plan.

After everyone heard it, their faces lit up with marvel.

Zong Ge nodded.

The dragonman youngster helplessly smiled.

Cang Xu exclaimed in admiration, “You are worthy of being a merchant alliance president! Based on what you said, this plan is perhaps the perfect solution to this matter.”

Chapter 257: Subterranean Hunting

Early morning, the hunting guild branch was bustling with people.

Zong Ge entered, attracting everyone’s gaze.

“Lord Shi Qi.” The receptionist immediately jogged over to personally receive him.

“Hm.” Zong Ge nodded calmly, then walked to the front desk to look up at the magic panel above it.

Very quickly, he said, “I want that mission.”

The receptionist suddenly exposed their pleasant surprise, then promptly replied, “Quick, process that for Lord Shi Qi!”

Snake Rat Island’s most plentiful resource was its magic beasts.

The island had an abundance of smelt rats and tide snakes; thus, the Hunting Guild established a branch here.

However, Snake Rat Island hadn’t been developed for very long, thus most of its residents were ordinary people, there were few cultivators. The island lord was only a silver level, not a gold.

Ordinary people were unqualified to deal with magic beasts. Cultivators were sparse; thus, the Hunting Guild branch had many missions piled up that were hard to complete.

Zong Ge was a silver level expert that was interested in completing these missions, this was great news for them.

Zong Ge swiftly used the name Shi Qi to register as an apprentice hunter.

“My Lord, this is the mission map, and on the back is detailed information on the mission.” The receptionist respectfully escorted Shi Qi out the door. “I wish your Lordship immediate success, that mission is very easy!”

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance store was conveniently located next to the Hunting Guild branch.

When Zong Ge left the Hunting Guild, he saw Cang Xu walking out of the merchant alliance store.

Cang Xu’s social skills clearly surpassed Zong Ge’s, and it was the same silver level magician that sent him off.

“I’m very sorry, we cannot cooperate with your group in this respect.” The merchant alliance looked cordial, but her tone was firm.

It was evident that talks between Cang Xu and the merchant alliance fell through.

Zong Ge and Cang Xu left the town together.

After they left, many others also left quickly too to pass on information to various parties.

Shi Qi received a hunting mission, the old magician, Zhong Ze, and the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance discussed the sale of magic beast parts.

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance’s refusal was explicit.

In a church.

A priest glanced at this information, causing him to mutter to himself.

He already learned about the conflict between Hei Fei’s group and the survivors last night.

The survivors contacted the Hunting Guild and the merchant alliance, but was their acceptance of a hunting mission a pretense? Or did they want to borrow power to deal with Hei Fei, or even Tan Qiu?

Everything still needed to be watched.

The purist put aside the intelligence and opened a door to a hallway.

It was time for morning prayer.

The Night Devil Hunting Group’s countryside encampment.

The hunting group’s commander was resting after tempering his battle skills when he obtained a report.

Long Fu and Shi Qi were getting involved in hunting missions?

“Interesting.” The hunting commander smiled, “Arms smuggling criminals also need to hold concurrent jobs? Hunting magic beasts is not something one can do with merely high combat power.”

The hunting commander was not at all nervous.

There were many hunting missions, for there were plentiful magic beasts.

it didn’t matter if some of it was given to outsiders.

Like Hei Fei and the others, he also saw through the survivors’ mercenary identity and maintained that they were secretly arms smugglers.

He did not think Shi Qi and the others would do well at hunting.

He waited for them to make fools out of themselves.

The castle.

Tan Qiu was enjoying breakfast.

He put down his black tea and glanced at the trees and fountain outside his ceiling high window. In his left hand was the latest intel.

At the top was the latest recorded actions of Zong Ge, Cang Xu, and the others.

“Before the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance opened its shop, it had reached an agreement with the Hunting Guild.”

“The Hunting Guild branch harvests the magic beasts here and will sell most of them to the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance, making use of the latter’s mature transportation channels to trade.”

“It’s a little strange.”

“Why did Long Fu and Shi Qi’s group suddenly start hunting magic beasts?”

“Hunting is not a simple nor shallow business.”

“Will their simple attempts be amusing? Are they probing the relationships between powers on Snake Rat Island?”

On a slave ship.

Hei Fei coldly snorted, “Shi Qi suddenly went hunting?”

“What is he doing?”

“Does he want to draw me out?”

“Long Fu hasn’t appeared…where is he? Are they doing a feint and conspiring to attack my ships?”

“Impossible.”

“This is imperial territory; this is Tan Qiu’s port.”

Hei Fei’s train of thought diverged, and he became a bit hot-tempered.

How last night’s slave rebellion occurred and the baffling reason they were able to rush out of the ship hold were still a mystery to him.

He shook a bell to call a subordinate, and sternly warned, “Reinforce the defenses!”

Ba Hu was the last one to receive the intelligence.

As a renowned bounty hunter, he also valued gathered intel.

However, because he was a singular person, and wasn’t in a force or a group, his information gathering efficiency couldn’t compare to Tan Qui, Hei Fei, or the others.

“Regardless of their aim, their conflict with Hei Fei’s group has nothing to do with me.”

Ba Hu was currently interested in working with Tan Qiu.

Since that banquet, Tan Qiu and he had privately conversed several times.

Tan Qiu promised: if Rou Zang was defeated, he would give a portion of the pirates, highly wanted criminals, to Ba Hu.

This aroused Ba Hu’s interest.

Ba Hu was a bounty hunter and developed at the Bounty Hunter Guild. Pursuing fugitives and completing bounties was how he lived.

He was a lone man and found it difficult to deal with Rou Zang’s large pirate group. But with the help of Tan Qiu’s strength, perhaps his guild rank could ascend substantially.

Snake Rat Island had massive subterranean caverns.

It was very easy to enter these subterranean areas, for there were many natural passages scattered across its surface.

There were both small and large caverns and tunnel entrances.

Thanks to the Hunting Guild branch’s operations, most of these caves had been scouted out.

The circumstances of these caves varied, those with abundant resources had many people entering them, while those that were dangerous and infertile had fewer hunters visiting them.

The Night Devil Hunting Group was Snake Rat Island’s largest hunting group, and occupied the best cave, enjoying all of its resources.

The mission Zong Ge chose was not at one of the popular caves, but in a remote area.

The further they went into the countryside, the harder it was to track Zong Ge and Cang Xu.

“This is the place.” Zong Ge compared it to the map.

This cave mouth was three meters wide. Half of it was open wide, allowing sun to shine through, illuminating it and allowing one to see the dense cyan vines growing inside.

Zong Ge and Cang Xu quickly vanished into the cave.

The scouts following them hesitated one after the other, none of them entered the cave.

“A smart calculation.” Cang Xu smiled.

After Zong Ge and Cang Xu entered the cave, they didn’t enter deeper, rather they waited for a spell, and didn’t notice anyone following them.

If one did, they would’ve disposed of them.

These were humble scouts and were not their opponents.

The tunnel was sealed and was an ideal place to bury a murdered corpse.

Even if it was exposed, the survivors would make their stand. They were hunting, and these people were tracking them, so they must have harbored dark thoughts, and were so evil that even death would be an insufficient punishment.

The two then officially explored the cave.

The terrain slowly sloped downward, and the deeper they went, the darker and damper it was.

Since Cang Xu was a magician, light was not a problem.

Zong Ge took out a cutlass and lifted a small wooden shield.

These were both common equipment. In this narrow cave, they were more convenient to use than his broadsword.

For this first mission, hunting was a side objective, their main job was to explore nearby caves. For example, bringing back intel on the topography, the distribution of magic beast groups, and other such things.

If those scouts had kept following them, they would have discovered something strange.

Because according to common sense, Zong Ge and Cang Xu should’ve encountered magic beasts already.

This was the exploration period, the nearby caves hadn’t been excavated yet, and there should’ve been many tide snakes living here.

But it took Cang Xu and Zong Ge 15 minutes to see a living animal.

It was not a snake, nor a rat.

Instead, it was a bird.

A scouting bird puppet.

When the bird saw Cang Xu and Zong Ge, it didn’t react, rather it continued to move its head to look all over the place.

When Cang Xu and Zong Ge saw the bird, they knew their exploration route was correct.

This time, they were merely follow-up troops.

The dragonman youngster had secretly set out a long while earlier.

With the Deceptive Disguise formation and the Bloodcore transformations, he could easily sneak into any location on Snake Rat Island. Let alone a place where people rarely tread.

Continuing forward, Cang Xu and Zong Ge successively noticed smashed rocks, splattered blood, and other traces of fighting.

It was very clear that many traces and clues had been intentionally cleaned up.

Then in one location, Zong Ge suddenly stopped and stooped down to pick up a scale.

This snake scale was as big as a hand palm. It had been roasted, its edges were uneven, and it looked like it had been torn apart in an explosion.

The half beastman gaze focused, “It seems our captain has already switched his battle qi. This battle qi is filled with the fire attribute and seems to have powerful explosions.”

This scale evidently hadn’t been cleaned up by the dragonman youngster.

Cleaning up a battlefield required perfection, which was unrealistic.

But Zong Ge, with a single snake scale, was able to infer many things that allowed his keen eyes to see the battlefield clearly.

The necromancer wasn’t surprised, Zong Ge was talented in this area, and had done this both on Mystifying Monster Island and Stone Egg Island.

Cang Xu nodded, “That’s right, in fact, it wasn’t only the Lord Captain. Our Miss Zi Di also broke through.”

Along the way, he sensed some residues of necromantic magic power.

The residues’ aura was very faint, and it wouldn’t be long before they completely dissipated. If Cang Xu wasn’t a necromancer, he wouldn’t have had the keen perception needed to sense them.

The dragonman youngster successfully switched his battle qi, while Zi Di once again became a magician, the survivors knew this was going to happen.

Zong Ge and Cang Xu were happy that their comrades had improved their strength.

The underground tunnel was growing more spacious.

At the beginning, the passage was at most three people wide, now it could fit two carriages side by side in it.

The dragonman youngster and Zi Di intentionally left behind secret marks, so Zong Ge and Cang Xu weren’t misled by the wayside, allowing the exploration team to follow.

By the time they caught up, they were just in time to see the dragonman youngster and Zi Di devastate a snake group.

Chapter 258: The Half Beastman is the Most Normal

The moist stone floor was littered with snake corpses.

The snake group leader’s corpse was the most striking, it had an iron level life aura that was quickly dissipating. It was 3 meters long, was as thick as an adult’s lower arm, and was missing its head, leaving only blood gurgling out its decapitated body.

Around it, there were some corpses that were badly mangled, and some that were strangely intact.

“You’re here.” The dragonman youngster turned around and smiled at Zong Ge and Cang Xu.

He wasn’t in combat, instead he was standing by Zi Di.

Zi Di had disguised herself as a young woman with average looks.

She didn’t greet her companions, as her attention was focused on commanding her ghosts engaged in combat.

The ghosts she controlled were not humanoid, but rather serpentine.

Cang Xu counted attentively, “12.”

Most of the snake ghosts were ordinary, and the two that had a bronze level aura stood out in comparison.

Zi Di treated the two bronze level snake ghosts as her main force, while the other snake ghosts were auxiliary.

The snake ghosts roamed through the air and traveled through the snake group.

The tide snake group wasn’t smart and was completely focused on battling these spirits.

Their tearing at and twisting around the snake ghost were utterly useless.

Only magic had the power to kill or injure the spirits.

The tide snakes had at least two types of racial magics. One was Freezing Ice Ray, the other was Flying Venomous Fang.

The situation was already under the dragonman youngster’s and Zi Di’s control. Not only was the young man guarding her, but there were also three swordsmen guard puppets scattered across this small cave, both to prevent snakes from escaping and to obstruct any unexpected reinforcements.

Apart from this, there were also three scouting birds circling in the air.

Zong Ge and Cang Xu didn’t act, Zi Di obviously was tempering her spell casting ability.

Cang Xu carefully observed the tide snakes.

Suddenly, the eyes of one tide snake shone, and burst with two beams of blue-white light.

The light shot towards a spirit, quickly dying it the same color as it.

The snake ghost stopped in the air, and like a weight, it smashed into the ground and shattered.

The bronze level tide snake destroyed an ordinary snake ghost, causing its condition to immediately fall and become dispirited.

Taking advantage of this chance, a bronze level snake ghost pounced and bit the tide snake’s neck.

The bronze tide snake trembled all over, then fell to the ground, its life aura quickly dissipating.

Upon seeing this, Cang Xu couldn’t help but size Zi Di up.

The young magician seemed to have a gift for using these snake ghosts. Cang Xu knew she recently became a necromancer, and that this was her first time seeing actual combat as one.

But her progress was rapid, and she already understood how to attack with the ghosts and other practical skills.

“Her talent isn’t bad. Only her bloodline shackled her to only the iron level while she was alive.”

“Her road as a necromancer will be longer than the one she walked when she lived.” Cang Xu said to himself.

Many magicians had mediocre talent, but after they became a necromancer, their limits would be increased further.

This was because the foundation of necromancy lay in spirituality and negative energy.

After becoming a necromancer, one’s aptitude is retained, while their body is changed.

Back then, Cang Xu was less talented than even Zi Di. By cultivating necromancy and becoming a corpse wizard, he was able to attain the iron level.

“But we now have a more perfect choice.” Cang Xu again looked at the dragonman youngster, his heart warm.

Zong Ge’s brows wrinkled.

He was once an imperial officer and had a poor opinion of necromancers.

But destiny could not be predicted, for he and a necromancer were comrades that relied on each other for survival on Mystifying Monster Island, and now, here on Snake Rat Island, he had to collaborate even closer.

The half beastmen was disgusted by these snake ghosts, but he also had to recognize the power of necromancy.

“These snake ghosts are fewer than the snake group, and have fewer with cultivation, yet they are suppressing the living snakes.”

“The Flying Venomous Fang is ineffective against the snake ghosts, only the Freezing Ice Ray works. But every time the racial magic is used, it instead gives Zi Di an opportunity to kill a bronze level tide snake leader.”

Like Cang Xu, Zong Ge’s gaze also quickly shifted towards the dragonman youngster.

“He is the one who truly decides the outcome of this battle.”

“If there was an iron level tide snake, Zi Di wouldn’t be its opponent.”

Zi Di was an iron level when alive, and after death, was an iron level ghost girl. But she just became a magician, and her magic power pool was small, thus she was only a bronze level necromancer.

Based on the traces left on the battlefield, Zong Ge could determine that they first used had the swordsman guards charge in to attract the snake group’s attention, then the young man advanced on the snake group and killed their chieftain, throwing the snake group into disarray, and achieving victory in one move.

The snake group’s chieftain was weak, thus the dragonman youngster exploded its heat with a strike.

The half beastman can’t help but feel curious of how strong the young man was now.

When the snake group completely collapsed at last, the survivors began to clean up the battlefield.

Zong Ge noticed there were a few alchemy puppets in the corners of the battlefield.

He immediately recognized that they were from the Deep Sea Monster Fish, they were called deep sea salvagers and looked like nagas.

As the name implied, they were good at salvaging and cleaning battlefields.

The deep sea salvagers were weak, thus when Zi Di battled, they concealed themselves.

Sleek snake tails slid across the stone floor as the naga deep sea salvagers’ six arms began to sweep the battlefield and clean up all kinds of traces.

The dragonman youngster, Zi Di, and the others also weren’t loafing about.

After all, the deep sea salvagers were only alchemy puppets, they needed their help to make up for deficiencies.

The survivors cleaned up the battlefield while chatting to each other.

Cang Xu told the captain and Zi Di that his business request was not approved by the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance.

“Apparently, the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance store and the Hunting Guild branch already have an agreement.”

“Hunting is the Hunting Guild’s core interest, and since the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance store was just built, they don’t want to cause any misunderstandings with the Hunting Guild.”

Zi Di was not surprised.

In fact, she expected that was the case.

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance store was located on the opposite side of the street to the Hunting Guild.

This minor act already hinted that the two powers had a close partnership.

“Our main goal has already been achieved. Whether we can collaborate with the Merchant Alliance is insignificant.” Said the dragonman youngster.

Their actions here were just the first step in Zi Di’s plan.

Attracting the gazes of other forces was a proactive stance. Actions were also a far more powerful explanation than words.

When the battlefield was nearly cleaned up, Zi Di and Cang Xu began to exchange their insights on using necromancy.

Zi Di’s necromancy inheritance was given to her by Cang Xu, thus the two could easily exchange their experiences.

Zi Di believed that although the necromancy inheritance had many necromancy spells, the essence of it was the Soul Control Spell!

The degree one grasped the Soul Control Spell decided how much power one could display from this necromancy inheritance.

“Ghosts are the foundation of this necromancy inheritance. Said Cang Xu, “We use the Soul Summoning Spell and High Level Soul Summoning Spell to make ghosts.”

“Use the Ghostly Possession Spell to store ghosts.”

“Use the Soul Control Spell to control ghosts.”

“The Soul Devourer Spell and Soul Healing Spell can make ghosts our casting materials.”

“Our primary objective is to gather enough ghosts to form a ghost army!”

After glancing at Zi Di controlling the tide snake ghosts, Cang Xu continued, “Bloodlines are different, life shapes are different, and thus, different ghosts can be created.”

“We can gather all kinds of ghosts and establish a troop.”

“Beastmen, elves, and humans are hard to bring together and form an army when alive.”

“But after they die and become ghosts, they can peacefully coexist. Under our control, they can work together sincerely. From that point of view, death is the greatest equalizer and can eliminate most of the differences in this world.”

Upon hearing this, Zong Ge couldn’t help and coldly snort out a retort, “Playing with souls will inevitably backfire. We should respect life!”

Zi Di nodded, “Therefore, I still think we shouldn’t make ghosts out of civilized races.”

Cang Xu explained, “I have never killed innocents to make ghosts. But human ghosts, elf ghosts, and beastman ghosts are important.”

“They have intelligence.”

“Miss Zi Di, your snake spirits lack intelligence and can only act on instinct. But if they have enough spirituality, you only need to give a single order for them to attack autonomously. That will greatly lessen the burden on your spirit.”

“During that fight, you had to focus your entire spirit to control every ghost, that is because you were a magician while alive and had both a mature spirit and meticulous controlling experience.”

“But that is not a long term solution.”

“Even You Shen, the founder of necromancy had limits to his spirit.”

“We need to look for and make intelligent ghosts, then emphasize their cultivation. This has the greatest prospects and profits.”

“So that’s how it is.” Zi Di had some misgivings in controlling ghosts of intelligent races, but upon hearing Cang Xu’s words, she immediately changed her attitude.

“We don’t need to commit murder, nowadays with the rampant pirates, we can run across some and kill them. That is the righteous way.”

Zong Ge sighed, “You two don’t need to explain that to me.”

Cang Xu and Zi Di deliberately did this in front of Zong Ge to probe him, using it to dispel any misunderstandings and biases, along with strengthening their understanding of each other.

Of course, Zong Ge understood.

He was a mature military officer, understood how to weigh the pros and cons, knew all’s fair in war, and was completely unscrupulous.

He also knew that the stronger Zi Di and Cang Xu were, the more helpful they would be towards their redemption.

But just because he understood it did not mean he endorsed it.

He never thought that he would be mingling together with two necromancers.

Despite Cang Xu and Zi Di having the Deceptive Disguise, and from the outside looking like normal magicians, Zong Ge’s beating heart felt thick conflict and disgust towards the two.

He wanted to talk to the young man instead of them.

After all, they were both battle qi cultivators, fought side by side on Mystifying Monster Island, and through the same life or death experiences, had formed a deep battlefield camaraderie.

Zong Ge decided to disregard the two necromancers and turn around to look at the dragonman youngster, intending to ask him about his battle qi.

As a result, he saw the dragonman youngster opening his mouth.

Hiss hiss…

The dragonman youngster forked out a dark blue snake tongue, his mouthful of dragon teeth had vanished, replaced with the venomous fangs of a tide snake.

The venomous fangs projected out, then glinted with a blue-white light.

Zing.

The venomous fangs popped out, then launched into the air like a throwing knife, and in the blink of an eye, inserted themselves into a stone wall.

Click, click, click.

The venomous fangs successively rose up and ejected.

A row of venomous fang throwing knives lined the rock wall.

Zong Ge was silent.

He couldn’t help but begin to reflect on a question: what kind of people do I have by my side?

Compared to these people, he, a half beastman, was actually the most normal of them all.

Chapter 259: Four Man Exploration Team

Every tide snake the dragonman youngster and ghost girl encountered while exploring had been absorbed by the Bloodcore.

Thus, the young man had collected a great amount of the tide snake bloodline, and now, the bronze level bloodline was complete, while the iron level was over half towards completion.

Some secrets of Bloodcore had been shared, and since Cang Xu was united at their side, the dragonman youngster and Zi Di didn’t need to be secretive in front of them.

Thus, it was far more convenient to do such things.

As for Zong Ge, although he currently did not know about the Bloodcore’s existence, he surmised that the youngster’s transformation abilities were related to the Bloodcore.

Cang Xu and Zi Di were of the same mind. The young man had intentionally displayed his abilities in front of Zong Ge to make it easier in the future to indulge the latter in the secret of the Bloodcore.

Apart from this, the young man also needed to practice the tide snake’s racial magics.

This let him understand the tide snakes much more.

This kind of understanding was very profound, for could anyone else turn into a tide snake like the young man could and experience how it felt?

After ejecting about ten Venomous Fang Knives, the young man now only needed to see a few movements to discern whether a tide snake wanted to shoot a venomous fang.

“Anyways, this transformed part, even if it perfectly coexists, does not improve my battle straight by much.”

The young man now had much more applicable experience.

Although these racial magics were beyond the capabilities of his body, they actually complemented it.

For example, take the Freeze Ray racial magic.

The power the magic displayed when he was a tide snake could not compare to when he used it as a dragonman.

The Venomous Fang Throwing Knives were more obvious.

This racial magic was fairly special; it needed casting materials – tide snake venomous fangs.

If the young man had a full mouth of dragon teeth, he could not use this racial magic.

“If I sought its might, I would need to be more of a tide snake.”

“If it was a hybrid transformation, although it wouldn’t be as powerful in a pure form, it would be an additional method of attack for me.”

In practical applications, the young man had become very prudent in using hybrid transformations.

If an enemy fled or left behind information, the young man’s transformations would provoke the attention of auspicious people.

After all, this kind of transformation was unusual!

Only if he was certain nothing would be left behind or if he was compelled by desperate circumstances would he do so.

Therefore, the young man still directed his development towards improving his battle qi and battle skills.

The young man’s face flashed with red light, and he became a dragonman again, the venomous fangs and snake tongue from before seeming to be nothing but a fleeting hallucination.

He looked at the silent Zong Ge and smiled, “After I obtained the Bloodcore, my beast transformation abilities were greatly strengthened.”

Zong Ge gazed deeply back, “That treasure was placed on the third level by War Merchant, it was more important to him than the two other divine artifacts. You were remolded by War Merchant, and have the beast transformation ability, thus, it should’ve originated from it?”

The dragonman youngster nodded, “Your guess is correct.”

Zong Ge sighed, then replied with a complex tone, “You must be careful, this ability is both weird and mysterious, perhaps every usage of it costs a hidden and heavy price. We can only always depend on battle qi.”

The dragonman youngster expressed his acknowledgment.

He still had some reservations about Zong Ge.

Although Zong Ge had fought by his side, his situation was different from Zi Di’s and Cang Xu’s, Zong Ge had once fought him for the position of leadership.

Not only was it because of this experience, but also because the young man understood Zong Ge, he was an ambitious man.

This kind of person was difficult to keep satisfied and any benefits offered as a lure would likely have the opposite effect.

Zong Ge was a peak silver and had a deeper battle qi cultivation than the young man, the iron level Cang Xu could not compare to him.

The dragonman youngster was able to suppress mainly with the Bloodcore’s help.

Zong Ge also had reservations with the young man and the others.

He came from an extraordinary family, and he exposed many clues through how he lived. These clues were all seen by the survivors to some extent.

After the battlefield was cleaned up, the four people continued to explore.

Cang Xu and Zong Ge joined in, increasing the survivors’ exploration efficiency by a large amount.

The dragonman youngster took action less often.

Zi Di often practiced necromancy, exhibiting her diligent side.

Zong Ge’s cutlass and shield were ordinary, however with peak silver level battle qi infused into them, they became vicious tools that reaped lives.

Cang Xu occasionally imparted Zi Di with guidance or experiences, he once served as a college professor and was very good at teaching. From time to time, he too acted personally to gather snake souls.

The necromancer displayed their formidable might in front of the young man and the half beastman.

Cang Xu and Zi Di gathered many more snake souls, then after the fighting died down, they could get even more snake souls from the corpses of fallen tide snakes.

These snake souls were mostly ordinary, but the tide snakes had a hard time dealing with them.

“As far as necromancers are concerned, as long as they have enough magic power, they can immediately raise an army from a battlefield.”

“Ordinary souls are the best cannon fodder, while cultivator souls can become an army’s backbone.”

“Fortunately, their spirit power has limits, and controlling ghosts will constantly consume that.”

“At the same time, the Soul Summoning Spell is not always successful, and failure is always a possibility. Every failed casting will always backfire and injure the magician. The only difference is how big it is.”

Zong Ge carefully watched and couldn’t help but fear the necromancy.

Their journey went smoothly.

Despite the tide snakes getting stronger, and the snake groups getting larger, none were the for man exploration team’s match.

It was mainly because Cang Xu’s and Zi Di’s strength was growing rapidly!

Anyways, the survivors did suffer losses.

The wear and tear on their scouting birds was growing more apparent.

These alchemy puppets were inharmonious with the caves and would easily trigger the aggression of snake groups and be hunted.

They assumed the arduous task of investigating and were the first to explore unfamiliar territory. Regardless of whether it was snake or rat groups, both had strong territorial instincts.

“After this, we need to make some serpentine or mouse shaped scouting puppets.” Said Zi Di.

The alchemy bird puppets had suffered great attrition, making her somewhat regretful.

She retrieved as many damaged alchemy birds as possible. After all, the birds’ alchemical materials could be reused.

Under current circumstances, the survivors’ resources were finite, and they had to haggle over every bit.

The more resources that were saved in the early periods of development, the more efficient and higher they could grow and strengthen.

The four cultivator team, with the help of the alchemy birds, quickly attained the objective of the Hunting Guild’s exploration mission.

The four did not care about that.

They left to explore another subterranean space.

Because they hadn’t found the Green Jade Gold Coffin yet.

The scouting birds had explored the entire island, including its towns and villages, and found nothing.

The survivors analyzed that it was very possible that these subterranean spaces might conceal the divine artifact.

At the time, the Green Jade Gold Coffin’s teleportation coordinates had been transmitted.

The transmitted coordinates were here, on Snake Rat Island.

Through a secret investigation, the survivors discovered that the island residents did not feel any fluctuations of teleportation on that day.

If instead, the Green Jade Gold Coffin was teleported into the depths of the underground, then the teleportation fluctuations would have been masked.

It was also possible that War Merchant had built a teleportation formation here.

From War Merchant’s point of view, he was on the imperial most wanted list, thus having a few escape routes was very rational.

Mystifying Monster Island was his headquarters, and Snake Rat Island was comparatively close to it.

It was possible that he left an escape route here.

This guess had a strong basis, and in the tower spirit’s data, the survivors discovered a few hidden teleportation points. Other places you could teleport to from Mystifying Monster Island.

“The air temperature has risen.” After going deeper for some time, the young man was the first to notice this.

“It seems we are lucky. There is an underground heat source here.” Zi Di smiled.

As they went deeper underground, the magic beasts they mainly encountered were tide snakes.

But Snake Rat Island had two magic beast species on it, tide snakes were only one of them. The smelt rats had always been in a deadlocked confrontation with the tide snakes.

These rats were heat resistant magic beasts and smelt rats with cultivation could bathe in magma.

Ordinary smelt rats all lived near heat sources, none of them liked moist environments.

The young captain glanced at a map.

This map had a wide scope and was very detailed and reliable.

The main path the four walked was the widest part of the cave. There were many scouting birds exploring other paths.

Therefore, the map displayed a main path, separated by subterranean spaces of various sizes and many other paths bifurcating from above ground.

The geothermal source was not in the direction of the main path.

“Let’s survey there.” The dragonman youngster decided.

The four left the main path and entered another that branched off.

After a short time, they saw their first live smelt rat.

It was as big as an adult bear and had brown-red fur.

Its fur was very thick and decorated with flame-like patterns.

The smelt rat’s head had a distinct layer of bone that looked like a black helmet.

The smelt rat’s eyes were an orange-yellow and emitted light, against its bone black helmet, it looked like two drops of magma floating in the darkness.

Silver level!

The smelt rat had an extraordinary life level.

The four people were not surprised.

Smelt rats with cultivation had a far larger movement range than ordinary ones.

Ordinary smelt rats did not like damp and cold environments, therefore they holed up near underground heat sources.

As a result, smelt rats had different territory structures than tide snakes.

Tide snakes with cultivation acted as their core, while ordinary tide snakes moved all over the place and acted in the group’s periphery.

Smelt rats with cultivation frequently roamed the fringes of their territory, while common ones gathered in the heart of it.

Smelt rats did not possess any abilities to conceal themselves, thus the four had discovered their existence earlier.

“I’ll go first.” Zong Ge took the lead and charged ahead.

With sword and shield in hand, he confronted the smelt rat.

The two slammed into each other and sank into a deadlock in the underground tunnel.

Although they were the same level, the half beastman’s strength could not compare to the smelt rat’s.

However, his whole body instigated his battle qi, causing his strength, speed, and defense to soar, allowing him to directly face off against the smelt rat.

At the same time, he also poured battle qi into his equipment.

Although the smelt rat’s sharp claws and teeth clashed against the ordinary sword and shield, the ordinary equipment did not break, and the blows only caused silver battle qi to splatter off of them.

As it turns out, he is also an expert at using a sword and shield like this.” The dragonman youngster evaluated internally.

In the prior fight with the tide snakes, Zong Ge’s did not display his skill.

But now when confronted with a silver level smelt rat, Zong Ge’s martial skill was truly exposed.

In the survivors’ mind, the weapon Zong Ge was most skilled at was the broadsword. Zong Ge had swung that broadsword powerfully and violently, and now, he used a shield to defend himself thoroughly and wait for an opportunity to stab decisively with his sharp cutlass.

His stature paled in comparison to the smelt rat, but the three people behind him felt a sense of security.

The smelt rat had finite intellect, and Zong Ge quickly understood how it worked. The latter relied on his adept martial skill to gradually gain the advantage.

The smelt rat’s flesh was lacerated by the cutlass, causing blood to flow from all over.

The pain intensified its vicious nature.

Suddenly, it screeched, and suddenly, flames bellowed from all over its body.

The flames looked like a layer of clothes covering its body.

Racial magic – Flame Battle Garb!

The smelt rat’s life aura increased sharply, and it charged into Zong Ge once again.

Zong Ge remained calm and fearless. But just as he was about to use his battle skill, he heard the dragonman youngster say, “Let me give it a go.”

The silver level smelt rat was worthy of acting upon.

Zong Ge retreated and allowed the dragonman youngster to take the field.

Ding.

With a reverberating echo of an explosion, the dragonman youngster’s speed rose dramatically and he slammed into the smelt rat’s bosom.

The smelt rat was caught off guard, the blow knocked it to the ground, and it caused it to cough blood.

Zong Ge’s eyes contracted, the dragonman youngster’s attacks were far braver and more fearless than his.

The half beastman’s sword and shield style had both offense and defense, like a barbed city wall. While the dragonman youngster was completely filled with offense, like an artillery shell!

“I have to consume more battle qi to withstand the Flame Battle Garb.”

“While he can rely on his dragon scales to easily resist it.”

The smelt rat was bullied further underground by the young man.

The young man didn’t have to use any battle skills and just relied on his new Bursting Explosion battle qi that filled his ordinary punches with power.

The smelt rat only had one racial magic, but it did not affect the dragonman youngster.

Under the young man’s rain of fists, the smelt rat’s condition continuously dropped.

The young man gradually pulled back, giving Zi Di the opportunity to attack too.

But this time, she didn’t achieve much.

When ordinary souls approached the Flame Battle Garb, they would be scorched away.

Bronze level snake souls could tangle with and impede it, however, the longer they were near the Flame Battle Garb, the weaker they would become.

Iron level snake souls could threaten the silver level smelt rat, but only a little.

“These snake souls are of low quality; we’ll need much more to deal with this silver level smelt rat.” Zi Di concluded.

Cang Xu had experienced this, but he didn’t block Zi Di’s attempt.

This kind of actual combat experience was very precious. Teachings were comparatively shallow to deeper personal experiences.

Under the survivors’ maltreatment, the smelt rat’s viciousness was suppressed, and it tried to flee.

The dragonman youngster caught up to it.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist!

The velocity of his fist soared, and in everyone’s view, a scarlet red shadow bled from his fist.

When his fist hit the sturdy smelt rat, an explosion rang out.

It sounded like a bomb going off.

The explosive force was so strong that the smelt rat’s thoracic cavity was penetrated, causing most of its internal organs to turn into a swirl of fleshly fragments and tempestuous blood that exploded outwards, covering large swaths of the tunnel walls with the slurry.

The smelt rat fell to the ground, dead as it was possible.

Upon seeing the young man use the battle skill, Zong Ge immediately recalled the tide snake corpse from earlier.

“Its snake head must’ve been burst apart by a Demolition Fist.”

“Its might truly is extraordinary!”

Zi Di hurried over to pick up the corpse.

“Lord Captain, please use that battle skill less often.”

“A silver level smelt rat corpse is worth more, and has many alchemy materials, they aren’t cheap. Even if we don’t sell it, we can still make use of it.”

“We can use them to make scouts in the form of smelt rats.”

“You know our financial situation is very tight right now.”

“Oh, of course, of course.” The dragonman youngster apologized at once.

If he used the Bloodcore, he could have absorbed it all and squandered nothing.

However, Zong Ge was here.

It was too inconvenient to absorb external bloodlines in front of him.

Cang Xu was exhilarated as he said, “President Zi Di, don’t worry about gathering these materials.”

“A silver level smelt rat soul is the best material to make a ghost out of.”

Zi Di immediately stopped messing with the corpse and doubtfully asked, “I can do that? I am still only a bronze level necromancer.”

“This is an outstanding feature of necromancy; we can call back souls whose life levels exceed ours.” Cang Xu explained, “In addition, if you don’t give it a try, how will you know what the result will be?”

Zi Di’s attempt failed.

The silver level smelt rat soul dissipated, and she had to bear the backlash of the magic.

“That’s fine.” Cang Xu Maintained his optimism.

“I noticed.” He looked at the ghost girl, “That this necromancy inheritance suits you more than me!”

“I am a semi-dead person, a corpse wizard, thus pure ghosts have a negative effect on my body.”

“But you are different, Miss Zi Di.”

“You are a pure ghost, and your life essence is more intimate with these ghosts. Therefore, under the same conditions, you can recruit more ghosts than me, control them for longer at a lower spirit consumption, and have a higher success rate with the Soul Summoning Spell. Even if you fail, the backlash will harm you less.”

“Perhaps in the future, you can establish an enormous ghost army, and as the leader of an army of ten thousand, be a ghost queen.”

But Zi Di shook her head, she was uninterested in the future Cang Xu described, “Compared to that, I’d much rather be resurrected.”

She unconsciously looked at the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster was smiling back at her.

Chapter 260: Proceeding with the Plan

The Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The alchemy cabin.

The alchemy formation’s light stabilized, and under Zi Di’s control, it quickly formed into the framework of a rat.

The final step: top quality lacquering.

The magic lacquer was a temporary mixture and had an extremely short shelf life. In essence, it was a coating of magic camouflage, part of a branch of alchemy skills that pertained to camouflage.

After a moment, the alchemy formation stopped for a break.

The lacquer’s strange odor was sucked out, and fresh air was pumped in.

As this phase of alchemy came to an end, Zi Di was pleased to see a hundred scouting puppets.

These freshly made alchemy puppets were no longer shaped like birds, but instead like rats.

I already mastered this step of making alchemy puppets.” Zi Di said to herself.

The craving for knowledge was an intrinsic part of all magicians.

Zi Di had been rather busy.

Mainly doing meditation, as through frequent accumulation she could raise her magician level.

After that, she practiced necromancy until her spirit and energy were squeezed to their limits.

Every day she would integrate the exploration maps and use them to calculate the possible teleportation points, thus attempting to discover where the Green Jade Gold Coffin might be.

After that, she also made time to study how to control the alchemy formations and make these alchemy puppets.

She let no chance to grow slip by.

“To be able to operate this alchemy formation could be considered a form of improvement.”

“But I still cannot create these alchemy puppets by myself.”

“As far as those alchemy blueprints are concerned, I lack too much alchemy knowledge and cannot comprehend most of them.”

“If I had more time…”

Zi Di sighed to herself.

She conducted a final inspection of the scout puppets.

She noticed a defective one, while the rest met standard.

The girl stored most of them, then took about 30 of them and gave them to Cang Xu.

Cang Xu had a space pouch, thus it was easy for him to secretly transport them.

“Tower spirit, how is the plan progressing?” Zi Di asked.

The white crystal ball flickered, then the tower spirit’s voice transmitted, “Currently, the scouting smelt rat alchemy puppet blueprint modification is 58% done. Estimated time to completion is 17 hours and 36 seconds. Repairing the tower spirit, constructing the corresponding alchemy formations, and using time magic can improve designing efficiency.”

Zi Di shook her head and said, “Continue to modify”, then left it to its devices.

The tower spirit was not in perfect condition, and had been badly damaged, at best it could only display 30% of its capabilities.

Repairing the tower spirit was very difficult, and while building supplementary calculation alchemy formations was feasible, they lacked the relevant alchemy materials. As for time magic, such a thing was rarely seen, and the tower spirit’s database contained nothing on it.

Even if there was, Zi Di probably wouldn’t even be able to cast it successfully.

Zi Di took out some blood and began to study it.

When she lived, she cultivated fire magic and researched medicine.

She quickly became engrossed, and in her concentration, completely forgot about the passage of time.

Only when an alarm rang did she snap out of it.

The test tube in her hand was filled with a blue medicinal liquid. If one shook it, they could still smell the scent of blood.

“Still a work in progress.”

“But I already see hope of success!”

Zi Di was very confident in her research into this medicine.

She took time to rest; she had to improve her condition so her meditation would be more profitable.

In a lively port market, vendors were selling all kinds of ocean fish.

The streams of people seemed to weave.

Most of them were gathered in one place, a temporary slave shop, where they loudly haggled with each other,

Hei Fei’s group was selling some beastmen slaves there.

In a dark street corner, a scouting rat was watching this unfold.

In the castle sewer, a few rats quickly crept through, and every time there was a fork in the path, one would branch off.

On a moored ship, there was a scouting rat quietly sneaking in.

These alchemy puppets were also crawling into caves.

The scouting bird models flew through the air, and monitored from above, while the scouting rat models hid in the darkness and spied from the shadows.

The survivors’ adoption of this scouting plan was very wise.

The scouts in the castle, church, merchant alliance shop, and Hunting Guild branch were all very cautious, and only skittered on the outside, and did not brave danger to go deeper in.

Those in the tunnels were far more reckless, and sometimes for the sake of intelligence, they would not hesitate to sacrifice themselves and even incited attacks.

The survivors had limited funds, and with the alchemy materials they purchased, they only had enough to make 500 rat model puppets and replace 60 of the bird models.

Their funds were nearly depleted.

But soon, they would obtain benefits from the Hunting Guild.

Completing missions for them had profit.

The survivors completed these missions well, not just to criterion, but exceeded the standards by a lot.

However, in reality, they were scouting even deeper. Delivering just a small piece of the scouting map was enough to complete a mission.

Thus, the survivors obtained some more funds.

Frequent completion of these missions also caused Zong Ge’s rank in the Hunting Guild to rise quickly.

Of course, this led to more scrutiny.

The scouting smelt rat was refined.

This special alchemy puppet model had stronger materials and was bronze level, surpassing ordinary ones.

A bronze level investigating puppet!

Because its construction cost was far greater than ordinary puppets, after careful accounting, Zi Di only made 5 of them.

The 5 were dispatched to the tunnels to search for the Green Jade Gold Coffin.

Getting what they paid for, the survivors’ exploration rate of the subterranean depths skyrocketed.

In the wake of this, something even more valuable was discovered.

“A smelt rat puppet was destroyed here.”

“A subterranean magma river.”

“Please let me see.”

Zi Di presented the magic image to the dragonman youngster.

When the dragonman youngster saw the small cluster of sparkling orange crystals, he asked, “Some kind of crystals? Are they valuable?”

Zi Di replied, “Of course. The scientific name of this is the flame crystal cluster. It forms in harsh conditions and is very expensive and rare, the Hunting Guild and Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance shop will purchase it at a high price.”

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance wouldn’t buy magic beasts, but in this aspect, they hadn’t disagreed yet.

“If we sell it, how much money would we get?” The young man asked.

Zi Di shook her head, “We need that alchemy material, and don’t intend to sell them, at least on Snake Rat Island.”

“This will let others know that we have already been to the depths of the cave systems.”

These flame crystal clusters aren’t easy to obtain.”

“It has magic beasts guarding it, many of whom are silver level, along with large numbers of iron level and other lesser beasts.”

“It is also difficult to harvest, even the slightest collision will cause them to explode into fragments.”

Please reclaim the smelt rat puppet and do your best to gather that crystal cluster.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, “I understand. It just so happened that I have a battle skill I need to practice in real combat!”

He moved at once and snuck into the subterranean space.

His arrival immediately provoked a rat group.

A fierce battle of one against many broke out on the bank of a magma river.

Battle skill – Surge!

During this fight, the young man frequently used this new battle skill.

This battle skill matched the Bursting Explosion battle qi, but it wasn’t an attack skill, rather it helped the young man move.

Every time he used it, the young man’s battle qi would suddenly rush forth, pushing his body where it surged from.

For example, when he used it on his fist, his fist’s strike would become faster.

When it was applied to the bottom of his feet, an explosion would detonate under his soles, allowing the young man to jump higher and run faster.

The Surge battle skill enormously increased the young man’s movement ability, but to use it well, he needed a lot of practice.

The young man was doing his utmost to adapt his body to this battle skill.

Only through constant combat would his body be able to use the Surge battle skill on instinct.

As he fought, the young man gained a lot of experience using the Surge battle skill.

However, he wasn’t able to obtain the crystal clusters.

During the fight, the smelt rats accidentally destroyed the crystal clusters.

The crystals exploded, and the leftover pile of fragments scattered across the battlefield.

The port, on a ship deck.

Zong Ge presided over military training.

“Continue striking, don’t stop!”

“Begin training one on one, the loser will jump off the ship and swim three laps!”

Zong Ge roared from time to time.

He drilled the sailors to their limits.

Not only in weapons training, but also in boarding actions, hook throwing, and other routine maritime combat drills.

Bang bang bang.

Sometimes, Zong Ge would order a ship or two to sail out into the sea and practice shooting cannons, creating a lot of noise.

After proceeding like this for several days, the survivors’ leadership gathered together again to discuss.

“I have bad news.” Zi Di murmured, “The Two Head pirate group has appeared in nearby waters.”

“The Two Head pirate group’s commander is Lan Lu, a two headed man-beating magician. He has dual silver level cultivation in battle qi and magic!”

“Their presence here is unusual.”

“According to pirate customs, these waters are Rou Zang’s hunting ground.”

“Lan Lu shouldn’t be here.”

“Perhaps, after Rou Zang was seriously injured, Lan Lu believed that he had been weakened and wanted to annex Rou Zang’s pirate group. Or perhaps Rou Zang invited Lan Lu over to deal with Snake Rat Island. Like how Tan Qiu wants to ally with others, Rou Zang is doing the same.”

Zi Di analyzed.

Cang Xu followed up, “I have turned over the town’s cemetery, but didn’t find any necromantic materials.”

“The Sheng Ming Sect priest here had cleaned and purified them too much.”

“Our necromancy resources are already bottomed out.”

Currently, these necromancy resources are mainly consumed by meditation.

With the help of the meditation room, Zi Di and Cang Xu built a negative energy environment. By doing this, although it could improve their meditation gains, it also depleted much of the necromancy resources.

Zong Ge also brought bad news, “After these days of training, our sailors have been voicing their discontent openly. The only change is that the formerly disobedient pirates now follow orders. Although the fisherfolk are more obedient, they lack killing intent, so don’t expect too much of them.”

“With Kuai Tu’s leadership, the shiba inu beastmen have distinguished themselves, unfortunately there are too few of them to assume the duty of being our backbone. Furthermore, everyday Cang Xu draws their blood and even peels their flesh off, now they all fear and are suspicious of him.”

Cang Xu took the blame for Zi Di’s actions.

Zong Ge continued, “The cannon training uses up our ordinary shells, which are currently still abundant. But the sailors’ shelling abilities have only shown small improvements.”

“I plan to lead a small team into the underground and conduct live combat drills.”

“Truth be told, don’t count on them becoming elites.”

“Alas, we need better soldiers.”

However, the survivors’ funds were once again hollowed out. The money they made these days was converted into materials that were used to make alchemy puppets.

The dragonman youngster laughed, “Those are unimportant matters.”

“Our plan has smoothly made progress.”

“Zi Di, you have already developed the medicine, right?”

Zi Di nodded, “I have already had the scouting rat models sneak the medicine into Hei Fei’s ships.”

“However, those ships are heavily guarded, if we want the medicine to take effect, we can’t be too hasty.”

“Apart from that, the rumors we slyly spread are also gradually taking hold.”

“Very good, then our plan will proceed.” The dragonman concluded.

Chapter 261: Three Party Transaction

The Hunting Guild.

Once again, Zong Ge brought back a lot of dead magic beasts.

“Lord Shi Qi, please sit down for a moment, we shall immediately settle your bill!” The receptionist was both cordial and respectful.

By this time, Zong Ge had become their most radiant hunter.

He not only cleaned out most of the exploration missions, but he also brought back large amounts of dead magic beasts.

There were many tide snakes and smelt rats.

Not only ordinary ones, but also bronze levels, iron levels, and even silver levels.

If this trend continued, the Hunting Guild’s mission pile would be gone soon.

Zong Ge withdrew into a small booth, and after taking a seat, the receptionist served him black tea and dessert.

“Your group’s strength surprised people.”

“My Lord, are you interested in this mission?”

“Recently, the reward for this kind of mission has been increased by 20%, a generous kickback.”

The receptionist seized the opportunity to market the Hunting Guild’s mission.

Zong Ge glanced at it and noticed it was a resource gathering mission.

It wanted two types of resources.

One was flame crystal clusters, which often grew near magma river banks.

The second was tide snake eggs, the bigger the tide snake egg, the higher the price.

Zong Ge shook his head, “We are resting, reorganizing, and drilling. We only do these missions in passing. This mission is too troublesome, and I don’t want to waste our time.”

Until the moment Zong Ge left through the Hunting Guild’s door, the receptionist did not give up trying to persuade him, “My Lord, won’t you reconsider?”

Not long after Zong Ge left, a docked ship welcomed a visitor.

The Night Devil Hunting Group commander.

He wanted to collaborate with the survivors to encircle and hunt a tide snake group.

“This snake group has many female snakes that recently laid many snake eggs.”

“Smelt rats give birth to live young, and their newborns only acknowledge other smelt rats. When tide snake eggs hatch and break out of their shell with secret breeding methods, the newborn small snakes will imprint on their breeder, making them an excellent source of magical pets.”

“Snake Rat Island’s largest resource is magic beasts, and its most profitable are flame crystal clusters and tide snake eggs.”

“But flame crystal clusters are very hard to gather, there are always smelt rats living nearby. And during the ensuing fight, the crystals are likely to be destroyed.”

“But snake eggs are different, they will be protected by the snake group to their deaths.”

The Night Devil commander earnestly persuaded.

Unlike before, the strength the survivors displayed caused him to sit up and take notice, leading him to think about cooperation.

But the dragonman youngster tactfully refused him.

The Night Devil commander did not know that in the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the survivors already had 1000 flame crystal clusters and 300 snake eggs, many of which were of higher levels.

If a human wanted to take these flame crystal clusters or snake eggs, they would need to fight, and hunt the snake or rat groups, difficult things to do.

But the dragonman youngster was different.

He had the Bloodcore!

By beheading many tide snakes and smelt rats, the young man could transform into a complete silver level of those magic beasts!

Now, he only lacked gold level ones.

It was because of this that the survivors delivered so many magic beasts to the Hunting Guild.

The dragonman youngster no longer needed to absorb them.

Meanwhile, because the Night Devil commander paid a visit to the survivors, Hei Fei’s group went on high alert.

“Lord Captain, the Night Devil commander just left. This subordinate made some inquiries, and it seems Long Fu won’t be collaborating with the Night Devil Hunting Group.” The first mate reported.

Hei Fei immediately let out a breath.

These days, his days were anything but relaxing.

With great fanfare, the survivors had been exploring the underground, causing him to be suspicious and vigilant.

The survivors’ great headway and good momentum made him even more wary.

Every time Zong Ge conducted military training, his prestige was apparent. Whenever Hei Fei heard the sailors’ battle cries, clanging blades, and most of all, the booming cannons, he felt contempt and provoked.

Whenever the survivors displayed themselves like this, Hei Fei would remember the time when he barely blocked the sword Zong Ge threw at him and knew that he had no choice but to swallow his anger.

He wanted to leave this place, but his ships needed to be repaired.

He was behind the survivors.

But the survivors’ ships hadn’t been repaired yet.

They always had new damage.

Hei Fei began to think of every possible method, including bribing personnel, to secretly repair his ships.

All failed.

He faintly realized that he might be stuck in some whirlpool and couldn’t get himself free.

He began to feel regretful.

If he had known earlier, he might have listened to his first mate: immediately leaving Snake Rat Island!

But he couldn’t now.

Hei Fei was very worried that if he fled to another island, the survivors would attack him. In the port, they wouldn’t dare to touch him.

But staying here was also terrible.

Tan Qiu had invited him to dinner many times, every time under the pretense of purchasing beastmen slaves.

But Hei Fei knew this wasn’t so!

Don’t be fooled by the size of Snake Rat Island, it did not require many beastmen slaves.

Because Snake Rat Island hadn’t been developed for very long, the island did not have many inhabitants, and under normal circumstances, the ruling lord wouldn’t let his native race be outnumbered by foreign ones.

If the beastmen slaves rebelled, one of them could take on three humans. Therefore, there needed to be three humans for every beastman to ensure the territory’s population was healthy and stable.

Tan Qiu took a fancy to them, and fancied Hei Fei!

Tan Qiu wanted to unite everyone to deal with Rou Zang, and as a silver level, Hei Fei was an external force he could draw support from.

The conflict between him and the survivors caused Tan Qiu to see an opportunity and hope.

Like Hei Fei wanted to deal with Rou Zang.

Besides that, recent gossip that had sprung up also made him fidgety.

The gossip involved the dawning war in the Wilderness Continent, the rampaging pirates, and that the selling price of slaves were constantly sliding downwards in other islands.

Hei Fei was worried incessantly about this.

There were many factors that affected slave prices.

Nowadays, slave merchants were slashing prices to sell beastmen slaves.

Hei Fei knew the reason why!

The empire’s development in the Wilderness Continent was going well, and they held the advantage in the war. If allied beastmen armies were defeated, not only would they lose their territory, many of them would also be enslaved.

Beastmen soldiers taken prisoner were snapped up by slave traders, and then the defenseless beastmen villages were also raided, causing all of its commoners to forfeit their freedoms and suffer enslavement.

No one believed the empire’s armies could lose!

Therefore, the general market was welcoming large quantities of beastmen slaves.

As time passed, the newer beastmen slaves would be of better quality and more numerous, if one still had a pile of old slaves, how would they be able to sell them?

Nowadays, selling them would make less money.

Furthermore, after making back their capital, the slave traders would invest more into capturing slaves that would sell for more in the future.

Hei Fei also planned to do this too.

But now he was trapped!

Trapped on Snake Rat Island.

As Hei Fei was thinking of a good idea, a flustered subordinate came over to report, “Captain, something doesn’t seem right.”

“What isn’t right? Are the smugglers coming to kill us?” Hei Fei immediately asked.

“No, it’s the beastmen slaves, they all look…sick.”

“Sick?” Hei Fei got up to check it out right away, they were his money, and he couldn’t be even a bit negligent.

The beastmen slaves were gravely ill.

Their skin was purple and was beginning to fester and sink.

Hei Fei’s group had some treatment methods, but none of them worked.

Hei Fei immediately asked a priest to come over.

But when the Sheng Ming priest personally cast Purify, it didn’t do anything.

“There are no traces of magic, nor magic medicine. This is some kind of germ that is flourishing in these beastmen, it is exuberant and lively.” The priest used a divine spell to find out the truth.

He was unable to cure it; however, he could use a divine spell to suppress it.

Hei Fei firmly declined.

Priestly divine spells were not free; they were actually very expensive!

Hei Fei then asked the merchant alliance magician to come over.

The merchant alliance magician also was helpless, “Treatment scrolls cannot cure them, on the contrary, it will stimulate the germs and make them multiply more within the beastmen.”

“Magic medicine can be used, but it needs to be specially mixed to target them specifically.”

“According to my understanding, there are no magic pharmacists on Snake Rat Island at this time.”

“Good news however, this sickness is specific to beastmen only, it will not spread to us humans.”

“Look, the shiba inu beastmen are sicker, while the oxmen aren’t as ill. Perhaps the shiba inu beastmen are the carriers and infected the other beastmen.”

“Your ship holds aren’t the best living quarters, it is crammed with beastmen slaves and lacks ventilation, sunlight, and cleaning. These are prime conditions for germs to flourish.”

Hei Fei despaired!

This was a day of alarming and sad news.

He confined himself in the captain’s room and didn’t sleep the entire night.

By morning, his eyes were very red as he walked out and shouted for the first mate to come over and receive orders.

He wanted to dump these beastmen slaves now, to stop any further losses.

“If we dress them up well and put them below market price, we can sell them!”

“I need to meet with Tan Qiu; did he not want my beastmen slaves?”

At this point, Hei Fei would say anything.

Tan Qiu greeted him with a smile, “You came just in time, I was in the middle of entertaining Long Fu.”

Hei Fei thought to himself, the survivors had already absconded with his beastman slaves. They now included these beastmen in their forces, then perhaps the survivors were interested in his remaining beastmen?

Thus, in the dining room, Hei Fei offered the beastmen slaves to the two.

The dragonman youngster expressed on the spot, “I am not interested.”

Tan Qiu smiled, “Captain Hei Fei, your beastmen slaves have all contracted a strange disease, I understand your desire to cut your losses. If you want me to take these rotten goods, it won’t just be a monetary affair.”

Tan Qiu brought up the same topic and hoped Hei Fei would be willing to help him deal with Rou Zang.

He also requested the same thing from the dragonman youngster.

Hei Fei looked agonized, and he considered what he wanted to say.

The dragonman youngster also looked grave.

The situation on the seas had gotten worse.

There was much evidence that the Two Head Pirate Group wasn’t attacking Rou Zang’s pirates, the two sides seemed to be cooperating.

Rou Zang and the survivors hated each other to the bone, and if the two pirate groups were cooperating, the survivors would be in grave danger when on the seas.

More crucially, the survivors hadn’t found the Green Jade Gold Coffin in the underground at all.

Before the dragonman youngster left, Tan Qiu spoke with him and promised a heavy reward.

The survivors were many times stronger than Hei Fei, and Tan Qiu likewise valued them more, thus was willing to spend more to attract them over.

However, at noon, a major event occurred on Snake Rat Island.

A gold level tide snake chief rushed out of a cave and attacked the Night Devil’s encampment, throwing everything into disarray.

As it turns out, the Night Devil Hunting Group was probing a tide snake community and eyeing the snake eggs covetously, causing the community to grow restless.

During this, friction between tide snakes sparked, and many tide snakes were killed.

The tide snake chief was so furious that it killed its way to the surface, causing the entire island to shake.

Faced with a gold level magic beast, Tan Qiu immediately invited the silver level cultivators, including the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge, over to fight it. It took half a day to behead the gold level tide snake.

During the celebratory feast, Lao Zhong (Cang Xu) personally paid a visit to Tan Qiu to express that because of recent military training, they required more beastmen, however they were worried about Hei Fei, therefore they asked Tan Qiu to propose three party transactions.

The three parties of this transaction would be Tan Qiu, the survivors, and Hei Fei’s group.

Hei Fei would dump these beastmen slaves on Tan Qiu.

The survivors would exchange a lot of human fisherfolk and some other resources for the beastmen slaves.

Tan Qiu was very interested in this deal.

He asked Hei Fei to come to his study to talk things over.

When the two spoke in private, Tan Qiu explicitly stated to Hei Fei, “Sell all of the beastmen to me at this price. Otherwise, tomorrow the entirety of Snake Rat Island will know that your beastmen slaves are sick with a contagious illness!”

The deal was immediately reached that very evening.

With great pains, Hei Fei unloaded his heavy burden, at least he didn’t make a loss.

He was even somewhat happy about it.

He didn’t expect that he’d be able to cheat the survivors in this three party deal.

Rejuvenated!

Tan Qiu felt very satisfied with the beastmen slaves in his hands now, he could now obtain a large number of humans. These Stone Egg Island fisherfolk were all ordinary people.

As for these sick beastmen slaves, all of them could be given to those arms smugglers.

What, all sick?

That had nothing to do with I, Tan Qiu.

Everything had been covered up by you and yours Hei Fei, go find him to balance the books!

Tan Qiu wholeheartedly awaited the conflict between the two parties; it would allow him to grab ahold of the wheel and thereby attract more people to deal with Rou Zang.

As for the survivors…

It was late into the night when the beastmen slaves were handed over, their germs had been secretly contained for the time being.

They also received a large sum of money.

It was almost ten times the amount of the sum of their previous mission rewards!

There were some treasured natural resources that could not be purchased on the market, as a noble, Tan Qiu controlled such channels for these.

The shiba inu young clan patriarch once again kneeled to express his gratefulness and loyalty.

Privately below, Zi Di and Zong Ge were talking, “I suggest that we go trouble Hei Fei and his men at dawn. He actually dared to sell us these sick and dying trash!”

The half beastman remained silent.

The dragonman bitterly laughed. This merchant lady would not let any chance to obtain riches off.

What he was most satisfied with this plan was where the fisherfolk ended up at.

The fisherfolk did not belong on the battlefield and forcing them to do so would only waste their lives.

Now, they would live on Snake Rat Island, this was what the fisherfolk wanted.

At the same time, they also contributed to the survivors’ cause.

Cang Xu inspected two to three times, then nodded to himself, “With this money, we can buy the alchemy materials we lack tomorrow. The Anti-investigation and Prophecy Formation can be built.”

Chapter 262: Anti-investigation and Prophecy Formation

The Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The boarding cabin.

A developing alchemy formation had reached the final step of construction.

“Final inspection.” Cang Xu stood up from lying on the floor with his belly, whacked his old waist, then continued to kneel and carefully examine the formation.

The newly advanced bronze magician, Zi Di, was also inspecting by the side.

After a short time, the two raised their heads.

“No mistakes detected.”

Connect the magic power reservoir. Deep Sea Monster Fish, transmit the magic power!”

Turbulent magic power was instilled into many formation nodes.

A hundred candles seem to ignite in the boarding cabin.

Soon after, these nodes began to shine and connect with each other, starting from the floor, and spreading to the walls and even the ceiling.

Finally, the hundred silver-gray lights converged in the center of the room, transforming into a solid magic alchemy formation.

“Success.” Cang Xu and Zi Di were just about to celebrate when suddenly, their complexions changed.

The final few nodes were not lit, the flowing magic power seemed to have met with a dam, the obstructed power would soon overflow and damage other nodes.

“Not good!”

“What happened?”

Cang Xu and Zi Di were helpless to do anything.

They only had a superficial attainment in alchemy and couldn’t discern what the problem was, let alone know how to fix it.

“Tower spirit, take action!” Zi Di called for help.

The white crystal ball half a human tall had been on standby in the corner of the boarding cabin, upon hearing the command, it immediately floated up and glistened with white light.

The white light quickly grew bright, indicating that the tower spirit was using all of its strength.

In tandem with the tower spirit, the two yellow crab alchemy puppets suddenly seemed to be infused with souls as their crab claws became very nimble.

The yellow crab puppets rushed into the formation and launched out their crab claws into the key nodes.

The tower spirit seized that opportunity to take control of the nearly finished alchemy formation.

The magic power fluctuations were gradually alleviated.

The tower spirit’s voice transmitted, “The formation will collapse in an estimated 30 seconds. Please decide on a course of action immediately. Proposal one, disassembling the formation. A third of the formation can be saved and the boarding cabin will be seriously damaged; the success rate is 75%. Proposal two, destroy the formation entirely, the boarding cabin will be mildly damaged, the success rate is 88%. Proposal three…”

“Tower spirit, we must repair this magic formation!” Zi Di shouted, cutting off the tower spirit.

The tower spirit paused, then gave three repair plans.

Zi Di and Cang Xu looked at each other, then chose the same option independently.

After receiving their orders, the yellow crabs morphed their limbs simultaneously to become hollow and lankier.

Magic power flowed through the crab leg tubes and into a yellow crab alchemy puppet.

After being harmonized by the alchemy puppets, the magic power stabilized.

At the same time, the tower spirit and the other yellow crab alchemy puppet worked together to repair the nodes.

After stabilizing the flow, the magic power finally lit up the remaining nodes.

The nodes conformed with each other and linked completely to form a refined whole.

At last, the formation took shape.

The Anti-investigation and Prophecy Formation!

The magnificent formation looked like the starry night sky interconnecting with each other. To Cang Xu, it looked like the Aquarius constellation. From Zi Di’s point of view, it looked like a six-armed crossbow.

As the flow of magic power ceased, the formation gradually faded away until it finally vanished.

The light turned back to normal, and the boarding cabin revered back to its original appearance.

“A close call, the magic formation nearly collapsed.” Zi Di coughed, the danger still palpable.

Cang Xu nodded, his face still startled from the unforeseen event, “If this failed, we wouldn’t have enough resources to construct a second formation.”

Building the Anti-investigation and Prophecy Formation was very expensive.

Most of the alchemy materials came from War Merchant’s treasury.

At the time, when the survivors took these resources, they planned to build the formation. However, upon searching War Merchant’s storehouse, they only found most of them.

Fortunately, the few materials they were missing were relatively common, and the survivors were able to gather them all on Snake Rat Island.

Ding.

A yellow crab alchemy puppet collapsed to the ground, motionless.

It was wrecked.

Because during the rushed repairs, it had used its own alchemy core to store the torrential magic power.

Cang Xu and Zi Di were unsurprised.

The tower spirit’s repair plan indicated that if they used this plan, a yellow crab alchemy puppet would be sacrificed.

“We cannot repair it at this time.” Zi Di sighed softly as she looked at the collapsed alchemy puppet.

“This was the best outcome.” Cang Xu consoled.

The other repair plans either had a low success rate, would wound the soul of either Cang Xu or Zi Di, or damage the tower spirit.

These costs were unacceptable.

Without a yellow crab alchemy puppet, the survivors’ alchemy strength dropped by a third!

But compared to the gains, this loss was acceptable.

The survivors entered the formation in groups.

The last two were the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge. It was because they had to stay behind to take care of the ships and also because there were many eyes on them, it was hard for them to slip away.

After the formation’s radiance dissipated, Zong Ge walked out.

“Did you feel anything?” The dragonman youngster asked.

Zong Ge shook his head, “I don’t feel any different.”

“So, this Anti-investigation and Prophecy magic is outstanding.” Cang Xu praised, “It affects my body and protects us, yet we don’t sense any difference.”

“This point proves that the alchemy grandmaster, War Merchant, also has remarkable accomplishments in the domain of magic too. He is worthy of being called a legend level magician!”
