Zi Di agreed, “The most wonderful point is that the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell can be combined with the Deceptive Disguise Spell, each increasing the other’s power.”

“If we only had the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell on us, others diving and predicting us would be greatly obstructed and eventually fail.”

“But now, when others try to divine and predict us, they won’t feel hindered, rather they will be misled. The outcome of the divination will appear as what the Deceptive Disguise spell shows them!”

“If we only had the Deceptive Disguise spell, any detection or divination magic aimed at us will rapidly consume the power of the magic.

“With just the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell’s protection, external divination and detection will instead consume that spell’s power.”

“Thus, the cost of casting these spells is greatly reduced!”

The Deceptive Disguise and Anti-investigation and Prophecy formations could be regarded as a specially made combined magic spell.

The survivors had spent a huge price to build them and would pay a costly fee every time they activated them.

Especially the Deceptive Disguise formation, for the main casting material it consumed was the Pearl Bubbles.

The only source of this divine artifact came from the master of the Mermaid Fairy Tale– the Charming Blue God’s divine power!

Therefore, the cost to cast Deceptive Disguise was extremely high.

It expended god level materials.

Despite the survivors having the divine artifact, Mermaid Fairy Tale, there was surely a limit to the amount of Charming Blue divine power it could store, thus the number of Pearl Bubbles it could produce was finite. Because they couldn’t replenish Charming Blue’s divine power, the amount they used was the amount they depleted.

When compared to each other, the cost of using the Anti-investigation and Prophecy formation was far smaller.

After it was built, the highest level sorcery material it used was gold level along with a large amount of magic power.

Zi Di carefully calculated, “According to the market exchange rate of crystals to gold, at present time, for every person that enters the formation, on average it will cost 6 gold.”

There were only ten of them, and in just this first round, they spent a hundred gold coins.

This was already very high.

Take Cang Xu, as a noble steward, he managed a noble’s territory and was only paid about 36 gold coins a year.

When Hei Fei invited over the priest to use a silver level divine spell to attempt to treat the beastmen slaves’ sickness, he was told it cost 12 silver coins.

Hei Fei couldn’t bear this price repeatedly.

If it was only him, he could have, but there were too many slaves that needed to be treated.

Zi Di’s words had two key points.

One was “at present” and the other was “on average”.

The higher the life level the magic formation was casting on, the more it cost. The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge used the most magic power. It cost 25 gold to cast the spell on the dragonman youngster and 22 gold to cast on Zong Ge. In other words, every time they took a trip in, they paid the cost of an average silver level weapon.

As an iron level, the cost to cast on Cang Xu tumbled to 9 gold, while Tripleblade was 8 gold, and Zi Di was 7 gold.

Meanwhile, it only cost 70 silver to cast the spell on Lan Zao, a bronze level.

Zi Di’s tone sank, “So, currently our biggest expense is the formations.”

“First was the production cost, it was equal to the cost of two and a half ordinary ships.”

“It will be difficult to do a second round for a short while.”

“We don’t have a steady income yet, but because of you Captain, we harvested a large amount of flame crystal clusters and tide snake eggs. Computing in these funds, if we use this formation three times within half a year, we will go bankrupt. At that time, we will be forced to sell off our industry, such as alchemy installations and even the Deep Sea Monster Fish.”

“Therefore, I suggest that we do our best to keep a low profile and solve our redemption as soon as possible!”

“The more people investigating us, the more money we will spend.”

When it concerned money, the ghost girl was very serious and earnest.

The Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell will be expended. The more investigation and prophecy spells that were used, the faster it would be expended.

Therefore, it was unknown how long this magic would last for.

These words rang in the heart of the young captain, Zong Ge, and Cang Xu.

They now thought of Zi Di differently.

Before, they were helpless against their lack of funds, however Zi Di had managed to solve this difficult problem.

Zong Ge had beastmen slaves to serve as fine soldiers and Cang Xu built the Anti-investigation and Prophecy Formation, the entire group benefited because of her actions.

Zi Di displayed her talent in business!

She clearly saw what all parties really needed. For instance, Tan Qiu wanted humans more than beastmen.

If there wasn’t a demand, she would create a demand. Like how she tricked Hei Fei into dumping the beastmen slaves at his own accord.

She could also move her resources efficiently. Such as when she helped the young captain absorb the magic beast bloodlines with the Bloodcore, allowing him to easily gather large amounts of flame crystal clusters and tide snake eggs.

In the original plan, the young captain would use his transformations to lure out a gold level magic beast to create a magic beast calamity in the town outskirts.

But due to the Night Devil Hunting Group’s actions, a gold level tide snake leader was attracted to the surface, an unexpected delight.

After Zi Di’s plan had been implemented, the survivors would be the ones who benefited the most.

More wonderfully, their relationship with the other powers would not worsen as a result.

When one lacked the strength to tackle a problem head on, one needed Zi Di’s finesse.

Chapter 263: Weapon Master?

Snake Rat Island, the port.

“Shi Qi! What are you doing!?” Hei Fei yelled.

Since the three party transaction when Hei Fei dumped those beastmen slaves and broke even, he had paid close attention to how the survivors moved.

If the survivors shifted even a little, he would get information.

This time, the survivors tried to find trouble with him without the slightest concealment, they even did it with great fanfare and deliberately slowly.

The survivors gave Hei Fei enough time to react.

Therefore, when Zong Ge led Tripleblade, Kuai Tui, and the others to arrogantly came calling, Hei Fei’s group had already assembled on the port in tight formation to protect the slave ships behind them.

The two groups confronted each other on the port.

Seeing the situation was looking bad, the surrounding people fled in a flurry.

There were also busybodies and bold ones that stood a distance away to watch.

“What’s going on?”

“A good show!”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Group has made a move. Recently, they confiscated the rebellious beastmen slaves that escaped from Hei Fei. I don’t know why they have come to find trouble with Hei Fei again.”

“That group is quite overbearing! I heard that when they wanted to buy Hei Fei’s slaves previously, they thought the price was too high. The price was actually quite fair; they then used some means to trigger a beastmen revolt that allowed them to take some beastmen slaves. The city guard couldn’t do anything. Recently, they forced Hei Fei’s group to give them their remaining beastmen slaves. Hei Fei submitted. But I didn’t think they would want to find trouble with Hei Fei still.”

The third party deal was ordinally a secret.

But Hei Fei used a sly trick to let slip rumors about it the evening it took place.

The rumor was of course partial toward Hei Fei’s group.

“Hmph, Hei Fei isn’t a good person either. Slave merchants have no conscience. The beastmen slave I just purchased fell ill when it came home. To treat him, I spent a lot of money.”

“Hehe, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Group is very strong, Rou Zang was seriously injured by their hands. I look forward to Hei Fei’s downfall.”

“They also have big guts. They unexpectedly caused trouble on the port; they didn’t even put themselves in his Lordship’s eyes. The city guard should be receiving news of this soon, right?”

“Hurry up and fight, or there won’t be one when the city guard comes over.”

The spectators’ tongues wagged, many of them both fearful and excited at the chaos.

Hei Fei was gloomy, as he tenaciously looked at Zong Ge and coldly snorted, “Shi Qi, don’t be too excessive. If you really want to fight, we won’t be easy to bully around!”

In fact, if he could fight back, Hei Fei would have done so already.

His first mate involuntarily swallowed his saliva.

As an iron level fighter, Shi Qi was not someone he could handle, his gaze was more focused on other iron levels.

Zong Ge had two iron levels behind him.

A human as short as a dwarf, the altered appearance of Tripleblade.

The other one was a dogman, he was slender, capable, shorter than Zong Ge, and his face barred his hatred. It was the dogman clan’s young patriarch, Kuai Tui.

Behind those two were a small group of sailors.

These sailors wielded alchemy crossbows and firearms.

One glance was all it took to know these firearms and crossbows were of excellent quality.

The sailors behind Hei Fei’s first mate stared at those firearms and crossbows, cowardice filled their hearts.

If they opened fire, those vicious weapons would riddle them like sieves, electrocute them, freeze them into mincemeat, or cook them to ash in an instant.

The one that attracted the most attention was always Zong Ge.

He disguised himself as a human, but his body still remained frighteningly tall and sturdy, and as he stood at the forefront, he looked like a wall.

He had a broadsword on his back, and was armor less. As the wind blew, pressing his clothes to his body, it made more visible his rocky hard muscles.

Although his broadsword was ordinary, upon seeing it, Hei Fei and his men couldn’t help but recall their confrontation that night.

Zong Ge hurled that broadsword and forced Hei Fei back.

The first mate and many sailors behind Hei Fei could clearly see that after their captain blocked that sword, his arms were incessantly trembling!

“Terrible, truly terrible.” The first mate’s heart trembled.

The situation was very clear; the survivors’ formation completely suppressed their opposition.

If fighting broke out between them, Hei Fei’s group had no hope for victory.

“Why isn’t the city guard here yet?!” The first mate thought to himself.

The survivors were looking to find trouble, thus Hei Fei’s group had already sent someone to notify the guard.

During the three party deal, Tan Qiu used the secret that the beastmen were sick to depress the price. Hei Fei and Tan Qiu at the time had an oral agreement: if the survivors made a ruckus, he needed Tan Qiu protection!

As both sides faced each other, the mood grew tenser by the moment.

Zong Ge was expressionless, and maintained his silence.

To tell the truth, he didn’t want to do this.

He liked outright attacks and wasn’t fond of resorting to crafty plots and machinations.

That was his nature.

But Zi Di persuaded him, “We need to coerce Hei Fei’s group. If we don’t ‘fly into a humiliated rage’ and seek trouble with them, it will instead become our flaw. Perhaps…we can also gain some riches. This money can be used to enlarge our forces in the future.”

Zong Ge found those words reasonable.

He was an outstanding officer who was well versed in The Art of War.

The Art of War has a saying: appear weak when you are strong, and strong when you are weak. Both were common tactics.

But besides Zi Di’s justifications, he also had other considerations.

He needed to unite his troops!

Tripleblade hated Hei Fei’s group. Hei Fei’s first mate had killed several goblins in front of him.

Kuai Tui also hated Hei Fei’s group, this went without saying.

Therefore, he came.

Kuai Tui gnashed his teeth, he hated he couldn’t attack and tear Hei Fei and the others to bits yet

Tripleblade and Zong Ge had been together for a long time, thus the goblin somewhat understood what the half beastman was doing.

Upon seeing Zong Ge’s silence, Tripleblade immediately took a step forward and loudly cursed.

Through that scolding, the spectators gradually understood what was going on.

As it turned out, the beastmen slaves Hei Fei had sold the survivors were all dying from disease.

“Hear, see, as expected, Hei Fei’s group are not good people.”

“They are not the Dragon Lion Mercenary Group’s match. Not even Rou Zang was!”

“How do they have such large guts?”

“I heard there was a three party deal. Our Lord was one of them. Perhaps…”

“Look, the city guard is coming!”

The spectators opened a path through.

The silver level city guard commander, along with a troop of men, separated the two groups from each other.

The commander coldly snorted, “What are you doing here? Calm down for me!”

The commander was unsurprised.

Previously, Tan Qiu specifically summoned him to give him sincere advice and instructed him clearly on the matter. On the one hand, he asked that he protect law, order, and stabilize. Stop the two sides from clashing and beating each other bloody. On the other hand, he also wanted him to act according to circumstances, after all, Tan Qiu hoped that he could use these two groups to join hands with him to deal with Rou Zang.

Upon seeing the city guard arrive, Hei Fei relaxed at once and promptly said, “My Lord, we aren’t looking for trouble.”

The silver level commander swept his eyes around, then fixed his gaze on Zong Ge and strictly shouted, “What do you think this place is? This is Snake Rat Island’s port, this is Lord Tan Qiu’ territory, conflict is strictly prohibited here. If there are criminals here, all of them will be hauled back to prison!”

But Zong Ge coldly snorted, and for the first time since this confrontation started, he spoke, “You and I are both silver levels, talking won’t do.”

He slowly took out his broadsword and looked at both the commander and Hei Fei, “Don’t say I have you both time to react, receive my move and use your strength to speak!”

The commander’s eyes glared at first in disbelief, then boiled up with anger.

Hei Fei was both scared and pleased, he stepped next to the commander and took out his cutlass, “Shi Qi, you are too arrogant. Today, I and the Lord Commander will give you a profound lesson!”

The spectators were dumbstruck, none of them expected Zong Ge to be so unyielding.

The busy bodies had been disappointed to see the city guard intervening; they didn’t expect the sudden turn of events. Zong Ge was so incomparably stubborn that he did not put Hei Fei and the city guard commander in his eyes.

Silver level battle qi bubbled from Zong Ge’s body, then poured into the weapon he held.

As a result, the broadsword began to glisten with a silver luster.

The formidable life aura pressured the others.

“Come, let me see your hues.” Zong Ge’s voice was steady, his face still expressionless.

“You are too arrogant!” Hei Fei took the lead, he was impatient to create the reality that he and the city guard commander would fight Shi Qi together.

A cutlass chopped down, the slash creating a fierce gust of wind.

Hei Fei’s cutlass was of silver level quality, and its dark blade was as black as ink.

The cutlass cut down towards Zong Ge.

Zong Ge lifted his hands, and used the heavy broadsword to effortlessly block the cutlass.

As the weapons clashed, the exploding silver battle qi looked like splashing molten silver.

“You want to fight me with an ordinary weapon, switch to a better blade!” Hei Fei complacently roared.

In this fight, he had the advantage!

The weaponry advantage was vast.

The broadsword in Zong Ge’s hand did not snap, because it had been instilled with silver battle qi.

But as a result, his silver battle qi consumption was very high. After all, he needed to offset the immense disparity between an ordinary weapon and a silver one.

By contrast, Hei Fei’s battle qi consumption was far lower.

But just as Hei Fei smiled, Zong Ge stepped forward and swung his arms.

The copious amount of force in the sword was transmitted to Hei Fei.

“Such strength!” Hei Fei’s expression changed as Zong Ge knocked him back.

He was human, while Zong Ge was a half beastman. Although both were silver level, Zong Ge’s strength surpassed his by a large measure.

Zong Ge did not press the attack, instead he charged the city guard commander.

The city guard commander hesitated, he didn’t want to attack, nor did he believe Zong Ge would actually attack him!

When he saw Zong Ge charging at him, he had no choice but to angrily take out his sword.

This sword was inlaid with a sapphire and was also a silver level weapon.

With a thought, the sapphire on the sword hilt burst open with radiance, and in an instant, the light covered his body and formed a translucent blue armor around the city guard commander.

Astonishingly it was a magic weapon, enchanted with the Sapphire Light Armor spell.

The city guard commander raised his left arm.

The armor on his left arm suddenly expanded into a thick shield half a man tall.

This was the well known alchemy defense armament, it was known as the portable arm shield.

The city guard commander was skilled at using the sword and shield, and instantly became a fort. His equipment was better than Hei Fei’s, while the only thing Zong Ge had was the ordinary level broadsword.

“I order you to desist now, Shi Qi!” The commander shouted.

But Zong Ge seemed to neither see his armor nor hear the latter’s voice as he continued to charge forth.

The broadsword’s point first hit the shield, then rose up.

Ding.

Zong Ge struck, aiming the blade at the top of the shield.

He looked like a barbarian bull as he pushed the commander back, the latter’s legs plowing marks into the tiles on the floor.

“You cannot break through my defense!” The commander peaked out from the side of the shield to look Zong Ge square in the face.

But Zong Ge had no visible signs of panic or impatience.

At that moment, with a yell, Hei Fei attacked Zong Ge’s flank.

Zong Ge took a deep breath, and with all of his strength, raised his left foot.

The experienced commander immediately made out that Zong Ge wanted to kick the lower edge of his shield, so he could smack it away.

He grunted, and swelled his strength as he firmly planted his shield.

The shield inserted itself into the floor tiles.

But Zong Ge immediately changed his movement slightly, and used his knee to strike the pommel of his broadsword.

The broadsword had been slanted against the shield, but now that the shield had dropped to the ground, there was nothing blocking it from hitting the commander’s face.

The broadsword had more room to move.

Zong Ge’s hands controlled the sword hilt as he stood off against the shield. At that moment, due to the immense attack power the sword pommel received, in conjunction with Zong Ge, the slanted broadsword thrusted at the commander’s face.

When the commander saw how close the broadsword’s point was, his head unconsciously flinched back.

The broadsword cut across his cheek, causing silver sparks to shower off the Sapphire Light Armor, then shot into a wall ten steps away, vanishing into the wall without a trace.

When Hei Fei saw that Zong Ge lost his only weapon, his heart was exuberant, and he exerted himself further.

The speed of the cutlass’ swing grew faster.

But he didn’t expect that a moment later, Zong Ge lightly moved aside and avoided the cutlass’ blade. He then reached out and grabbed the blade’s hilt.

Hei Fei’s physical strength could not compare to Zong Ge’s, allowing Zong Ge to exploit the momentum of his swing,

The cutlass’s trajectory jerked forward towards the commander.

The commander’s shield was in the ground, and there was no time to evade, thus he lifted his one handed sword to block the cutlass.

A clang echoed.

When the cutlass and one handed sword crossed, the sound of it nearly punctured the eardrums of the spectators.

When Hei Fei saw his attack being exploited by Zong Ge, he pulled back his blade with both anger and worry.

But the commander didn’t think of drawing back, and he seized the opportunity to take the advantage. In his eyes, Zong Ge had lost his weapon and had no armor, thus he was vulnerable.

Zong Ge seemed to guess their trains of thought.

This time, he was the one who took advantage of the new situation, he grabbed the one handed sword’s hilt and gave it a boost.

The commander was now in the same situation as Hei Fei was, and as his sword was thrusting towards Hei Fei, he pulled back his thrust.

But Zong Ge immediately exploited that instant of restraint to attack again, this time he targeted his shield.

The three tangled together in close quarters combat.

The commander and Hei Fei felt out of sorts, they were unable to use their full strength, and every attack was wary of being neutralized or exploited by Zong Ge.

What made them cough up blood even more was that their swords or shields didn’t seem to be theirs, rather they were Zong Ge’s weapons!

The fight dazzled the people and made Hei Fei dizzy.

Suddenly, Zong Ge’s elbow loomed in his field of view!

Silver battle qi nearly burst from the tip of his elbow.

What shocked Hei Fei even more was that in the silver, he saw a trace of gold.

Peak silver level!

“Shi Qi is on the verge of becoming a gold level!”

Thump.

Zong Ge’s elbow smashed into Hei Fei’s face, flinging the latter into the air as scarlet blood sprayed from his nose.

With mingled fear and fury, Hei Fei stood up, then stared blankly.

He saw that the city guard commander had also fallen on the ground.

The commander’s shield had fallen nearby, while his sword was now in Zong Ge’s hand.

Zong Ge lifted the point up to the commander’s chin, while in his left hand, he also held the black cutlass.

“My blade!” Hei Fei now realized that Zong Ge had also snatched away his sword too.

“Absurd!” Hei Fei’s eyes widened, even though he had sampled defeat, he still found it hard to believe.

How did he lose?

They were fully armed, how were they defeated by someone with almost no equipment?

The spectators were also in an uproar.

This outcome seemed too dramatic, if it were not for how Hei Fei and the commander looked, they would have thought this was all a performance.

Hei Fei’s first mate and other people, as well as Kuai Tui, Tripleblade, and more watched on dumbstruck.

The city guard commander’s face was red from shame and anger, but then it changed slightly as he thought of something. He looked at Zong Ge and asked with indeterminate bewilderment, “You are a weapon master?”

Although it was phrased as a question, his tone was very certain.

Hei Fei was flabbergasted.

He thought of the fight and noticed that Zong Ge had utilized his cutlass many times; it had been Zong Ge’s weapon, it was just in his hands, nothing more.

Not only was that the case with the cutlass, but also with the commander’s sword and shield.

Weapon master was a title.

To reach this state, a fighter needed to be proficient in all kinds of weapons. Regardless of what weapon it was, he could quickly master them all. After reaching a level of training, the weapon and fighter would seem to fuse and become an extension of the fighter’s body, it moved where the heart wished, as the arm moved the finger.

Hei Fei and the commander were proficient with their own weapon. But their familiarity with them was unquestionably crushed into dregs compared to Zong Ge.

Zong Ge did not respond to this guess, and he tossed the swords back to their respective masters. “You lost.”

The commander and Hei Fei turned pale and they hung their heads low.

Under the multitude of staring eyes, they had no face, no fighting spirit left.

Tripleblade seized the opportunity to step forward and demand compensation.

The specific price was carefully calculated by Zi Di and hit Hei Fei’s bottom line.

Hei Fei had no choice but to swallow the bitter fruit of a lost battle.

Instead of continuing to tangle, Zong Ge instead stopped while he was ahead and swaggered off with his people.

Despite not killing his foe, Kuai Tui was gladdened to see Hei Fei looking so down. He walked behind Zong Ge and stared at the latter’s sturdy shoulders, inevitably exposing the adoration in his eyes.

With the conflict ceasing and the city guard demoralized, the commander quickly departed the pier with them.

The unsatisfied spectators gradually dispersed.

The port quickly resumed its former orderliness and liveliness.

As time passed, everything to do with this conflict was disseminated like the wind to every corner of the town.

The survivors’ reputation once again rose greatly!

Chapter 264: Recruitment

In a faintly lit tavern, there was a boiling cauldron of voices.

A customer put their oaken wine cup down onto the thick table, cleaned the wine suds from his bead, and bellowed, “Two people lost against one, they truly lost face!”

Everyone knew what he was talking about.

These last several days, the entirety of Snake Rat Island had been discussing Zong Ge.

“I heard you were at the pier, tell us what you saw in detail.” A companion curiously requested.

The drinker exposed an awkward look, “I don’t know, either. In short, there was a spate of fighting, then both the commander and Hei Fei collapsed. More embarrassingly, their weapons were snatched away by that mercenary, Shi Qi.”

Ordinary people could not discern the moves.

His companion couldn’t help but purse his brows, “Are you certain you saw that with your own eyes?”

These last few days, he also heard many rumors pertaining to this fight.

After all, on Snake Rat Island, silver level cultivators fighting was rarely seen, and would attract the enthusiastic discussion of ordinary people.

The drinker quickly refuted his fellow customer, “I saw it with my own eyes!”

The drunk customer was red across his entire face, “Shi Qi suddenly roared, pushed with both hands, and then there was a terrifying explosion of red light that looked like a fierce dragon breath!”

“Hei Fei and the commander also used the strongest battle skills of their lives, and the three’s attacks collided in the sky.”

“The ground was blown away and the sea waves reached a hundred meters!”

“The light was too harsh, and by the time we could open our eyes, Hei Fei and the commander had already fallen, their bodies all bloody.”

The retorting customer scowled greatly and berated, “Nonsense!”

But most of the tavern patrons were fixed on the customer that was loudly bragging.

Even the customer’s companion also listened attentively with zeal.

As far as these ordinary people were concerned, they wanted to hear the more exaggerated descriptions more.

Rou Zang’s capable man, the silver warlock, Mo Kong, was also mingling with these customers disguised as an ordinary person.

These days, he was secretly using collected life aura to arrange magic formations while also not forgetting to investigate and deepen his understanding of the survivors’ group.

From these false exaggerations, Mo Kong still parsed some useful information.

“The one known as mercenary Shi Qi, seemed to be close to the gold level.”

“A weapon master?”

“The city guard commander and Hei Fei had their weapons taken away. But that alone is not enough to deduce him as a weapon master.”

“Anyways, at minimum his close quarters fighting skills are very good.”

The tavern door was pushed open and Tripleblade walked in.

Upon seeing his familiar features, the tavern quickly went quiet.

A similar scene had happened before.

At the time, Hei Fei’s group was pleasure seeking in the tavern.

Now, Hei Fei’s group was the target of ridicule. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Group suppressed them, and Tripleblade was one of its members.

Numerous eyes focused on Tripleblade.

Tripleblade walked to the counter and said to the barkeeper, “Let me announce that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Group is recruiting! As long as you are proficient in a particular field, and are willing to work, you can become a member.”

“Of course, you must pass through our filter.”

He was purposely loud.

Therefore, all of the customers drinking there could hear him clearly.

They stared at each other, and all of their faces fluctuated.

Tripleblade then turned around and looked at the customers, “Drink to your heart’s content, I will be entertaining you tonight!”

Thus, joyous shouts suddenly rang out as an immense clamor nearly blew off the tavern’s roof.

The wine customers offered their thanks to Tripleblade’s generosity, and the noise attracted nearby pedestrians outside the tavern.

As people came and went, the news that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Group was recruiting spread like wildfire.

Tripleblade did not sojourn around and left the tavern soon after.

The warlock, Mo Kong, was mingling with the crowd and watched Tripleblade’s back vanish as the door shut.

“Should I seize the opportunity to mix in with them?” The thought popped into Mo Kong’s mind, but he dispelled it.

Why should he take the risk himself?

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Group had an impressive background, who knew what kind of detection method they had?

The scouting birds from before already alarmed Mo Kong.

“I’ve gathered a lot of information already, including their aura, I don’t need to collect more.”

During the gold level magic beast ruckus, Mo Kong had gathered the dragonman youngster’s life aura in passing.

“As long as the magic formation is arranged well, after it is activated, they will know how terrible we are!”

“Hehe.”

“With this merit, I have enough to report back to the captain.”

At dawn, new announcements were posted on bulletin boards on the main road that attracted the attention of many people.

Ordinary people were uneducated, and few of them could read.

Most peasants couldn’t even write their own names by the end of their lives.

But around every posting, there was a speaker preaching.

This hadn’t been invented by Tan Qiu and was done by every feudal lord.

The preachers loudly and quickly read through the bulletins to the assembled people.

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps are recruiting?”

“That mercenary group is very strong, not long ago, Shi Qi beat down Hei Fei and the Lord Commander and publicly humiliated them!”

“I heard that even Rou Zang ate a great loss at their hands.”

“If I could join them…”

Many people couldn’t help but be tantalized.

Snake Rat Island hadn’t been developed for long, and all of the island’s inhabitants were immigrants to this place.

Many of these people were very adventurous.

But someone expressed their misgivings, “The Dragon Lion Mercenary Group treated their sailors as slaves to sell off, if we become members, will we not sell ourselves into slavery? Being sold off in the future will be certain misery!”

“You’re wrong.” Another voice from the crowd immediately said, “Those sailors were all Stone Egg Island fisherfolk, they were saved by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. They weren’t strong enough and could not fight, thus the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, our Lord, and Hei Fei made a deal together. Now the fisherfolk have become our fellow islanders, and some are even my neighbors.”

The bulletin board was lively and attracted the attention of even more pedestrians.

The people gathered in three groups and their discussions grew more ardent.

One among them mentioned the material benefits of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

If one was selected for the marksman squad, they could receive an alchemy crossbow or firearm. In the future, if they had enough merit, the sailor could be given that weapon!

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps also had beastmen slaves, these were top-notch cannon fodder, and the humans that joined would be above them.

After the survivors cleaned out their previous people, they needed to replenish their manpower.

Although they had many dogmen and oxmen, they couldn’t have only beastmen, they needed the bulk of their forces to be human to maintain stability. It was the same principle Tan Qiu had when structuring the population demographics of Snake Rat Island.

Of course, Zong Ge’s previous bitter and heavy military training also allowed some people to distinguish themselves, those people are all currently part of the marksmen squad.

Zi Di was standing in a far off location, watching the people discussing around the bulletin board, she nodded slightly.

This was the third place she had inspected.

In the crowds, she had arranged people to refute rumors, answer questions, advertise the benefits of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and attract more people to come to the assessment.

The opportune moment was nigh.

Zi Di, the big guy, Mu Ban, and the others would use this cover to show their face and officially become members of the mercenary group.

Snake Rat Island, the Sheng Ming church.

As the morning sunshine passed through the multicolored glass window and illuminated the chest of the god’s likeness, morning prayer came to an end.

Almost every prayer was overseen personally by the priest.

He treated his congregation of mostly ordinary fisherfolk and peasants amiably and gently, and he attentively listened to their petitions.

As a result, after morning prayer finished, some of the participating residents stayed behind and sought the priest for aid.

Under normal circumstances, the church would solve some of the residents’ troubles. Such as supplying food and clothing and treating sicknesses.

But today, after the priest gave a brief explanation to the residents, he withdrew from the holy prayer hall.

He came to the building wing behind the church and went to the third floor.

In his study, he saw a guest secretly paying him a visit.

It was because these days, Zong Ge was rather famous.

Zong Ge had come seeking treatment.

His arm was injured, and hadn’t yet been cured.

Whenever he used his battle qi, the power of it would erode the fractures around his arm wound, causing great harm.

His arm injury was Zong Ge’s greatest hindrance that prevented him from displaying his full strength.

Unfortunately, he still hadn’t obtained suitable treatment yet.

In his previous fight with Hei Fei and the city guard commander, don’t be fooled by the fact that Zong Ge didn’t use any battle skills, he had actually moved all of the silver battle qi within him.

After he returned to his ship after the battle, his arm injury could no longer be suppressed, and once again, a bloody circular scar appeared.

This time, because the Anti-investigation and Prophecy formation was built, and paired up with the Deceptive Disguise spell, after Zong Ge discussed it with the others, he took a risk and came to the Sheng Ming church in search of treatment.

He previously dispatched someone to make an appointment with the priest and requested that this treatment session be private.

The priest understood why Zong Ge had asked this and agreed to it.

But after examining Zong Ge’s injury, the priest’s brows pursed.

“A truly dreadful wound.”

“Your arm was completely severed!”

“Regretfully, since you didn’t receive effective treatment at the time, serious repercussions remain.”

“So sorry.” The priest shook his head, “My divine spells are only silver level, insufficient to cure you.”

“A gold level divine healing spell should work.”

“A gold level healing medicine or scroll can also be tried.”

Gods bestowed believers with divine spells, and every god gave their clergy different divine power, thus each church had their own strengths.

The Sheng Ming Church was not good at healing.

Zong Ge had no choice but to regretfully leave the church.

That night, he received a letter from Tan Qiu.

The contents of the letter, as well as the humble passion of the courier, conveyed the message – Tan Qiu invited Zong Ge to dinner.

Since Tan Qiu only invited Zong Ge, it subtly wished that Zong Ge would keep this a secret.

Before leaving, Zong Ge informed the other survivors of this news.

He came to the castle alone, and joined the private banquet Tan Qiu specially prepared for him.

As the moonlight spilled across the water and delicacies, Tan Qiu made his intentions bare to Zong Ge, “Your distinguished self is close to the gold level.”

“In light of your sire’s strength, why should you be working under a semi-dragonman?”

“It would be better if you joined our side, and become a member of Snake Rat Island!”

“If you have any conditions, do not hesitate to raise them. I will do my best to meet them.”

“For example…”

When he mentioned that, Tan Qiu paused and apologized, “I don’t intend to pry on your secrets, Sir Shi Qi.”

“But I can’t bear to see a warrior like yourself be encumbered by injury.”

“I won’t hide it, I have a bottle of healing medicine on me.”

“Gold level.”

Chapter 265: Boarding the Ship

For the last several days, the pier had grown even more livelier.

People were lining up.

At the end of this line, a long table and stools had been set up. Tripleblade kept watch and was responsible for checking in the attracted fresh talent. Lan Zao also led a team of sailors to maintain order there.

“Next in line.” Tripleblade called out.

“What is your name?”

“Wei. Lord, I am called Wei.”

This person was as thin as a match and his face was emaciated, but in front of Tripleblade, he straightened his chest and did his utmost to look his best.

“What skills do you have?”

“I. I am a farmer.”

Tripleblade shook his head, “Next in line.”

Wei immediately became dismayed. He opened his mouth and wanted to say something and fight for his case. But the person behind him pushed him away.

“My turn, my turn.”

Tripleblade asked again. “What is your name?”

The same process, the same questions culled one person after the other.

Ever since the survivors posted their announcement and disseminated it through the tavern, there was an endless stream of people wanting to join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Most of these people were ordinary, none met the recruitment standard.

It didn’t matter.

The survivors had anticipated that this would happen.

By proportion, as long as there was enough people, they could find the best within them.

After the survivors discussed, they decided to put Tripleblade in charge of checking them.

Tripleblade had once been a mercenary commander, and his shrewd and ruthless sight could make out the ins and outs of many people.

Next in line, next in line, next in line….

Tripleblade quickly weeded out many people, however, the line didn’t seem to shorten, as there were inevitably newcomers who wanted to try their luck.

Three fisherfolk came together.

“What are your names?” Tripleblade asked.

“Da Tou.”

“Xian Yu.”

“Ji Mao.”

“What do you do?”

“We are fisherfolk.” The eldest of the three, Da Tou, grinned.

The other two lowered their eyes submissively.

Tripleblade ran his eyes over the three, then lingered his gaze on Da Tou’s thumb and forefinger.

“Hmph.” Tripleblade coldly snorted.

Da Tou’s heart suddenly shivered, he knew his pirate identity had been discovered.

“Your past identity is unimportant, what is important is how you do in the future. Understood?” Said Tripleblade.

“Understood, understood.” Da Tou nodded at once.

“Up you go, comply with their arrangements.” Said Tripleblade, he then took three wooden identification plates from the table and gave it to the three people.

To lessen the workload and increase efficiency, the survivors employed distinct wooden plates to differentiate the level of newcomers.

After seeing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps recruit those three, the people behind them grew restless, many of them threw envious gazes at them.

Not long after.

“Hello, my Lord.” A long bearded and candle yellow faced imperial eastern person nodded his head and bowed to Tripleblade.

Before Tripleblade could give his questions, he immediately gave his answers. “This humble one is known as Xu Ma, hehe.”

He was thin and tall, and was actually taller than Zong Ge, but gave people the impression that he was too weak to stand up to the wind.

Tripleblade’s gaze focused, and he sensed Xu Ma’s bronze level aura.

As long as they were a cultivator, they were worth recruiting.

Tripleblade examined Xu Ma thoroughly, then pursed his brows, “You are a thief?”

Xu Ma’s heart trembled with fear, and he promptly waved his hand, “A small time pickpocket, a small time pickpocket…that is all in the past, I no longer do that kind of work.”

“You can do that work, but you must listen to orders.” Tripleblade took out a bronze token and threw it to him, “Go ahead.”

“As you bid!” Xu Ma happily caught the token and walked up onto the ship.

When he boarded the ship, he waited to be sorted further.

He had been the second bronze level fighter.

As for iron levels, they still hadn’t recruited any yet.

The queue was beginning to shorten.

Next was Mu Ban’s turn.

He carried some simple and crude carpentry tools on his back.

Tripleblade treated him as if this was the first time he had seen Mu Ban, he glanced at the carpentry tools on Mu ban’s back. “You can do carpentry?”

Naturally, Mu Ban also had the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell on him, and his looks had changed drastically from his prior appearance.

Mu Ban promptly replied, “I can do a bit.”

“What is your name?”

“Your Lordship can call me Lao Mu Tou.” Mu Ban looked like a yes man.

“Good, our ships need a carpenter, then you can wait in the carpentry maintenance room.”

“Yes, my Lord.”

During lunch time, recruitment was suspended for a break.

It continued in the afternoon.

The line was even longer than it was in the morning.

One turn, one weeded out, causing many of the rejected to complain that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ standards were too high.

But at the same time, it also caused many others to look up to the survivors’ group.

Suddenly, the ground began to lightly rumble.

A disturbance broke out in the crowd.

A small giant took a big step forward and quickly approached.

He was gigantic, and his ugly looks caused many people to become agitated, restless, and look fearful.

“Beat it, let me through!” The small giant had a malicious aura and wanted to sweep away the people in front of him.

“What are you doing?!” Tripleblade stood up at once and shouted.

The small giant was not a cultivator, and upon feeling the iron level life aura, he huddled his head and stopped moving.

Order had been maintained.

The small giant shamefully growled, and glared at Tripleblade, “You won’t accept me?”

Tripleblade looked at him and hesitated, “I need to ask for instructions from the higher ups.”

He put on an act and went into the ship, then quickly came back out.

Zong Ge also came out with him.

“Wow, its Lord Shi Qi!”

“He is Shi Qi?”

Countless people looked up at Zong Ge, their eyes filled with adoration and curiosity.

“Lord Shi Qi, that’s him.” Tripleblade pointed at the small giant.

Zong Ge stood on the side of the ship and overlooked the scene below, then he beckoned to the small giant, “Come up.”

The small giant ecstatically howled with laughter, “Hehehe!”

Under the vigilant gazes of the fearful sailors, he took only three steps up the wooden stairs to ascend to the deck.

“Move aside!” He bellowed as he chased away sailors until he had a section of deck to himself. Afterwards, he sat down, leaned against the side of the ship, and closed his eyes.

“A good performance.” In the aft room, the young captain had a panoramic view of this scene.

The small giant was the big guy.

His appearance was likewise changed. Although his height was the same, his features had been altered drastically and now had a copper horn protruding from his head.

In order to distinguish himself from his previous rank on the Hog’s Kiss, Fei She had personally instructed him in how he should act.

After no less than a hundred training sessions, the result was worth the investment.

The deck was gradually filled with many more newcomers.

Bai Ya mixed into the line, and also climbed onto the deck. He was ordinary and didn’t garner anyone’s attention.

Soon after, Fei She also became a member, with his fat body, he claimed to be a cook.

The deck was now jam-packed with people.

Sailors were whispering in each other’s ear, the most lively of them was Xu Ma.

He was chatting to everyone and everywhere.

He noticed that Bai Ya looked uncomfortable, and decided to come by his side, “Young man, is this your first time on a boat?”

Bai Ya couldn’t help but be flabbergasted by Xu Ma, “Ah? Yes, yes, it is.”

“Hehe. Follow me, I will protect you.” Xu Ma’s eyes glittered as he roped him in.

“Ah?”

“Ah, what, ah, I can see this is the first time you’ve gotten on a ship, you don’t know the rules.” Xu Ma raised his head, “Young men who leave home to make their way in the world have to mingle to be able to eat well, find your place, and find good relationships. Those alone and excluded are very easy to bully. Look at those people over there.”

Bai Ya shifted his line of sight as Xu Ma whispered into his head, “Their leader is called Da Tou, that one with the waned face is called Xian Yu, and that one with the lush chicken nest shaped hair is Ji Mao. Those three are standing together, they are a group. They have a close relationship, and so aren’t easy to bully, and will even bully others around.”

“Pay attention to Da Tou, his hand has calluses there, that is a sign of blade usage. His face is calm and doesn’t sway as much as the other two either as he sizes up the masts and cannon ports. He might be a former pirate.”

“Is that so?” Bai Ya was somewhat skeptical.

Xu Ma glared back at Bai Ya, “If you don’t believe me, you can ask them yourself.”

Bai Ya was curious and went up to quietly ask. Da Tuo coldly snorted, and didn’t deny it, nor did the other two by his side.

Bai Ya returned to his original spot, and respectfully said to Xu Ma, “Senior, your eyes are very accurate. I didn’t expect the mercenary group to recruit such people.”

Xu Ma smiled, “Such pirates are very common. Plenty of pirates who are either injured, weary of fighting, or have enough money live on the islands for a time.”

“After a long time, or their injuries are healed, these pirates begin to loathe ordinary life or flush all of their coin away, they will go out to sea again.”

“If you board a pirate ship, you are a pirate. If you board a merchant ship, you are a sailor. If you board a mercenary ship, you are a member of a mercenary group.”

Xu Ma then asked, “Can you fight those three by yourself?”

Bai Ya shook his head again.

“That is correct.” Xu Ma clapped Bai Ya’s shoulder and sighed, “With just a look, I knew you were an outsider, I am one as well. We should be in solidarity, so we aren’t bullied on this ship in the future and strive for more spoils of war, right?”

Bai Ya could only nod.

Xu Ma felt satisfied, he had been a thief for many years and his sharp vision had detected Bai Ya, a “good seedling” in the crowd at once. No matter how one saw it, he was a newcomer, and such a person was very obedient.

Xu Ma consoled, “Your luck is pretty good.”

“Good?” Bai Ya didn’t understand what he was hearing.

“It’s your first ship and it turned out to be a great ship. You chanced upon two silver level fighters setting up a team, this kind of opportunity might not appear again in your life!” Xu Ma lamented, he was really emotional.

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary group is not ordinary; they were able to inflict heavy losses on Rou Zang! Recently, Shi Qi fought both the city guard commander and Hei Fei and dealt a great blow to their character. If the first mate is already that strong, then the leader is bound to be even stronger.”

“During the gold level magic beast kerfuffle, I saw Lord Long Fu fighting. Such a dashing figure! I can only say this, he is worthy of being the man above Lord Shi Qi!”

Xu Ma gave a thumbs up.

Bai Ya adored the dragonman youngster.

His life had been saved by the young man.

When the youngster exposed the truth of being a beast knight, led them to eradicate Jia Sha, and fled Mystifying Monster Island Together, the admiration and love Bai Ya had for him grew.

“Don’t have the mentality of making quick coin, just follow them and work well.” Xu Ma clapped Bai Ya’s shoulder again.

Bai Ya was in a weird mood, he didn’t expect that a senior like him would actually be taught by a newcomer.

Bai Ya would not expose the truth.

Using new appearances, the old members snuck in and pretended to not know each other, they would slowly be promoted in the future.

Bai Ya remained unmoved, and caused Xu Ma to misunderstand him somewhat, he sighed, and his tone became a little melancholy, “Smelly youngster, you really are too young, you don’t know how treasured this opportunity should be! Alas…”

The middled aged man with a yellow face had a complex expression, as if recalling his own early life, “We are people without good parents, bloodlines, aptitude, or property, this is how our lives will be.”

“Therefore, when such an opportunity appears, we must grab hold of it tightly! Otherwise, how will you stick out?”

“You are fortunate to be on this ship, and even luckier to meet me.” After rambling for a bit, he finally got to the main point.

“Ah?” Bai Ya was confused again.

“I came to teach you, youngster, and to keep an eye on you.” In order to sooth this little brother, he began to demonstrate.

He approached Mu Ban and greeted him warmly.

Mu Ban used an ambiguous gaze to look at Bai Ya that seemed to say, “What’s up with this guy?”

Bai Ya winked, indicating that “I also just met him”.

For Bai Ya’s face, Mu Ban said a few perfunctory things to Xu Ma.

Xu Ma returned, pleased with himself, “I have already wangled with the ship’s carpenter.”

“Did, wangled?”

“Of course.” Xu Ma clapped Bai Ya’s shoulder, “We want to build good relationships with some people, that way our lives on board will be better than the others. Don’t be fooled that I am a bronze level, while he is an ordinary person. He is actually very valuable, because he is a carpenter! Even a new ship can break. A carpenter can repair ships, he is an indispensable person. He can sleep in his own workroom, while you have to sleep in a hammock. Do you understand?”

“Oh.” Bai Ya could only nod.

“We also need a good relationship with the cook. I remember he is called Da Fei.” Xu Ma whispered.

He was very diligent.

He attentively listened to Tripleblade asking the other people questions and made some inquiries for information.

“Look at this.” Xu Ma set out again.

“Hey, Senior Brother Da Fei.”

“Hm.”

“I am called Xu Ma, hehe.”

“Hm.”

“Hic…in the future, please take care of us.”

“Hm.”

Xu Ma returned, and grumbled to Bai Ya, “He is only an ordinary person, yet he is so arrogant.”

Bai Ya’s heart wanted to explain why Fei She was like that, he was not arrogant, he had a stutter. But to not expose this distinct trait, he had no choice but to remain silent on it.

Xu Ma continued, “Therefore, you can see that I, a bronze level expert, am humble and make friends with ordinary people. In the future, all of them on this ship will give me face.”

“Face will not be me giving it to myself, it will be others giving it to me. This is why some people are good at mingling, and some people are bad at mingling, this is the reason.”

“Boy, if you mingle with me, you won’t be at a disadvantage.”

Bai Ya: “...”

As Xu Ma chattered on, a commotion suddenly occurred on the ship.

A magician flew in at low altitude over the line of people, and landed directly in front of Tripleblade.

He had a bronze level life aura.

A bronze level magician!

Tripleblade stood up, and with rapt attention, sized up the cultivator cautiously.

The magician lived their hood, exposing an average looking woman.

She was Zi Di.

Through the Deceptive Disguise spell, her iron level aura had been disguised as a bronze level one.

Zi Di quickly and easily became a member.

Her arrival caused the people on board who had been reviewed to grow even more excited.

“A magician!”

“A magician actually joined us, how truly wonderful.”

Magicians were more uncommon than fighters.

If they had the same life level, magicians were more popular than fighters. This was not because magicians were stronger than fighters, but rather because magicians had more abundant methods.

When Zi Di joined, the newcomers sensed that their futures were getting brighter.

She didn’t stay to socialize on deck, instead she was called to the captain’s room to be interviewed by Captain Long Fu personally.

When she became a member, her status would not be ordinary.

Chapter 266: All of My Talents Have All Been Recruited

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps continued to recruit.

However, as the crew was gradually filled, so too did the standards rise.

The pier no longer had a line of people, and now one needed to be at least a bronze level to have any hope of joining the mercenary group.

Besides the Deep Sea Monster Fish hiding on the nearby seafloor, the survivors also had increased their ships from five to six.

Before, when Zong Ge found trouble with Hei Fei, the latter had no choice but to give up his profits to quell the conflict.

In the end, he would rather give up one of his ships to save some of his gold coins.

After inspecting it, the survivors confirmed that the ordinary ship had been used to transport slave, and thus, its hold needed to be remolded somewhat.

The survivors’ recruitment plan was very successful, and the six ships were nearing full capacity.

Every day, Zong Ge would call the sailors together to be personally trained by him.

His arm was injured, which greatly reduced the amount of time he could cultivate battle qi. Since he had surplus time and energy, he decided to use it to train these people.

An open area of the pier was packed with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Group’s people.

Bai Ya, Mu Ban, Fei She, and the other survivors also mixed in with them.

Zong Ge stood in front of everyone, “Today, we will begin training with weapons.”

In front of him was a pile of weapons, including axes and cutlasses.

All of them were made out of wood.

Not only that, but the wooden weapons were also wrapped with cloth, they were all equipment specialized for training.

“There are many kinds of weapons.” Zong Ge extended his leg and lightly kicked up a wooden dagger from the weapon pile.

He grabbed the wooden dagger, and did a thrusting motion, “Dagger are most suitable for surprise attacks, and are the best weapon to use in the narrow confines of ship holds.”

He tossed the dagger, then picked up a simple axe, “A boarding axe. We often use it to cut rigging and rope netting, of course, it can also cut the flying hooks of enemies. Many times, this weapon can also help us board and pry open cabin doors.”

He cast away the wooden axe and seized a wooden cutlass, “A sailor cutlass! This is our main weapon, this kind of blade is shorter than normal swords. Thus, it is more suited for close quarter fights on ships. We use it to slash and chop, sometimes to cut down the enemy, and sometimes as table knives to cut food with.

“In addition to those, there are also short swords, flying hooks, firearms, cannons…”

Zong Ge presented them all.

Short swords were used for stabbing and blocking, needed technique, and their slashing was weaker than sailor cutlasses.

Flying hooks were simple to use. They were thrown and hooked into the sides of enemy warships.

Firearms were very expensive, and not everyone was outfitted with one. Since they were also simple to use, Zong Ge did not plan to conduct training for them.

As for cannons, that was another matter entirely.

If one wanted to be a famous and excellent pirate, they actually needed to master many things.

Not only did they need to work the sails, almost all pirates needed to simultaneously know how to use both cold and hot weapons.

“Next, I will teach you the most basic movements.” Zong Ge began to demonstrate.

Daggers, boarding axes, sailor cutlasses, short swords, flying hooks, and other weapons were all used by him once through. As a military instructor, Zong Ge was very familiar with doing this.

“You are free to practice now.” Zong Ge ordered.

Some sailors clumsily brandished them, while others were experienced with them, the level of skill varied.

Zong Ge pointed out mistakes in movements and observed combat experts.

Some people distinguished themselves, and were remembered by Zong Ge.

“So boring.” Xu Ma was playing with a wooden dagger. As a thief, his best weapons were small arms. Of course, he was still better at using axes, daggers, and other weapons than ordinary people.

“Hey, that young man is making an effort.” When Xu Ma saw the earnestly training Bai Ya sweating profusely, he commended him.

Under the scorching sun, Bai Ya’s bright eyes and sparkling sweat refracted the sunlight and his youthful energy.

He put a hundred percent into his practice.

On the one hand, it was because he was inspired by the noble Miss Xi Qiu’s love to become a knight. On the other hand, the danger of Mystifying Monster Island made him crave for great strength.

However, when Zong Ge’s gaze swept past Bai Ya, it did not stop on him.

Bai Ya did not lack courage, and on Mystifying Monster Island, he confronted the terrifying and twisted magic beasts on the front lines. He also did not lack practice.

On Mystifying Monster Island, he began to practice with weapons.

He lacked a bloodline.

When free practice ended, Zong Ge divided up everyone into groups based on what he observed.

He did his best to put sailors of the same level together in the same group.

This would make future training easier and improve training efficacy.

This training was seen by Tan Qiu.

Right now, he was standing on a ship and watching, completely entranced.

The dragonman youngster was accompanying him.

Although the survivors had already been on Snake Rat Island for some time, as the local lord, this was the first time. Tan Qiu had personally paid a visit to them.

The reason was because the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps recruitment move was too big and noisy, the entire town was abuzz with it.

If there was a lot of thunder, but little rain, it would have been fine.

But the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had gained a lot from their recruitment!

Tan Qiu gazed from afar with rapt attention, and with a sour tone, he said, “Your group’s strength had grown rapidly. These people are of decent quality, no wonder your group has been selecting carefully these days.”

Tan Qiu also saw things, even if his eyes weren’t as shrewd as Tripleblade’s, he still noticed many things the longer he watched.

The sailors that were training had physiques and stamina that exceeded that of ordinary people. Some were adept with weapons, and one could make out that they were experienced at fighting on land and at sea.

The dragonman youngster smiled, “There was no other way, our financial resources are limited, and our ships are small. If we wanted to grow our strength, we had to select the most elite sailors.”

“Not like you, Lord Tan Qiu. On this abundant island, not only do you have elite troops and generals, but you also have many other soldiers.”

The young man sung praises of Tan Qiu.

But Tan Qiu shook his head, “I have no general. Last time, didn’t my city guard commander get publicly defeated by Shi Qi? What a disastrous loss!”

Soon after, his gaze focused on a beastmen sailor, his tone casual as he said, “Did you actually bring those slaves back from the brink?”

The dragonman youngster smiled as he explained, “Our group’s magician, Hong Teng did that.”

“Although she is only a bronze level magician, she has studied medicine deeply.”

“Not only has she dipped into standard magic medicine, but she also learned about the barbarian race’s herbal medicines.”

“It was she who brought those beastmen back to life.”

When Tan Qiu heard that, he turned to look at the dragonman youngster, his eyes containing deep meaning.

He had heard that earlier.

But he found it difficult to believe.

The lord of Snake Rat Island recalled the three party deal from beginning to end, then suddenly realized that the survivors were its biggest beneficiary!

“It was said that they were the ones who manipulated those beastmen slaves. If that is the case, then they exploited both Hei Fei and I.”

As far as that was concerned, Tan Qiu had no evidence of it.

But it did not prevent him from valuing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps more and even fearing them.

“Let’s talk about that magician, Hong Teng. I am quite curious about them.” Tan Qiu continued to pry, “Why don’t we meet her?”

“She is making medicine in the medicinal cabin; I dare not disturb her.” The dragonman youngster tactfully declined.

Tan Qiu nodded, “I understand, after all, they are a talented person, even though they lack cultivation.”

“I heard there is also a small giant on your ship?”

The big guy didn’t participate in training.

His intellect was too low, and couldn’t even cultivate a battle qi technique, let alone martial skills.

To prevent him from exposing inside information, the dragonman youngster did not arrange anything for him.

“I had him set up in a room at the bow by himself. The entire bow room had been remodeled to fit his large body size.” The dragonman youngster gave a brief introduction of the big guy’s circumstances.

“If your group did not recruit him, I wouldn’t have expected that my Snake Rat Island had so many hidden dragons and talents on it.” Tan Qiu’s entire face filled with regret.

If the magician, Hong Teng, had actually solved the beastmen’s disease, then her value was quite high.

Then there was the small giant, even if he wasn’t a cultivator, with just his body, he could become the tip on the battlefield. If he became a cultivator in the future, then that would be even more wonderful.

Even if he couldn’t fight, he could labor at astonishing efficiency.

Tan Qiu recalled the time when his castle was being built, it had been difficult. If he had the small giant helping during that time, it would have saved so much trouble.

He inwardly calculated how strong the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were.

Currently, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were mostly composed of humans, these people were filtered by levels and were of high quality.

Second were the beastmen slaves, composed of dogmen and oxmen. These beastmen were tall and sturdy, and after going through the chaos of war, were selected to be sold as slaves. Their vitality was exuberant and many of them had potential that could be excavated.

What made Tan Qiu’s heart beat faster was that dogmen and oxmen were the most loyal of beastmen and the easiest to manage.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps also had many cultivators.

They had 12 bronze levels, including one magician.

There were 3 iron levels. They were Tripleblade, Cang Xu, and Kuai Tui, all of them were worth having.

Then there were the 2 silver levels. Zong Ge was a peak silver level and suspected weapon master. The dragonman youngster’s bravery during the gold level magic beast incident had left an extremely deep impression on Tan Qiu.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Group had been on Snake Rat Island for a long time, and the speed at which their strength swelled could be seen with the naked eye.

“Perhaps, I also should have a large-scale recruitment of fresh outside talent?” The thought inevitably stirred in Tan Qiu’s mind.

Even though Tan Qiu had Snake Rat Island as his foundation, he couldn’t help but be envious.

In some respect, when the survivors recruited so many people from Snake Rat Island, it also weakened the strength of Snake Rat Island.

“Anyways, if I can get Shi Qi, losing these talents won’t matter.”

Tan Qiu once again recalled his sly poaching of him.

Zong Ge responded that he needed time to think it over.

But consideration was not enough to satisfy Tan Qiu.

He wanted the entirety of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Group to help him deal with Rou Zang!

“With your group’s pool of talent, your prospects look bright. However, according to what I see, you still lack some people. Has Commander Long Fu looked into battle teams?” Tan Qiu asked.

“Battle teams?” The dragonman youngster thought of Rou Zang’s hurtling sword and shield force.

Tan Qiu said, “Battle teams are quite the important force on a battlefield. Battle teams are slowly shaped, and aren’t easy to foster, however any organization that has them will substantially increase their battle power.”

“It is different from battle qi techniques, and related to how the battle team is established, every organization keeps that information secret, and it is very difficult to purchase.”

“I want to give your group a battle team organization method. It is called the Drunken Shrimp Mud Spear Army!”

“They cultivate the same battle qi technique, the Peasant Technique, and use the same weapon, the shrimp spear. This spear force can quickly march through mud and even slink through marshes. They can also be adapted to the sea and can fight in water.”

Tan Qiu’s presentation inevitably aroused the attention of the dragonman youngster.

Battle teams were something the survivors really wanted, and the earlier they developed them, the better.

The dragonman youngster had no battle team organization plans currently.

War Merchant’s database had nothing related to this topic in it.

Now, there was a battle team organization plan directly in front of the young man.

“But…it won’t be easy to take.” The dragonman youngster sighed to himself.

This was clearly bait Tan Qiu was tossing to him.

He wanted to recruit the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps over to deal with Rou Zang.

This thought, along with the survivors’ swelling strength, had increased his determination.

As expected, Tan Qiu followed up with his want to unite everyone and eradicate Rou Zang.

“Lord Tan Qiu is sincere, I can sense it. But this is a significant matter, I still need to think it over.” The dragonman youngster looked embarrassed, and dodged the question for the time being.

Chapter 267: Zong Ge Stands Alone

After Tan Qiu left, the survivors’ leadership met again.

“Snake Rat Island’s ruling lord’s determination to eradicate Rou Zang is quite resolute.

“This time, he used the battle squad organization plan to obtain our strength.” The dragonman briefed them on the situation.

“Battle squad?” Cang Xu stared, “The Sha Ta Clan I served had two battle squad types. One is the Violent Sand Wind Archers and the other is the Tower Shield Guards. The specific organization of them is one of the secrets of the Sha Ta Clan, and is one of their pillars that supports them.”

“Battle squad plans are indeed rarely seen on the market, and whenever one is auctioned off, it triggers a mad scramble for them.” Said Zi Di.

“The Drunken Shrimp Mud Spear Army…” Zong Ge pondered, “I know of this battle squad. Its greatest strength is stealth, because it can travel underground for some distance. During the Green Acid Swamp Unification Campaign, the battle for Golden Steel Cliff, and the Star Prairie Joint Operation, this battle squad performed brilliantly.”

“In the Green Acid Swamp Unification Campaign, there were at least three contingents of the Drunken Shrimp Mud Spear Army. The commander hid these spearmen in the swamps and waited until the lizardmen tribesmen passed by to ambush them, regularly causing disastrous losses to the lizardmen’s archers. Without long range firepower, the lizardmen sank into a large disadvantage. During battles, the imperial army easily exploited this disadvantage and used it to achieve victory.”

“During the battle for Gold Steel Cliff, the empire had been beaten back by the dwarfs. After the imperial generals talked it over, they launched their combined arms attack. In the sky there were griffin and eagle knights, while on the mountain the main force’s catapults and other units stormed it head on. After magicians used the Change Stone to Mud spell to soften the insides of the cliff, a unit of Drunken Shrimp Mud Spear Army seized that opportunity to attack the underground. However, their main mission was to protect the legend level druid, Lu Pan. Lu Pan transformed into a magic beast pangolin and dug into the foundations of Gold Steel Cliff’s fortress, creating an opening in the enemy’s defensive line. The Drunken Shrimp Mud Spear Army was nearly wiped out in this engagement.”

“During the Star Prairie Joint Operation, the horsemen’s khan was poisoned. The imperial commanders had a small unit of 16 Drunken Shrimp Mud Spear Army go underground to sneak in and directly raid the khan’s tent. Their main purpose was to be bait, and they successfully distracted the cultivators in the army. Due to this, the horsemen’s army lacked its cultivators’ oversight, allowing the imperial forces to rout it outright.”

Zong Ge proved he was worthy of being an outstanding officer when he immediately recounted the military campaigns the Drunken Shrimp Mud Spear Army were involved in.

This battle squad had undertaken many major missions and contributed much to achieving victorious wars. However, after the young captain had heard it all, his brows pursed.

He reckoned in his heart, “During the Green Acid Swamp Campaign, the Drunken Shrimp Mud Spear Army were used for ambushes. During the fight for Gold Steel Cliff, they were the army that protected the legendary druid, Lu Pan, and were effectively depleted for that purpose. Then in the Star Prairie Joint Operation, they were candidly assigned as bait.”

When Zong Ge saw the young man frown, he understood what the latter was expressing.

He immediately illuminated him, “I don’t endorse the establishment of a battle squad like the Drunken Shrimp Mud Spear Army.”

“How the imperial commanders used this battle squad reveals how much they think it is worth.”

“Besides in the swamps, other battle squads will perform better. In other places, they need to cooperate with others to be a threat. But even then, they are more often than not bait or cannon fodder.”

Zong Ge continued, “The primary goal for developing a battle squad is to check and balance an enemy expert in the absence of our own.”

“Through circulating the same battle qi technique and using the same equipment, they can train a cohesive unit that relies on cooperation. This is using quantity to compete with quality.”

“But the results were not good.”

“This battle squad could indeed inconvenience cultivators but can only inconvenience them.”

“The reason why battle squads have been circulated in present day, have many varied types, and are very popular on all continents, is because people noticed that although battle squads are not good at dealing with the strong, when dealing with enemies of the same level, the one with the battle squad will often crush them in battle.”

“Take our current situation.”

“Even if we establish a Drunken Shrimp Mud Spear Army, we cannot use it to deal with Rou Zang, on the contrary, Rou Zang will inflict heavy losses on it and even destroy it to a man.”

“And to deal with those pirates, we don’t require a battle squad. Because ships have limited space and our men are finite, while the Drunken Shrimp Mud Spear Army requires large quantities of them.”

“The threshold to establish a Drunken Shrimp Mud Spear Army is low, and is comparatively easy to form the broad scale needed for stable battle strength.

“We need a higher threshold, and to pursue quality over quantity for our battle squad. This smaller squad also needs to be capable of making an impact.”

“Secondly, the Drunken Shrimp Mud Spear Army is unsuitable for naval battles. Although they can go into the water, going underwater is different from going underground, and water is easier to examine than mud. They will find it difficult to sneak about and conduct ambushes.”

Zi Di nodded, “If that is so, then even if we set up this type of battle squad, won’t the earrings of it be low?”

“No.” Zong Ge nevertheless shook his head, “If we don’t have any other organization methods, I still suggest that we think about obtaining this battle squad organization method.”

“Anything is better than nothing.”

“And right now, I have a battle squad organization method.”

At that moment, Zong Ge got the attention of everyone.

Everyone was slightly surprised, but soon accepted it.

After all, Zong Ge had shown many hints of his extraordinary background.

“Describe it in detail.” Said Cang Xu.

Zong Ge nodded, “I believe the most suitable battle squad is an archer squad.”

“We have many alchemy firearms and crossbows on us. With those are our base, we can create Arrow Rain Archers and Arrow Storm Archers.”

“Kuai Tui and his dogmen are fit for establishing a Quick Knife Hand battle squad.”

“There are too few oxmen. We can provide them with heavy armor and turn them into the core power of small scale battlefields - Healing Tower Guards.”

Despite somewhat anticipating it, when he heard Zong Ge’s explanation, the young man and the others still felt amazed.

It was very clear that these were not the only battle squads Zong Ge could make, they were the ones he picked as they were the most appropriate for their situation.

Compared to gathering battle qi techniques, collecting battle squad organization methods was far harder.

Because battle squad organization methods contained one or more battle qi techniques, the equipment used to fight, special training regimens, and other such things.

Many organizations or powers even deliberately disseminated battle qi techniques. But no power would circulate how they created their own battle squads.

The reason for both was actually the same – for the sake of organization.

Keeping battle squad formation methods secret was protecting the foundation of an organization. Spreading battle qi techniques filtered for suitable newcomers and allowed the continuous absorption of new blood that strengthened the organization.

It was rare that someone like Zong Ge could provide three battle squad formation methods in short succession.

Even Tan Qiu would be hard pressed to accomplish this.

“So, what family did Zong Ge come from?”

“He actually knows this many battle squad organization methods?”

Everyone was amazed and curious.

Anyways, the sudden fact that they had so many battle squad organization methods was a boon to the survivors.

Previously, the Drunken Shrimp Mud Spear Army was alluring.

“In that case, do we have the resources to set up battle squads?” The dragonman looked at Zi Di with some concern.

Zi Di controlled the survivors’ money and knew it like the back of her hand. But when it came to how battle squads were formed, she was uninformed.

The ghost girl looked at Zong Ge.

Zong Ge calmly ran his eyes across the room, they looked straight into the dragonman youngster’s eyes, “If I am to contribute these battle squad organization methods, I will need to be compensated.”

Everyone was immediately silent.

The dragonman youngster nodded, then sincerely replied, “Indeed, they belong to you, Zong Ge, your requirement is reasonable and fair.”

“I was just negligent.”

“May I ask what you require?”

Zong Ge bluntly replied, “I need a ship, my own ship. I also need manpower, Kuai Tui and the dogmen can serve as my private guard.”

The other three were once again silent.

Zi Di’s face sank.

Cang Xu’s gaze also hardened.

Everyone felt that Zong Ge was crossing the line. He was openly making his own small group, it was very evident that he planned to part ways in the future.

Despite the competition earlier on Mystifying Monster Island, everyone was accustomed to cooperating with each other fully in solidarity.

Zong Ge’s request faintly betrayed everyone’s feelings.

The young man’s face did not change, and he sighed to himself.

However, upon careful thought, Zong Ge’s request was actually quite reasonable.

Before on Mystifying Monster Island, when he first met Zong Ge. He had been a man of unfathomable ambition and a grand person unwilling to be under someone else.

During the later stages of Mystifying Monster Island as well as dealing with Rou Zang, in order to survive and compete with powerful enemies, the survivors had to join forces and unite tightly.

Upon reaching Snake Rat Island, and resting and reorganizing for so many days, the external circumstances had become far more relaxed, thus the unity that bound everyone naturally began to show cracks.

“Zi Di and I are very close, while Cang Xu is a necromancer, and because of that, he finds it very difficult to integrate with normal society or organization, in addition to the immense benefits of the Bloodcore. But Zong Ge…”

“Up until now, I still don’t know his background.”

“Anyways, I understand his nature. He really is that type of person.”

“Upon thinking that, the young captain nodded to Zong Ge, “You request is not excessive, and even quite low.

“I hereto accept your request Zong Ge, my comrade in arms. I shall give you a ship and the dogmen.”

“Not only that, but in accordance with the concrete market value of battle squad organization methods, in the future, I will give you more manpower and ships.”

Zong Ge was pleased with the young captain’s response.

The young captain’s reaction was within his expectations.

Like how the young man understood Zong Ge, Zong Ge too understood the young man’s character.

The speech the beast knight had given still left a deep impression on him to this day.

He knew the young captain always had the nature of a knight, Zong Ge even liked him more than templar knights. As for his magic beast transformations, it was just a way to elevate his battle strength, nothing more.

Zong Ge obtained what he wanted, thus he answered the query Zi Di had earlier, “After this, I will give you a list of items and the specific details on how to organize those three battle squads.”

“Although there are many things on the list, don’t be nervous.”

“In short, Tan Qiu was right.”

“Battle squads need time to form.”

“Because we need to use a specific battle qi technique, equipment, and drilling regimens, as well as selecting suitable members for the squad.”

“There are countless bloodlines, each unlike the other, thus selecting members is quite the long process.”

“We can form the three squads at the same time with very low investments in the early stages. You may even find that no one can be used, because there are no suitable candidates. During the later stages of formation, the cost to maintain the battle squads will gradually increase and we will need to pay attention to the upkeep cost.”

“Normally, the more difficult the member selection is, the stronger the battle squad will perform in combat.”

“The three battle squads we have selected all have a much higher selection criteria than the Drunken Shrimp Mud Spear Army.”

Zong Ge’s standards for troops was very high, and he believed the sailors he had previously were of too low quality, they could not meet his eye, and he immediately gave them up.

The sailors now could barely reach his minimum standards.

Among them all, he valued the dogmen the most.

Thanks to his many years of leadership experience, he felt that after he trained these dogmen, they would become one of his cornerstones in the future.

After the brief discussion finished, everyone received an alchemy device.

Zong Ge left the captain’s room, he called over Tripleblade and Kuai Tui, began packing up his things, started mustering a group of people, and began the relocation to his own ship.

It was very noisy.

Tripleblade was very excited, while Kuai Tui already began to adore Zong Ge, thus he animatedly led his clansmen to accompany Zong Ge’s relocation.

“What’s going on?”
