“Is there a conflict going on?”

“I heard Lord Shi Qi’s cultivation has reached the peak of the silver level, he is only a step away from the gold level. It is normal that Captain Long Fu can no longer keep him under him.”

The other sailors were privately discussing.

Zong Ge was expressionless.

When he arrived at his own ship, he put his hand onto its side and sighed to himself, “This is my starting point.”

He knew what kind of vile effects his action would bring.

But he had to do this!

He was ambitious, and wanted to start an undertaking. He had to have a clear idea of who his troops would be.

Not only him, but also Tripleblade, Kuai Tui, as well as the dragonman youngster, Zi Di, Cang Xu, and the others.

“This group does not have a future, and sooner or later it will break apart.”

“Growing it now is indeed a bad influence. However, if I blindly compromise and mix up my benefits with them, when I separate in the future, it will be harder to explain, and I might have a falling out with them.”

“Me doing this is absolutely the correct decision!”

“He does not know what he is missing. Alas…” Cang Xu shook his head and sighed.

The qualitative changes the Bloodcore provided could turn heaven and earth upside down for anyone and any organization.

The Bloodcore also helped immensely in the formation of battle squads.

The main difficulty in forming battle squads was finding the people.

If one had a Bloodcore, would they still need to find people?

The greatest obstruction no longer existed for the young captain, for he had the Bloodcore!

“He won’t miss it.” The dragonman youngster shook his head, “It was a spoil of war that belonged to everyone, Zong Ge also has the right to have a part of it.”

“It was only because of how important it is that we concealed it from him.”

“This is only temporary.”

“We will wait for an opportune moment to mature in the future to tell him about it.”

But it was undeniable that because of Zong Ge’s act today, such an opportunity would be further delayed.

Zi Di began to calculate the finances, “The battle squads need people and equipment.”

“We can do alchemy, but to create war gear, we need a blacksmith.”

“We don’t have the ability to create the needed equipment, but the price of then is comparatively low. Our alchemy capabilities can be used in other aspects to be more productive.”

“Furthermore, we lost a yellow crab alchemy puppet when building that formation.”

Altogether, they only had two yellow crab alchemy puppets, they were the main ones doing the physical labor of alchemy.

The tower spirit only directed them, and although the Deep Sea Monster Fish had alchemy formations that were very strong, they only had singular functions.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish had very limited space, and there was no way to lay out even more alchemy formations.

The Anti-investigation and Prophecy Formation the survivors built took up an entire cabin. To say nothing of the packed alchemy cabin, the other holds wouldn’t do either. The boarding cabin had the most space, and they were barely able to meet the space required for the alchemy formation.

“We lack a steady income, and currently our finances are always unhealthy.”

“Even if I get in contact with my merchant alliance, it will still be hard to solve this predicament.”

Zi Di was the president of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance.

But the Wisteria Merchant Alliance had already broken up and did not have much sway in the seas.

In addition, before they successfully redeemed themselves to the empire, she could not overly associate with the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, lest she trigger suspicion.

Zi Di put forth a proposal, “Perhaps, we can place an alchemy factory on Snake Rat Island.”

“I have looked through all of the blueprints pertaining to one.”

“A part of the alchemy factory can produce automations.”

Snake Rat Island has vast underground spaces, and with our great achievements, we have mapped out far more parts of the underground than Tan Qiu or the Hunting Guild.”

“We can pick an area in some remote corner, secretly construct a small alchemy factory and have it operate autonomously. We will dispatch a few alchemy puppets to garrison it and obtain raw materials from hunting magic beasts in the underground. These raw materials will be sent to the alchemy factory and be processed into products.”

“Every so often, we will come to reap the harvest here.”

“If we sell them without issue, it will greatly alleviate our financial predicament.”

Zi Di’s suggestion aroused everyone’s interest.

But after Cang Xu carefully thought about it, he shook his head, “How will we get the resources to build an alchemy factory? It is impossible currently! Such a thing needs far more than setting up battle squads.”

“It is also not safe to put it on Snake Rat Island. The ample magic beasts here are bound to be exploited much more in the future.”

Zi Di nodded, “That is indeed a hidden danger.”

The dragonman youngster followed up, “But it is still a good idea. There are many small uninhabited islands in the ocean, we can pick those that meet our standards and covertly build some alchemy factories on them.”

“As for right now, I must go and stabilize morale.”

When the young man said that he forced a smile.

Zong Ge’ independent attitude had caused the recently recruited people to become restless.

The young captain disseminated the following to everyone: the reason why personnel was being moved was mainly to prepare for the future development of branch groups.

After Zong Ge heard that, he also affirmed the sentiment, turning the worry the sailors felt into happiness.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had already drawn up a long term plan; it seemed that this organization had a bright future.

After hearing this, the most excited was Tan Qiu.

“Eh?”

“Such a thing really happened!”

“Hehe, my prior attraction is taking effect.”

“Of course it should be like this.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ leader is not only a dragonman hybrid, but he also has a lower cultivation than his subordinate, how can he sway the others?”

“Hehe, come everyone, I want to invite Shi Qi to dinner.”

“Keep it secret!”

“This is a private invitation, I don’t want others knowing about it.”

Tan Qiu exuberantly rubbed his hands as he left.

Previously, when the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wantonly recruited many of Snake Rat Island’s future talents, Tan Qiu felt sick.

This gave him more motivation to recruit Zong Ge.

“If I can swipe their second in command, what does it matter if they leave with those talents?”

“Hehehe.”

Upon thinking of the advantages, Tan Qiu’s face couldn’t help but smile.

Chapter 268: Setting Up Battle Squads

“Hm! Ho! Ha!”

Under the sunlight, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ sailors dripped sweat.

They were doing stamina training, and distance running through the town while carrying a heavy load. They practiced with weapons, using them to beat wooden humanoid targets into unsightly things. They practiced sailor jobs, like climbing masts, quickly hanging the sails, throwing hooks, cleaning the deck, and other tasks.

Zong Ge used army drills to train them.

This was rarely seen.

It was already beyond their scope, yet Zong Ge was not satisfied with them. His goal was very ambitious, and neither the ships nor the mercenary corps he had now could meet it.

He had an entire set of mature training regimens.

Every stage, he had a clear training goal.

“This is only the first stage, the main goal of this is that they comply with orders and foster a mutual understanding with their fellows.”

“They are still of inadequate quality.”

Soldiers not only needed physical strength, talent, battle abilities, but also the right temperament.

Many senior sailors were wily old foxes, been on the battlefield, been army deserters, and been cheating cowards, it was easy for them to comply with orders given by their superior.

Zong Ge hoped his troops would be able to reach the stage where they would execute every order without fail.

Unfortunately, he knew it would be difficult for these people to attain such a training target.

Only the dogmen could possibly meet that expectation.

Although the oxmen were genuine, they lacked the desire.

Therefore, he requested that the dogmen be transferred to him so that they may serve as his private guard.

Zong Ge was injured, and could only cultivate battle qi for a short time. But that had its own advantages, now he could spend more time and energy drilling his troops.

As the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ mercenaries endured the arduous training, they gradually showed a fierce aura.

Their group training also grew more momentous, and attracted the gaze of innumerable eyes.

“If I had a barracks, my training would be even more efficient!” Zong Ge could only lament to himself and pick out the qualified ones.

Unless he relied on Tan Qiu for help.

Since he had decided to assert his independence, Tan Qiu constantly dispatched people with secret dinner invitations.

But why would Zong Ge shackle himself to Snake Rat Island?

If it were Zi Di, perhaps she would play along and deliberately be ambiguous to obtain benefits, but Zong Ge had a firm character and never thought of doing that.

Despite repeated refusals, Tan Qiu did not give up and still tried to talk to Zong Ge and persuade him.

As the sun set in the west, the ocean horizon lit the clouds a burning color.

“Today’s training ends here.” Zong Ge declared what the mercenaries most wanted to hear.

“Ah!” Finally finished.”

“I endured today too.”

“Too difficult, truly too difficult. I only wanted to go out to sea, but I ended up suffering through training here.”

Many of the mercenaries collapsed onto the ground, some grimaced in pain and rubbed their bloody and swollen palms, others rubbed their hungry bellies with great appetite.

The meals the survivors gave the mercenaries were always sumptuous.

Heavy training without rich and nourishing food would instead injure these mercenaries.

Thanks to the three party deal, the survivors were able to provide these training supplies.

On Mystifying Monster Island, when the dragonman youngster was training archers, he had barely any resources and staked everything in one throw, but now, the situation was far better.

The mercenaries boarded the six ships and lined up to eat their meals.

This was also part of the training.

It ingrained the habit of dining on the ships into them. The meal venue was also specifically arranged.

Zong Ge had his own captain room.

The dinner in his room was naturally finer than the mercenaries’, especially the expensive magic beast meat in it.

Zong Ge quickly ate his meal, then began to study his drilling manual.

The manual listed the training events that would occur every day for the following week. It comprised weapon duels, conducting shooting drills in the deep underground, setting sail to undertake shelling training, and more.

Zong Ge was a notetaker and every day he recorded the results of training.

Afterwards, he immersed himself in the contents of the training manual.

He did his best to conceal the key parts of the training so outsiders watching wouldn’t grow suspicious.

If he began to train the dogmen in knives and shooting or give the oxmen weights that simulated heavy combat armor, it would have made things rather obvious.

Tan Qiu could organize battle squads. It was likely that when he came by, he would see that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was establishing their own battle squads.

Not just one, but three or four.

It would have been too astonishing!

Even if the backer of arms smugglers had such power, this backer would not have given this transportation team these things.

“If I had a barracks that I could manage and isolate from the world, I wouldn’t have such misgivings, and could proceed to conducting the pertinent drills for battle squads.”

“Furthermore, a relatively sealed environment would foster even more of a tacit understanding as well as compliance towards the officer.”

“At the present stage, a barracks is unnecessary. Let them use weapons familiar to them, it will help them increase their battle power. But at this stage, the true training projects need to be mixed with other events, decreasing their efficiency greatly.”

Zong Ge’s mind tossed and turned as his hand gradually flipped to the final page of the manual.

This page had a detailed list of the mercenary corps’ members, what battle qi they cultivated, how they usually fight, as well as how much fighting experience they had and other related information.

Zong Ge had an overall understating of every member of the mercenary corps.

Of course, there were bound to be some that had undisclosed strength or abilities.

It was unnecessary to have such a deep examination of them all, this level was sufficient.

With this information, Zong Ge understood his mercenaries.

“Its nearly time. Tonight, I will give them battle qi techniques.” After calculating and thinking about it, Zong Ge issued his decision.

The meal cabin onboard was packed with people.

In a corner, Bai Ya was squatting on the ground and wolfing down the meal in his hand.

The heavy training made him very hungry.

The others were just like him, gulping and devouring.

One of them choked, causing them to cough a bunch.

Some of the mercenaries who finished eating began to grumble.

“Training, nothing but training all day! How long are we going to stay here?”

“I want to go out to sea; I want to go out to sea!”

“If this continues, staying in this mercenary group is meaningless, I might as well…”

As a matter of fact, it wasn’t that none of them left, only a few of them did. Some of them couldn’t stand the daily tedious training and left.

But the great majority of them chose to remain.

The people who had been recruited all had passed Tripleblade’s strict filter.

The majority of them weren’t stupid and knew the harsh training of this mercenary corps had benefits. The stronger the mercenary corps, the better their future lives will be.

Those that left did not have the determination, resolve, were blindly stubborn, irritated, and more.

Their departure also benefited the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, as it sharpened its essence.

“I’ve been hearing more and more grumbling.” Bai Ya sighed to himself.

After the previous sailors were traded out, these newcomers had begun to call Zong Ge things like “strict”. “terrifying”, “abnormal”, “devilish instructor”, and other such things.

They scarcely realized how far they were from meeting Zong Ge’s standards.

“Ah, there you are, boy.” At that moment, Xu Ma walked over.

These days, he and Bai Ya walked together.

When Bai Ya saw him, he immediately got up to say hello, then shortly after, was fascinated by Xu Ma’s meal.

Xu Ma’s food was different from most others. It was small spiders stewed in mushrooms, one big spider as big as a hen that was roasted black and had a wafting aroma. The spider head had six bright red eyes that looked like cute strawberries.

It was magic beast meat.

A bronze level one.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ benefits included supplying their cultivators with magic beast meat at 50% of market price.

This policy was mainly to win over the people’s hearts.

Compared to ordinary people, cultivators were more important.

Currently, if the survivors included themselves, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had 12 bronze levels, this was their foundation.

Above them were the iron levels as their core, and the silver levels as their higher ups.

Despite Zong Ge’s bitter training these days, the 12 bronze levels did not leave. Benefits such as this were a big factor.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was in its infancy, any bronze level that left was a loss.

Think about Tan Qiu, he held Snake Rat Island and had a great foundation, yet when he saw the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps successfully recruit his people, he too felt faintly sore.

The world belonged to the strong.

Sensing Bai Ya’s gaze, Xu Ma smiled.

If it weren’t for the purpose of showing off, he wouldn’t be swaggering everywhere.

Bai Ya’s admiring and curious gaze satisfied his spirit immensely.

He said, “I cultivate the Spider Walk battle qi technique, this magic beast meat is good for me. Other types will barely do anything for me.”

Bai Ya did not understand, “Can you explain why you are eating that magic beast meat?”

“Of course. Previously, the magic beast meat I was eating had two types of magic power, and I was only able to absorb one of them, thus wasting part of it. Now, this type of magic beast meat only has the dark element, and I am able to absorb it all.”

“Yesterday, I heard that the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance took in some goods. Our mercenary corps should have purchased this magic beast meat from them.”

The small spiders were not produced on Snake Rat Island.

Currently, the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance was the only large merchant alliance on Snake Rat Island. Snake Rat Island’s people had a large dependence on the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance.

Bai Ya grew more inquisitive, “Magic beast meat can really increase a fighter’s cultivation speed? How much effect can one meal have?”

“Of course it helps.” Said Xu Ma, “Whether or not we fighters can eat magic beast meat makes a big difference. Undoubtedly, the most important thing is long term consumption, eating it once or twice doesn’t do much.”

“As for you, it is too early. You just started, and an ordinary person like you eating magic beast meat would be disastrous.”

Xu Ma then changed the subject sharply, “Of course, if your aptitude is outstanding, you can still eat it. A little bit will be enough.”

Compared to Bai Ya, Xu Ma was much more experienced at cultivating.

Bai Yai’s curiosity was piqued even more, “I can also eat some? What kind can I eat?”

Xu Ma replied, “No poison can be without its dosage. Magic beast meat is also like that. If you absorb and convert the bit you eat, why not?”

“Many nobles will hire high level magic chefs, these magic chefs are experts at rigorously controlling the dosage of magic power in meals. Many noble children start off as ordinary people, yet they can enjoy those culinary delicacies.”

When Xu Ma said that he curiously mused, “Our mercenary corps does not have a magic chef. Then look at your talent. If you have great aptitude, you can digest a small bite.”

Bai Ya thought about it, “I feel that I can.”

The young hunter couldn’t conceal his mood, and a prick of pride peeped through his forehead.

This made Xu Ma even more curious, and he couldn’t help but think, “Don’t tell me you are a small genius?”

“Wrong.” Xu Ma shook his head, “If your aptitude was extraordinary, why aren’t you a cultivator yet?”

Bai Ya answered as expected, “Because I just started cultivating battle qi.”

Xu Ma sighed, and was just about to ask something when Lan Zao came in and announced something.

It caused a commotion amongst everyone.

“We can cultivate any of these battle qi techniques as we please?” Many mercenaries found it hard to believe.

To evaluate the newcomers’ aptitude and bloodline, the survivors began to provide many battle qi techniques. Of course, some of the battle qi techniques the battle squads used were hidden among them.

Lan Zao nodded, “The first stage of testing is finished, those that remain will naturally enjoy our mercenary corps’ benefits in kind.”

Many mercenaries’ ears twitched when they heard the words “first stage” and “testing”. They immediately recalled the small number of people who had left, and each of them felt infinite joy welling from their hearts.

Lan Zao raised his voice, “Everyone, what do you think the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is? It is no ordinary mercenary group, and henceforth you will feel that too.”

There was no need for the future, they could feel it now.

To be given so many battle qi techniques was rarely seen.

In general, mercenary groups recruited talents based on the foundation of their strength, and attracted those that matched their mold.

Providing these battle qi techniques was the fostering policy of a large power.

Only a great power or large organization could have collected this many battle qi techniques.

Many ordinary people were excited.

They rarely touched this many battle qi techniques, and even if they could choose some, their selections were very slim.

But now, with so many battle qi techniques, they could try one after the other to test which suited them the most.

However, Xu Ma and the other cultivators were not excited by this.

To become cultivators, they had to cultivate battle qi for quite some time.

They had ample cultivation experience, and knew how inconvenient changing their battle qi was.

How much of them truly suited them?

Changing battle qi was too costly.

The profit of changing it was also usually unclear.

But rather quickly, the bronze level fighters also began to clamor.

Because Lan Zao announced the benefits in more detail.

“What, cultivators that switch their battle qi technique can get the corresponding magic beast meat free of charge?”

“For free?”

“Can I enjoy this benefit until I leave the mercenary corps?”

Xu Ma’s and the other bronze level fighters’ eyes popped.

Before, the magic beast meat they ate was purchased at 50% of market value. In other words, they paid half, and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps provided for the other half.

But now, as long as they switch their battle qi, they can eat free of charge?

Even if it was one or two meals, the crucial point was that they could eat like this until they left the mercenary group!

“That is a lot of money!

“We earn what we eat.”

“The longer I stay in this mercenary corps, the more money I save!”

The bronze fighters’ were interested.

Lan Zao fished out a contract and continued, “The specific details are on this contract, you all can examine it thoroughly.”

“Give it to me.”

“I want to take a good look at it.”

“So do I!”

Xu Ma and the other bronze fighters became very eager.

Chapter 269: Cultivation

Although Bai Ya had already chosen one, to maintain his disguise, he still chose a few other battle qi techniques.

After dinner finished, it was cultivation time.

Basic battle qi cultivation was through exercising one’s body.

Bai Ya ran above deck, grabbed a wooden cutlass and a circular shield, then began to practice chopping motions, blocking motions, and other movements.

This was different from the simple exercise during the day, Bai Ya had to focus his mind, with his attention mainly focused on following the circulation of the battle qi technique.

“There it is, I produced it again!”

Very quickly, he sensed that his body had quietly produced a thread of faint battle qi.

It looked like a wisp of smoke in a spacious ship hold.

The battle qi slowly being produced once again dissipated quickly.

“So tired!”

After practicing for a short while, Bai Yai could no longer go on, he was utterly exhausted and dripped with sweat.

He reached the limits of his body.

As the ship’s carpenter, Mu Ban had his own work cabin onboard.

The other sailors were crammed into the bottom hold, and slept in hammocks, but Mu Ban could sleep in his work room.

The trace of battle qi his body produced quickly faded away.

His heart exasperatedly sighed, but to fool others, Mu Ban still showed “high spirits”.

Lan Zao swung his arms in a sailor room.

Every time he swung his arms, the new battle qi looked like a thick fog around his arms.

He cultivated the Sea Grass battle qi technique, it was ordinary, but it suited him well.

Lan Zao too could only cultivate a short while before stopping.

He was still a distance from the limit he could temper his body.

He could still continue to cultivate a bit more.

But it was unnecessary.

Lan Zao ate some magic beast meat, completely absorbed all of the magic power in it, replenishing the battle qi he had used during the day.

Dong, dong, dong.

Lan Zao had just stopped cultivating when suddenly, there was a knock at his door.

“Who is it?”

“Lord, it is I, Xu Ma.”

Lan Zao’s brows raised, and the image of a thin and tall man came to mind.

Xu Ma had a distinctive slim look. More crucially, he took the initiative to protect Bai Ya, thus gaining more of the survivors’ attention.

Lan Zao opened the door, and saw Xu Ma bowing and nodding his head, a bottle of wine in the latter’s hand.

“Lord Lan Zao, a small token of my appreciation.” Xu Ma flattered with a smile.

“Do not address me as lord, you and I are the same, just bronze levels.” Lan Zao did not take it, his tone placid.

“We are not the same!” Xu Ma immediately shouted, “My Lord, you are an old guard of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, one of the commander’s trusted men, and the sailor’s senior. The reason I came was to consult your Lordship about changing my battle qi technique.”

Lan Zao glanced at him, then opened the door fully and let him inside, “Come in then, let’s talk about it.”

Under the darkness of night, Tripleblade was patrolling the ship.

His cultivation had already finished earlier.

Tripleblade had an iron level bloodline.

Every time he cultivated battle qi, the battle qi that was produced looked like thick fog that congealed into water, its efficiency far outstripped the bronze level Lan Zao and took less time.

Bloodlines decided one’s limits.

Tripleblade knew he had reached his limits, and that he would be at the iron level for the rest of his life.

He spent much more energy helping Zong Ge.

Tripleblade backed Zong Ge’s decision to go independent and have a ship to himself.

“This is his Lordship’s foundation, and in the future, it will certainly expand!”

Tripleblade had great confidence in Zong Ge’s future.

He entered the ship hold and examined the cultivating dogmen.

The majority of the dogmen had already changed their battle qi technique.

“Lord Zong Ge values these dogmen.”

“I hope that some among them can cultivate the Quick Knife battle qi technique, the more of them the better.”

Zong Ge really valued how changing battle qi techniques would result in.

Anyways, as he waved his broadsword, such diverse thoughts were tossed to the back of his mind.

Whish, whish, whish.

Every sword swing created a rush of wind.

Accompanying every sword swing, the newly produced battle qi was like a vast ocean that flooded every corner of his body.

Afterwards, the battle qi was constantly condensed and compressed.

In the clear silver battle qi, there was a faint smudge of gold luster!

“Not far from the gold level.” Zong Ge vaguely sensed, “After I enter the gold level, my situation will be vastly improved.”

“I may even be capable of wresting back the position of leader.”

“But that is unnecessary.”

“These people are very good.”

Zong Ge loathed Cang Xu, knew Zi Di and the dragonman youngster had a firm relationship, and looked down on Lan Zao, Bai Ya, and the others.

Battle qi cultivation could raise one’s life level.

This was a slow process of accumulation.

Zong Ge had great confidence he could break into the gold level, and never thought he would fail to do so.

Because he had an unsurpassed bloodline.

“My future will not just be the gold level, it will only be a part of my life.”

“Like Mystifying Monster Island and my association with these people, these are just things I experience in my life.”

“Right now, my main trouble is still this injury…”

The half beastman gazed at where battle qi was condensing around his broken arm, once again, a scarlet ring formed around it.

After several days, the results of the mercenaries’ battle qi technique cultivation attempts were in.

There were 3 people that suited the Bullet Hail battle qi technique, 5 people that suited the Arrow Storm battle qi technique, 2 people that suited the Healing Light battle qi technique, and 11 people that suited the Quick Knife battle qi technique.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had several hundred people, but very few of them were suited for their battle squads.

The greatest perquisite for setting up battle squads was the battle qi techniques.

Only by cultivating a battle qi technique and battle skills together could quantity trigger a qualitative change.

The results were terrible.

With only two or three suitable candidates, wanting to set up battle squads was unspeakable.

But the survivors were unsurprised.

They had anticipated this outcome earlier.

Any power that established battle squads had met similar circumstances.

Therefore, battle squads needed time to be built.

Only the number of people suited to cultivate the Quick Knife battle qi technique reached the double digits.

Zong Ge issued an explanation, “The Quick Knife battle qi technique has its origins in beastmen communities. In history, all renowned Quick Knife battle squads mainly relied on dogmen.”

Its no wonder the half beastmen decided to demand these beastmen.

11 people could form the initial form of the battle squad, and could train some of the daily training fundamentals.

The battle squad’s main training was combined assaults as a team.

Lacking this, even if they cultivated the same battle qi techniques, they would be like a sheet of loose sand and a disorderly band.

The lofty sun hung in the cloudless sky.

It was bombardment training time.

Zong Ge led three ships through the waves that chopped through the azure ocean.

“Prepare, fire!” With oral command and flag signals, the order for bombardment was quite practiced.

Boom boom boom…...

With an orderly series of booms, ten artillery shells flew through the air and formed a barrage.

The artillery shells hit the seawater in succession, causing ten jets of water to rise up.

As the jets of water faded, the target buoys fiercely swayed in the waves.

None of them hit the target, causing the sailors who had fired to wring their hands and sigh.

According to the rules Zong Ge laid down, those that hit the target would receive a reward – usually a monetary bonus.

If the shot artillery shell did not hit in the bombardment target zone, there was also a corresponding punishment.

“Not bad.” Upon seeing the results of the bombardment, Tripleblade smiled, “We’ve made big progress!”

Hitting the buoys was considerably difficult, especially for ordinary cannons.

Compared to when Rou Zang had attacked them, the survivors’ bombardment had made visible improvement.

Previously, it was horrendous, the shelling was disjointed and completely inaccurate.

But now, it could hit a bombardment area, it undoubtably met standards.

Zong Ge was calm, “On the one hand, we have been constantly training. On the other hand, we have also increased the quality of our troops by a lot.”

After all, Tripleblade had stringently filtered the recruits, and now there were many experienced people within the sailors’ ranks.

Unlike before when they were mostly composed of fisherfolk.

Zong Ge faintly sighed, then said, “We still need a real gunner!”

He thought of the gold level ship cannon.

It was one of the survivors’ advantages, but without a gunner, it could not display its true might.

Previously, it was mainly able to inflict serious damage on Rou Zang because it had been a surprise and caught the other party off guard.

Now that the gold level cannon had been exposed, it was impossible to recreate the prior battle result, and would even be responded against.

Not far from where Zong Ge was conducting bombardment training, on some nameless isle, the dragonman youngster was training too.

He was standing in sea water that reached up to his shoulder, only his dragon head was above water.

The young man was boxing in the water.

Thump, thump, thump!

Every time a fish rushed forth, it would cause a small explosion.

He was refining Bursting Explosion battle qi.

The Flame Dragon King bloodline gave him a legend level aptitude, giving him astonishing battle qi refinement efficiency.

Very quickly, he had abundant battle qi within him.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist!

Boom.

With a loud sound, the seawater in front of him immediately exploded, creating a half sphere gap in it 2 meters in diameter.

A moment later, the surrounding sea water rushed in to fill it in, forming a chaotic shockwave.

The shockwave hit the dragonman youngster’s red dragon scales, but the young man remained sturdy and unmoved.

He continued to use this battle skill, creating gaps in the water from small to large, then from large to small.

He was controlling the might of Demolition Fist!

This showed that his mastery over this battle skill had gone from basic to proficient.

Through practicing this battle skill in the water and seeing the gaps in the seawater, one could more objectively evaluate the form of this fist’s power.

This was also a small trick the young man thought of for his training.

After swinging his fits ten times, the dragonman youngster’s internal Bursting Explosion battle qi bottomed out.

“Although this battle skill is very powerful, it consumes a lot of Bursting Explosion battle qi.”

“Furthermore, controlling the might of the explosion isn’t easy either.”

“This is related to the characteristics of the Bursting Explosion battle qi. If it was the White River battle qi, it would have been easier to refine, while the Bursting Explosion battle qi is much harder.”

The experience of practice was constantly accumulating in the young man’s heart.

After taking a few breaths, the dragonman youngster stopped using Demolition Fish and began to follow his battle qi technique to refine more battle qi.

He was a half dragonman, and his body far outstripped humans of the same life level, thus he could cultivate longer. And because of his bloodline giving him astronomical efficiency, he quickly had abundant battle qi once again.

Chapter 270: As It Turns Out, I am Only an Ordinary Person

After practicing the Demolition Fist, the dragonman youngster began to cultivate the battle skill - Surge.

This battle skill was quite compatible with the Bursting Explosion battle qi, and could help him increase his speed greatly.

Like Demolition Fist, when the dragonman youngster also practiced Surge, he began to control its power.

Battle qi consumption rate, directional tuning, and what kind of drive is formed, all of these needed lots of practice for one to engrain it into their body’s muscle memory.

Only when he had muscle memory could he use Surge instinctually in actual combat.

The dragonman youngster submerged himself into the seawater and floated in it to practice Surge.

As he floated in the current, firecrackers crackled from all sorts of places on his body from time to time, producing force that pushed him in any direction.

The persistent practice made the young man gradually feel hungry.

He swam out and trudged onto the beach, took out some magic beast meat stashed in the rocks, and devoured it.

The beach also had someone else on it.

It was a giant and human hybrid – the big guy.

The big guy was huffing, he had been practicing the basic fighting steps.

He occasionally moved horizontally, jumped about, kneeled, and rolled, strictly following the training the dragonman youngster had drilled into him through repeated lectures.

The dragonman youngster took good care of the big guy, and had carefully selected his battle qi technique.

But the big guy was dumb, and never succeeded in refinement, thus he was still like this.

On the ship, the big guy pretended to be vicious, however, the survivors knew what he was really like.

Therefore, while the mercenaries were doing bombardment training, he and the big guy came to this island so that he could cultivate battle skills, and the big guy could train.

Since he couldn’t breakthrough with battle qi techniques for the time being, the young captain decided to teach the big guy wrestling techniques.

The most basic of these skills was steps.

The big guy was very obedient, and after the dragonman youngster told him how to train repeatedly, he had been doing so without the thought of resting crossing his mind.

But upon seeing the big guy training hard, the dragonman youngster nevertheless shook his head.

Due to the big guy’s subpar intellect, he had already forgotten the stepping technique the young man had painstakingly taught him, and now was moving clumsily, covered in flaws.

“Oh dear, practicing like this is out of the question.”

The young man recalled how the big guy performed on Mystifying Monster Island.

In the barracks defense battle, the big guy had fought with a wooden club. As a result, when he met face to face with a Strong Life White Rhinoceros, he was pierced in the belly by its horn, the heavy injury taking him out of the fight.

Due to the magic prohibition environment, the big guy’s superior body actually made him perform better there.

However earlier, he was easily overpowered by Zong Ge. Later on, he became a laborer for the ship building effort and could only devote his body to that.

The big guy’s vicious tendencies were not what he was inherently, rather it was for the sake of the old boatwright’s safety and was provoked when something threatened the latter.

He was naturally cowardly, fearful, and had low self-esteem.

In his gigantic body, there was a weak and pitiful soul.

The dragonman youngster sighed to himself, pity welling from his heart.

“Stop for now, let me teach you again.” Said the dragonman youngster.

“Father.” The big guy immediately lowered his head, and prudently walked over to face the young man, he also realized he was doing it all wrong.

“It doesn’t matter, we’ll do it together again. Watch carefully.” The dragonman youngster smiled patiently.

The big guy’s eyes opened wide, and he did his best to watch and follow.

The dragonman youngster taught him how to move his hands, and the big guy copied it decently.

The dragonman youngster treated this as a break.

After a short time, he allowed the big guy to practice alone and dove back into the sea.

He continued to practice Demolition Fish and Surge, and when he ran out of battle qi, he followed his battle qi technique to refine more battle qi.

By the time he returned to the beach, he once again saw the big guy’s movement had been completely deformed.

“Stop, practicing like this won’t result in anything, on the contrary, this kind of practice will injure yourself.” The young man stopped the big guy.

The big guy trembled all over, huddled his body, carefully waddled back to the young man, and softly whispered, “Father, don’t be angry at this small, foolish child.”

When he saw the big guy dripping with sweat and holding his breath, despite wanting to plainly pant, the young man’s heart softened, “I was worried that you would harm yourself. Your practice movements need to meet standards.”

“But it’s fine, it’s fine.”

“You will get better and better.”

The dragonman youngster patted the big guy’s knee and warmly consoled him.

The big guy’s mood gradually steadied, and he gazed down at his new father, the corners of his mouth gradually rising to expose an innocent smile.

He noticed that no matter how terrible he did, the dragonman youngster would always show concern and not harshly criticize him and was very patient in instructing him.

“Father, likes, Xiao Gui.” When the big guy realized this, happiness flooded both his body and mind.

As Zong Ge was leading the three ships back, Mu Ban was hammering away.

The survivors had six ships, and while three were out training in naval warfare, the remaining three were being retrofitted and remodeled.

Clang, clang, clang.

Mu Ban struck iron nails firmly into a plank, then he tugged at it with all his strength.

The plank did not move a single jot.

“Your craftsmanship is quite good.” A boat repair master on the dock praised from the bottom of their heart.

“Are you interested in staying and working here?” A foreman asked, trying to recruit Mu Ban.

Mu Ban smiled and shook his head.

He wasn’t a genuine expert at this kind of carpentry work.

Of course, he still liked it.

“Following with the ships is very risky, pirates are too rampant nowadays, they kill, loot, and commit any crime imaginable.” The foreman did not want to give up.

But Mu Ban had already withdrawn himself into his work and continued to hammer away.

The clamor stilled his mind.

“Ah, they’ve returned.” Fei She saw the group of training mercenaries through a porthole, and immediately became anxious.

He was responsible for the food, but this work was quite heavy.

“I have to pay close attention.” Fei She sliced an onion, then tossed it into a pot.

The iron cooking pot was half as tall as a person, and inside it had potatoes, legumes, tomatoes, and other ingredients.

After adding in the onions, Fei She covered the pot and increased the heat.

A rich aroma began to waft into the air.

Fei She coughed, and began to observe the yellow crab alchemy puppet.

The latter was cooking magic beast meat in the kitchen.

Fei She was only an ordinary person, he could not do such a thing.

Using the yellow crab alchemy puppet to cook was like using a sledgehammer to crack a nut, but they had no choice, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps lacked a magic chef. Zi Di could actually do it, however, she was busy elsewhere and could not spend valuable time and energy doing that.

The yellow crab alchemy puppet was their temporary magic chef, and they had installed kitchen utensils onto its crab claws.

Magic beast meat went into a cooking pot, with one crab claw providing heat and the other stirring the spoon.

Still, let it be said that it was quite decent at it.

After watching it for a spell, Fei She lost interest.

He looked at his own firewood, then carefully took out a list and studied it.

The list was densely packed with the price of goods.

By studying regional prices, one could analyze therefrom the circumstances of the market, as well as how to make money. This was the type of work that Fei She felt the greatest sense of achievement in.

Very quickly, he immersed himself in it and forgot about everything nearby.

Fei She was only snapped out of it when hot water splattered from the pot, scalding him.

After dinner, it was time for cultivation again.

Bai Ya was tired and sweaty.

“I can sense that I am improving every day.”

“It feels truly wonderful!”

Bai Ya clenched his fists, and despite his exhaustion, he was still excited.

He felt that every day had a purpose.

Xu Ma had been somewhat skeptical the first time he saw him cultivate.

“Hold on a second, Hei Ya (Bai Ya’s alias), my junior brother, why do you spend so long cultivating battle qi every day?” Xu Ma asked.

Normally, the greater one’s aptitude, the longer they would cultivate.

Bai Ya hadn’t cultivated for long, but he told Xu Ma previously that his talent was pretty good!

This made Xu Ma confused.

Bai Ya shook his head.

Xu Ma interjected, “It seems you are quite beaten right now.”

Bai Ya shook his head again, “Of course not, I’m doing quite well right now. After all, bombardment training uses up less stamina compared to weapons practice.”

“Today, I will cultivate a bit longer.”

Xu Ma: ?!

He blankly stared back, “What is your aptitude actually like?”

Bai Ya was full of confidence, “I feel it’s pretty good. Many battle qi techniques suit me, I just picked one amongst them.”

“That’s…all?”

Bai Ya grunted.

Xu Ma covered his eyes with his hand, sighed, then put his hand down, “Are you misunderstanding what determines cultivation aptitude?”

“Listen to me well, youngster.”

“Cultivation aptitude is not determined by how many battle qi techniques suit you.”

“Many powerful bloodlines can only one or two battle qi techniques.”

“Battle qi aptitude is determined through how efficient one can refine battle qi.”

“For example, when a bronze level bloodline cultivates, their battle qi looks like a murky mist. An iron level one looks like raindrops from congealed fog, a silver level looks like a gurgling stream, and a gold level one looks like a torrential waterfall.”

Bai Ya was struck dumb by those words.

Xu Ma then asked, “What are you?”

Bai Ya: …...

He finally gave his answer to Xu Ma.

Xu Ma sighed again, “Smelly youngster, stop training, there is no future for you there.”

“Don’t worry, I will still protect you.”

“Just sincerely follow me.”

“What do you mean by no future?” Bai Ya nervously stooped up, “Are you telling me I can never become a cultivator?”

“Basically.” Xu Ma said as he threw up his hands

Bai Ya turned pale, he was unwilling to abandon his last bit of hope. “Basically? Is there still a way?”

“Sure, maybe your bloodline will be awakened one day.” Said Xu Ma.

“What is a bloodline awakening?”

Xu Ma explained, “Sometimes, in the beginning, a bloodline hidden within a body does not appear. As one continues to grow, the bloodline might appear. Perhaps due to some enormous event, the bloodline is stimulated and appears prominently, that is a bloodline awakening.”

“Maybe you have a bloodline that might be awakened in the future?”

Bai Ya’s face became flushed, and he anxiously asked, “When will such a possibility happen?”

Xu Ma gazed at Bai Ya, he wanted to shake his head, however, he decided to shrug his shoulders, “I don’t know, I am only a bronze level fighter.”

“So, it is.” Bai Ya was at a loss.

Xu Ma extended his hand and took the other’s hand, “Don’t think about it, bloodline awakening is not something you can decide. Since your cultivation today is finished, why don’t you go with me on a trip.”

“To where?”

Xu Ma put on a mysterious tone, “We shall visit a crucial person!”

A short time later, Lan Zao opened his door to see Xu Ma and Bai Ya.

Xu Ma stooped down and cajoled with a smile, “Ah, Lord Lan Zao, a token of my consideration to thank you for your help last time.”

After saying that, he pulled out some rum.

Lan Zao looked at both of them, he showed no trace that he recognized Bai Ya. He didn’t take the wine. “I didn’t help you with anything.”

“Aiya.” Xu Ma stuffed the bottle into Lan Zao’s hand, “My Lord, I just wanted to get closer to you!”

Lan Zao coldly glared, “You and I are both bronze fighters, as long as you work diligently, the mercenary corps will not treat you unfairly.”

Lan Zao saw that Xu Ma intended to use him to climb the ranks, and he despised such people from the bottom of his heart.

Xu Ma smiled and flattered him even more, his fawning remarks seeming to slip out of him.

Lan Zao’s expression grew even colder.

When Xu Ma gradually ran out of ideas, he suddenly pulled Bai Ya towards Lan Zao, “Look, I brought my little brother with me. Ever since he heard about you, he has admired you in particular. He is the reason why we came to pay you a visit.”

Bai Ya: …...

Lan Zao: …...

Xu Ma nudged Bai Ya’s back, “Hei Ya, didn’t you have some difficult trouble?”

Lan Zao keenly noticed that Bai Ya looked somewhat amiss.

Bai Ya struggled to open his mouth, “Lord Boatswain, my aptitude isn’t good. When I heard Senior Xu Ma explain it, uh…do you know about bloodline awakening?”

Bai Ya’s gaze drooped down.

Bai Ya misunderstood his own aptitude, but the other survivors understood it clearly. It wasn’t that they wanted to play a joke on this young man, rather, after going through trials and tribulations together, the survivors didn’t want to tell him the truth and were afraid of dealing a shock to the youngster.

After all, Bai Ya wanted to become a knight and marry his sweetheart. Everyone knew of his affair with the noble lady.

Based on Bai Ya’s aptitude, this was impossible.

Knights were not calvary, they had to be cultivators!

If the truth was spoken, Bai Ya was bound to die of heartbreak.

But even if it wasn’t said, Bai Ya would notice it sooner or later.

And now, he knew about it.

Bai Ya had saved Lan Zao’s life; thus Lan Zao pulled the door open, “Fine then, come in.”

Bai Ya walked in.

Xu Ma giggled and wanted to follow.

Ding.

A moment later, Lan Zao closed the cabin door and nearly hit his nose.

Chapter 271: New Formation Construction Plan

Beloved Miss Xi Qiu:

I am Bai Ya.

Since I escaped Mystifying Monster Island, I have written 17 letters to you.

I am very sorry, like the others, I have no way of sending this to you either.

Every time I write you a letter, I can feel my spirit calm.

Thank you!

During this time, as one of Priest Jia Sha’s murderers, I have always felt apprehensive. Sometimes, I even will have nightmares at night, where I dream about Priest Jai Sha demanding my life, or because of my crimes, I have sunken into hell to be subjected to torment.

You have always helped me.

Today, the pitiful and lamentable me has no choice but to seek your assistance.

I heard sad news today.

Heavens!

I don’t even know how I returned here.

My aptitude was not as good as I envisioned, in fact, it is very…terrible!

Every time I cultivate, my battle qi refinement efficiency is always very low, it doesn’t even look like mist, and can only be considered strands of smoke.

This is the aptitude of an ordinary person.

Many people can never become cultivators, because they are like this. Their battle qi production efficiency is too low, and unable to be stored, thus they cannot reach a qualitative change.

Mu Ban, Fei She, and others are like that.

So…am I.

My hope is nearly gone.

I feel my world has gone dark

But Senior Xu Ma gave me some light.

There is still the bloodline awakening.

I don’t know how much of this you will understand.

I shall tell you all that I know right now.

Thanks to the survivors keeping an eye on Bai Ya on the sly, he slept in a remote corner of the cabin.

The people around him were snoring, but through the starlight peering through the porthole, he was quietly writing on some paper.

The faint light was not bright enough to make his handwriting visible.

Bai Ya also wasn’t using a pencil, rather he was rubbing his finger on the paper.

His face was immersed in black shadows, the light glimmering off the tears in his eyes.

The Bloodline Purification Formation!

In the Deep Sea Monster Fish, Cang Xu and Zi Di were researching this incomparably miraculous alchemy formation together.

This magic formation had been developed by War Merchant.

At the time, his artificial magic beast project reached an impasse, thus he decided to no longer forcefully implant external bloodlines, instead he tested as much as possible on how much he could unearth the bloodlines accumulated within bodies.

The Bloodline Purification Formation used the Bloodcore as its center, and its objective was to raise the concentration of bloodlines.

Both Cang Xu and Zi Di had dimmed eyes, they were confused and disoriented.

This alchemy formation was too profound, and neither of the magicians’ had the minimal alchemy attainment required to decipher it.

“We need a true alchemy master.” Cang Xu sighed.

“Don’t worry, it’s difficult for outsiders to trust us.” Replied Zi Di, “We have a complete blueprint, and there is still a lot of data on how to use this alchemy formation. Just like how we built the Deceptive Disguise Formation and Anti-investigation and Prophecy formation, we can also construct this formation too!”

After Cang Xu nodded his approval, he said, “It will still be difficult.”

“We lack the resources. The precious materials we took from the island were all used to build the Deceptive Disguise Formation and Anti-investigation and Prophecy Formation. In fact, we needed to get some of the basic materials from Snake Rat Island.”

“Therefore, we have to collect these materials. To account for construction failures, we need to collect at least twice the materials.”

They had nearly failed when building the Anti-investigation and Prophecy Formation.

Fortunately, the tower spirit came to their aid, if not for it, they would have had disastrous losses.

“If the tower spirit could be restored, even if we only had one set of formation materials, it should be able to do it?” Zi Di speculated.

Cang Xu shook his head, “Restoring the tower spirit is unlike building formations.”

“In fact, even if we had the Bloodline Purification Formation, it still won’t be enough.”

“That’s right.” Replied Zi Di, “we also need to collect bloodline with the Bloodcore.”

“Before we collect bloodlines, we have to know the specifics of our objective.”

“Therefore, we have to analyze or bodies, and determine what bloodlines we have.”

A person might have many bloodlines within them.

But because of concentration, some bloodlines appeared outright, and others were hidden.

Could those like Mu Ban and Fei She have extraordinary bloodlines in them?

There was a high probability.

Their concentration was just too low, thus they could not become cultivators.

Zi Di was similar, she had a iron level bloodline, was it possible she had other extraordinary bloodlines too?

That was also possible.

Their concentrations were just low, and her iron level bloodline was dominant.

“Detecting bloodlines…” When that was mentioned, Cang Xu couldn’t help but show hesitation.

There were few ways of detecting bloodlines.

The most accurate methods were known by the Life God Church.

This church had special divine spells, like the Bloodline Level Detection Spell and the Bloodline Breed Detection Spell, among others.

By using this divine spell, one could detect the circumstances of the target’s bloodline.

It was obvious that the survivors did not enjoy such a pleasure.

Their identities could not be exposed, and they had the Deceptive Disguise spell and the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell still on them.

The most widespread ways were battle qi techniques and meditation methods.

If a lifeform cultivated a battle qi technique or meditation method and had a special result, he, she, or it could usually determine their bloodline.

Of course, this way was not accurate.

This was because there were usually not just one or two battle qi techniques or meditation methods.

Sometimes, because of opportunity, limited choices, or a variety of outside reasons, cultivators are unable to examine what cultivation method was most suitable for them.

Because of cultivation requirements, this kind of verification method was both costly and took a long time.

This was also why every organization and power realized that they should spread their battle qi techniques and meditation methods.

Many nobles also used this method to find their bastards.

Magicians of certain paths also used this to find apprentices that matched their own.

“Neither methods are suitable for us.” Said Zi Di, “I have already decided to use medicine to conduct testing.”

Cang Xu nodded, “This is indeed the best method for us. However, if we do this, we will need to refine a lot of medicine, and we lack the resources to do so.”

Zi Di helplessly laughed, “There are some remedial measures. For example, by combining ancestry data, along with the results of battle qi or meditation method cultivation results, we can filter out the possible options and mix the right testing medicine.”

“But there is still a problem.”

“My medicinal knowledge is insufficient for the task.”

“There are many bloodline detection medicines, and the workmanship of them is high, they exceed the limits of my abilities.”

Therefore, we can either find a wise pharmacist, purchase as many as we can from the market, or find another way of refinement.”

After the Deceptive Disguise Formation and the Anti-investigation and Prophecy Formation were built, the Bloodline Purification Formation was the survivors’ next project.

However, along with the precious materials from Mystifying Monster Island being used up, the Bloodline Purification Formation needed many other materials they lacked.

Furthermore, the Bloodline Purification Formation was more complex.

Simply just building this magic formation was not enough to complete their goal.

The survivors also needed many magic potions to accurately detect their bloodline types. After obtaining this information, they also needed to collect the corresponding bloodlines with the Bloodcore.

But regardless of the challenges, this project was imperative.

After the two people discussed the project with the other survivors, they quickly gained their approval.

Zi Di began to personally contact the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance to try and gather bloodline detection magic medicine.

After she had “officially” become a member of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, it had freed up Cang Xu to a degree.

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance had the greatest maritime transportation abilities in all of the Sheng Ming Empire, and despite Snake Rat Island’s remote location, every so often, the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance storefront would have their merchandise restocked.

“Delighted to trade. I look forward to that intelligence potion.” Zi Di left the hall of the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance store.

The merchant alliance’s silver magician sent her off.

Originally, she did not value the bronze level magician, Hong Teng, and was even somewhat resentful.

Previously, she had barely tolerated Cang Xu, the iron level that had been dispatched. But this time, a bronze level magician had been sent, were they looking down upon her? She was a silver level!

But after interacting with her several times, the magician adequately sensed how good Zi Di was at haggling, and how she never failed to get her way.

The magician began to understand the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ plan: it’s no wonder they had Zi Di as their delegate, to some degree, it was putting a person to good use.

Her negotiation abilities were so strong that the magician’s heart beat incessantly at it, and she had wanted to recruit Zi Di on the sly to make her the store’s manager.

Zi Di tactfully refused, and exploited her mentality to ruthlessly cut prices several times.

“My Lord, please look at this.” When no one was around, Zi Di took the dragonman youngster to the fighting cabin.

This was the room the dragonman youngster cultivated in.

The dragonman youngster immediately noticed that there was something new in the cultivate room.

“That is an iron level sandbag, it is filled with sand made from processed iron level materials. It is capable of absorbing external force and is able to rapidly disperse it through every grain.” Zi Di presented.

“It suits my battle qi cultivation, thank you.” The dragonman youngster was pleasantly surprised.

Zi Di then said, “There is also the intelligence potion business, It is beginning to take shape.

The dragonman youngster looked forward to that even more.

“With the intelligence potion, perhaps the big guy will be able to cultivate battle qi.”

“You have my thanks for figuring out this method.”

Zi Di shook her head and denied the credit, “It is only a possible solution. This is a specially made potion, it is tailored for half giants. If we hadn’t recently increased our trade with the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance, they wouldn’t have agreed to trade us this custom-made potion.”

“What is the rate of progress with the Green Jade Gold Coffin search?”

“It has always been smooth, we have examined an estimated 80% of Snake Rat Island’s underground, many fringe regions have already been searched.”

“There hasn’t been a single hint of the Green Jade Gold Coffin.”

“If it is in a gold level magic beast’s territory, it will be quite troublesome.”

“A mere inconvenience.” The dragonman youngster was confident in his statement.

He had been living a fulfilling life these days.

Every day, he was tempering his battle qi, the growth of his strength could be seen by the naked eye.

He was increasingly sensing how amazing his legend level bloodline was.

Bursting Explosion battle qi was also very agreeable with the dragonman form, they complemented each other exquisitely.

The entire mercenary corps’ strength was also constantly increasing.

Through Snake Rat Island’s markets and blacksmiths, the mercenary corps’ equipment was replenished.

Zong Ge’s training was very efficient.

Not only did he do bombardment training, but he also led mercenaries into the underground to conduct live combat training. Although he hadn’t organized long range battle squads, the marksmen team’s performance was growing more satisfactory.

11 people made up the initial form of the Quick Knife squad, and was highly anticipated.

If the Green Jade Gold Coffin was hidden in some gold level magic beast’s territory, the young man was confident they could obtain it.

His confidence not only originated in his own Dragon Lion Mercenary group, but in others too.

“If we can’t take it on, with the help of the Bloodcore transformations, I can lure the gold level magic beast up to the surface.”

“Lord Tan Qiu is a good lord!”

Chapter 272: The Two Headed Ogre Lan Lu

A Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance fleet was sailing through the seas.

The brown-red hulls, pure white sails, and azure ocean surface created a beautiful scene.

The navigator had a ruler, compass, and other tools laid out on a large ocean map, using them to accurately gauge their exact position on it.

On this ocean map, there were symbols that marked Snake Rat Island, Stone Egg Island, and other such places.

After the navigator finished their measurements, he happily said, “Lord Captain, we are only two days from Snake Rat Island.”

The captain nodded, “That is good. Speaking of which, our luck has been pretty good, we haven’t even seen the shadow of a pirate.”

“Within a day, we’ll enter Snake Rat Island’s protection.”

“When that happens, we can relax a bit…”

Just as the captain said that the lookout suddenly shouted, “Enemy attack, pirates found to the right side!”

The captain’s brows immediately pursued, and all of the sailors in the fleet grew tense.

The lookout then shouted in panic, “That flag! Oh no, it’s the Two Headed Pirate Group!”

The sailors immediately grew tumultuous.

The captain’s face also changed.

The Two Headed Pirate Group were notorious, their commander, Lan Lu, was a two headed ogre who cultivated both battle qi and magic. His troops were also all ogres.

Ogres had thick skin and astonishing strength.

At the same time, they were omnivorous, and liked to eat elves, dwarfs, and humans.

Those unlucky enough to be attacked by the Two Headed Pirate Group would not only have their property snatched, but they would also be treated as food and end up in an ogre’s belly.

“Flee now!” The captain ordered, “Issue a distress message to Snake Rat Island.”

With a head filled with cold sweat, the navigator made to do so at once.

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance fleet had excellent and comprehensive equipment, including magic communication devices that could send messages over long distances.

However, even if Snake Rat Island received this distress message, they were too far to be able to assist them now.

The merchant alliance fleet activated their alchemy devices, and their sails fell, and an artificial sea wind blew from motors at top speed.

The fleet’s speed increased by 30%.

“Hehehe, you think you can escape?” The Two Headed Pirate Group’s captain, Lan Lu, was sneering on the ship’s bow.

He was 3.2 meters in height, had two heads, and had a very plump belly. The hair that covered his body was thick and his skin was blue suffused with purple. A stench emitted from his body that only had simple yellow leather clothes around it.

He only had a wolf teeth club in his right hand, and a crooked willow wood casting staff in his left.

“Give me chase!” Lan Lu shouted, exposing his mouth full of yellow-black teeth.

The pirates joyfully cried out, and steered their sole ship, rapidly closing the distance.

“Not good, they have a magic ship!” The sailors grew more frightened.

“The Sapphire…” The merchant alliance captain gnashed his teeth.

Heh knew much more information.

The Sapphire was a bronze level magic ship, it once belonged to the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance, but when it was in a repair dock, a novice Lan Lu had stolen it away.

With this magic ship, Lan Lu led his clan to have a foothold in the ocean, he looted to survive, continuously grow, and gradually expand.

“Captain, toss the goods, the other side will chase after it!” The navigator shouted.

The captain’s face was filled with hesitation and struggle.

These goods were very valuable, if they were lost, he would no longer be a captain.

His future in the merchant alliance was bound to grow dark and lightless.

But if he didn’t toss them, and the pirates caught up, they would lose all of their lives.

“Toss them, toss the goods, save only the valuable ones. Toss anything heavy off all of the ships!” The captain finally issued the order.

As merchandise was thrown into the seawater, the ships buoyed higher in the water, and the merchant alliance fleet grew faster.

But due to the captain’s hesitation, it was too little too late.

The magic ship had gotten close enough, and now they were within Lan Lu’s spell casting range.

Lan Lu raised his casting staff high.

Quick cast!

Cast successful!

Bang, boom.

A series of ten lightning bolts appeared in the clear sky.

The lightning cracked into the decks, causing wood splinters to fly and sparks to shower. It cracked into the sails, tearing and igniting them.

Some unlucky sailors were struck by the lightning, turning into ash, deader than dead.

“Halt! I pledge that I will not eat anyone that can work.” Lan Lu’s left head roared, “If you try to escape again, I will eat everyone.”

The merchant alliance sailors were very frightened, and when they heard those words, they all hesitated.

Everyone looked at the merchant alliance captain.

The captain hesitated again.

During this time, Lan Lu’s right head chortled, “That’s right, just like that. They hesitated!”

“First, we deceive them, then we take them all prisoner. Eat any disobedient ones on the spot. The obedient ones will be imprisoned and become our food reserves.”

“Ah, I’ve proven myself, so when I came up with this brilliant scheme.”

“I have been starving for many days, I want to eat people, I want to eat people!”

As he said that, Lan Lu’s right hand raised his wolf teeth club as saliva flowed from the side of his mouth.

“Idiot!” Lan Lu’s left head exploded in anger.

His troops, the other ogres, covered their heads. “The captain said his true feelings again!”

After hearing this conversation, the merchant alliance fleet’s last hope had vanished.

“Flee!” They cried out in heart and soul.

Unfortunately, however, because of the lightning, their ships had been greatly slowed.

The Sapphire had caught up.

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance had to retaliate with artillery.

Artillery shells struck the Sapphire, but they only created sparks against its hull.

The Sapphire had a formidable defense, although it was only a bronze level magic ship, it had a layer of hard shell that could resist silver level cannon fire.

Like a fierce wolf pouncing on a flock of sheep, the Sapphire tore into the merchant alliance ships. Although they were more numerous, they were in disarray. Their counterattack could not harm the Sapphire sufficiently.

After two hours, the ocean pursuit and ensuing battle had finally come to an end.

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance had suffered a total defeat, some ships were taken captive, while the other part was either sinking or had sunk into the water already.

Lan Lu sat cross-legged on the Sapphire’s deck and gnawed on the captain’s thigh.

The blood filled mouth still had scarlet leg tendons handing between its teeth.

“The flavor is pretty good; it is a silver level human. Hehehe.” Lan Lu enjoyed it.

The ogres around him watched their captain enjoy the delicacy, all of them secretly swallowing their saliva.

Strangely, Lan Lu had a human standing next to him, one who did not belong to the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance.

This human was trembling with fear and covered in cold sweat, yet still tried to remain calm.

“Boss, the enemy ships have been plundered, most of the goods they tossed into the ocean have been picked up, while the rest have sunk into the water, they cannot be salvaged.” The ogre’s first mate reported.

“All of what?” Lan Lu asked offhand while continuing to chew on the human thigh.

The ogre first mate read off a list of goods.

During this plunder, the Two Headed Pirate Group had a great harvest.

When the human saw that Lan Lu looked pleased, it strengthened his nerves enough for him to ask with a trembling voice, “Lord Lan Lu, the information I gave to you wasn’t wrong? This is a gesture of Rou Zang’s good faith, and also a commission. Was it to your satisfaction?”

“Satisfied, of course I am satisfied.” Lan Lu’s left head beamed, “I have heard that Rou Zang is a hero. It seems that cooperating with Rou Zang’s pirate group is always a good choice. I look forward to working together to attack Snake Rat Island.”

The human pirate from Rou Zang’s pirate group coughed, then smiled, “It is for the best…”

But before he could finish, Lan Lu’s right head spoke, “Rou Zang is a fart!”

“He couldn’t take care of two silver levels and was seriously hurt by a single cannon shot.”

“Yet now he still wants to use me to vainly attack Snake Rat Island?”

“What kind of qualifications does this weak person have to collaborate with me?”

“Is he still a gold level?”

“Hehe, I have never eaten a gold level human fighter before!”

“First we will feign agreement, and attack Snake Rat Island together.”

“Then when I see a chance, if Rou Zang is weak, don’t blame us for attacking him. Ahahaha, I look forward to how he tastes!”

“Moron.” Lan Lu’s left head angrily rebuked himself.

“Boss!” An ogre exclaimed at the same time, “You spoke your true feelings again!”

The human pirate’s eyes widened, and his mouth fell open, he was scared speechless.

Splat.

Lan Lu’s club crushed the human.

“You can eat now.” Said his left head forthright.

His left head then spoke, “Summon Rou Zang’s pirate group, say that he died during the battle. Let them send another one.”

“But boss, that was the 7th one.”

“Be at ease, they will agree.” The left head sneered, his tone certain.

By the time Tan Qiu led his fleet to the battlefield, the Two Headed Pirate Group had swaggered off long ago, leaving only mostly corpse behind which were quickly eaten by fish.

When Tan Qiu’s ship returned, the news rapidly disseminated throughout Snake Rat Island.

The survivors thereby found out about this misfortune through that.

“The intelligence potion we ordered was on those ships, Lan Lu stole it.” Zi Di shook her head, “Our deposit is nonrefundable. Furthermore, Snake Rat Island’s market has suffered, prices will go up.”

The dragonman youngster helplessly sighed, without this potion, the big guy could only continue to temper his basic steps.

Cang Xu analyzed, “It seems that the Two Headed Pirate Group and Rou Zang are collaborating. As to what degree, it is currently unknown.”

“The waters surrounding Snake Rat Island are getting worse.”

“The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance is bound to react to their losses to some extent. It is very possible that the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance’s magician will back Lord Tan Qiu’s crusade against Rou Zang’s allied forces.”

“We have to find the Green Jade Gold Coffin as soon as possible, then separate ourselves from this den of trouble.”

“Any further delays, and I fear we won’t be able to leave.”

As a former noble steward of the Sha Ta Clan, he had considerable insight into the current political situation.

Tan Qiu had always planned to create an alliance to suppress Rou Zang’s pirate group.

To him, the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance losing a fleet was not a bad thing.

The Two headed Pirate Group’s arrival put immense external pressure on Snake Rat Island. It was possible that it would prompt an alliance even faster.

If such a great power formed, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Group temporarily berthed on Snake Rat Island would be in an awkward situation.

Tan Qiu might unite other aspects and put pressure onto the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, forcing the survivors into joining his alliance.

Zong Ge spoke, “Our current difficulties lay with the Green Jade Gold Coffin.”

“We have been searching for it for many days, yet we have never found it.”

“The entire underground has been scouted out by us, the only remaining blank spots left are the domain of gold level magic beasts, places that are difficult to approach.”

“It is very possible that War Merchant has a teleportation formation in their territory.”

Chapter 273: Space Lock

Did they have to deal with these gold magic beasts?

This was the greatest challenge ticking before the survivors’ eyes.

They could not storm in, the recently established Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could not stand against such powerful foes.

Even with the young man and Zong Ge working together, it would be a great challenge to deal with a gold level magic beast.

The gold level tide snake that attacked previously required Tan Qiu, ten silver level cultivators, and almost all of the other high level figures on Snake Rat Island to face and kill it.

Luring a gold level magic beast from the underground and onto the surface so that Tan Qiu and the others could deal with this beast calamity was also risky.

If they were exposed, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Group would be besieged on all sides.

Even if the others were kept in the dark, the dragonman youngster couldn’t keep luring magic beasts out into the open.

The more magic beasts that appeared, the more suspicious others would become, thus instigating investigations.

Furthermore, with two pirate groups on the seas working together, if a gold magic beast attacked. Tan Qiu might take a gentler approach like diverting the magic beast elsewhere or placating its anger.

After all, battles were risky.

After Zi Di and Cang Xu looked at each other, she said, “It seems there is only one way.”

“In War Merchant’s database, there is an alchemy device called a Space Lock. Its effect was to null spatial changes within a specific area. For example, spatial passages, spatial cuts, teleportation spells, dimensional gates, and other such magics would fail or be suppressed.”

Zong Ge marveled, “I know of this alchemy device, it has played a crucial effect in many military campaigns. But normally, space locks are at minimum, gold level items. Are you saying we have the ability to make a gold level item?”

Cang Xu shook his head, “We do not.”

But we don’t need to make a complete space lock, we only need to refine a part of one.”

“The principle behind a space lock is this: a spatial node is manufactured, then the node is used to form a magic barrier that stabilizes space. To make a spatial node, one of the prerequisites is that the node has the ability to determine the current situation of space within its range. If space is not stable, or if there are other spatial nodes, the spatial node will sound the alarm.”

When Zong Ge heard that, he immediately realized what Zi Di and Cang Xu were intending, “Are you saying that we only need to refine the most basic part of a space lock, then use it to examine the condition of nearby space, allowing us to discover the teleportation formation?”

“Correct.” Zi Di nodded, “We have already mapped out almost all of the underground, only the gold magic beast territories are left.”

“By using an incomplete space lock, we can stay on the outskirts and determine the spatial condition of those territories.”

“If War Merchant put a teleportation anywhere there, he would have surely distorted nearby space, and formed a stabilization spatial node. We can find that.”

Zong Ge pursed his brows, “Since we have such a device, why haven’t we been using it?”

Zi Di shook her head, “First, it is very expensive to refine this device. Before the three party deal, we didn’t have the funds. But now, after putting our full effort into it, we were barely able to collect enough materials.”

“Second, our current alchemy capabilities might not be enough to succeed. It is possible that we will fail and cause an immense deficit. After all, it is part of a gold level alchemy device.”

“Finally, it takes a lot of magic power to both start and keep this device running. The magic power requirement is very high, and so are the manufacturing costs. We are unable to have a large survey range. If we hadn’t mapped out almost all of the underground, except for those remaining territories, using such a thing was unfeasible.”

“Our finances are quite bad, and we have no steady income streams. After the three party deal, we finally had capital. But since then, we have had constant expenses. Recruiting manpower, organizing battle squads, building and using the Anti-investigation and Prophecy Formation, purchasing equipment, purchasing magic beast meat, and buying daily necessities, along with ship maintenance, maintaining defense and scouting puppets…..”

“Currently, our main source of revenue is from hunting subterranean magic beasts. It is not enough to offset our expenses.”

“If we want to risk refining a space lock, we’d need to sell some flame crystal clusters and tide snake eggs.”

Those two resources had been obtained though the dragonman youngster’s Bloodcore transformations, allowing him to swipe them from underground areas.

Originally, they didn’t plan on selling them on Snake Rat Island.

It attracted too much attention.

If they were to be shown, it was bound to attract the envy of others – it’s so difficult for us to gather these resources, how did your mercenary corps obtain so many, you must have some special trick to do so!

The Night Devil Hunting Group had provoked a gold level tide snake when they tried to obtain tide snake eggs, causing disastrous casualties. If they saw the Dragon Lion Mercenary Crops wantonly take out many more tide snake eggs, what would they think?

Lord Tan Qiu would especially have misgivings.

When the survivors did mass recruitment, Tan Qiu felt incessantly distressed.

If he suddenly saw the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps display so many uncommon resources, Tan Qiu might suffer a stroke.

They had all been produced by his territory.

Yet outsiders secretly absconded with them, leaving Tan Qiu with nothing.

More crucially, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps also had an extraordinarily deep understanding of the underground. Although they had explored it all, the lord of the territory, Tan Qiu, did not have such knowledge.

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has done something to my territory, they must have left some passages behind.”

“My territory is not safe!”

Tan Qiu thinking this way was not odd.

What would he do then?

Would he attack the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?

He would muster the Hunting Guild and the local Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance, along with military force to threaten the survivors.

As the overlord, he also had many other methods. Such as levying heavy taxes!

Most of the survivors’ harvest was bound to be taken back, at minimum, their comprehensive map of the underground would be obtained by Tan Qiu.

Survival was not easy, it needed strategy.

On Mystifying Monster Island, everyone utilized all kinds of tactics to survive. On Snake Rat Island, it was the same.

The opponents the survivors faced now were stronger and more intelligent.

Don’t be fooled by the fact that Snake Rat Island sheltered them, they still needed to be vigilant against Tan Qiu and the others. As long as the benefits were big enough, Tan Qiu would act against the survivors.

To refine the foundational part of a space lock, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps began to outwardly sell flame crystal clusters and tide snake eggs.

These were some of Snake Rat Island’s rarest resources.

The main partners were the Hunting Guild and the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance storefront.

They sold them a bit at a time, prudently probing the attitude of the island’s big powers.

Snake Rat Island, the town castle.
