Tan Qiu was standing in front of a window, his eyes following the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance magician leaving, climbing onto her carriage, then departing the castle in it.

Tan Qiu was gloomy, “Merchants are self-interested and never take responsibility!”

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance transport fleet had been completely wrecked by Lan Lu, Tan Qiu did not have enough time to save it.

With the help of this incident, he opened discussions with the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance and hoped they would join his alliance to deal with Rou Zang’s pirate group.

No, to be more accurate, the combined pirate forces of Rou Zang and Lan Lu.

At first, the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance expressed the desire to cooperate, however, during the discussion, they asked for much more benefits if they were to go to war.

Their proposed price was very high, and to Tan Qiu, it was no different than seeing a lion opening its gaping maw.

“Those people suffered at the hands of pirates, now they want to bite me and obtain compensation!” Tan Qiu huffed, he couldn’t have a falling out with them.

Because he knew the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance was too big.

Although they may have lost a small transportation fleet, they still have several hundred others!

They could bear this small loss.

They considered the costs, risks, and profits.

When Lan Lu stole one of their ships, the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance only issued a bunty, they didn’t personally chase him down.

Did they lack the strength?

Of course not.

They just calculated the costs. Chasing Lan Lu through the boundless ocean was very expensive and unprofitable.

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance was a colossus, they had many enemies that made things difficult, Lan Lu was unremarkable among them.

Contrary to that, the magician of the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance storefront wanted to eradicate the combined forces of Rou Zang and Lan Lu.

Because the short period without a transportation fleet would greatly influence her performance.

But this was just her personal opinion, and she yielded to the Merchant Alliance’s higher up’s commands.

After sitting back down at his desk, Tan Qiu internally examined the powers on the island.

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance storefront, the Sheng Ming Church, the Hunting Guild, the Night Devil Hunting Group, the bounty hunter Ba Hu, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps…

“Mye negotiations with the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance has hit an immense obstruction.”

“The church’s small Templar Knight squad is a powerful battle squad, however, the priest won’t yield them to me.”

“The Hunting Guild has no experts, and scrupulously abides by neutrality, I cannot count on them.”

“The Night Devil Hunting Group bore the brunt of damage from the gold magic beast incident, they suffered disastrous damage.”

“Ba Hu is interested in joining, however, he is only one man.”

“Hei Fei’s ships are behind the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’, they have never gotten repaired. After the three party deal, their desire to leave the island has gotten fiercer, I cannot rely on them.”

“As for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps…this group of people not only recruited my talents, they also have been peddling flame crystal clusters and tide snake eggs! I have always considered them in my plans. That Shi Qi, he unexpectedly rejected my offer! Simply preposterous.”

Tan Qiu’s troops not only had the silver level city guard commander, but also the silver level female thief, the latter was very good at making discreet inquiries.

Every day, Tan Qiu would receive a lot of intelligence, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was the chief subject among it.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ expansion could be seen by the naked eye.

The survivors’ strength had become an irksome presence to Tan Qiu.

Previously, when Tan Qiu tried to recruit the survivors, it had been very cordial, because they had exploitable worth. But Tan Qiu was never able to make use of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and as the highest authority, he would naturally be secretly irate.

These days, Tan Qiu wasn’t doing well.

To be more accurate, since the Pirate Throne had appeared, Tan Qiu’s days had been getting consistently worse.

The pressure on him was immense.

Rou Zang had been seriously injured, yet after so many days, his injury would have been healed.

Obviously, this had been an excellent opportunity, yet this group of damnable people relinquished it.

All of them were scheming, regardless of the big picture!

Now, there was not only Rou Zang, but there was also the Two Headed Pirate Group.

Tan Qiu’s head swelled.

He had always strived to be a mediator, a constant negotiator, and had wanted to establish an alliance, however, he never made any notable headway.

A few days later, Tan Qiu received even worse news.

His tax collection ship had been captured by the Two Headed Pirate Group, all of his taxes had fallen totally into the pirates’ hand.

Tan Qiu flew into a rage.

Those taxes were his territory’s main income.

Without those taxes, not to mention developing Snake Rat Island, he would find it difficult to pay for salaries and provide for his people.

Tan Qiu was forced into a dead end, thus at last, he made a firm resolution, “Kill, I must kill those repulsive pirates.”

“Even if it means losing my family fortune, I will spare no expense!”

Chapter 274: A Knight Who isn’t a Knight!

The Snake Rat Island tavern.

Customers were discussing amongst themselves.

“Did you hear? Tan Qiu’s tax collector ship was plundered.”

“The Two Headed Pirate Group was also the culprit!”

At this time, Lan Lu was very lively. On the contrary, Rou Zang’s pirates were nonexistent.

“How much of the taxes did they loot?”

“A lot!”

“Tan Qiu’s fleet didn’t protect it?”

“That I don’t know.”

“Our overlord’s taxes weren’t being protected? Oh dear…”

News of the stolen taxes quickly set the entire island abuzz.

The survivors also learned of this information.

As this was within Cang Xu’s purview, he knew what was going on, “There are many islands under Snake Rat Island’s jurisdiction, not just Stone Egg Island. If every island’s tax collector ship had a fleet protecting it, Tan Qiu’s fleet would be at least ten times its current size.”

Zong Ge analyzed, “Tax collector ships move on the same route, said routes are rigorously kept confidential. In the boundless ocean, it is difficult to catch swift ships. The Two Headed Pirate Group being able to accurately capture one indicates that Tan Qiu likely has a pirate spy in his inner circle!”

“That isn’t difficult to analyze.”

“Right now, Tan Qiu’s wish to establish an alliance has become more difficult. Unless he is able to uncover this traitor.

The dragonman youngster expressed his doubts, “Is there not a safer way to collect taxes? With the Pirate Throne appearing, the pirates have been moving more rampantly. Tax collector ships are optimal plundering targets for pirates. While having a fleet to escort them is too costly.”

“There is.” Said Cang Xu, “Generally, major imperial cities or territories that contribute a lot of taxes will build a fixed teleportation formation. These teleportation formations are either quite hidden or have a massive military presence guarding it.”

“Snake Rat Island has only been briefly developed, Tan Qiu also doesn’t have enough funds to support a mass construction of teleportation formations.”

“However, the Sheng Ming Church over there has a teleportation formation.”

“That is a Sheng Ming Church parish, they also collect taxes. Of course, it isn’t called taxes, rather it is monetary church donations.”

Compared to small nobles like Tan Qiu, the Sheng Ming Church’s patron is far larger, they were easily able to install that teleportation formation and now have a stable channel of collecting taxes.

“If this matter continues to expand, Tan Qiu might have to rely on the church to collect taxes.” Cang Xu speculation was quickly validated.

After the tax collector ship was plundered, Tan Qiu found the church priest to discuss a matter they could collaborate on.

Both parties quickly reached an agreement.

During that process, the survivors also obtained something they wanted.

The space lock!

Well only the foundation of one.

If the space lock was complete, it would have been a gold level item.

The space lock (base) currently only had a pedestal, this pedestal looked circular, and if one looked down at it, it would resemble a gigantic magnifying glass that was 2 meters in diameter.

The inside of the circular pedestal was made out of a crystalline slab, while the exterior was wrapped around by a metal structure. The alternating white and gold gave it a classical arrangement.

“We were very lucky, we only had one chance, yet we managed to refine it.” Cang Xu rejoiced.

In order to create this thing, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ external procurement strength had been reduced by 80%. Equipment purchases had stopped completely, and they took a risk to sell many flame crystal clusters and tide snake eggs.

Thanks to the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge’s prior success in wounding Rou Zang, as well as the rampant pirates creating a dangerous environment, Tan Qiu and the other powers restrained themselves, none of them acted against the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Activation testing.

Paired up with the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s detection equipment, the space lock first scanned the entire town.

They noticed many spatial nodes.

The Sheng Ming Church had the most spatial nodes and the most stable ones.

The survivors were surprised, the Sheng Ming Church used them to collect monetary donations.

The Hunting Guild and the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance storefront also had a few spatial nodes.

Zi Di surmised, “Those should be communication devices. Those few spatial nodes cannot form a teleportation formation, they can only deliver information.”

Everyone also noticed that in the depths of Tan Qiu’s castle, there was a pile of spatial nodes.

“It seems that Snake Rat Island’s overlord maintains an escape route for himself.” Cang Xu smiled.

That was normal.

If Snake Rat Island was besieged, the boundless ocean would make it difficult for a trapped overlord to escape.

Traditional secret passages were ineffective, therefore a teleportation formation was the best choice.

“Tonight, we will move this pedestal to the underground area. We will scan those gold magic beast territories.” The dragonman youngster issued his order.

Because of the addition of the space lock (base), headway had been made in their search for the Green Jade Gold Coffin.

Like Zi Di and Cang Xu had anticipated, the survivors only needed to walk around the edges of the gold magic beast territories to scan the condition of nearby space.

Every gold magic beast territory was eliminated.

The survivors had agreed that as long as they reclaimed the Green Jade Gold Coffin, they would depart this sketch area!

At the same time, the maritime situation also continued to get worse.

The Two Head Pirate Group was beginning to attack islands.

There was still no trace of Rou Zang, but his subordinate groups, the Straw Head Pirate Group and the Flower Wall Pirate Group had appeared, joining hands with the Two Headed Pirate Group to successfully pillage three islands in a row.

More and more refuges were pouring into Snake Rat Island.

Snake Rat Island was gradually growing anxious.

“The pirates are too rampant. With two pirate groups working together, only Snake Rat Island is strong enough to defend itself.”

“What will our feudal report to his superiors? Could it be that his fleet is merely for show?”

“It isn’t like that, Tan Qiu has dispatched his fleet, but every time, they miss their target.”

“Snake Rat Island must have a traitor in it! A great traitor!”

“I think that our fleet shouldn’t go out so recklessly. Snake Rat Island only has one fleet, if it is ambushed, our Snake Rat Island will also be in danger.

The people began to lose faith in Tan Qiu.

The survivors’ mood was also growing heavier.

Besides the maritime situation growing worse, they had now scanned every gold magic beast territory, yet they didn’t discover any spatial nodes!

“The situation is terrible. Its possible that War Merchant used a unique method to conceal spatial nodes. The space lock (base) we have cannot detect it.”

“Perhaps it was a single use teleportation formation. After the teleportation was completed, it was destroyed. After so many days, the space has also recovered its stability, and subsequently wiping any previous traces away.”

The survivors felt awkward.

They had spent a large sum to refine the space lock (base) so they could find the Green Jade Gold Coffin, yet in the end, they hadn’t.

“Perhaps, the Green Jade Gold Coffin was discovered. Maybe one of the island’s powers secretly collected it and even sent it away?” Zong Ge proposed a notion.

“Unlikely. If a divine artifact suddenly appeared, it would have incited a commotion. Even if it was covered up, Snake Rat Island would at least have a strong cultivator hidden behind the scenes and conducting a clandestine investigation.” Cang Xu analyzed.

What to do next?

Perhaps they should give up on the Green Jade Gold Coffin?

Everyone looked at the dragonman youngster.

At this crucial juncture, only their leader could make the decision.

The dragonman youngster was also hesitant, “Let me think it over.”

That night, more sad news arrived.

The Thousand Bird Island was invaded by pirates, most of the islanders were murdered and the pirates swaggered off before Tan Qiu’s fleet could reach them.

The Sheng Ming Church’s priest flew into a rage, he publicly expressed on the spot that these blasphemers would pay with their lives.

The priest’s change in attitude was obvious, because this time, the pirates had crossed the line.

Perhaps because of their repeated successes, the pirates had become savage and as they devastated the island, they unscrupulously overturned the Sheng Ming Church’s sacred altar.

Thousand Bird Island didn’t have a religious altar, only a simple one.

Sacred altars were very important, and anyone who destroyed them was blasphemers.

The gods could not be disgraced!

The priest got in touch with Tan Qiu, and changed his prior conditions, he now stated that he could distich the church’s small templar knight squad. Not only that, but he also wanted to personally go to war to crusade against the enemies of his god.

Tan Qiu was both angry and delighted.

He was angry that Thousand Bird Island was ransacked, and that a tax generating place had been severely damaged, this was all losses he suffered.

He was delighted that the Sheng Ming Church’s attitude had greatly changed, and was now the most powerful advocate of the alliance.

Tan Qiu represented royal power, the priest represented divine right, and when divine right and royal power united, a great power began to form. Tan Qiu and the priest pushed with all their might, and the other powers were bound to join the alliance, one after the other.

In order to spur on the newly forming alliance, Tan Qiu and the priest endured all night to plan out a scheme. They mainly discussed how they would coerce and tempt all of the island’s powers to unite together. Their discussion focused on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

At dawn, a magic ship floated into the port.

It was the Sapphire!

Tan Qiu mustered a large force and stood on the port in formation.

A tall human fighter stood on the ship and looked down at the troops, his tone tranquil, “Excuse me, are you the overlord here, Tan Qiu?”

Tan Qiu tightly gazed at the human fighter, the latter looked ordinary and normal, he was an exhausted middle aged man.

The silver level thief quietly whispered into his ear, and informed him of what she had learned.

Tan Qiu’s doubts quickly dissipated into pleasant surprise and respect. He saluted the middle aged man, “Lord Guang Ke, I am Tan Qiu, we are honored to see that you could make it here!”

But the human fighter sighed, “I am just a commoner, not a lord.”

“Lan Lu is my captive, this ship no longer has the power to resist. Overlord tan Qiu, you can take this person, and deal with the other ogre captives on board.”

Tan Qiu waved his hand, and the city guard commander immediately led his troops onto the ship and poured into the Sapphire.

Tan Qiu personally escorted Guang Ke off the ship cordially.

The Two Headed Pirate Group had been annihilated!

This was explosive news, and as soon as it was propagated, it immediately triggered a sensation.

The fighter who had destroyed the Two Headed Pirate Group was a man called Guang Ke.

A gold level!

Lan Lu was an ogre with both silver level battle qi and magic, and was capable of fighting against gold levels.

But Guang Ke was no ordinary gold level fighter.

He was a legendary character, and his story had been spread widely among the commonfolk.

He was born a commoner, but after cultivating the battle qi technique the Yao Wang Clan had disseminated, he became a remarkable fighter.

He then discovered his past, he was a bastard abandoned by some member of the Yao Wang Clan. His father had played with his mother, then dispassionately discarded the woman he once loved dearly.

The Yao Wang Clan wanted to recruit Guang Ke, and was willing to punish his father severely and give his mother preferential treatment.

His mother refused, but wanted Guang Ke to return to the Yao Wang Clan, so he could develop better.

But Guang Ke refused to join, he renounced readily available wealth and rank, and instead held fast at his mother’s side.

Not only that, but he also used his strength to freely assist the commoner friends at his side.

After his mother passed away, Guang Ke began to live like a knight-errant, he traveled the world, experienced heaven and earth, and intervened when he saw injustice.

He upheld justice for commoners many times, even if it angered local officials as a result.

He was the target of many assassinations, and even people he had aided betrayed him.

But when he noticed injustice and met feeble people who appealed to him, he still did his best to defend righteousness and to help the weak.

He would never become a night, for a knight was part of the nobility.

Therefore, he had a nickname – the Knight Who Isn’t a Knight.

Guang Ke didn’t accept Tan Qiu’s kindness or entertainment, and didn’t join the banquet.

He instead came to the home of someone asking for help.

This was the house of a commoner, the only residents in it were an old grandmother and her grandson.

“Your son asked me for help while he was dying. I am very sorry, I was unable to bring him back alive.” Guang Ke sorrowfully recounted.

The grandmother cried, but she wiped the tears away.

She expressed her most sincere thanks to Guang Ke, she had been prepared to hear news of her son’s death.

She wanted Guang Ke to stay so that she could make him dinner.

Guang Ke refused her too.

Because he knew that this dinner would probably use up the family’s most valuable supplies.

Guang Ke stayed at the tavern.

The tavern was surrounded and packed with people.

But regardless of whether it was the customers or those who had come specifically for him, all were silent.

“Hush.”

“Don’t disturb Lord Guang Ke.”

“He is very tired and needs to have a good rest.”

No one in the thronging crowd uttered a word.

Chapter 275: Finding the Divine Artifact

The dragonman youngster also came to the tavern.

The wordless crowd shocked him!

“He is a true knight!” The young man’s heart was dashed.

Zong Ge, “Ah Guang Ke, I heard of this man many times.”

Cang Xu sighed ruefully, “He is worthy of being a gold level. If he acts, he can alter the entire situation.”

The Sapphire’s entrance into the harbor had been witnessed by many of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps personally. When the Sapphire was cleaned up, the watery blood and corpses that were swept off were the best proof of Guang Ke’s strength.

Originally, the Two Headed Pirate Group and Rou Zang’s pirate group were a black cloud that suffocated Snake Rat Island and made its future dark.

But now, Guang Ke suddenly appeared and wiped out the Two Headed Pirate Group.

By the time the Sapphire sailed into the harbor, almost all of the ogres on board were slain in battle. Among the lucky survivors was the two headed ogre, Lan Lu.

Guang Ke captured Lan Lu.

The pressure on Snake Rat Island plummeted.

The island residents’ morale suddenly rose from the depths to the highest peaks.

“This is the world of the strong! Strong individuals can solve most problems.” Zi Di sighed.

Snake Rat Island had many silver levels, however, they found it difficult to unite together.

Guang Ke’s battle strength was greater than all of those combined, and only he dictated what he determined. Alone he eliminated the incomparably rampant Two Headed Pirate Group.

“We have the Bloodcore, and with enough time, we too can be just as strong!” Cang Xu looked at the dragonman youngster, the necromancer’s heart was ardent.

For Tan Qiu, this was simply joy from the heavens!

He had wanted to hire a gold level cultivator to help him eradicate Rou Zang’s pirate group yet never found one that would agree to it.

How could one just request the help of a gold level expert?

Tan Qiu was just a silver level and lacked prestige The things he could offer were also limited.

Those who reached the gold level were usually the ruler of a region, and didn’t lack individual strength, power, or wealth.

Gold levels like Guang Ke were seldom seen.

Influence and wealth were within his grasp, yet he renounced them and instead traveled the world to help the weak and defend righteousness.

It was because of this that his deeds were widely spread.

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance magician found Tan Qiu immediately, she wanted the Sapphire.

That magic ship used to belong to the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance, it was a transportation ship, and its main purpose was to transport sapphires from the Gem Sea.

Lan Lu just stole it away, and the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance didn’t spend much energy trying to take it back.

Of course, the Sapphire was not Tan Qiu’s.

But as the feudal lord, Tan Qiu had received Guang Ke, thus he was automatically responsible for helping Guang Ke deal with his spoils of war.

This time, it was quite convenient to deal with,

The magician had many plans for the Sapphire.

If she recaptured this ship, her evaluation with the merchant alliance this year was bound to be excellent!

Tan Qiu and the magician negotiated.

This negotiation was entirely different from before.

Tan Qiu firmly held the initiative.

But Tan Qiu didn’t worry about facilitating cooperation, he still had more important things to do.

He secretly gave orders to a group of commoner volunteers.

When Guang Ke woke up on the second day and walked into the tavern’s dining room, he saw some kneeling petitioners.

These petitioners lamented to him of how savage Rou Zang’s pirate group was, of how they had suffered under his hand, and that they hoped Guang Ke would rescue them from his grasp.

If Tan Qiu had asked for help himself, Guang Ke would have disdained his plea.

But these commoners were different; they moved Guang Ke and filled his heart with pity.

That afternoon, Tan Qiu showed up to show Guang Ke that he was preparing to build an alliance that would crusade against Rou Zang’s pirate group. If Guang Ke joined, then he would treat the island residents well and invest a large sum into the people’s welfare. He recounted how important the future was, if Rou Zang was destroyed, then the people of all islands here would live in harmony and happiness.

Despite getting sleep, Guang Ke looked disheveled.

He looked deeply into the tense Tan Qiu, “Were those people this morning sent by you?”

Tan Qiu stared at him, then nodded with a forced smile.

Just as he was about to explain, Guang Ke raised a hand to stop him, “No need, they rushed to me because their words are without falsehood. Rou Zang’s pirate group is indeed a malignant tumor to this place!”

“My Lord, it is great you have come here to uphold justice!” Tan Qiu was ecstatic, “Please come to the castle, let me offer you hospitality.”

This time, Guang Ke accepted Tan Qiu’s invitation.

“I really do need to rest well.”

“My equipment also needs some repairs.”

“Establishing an alliance these days has troubled you.”

“As for rewarding me for my help, that cannot be avoided.”

Guang Ke would freely help commoners, but he was not as polite with nobles.

“Absolutely not a problem!” Tan Qiu clapped his chest and pledged.

As everyone cheered around, Guang Ke left the tavern with Tan Qiu.

The news that he had joined the Rou Zang Suppression Alliance propagated rapidly.

When the church priest heard this news, he immediately left for the castle to meet Tan Qiu.

Tan Qiu was just about to see him.

Tan Qiu spoke forthright, “I want to ask the legendary gold level, Guang Ke, to help us, however I don’t have enough to compensate him.”

That he even had a gold level here to help was already very lucky indeed.

Guang Ke’s reward would be very high, but he wouldn’t demand more.

“Of course.” The priest considered it, then agreed on the spot, “The church has a few months of monetary contributions saved.”

“I can take it all out.”

“Anyways, I hope this alliance can promote the name of my religion.”

Tan Qiu hesitated.

In his plan, the alliance should be headed by him, and answer to his name and actions.

Now however, the priest demanded that he publicize the Sheng Ming Church’s name.

How could his name compare to the Sheng Ming Church?

If he agreed, after the alliance was triumphant, the Sheng Ming Church’s prestige would receive an enormous boost. Meanwhile his efforts as Snake Rat Island’s feudal lord, the one who had been working to build this alliance, would instead be believed to have depended on the Sheng Ming Church’s condemnation to accomplish that feat.

On Snake Rat Island, the longer time went on, the prestige of the Sheng Ming Church would exceed Tan Qiu’s.

“I accept your condition.” After considering it, Tan Qiu finally agreed through clenched teeth.

He had already decided to lose a family fortune to build this alliance and deal with the combined forces of Rou Zang and Two Head pirate group.

The current situation was already far better.

This problem was something to deal with in the future.

After obtaining the priest’s full cooperation, Tan Qiu immediately invited all powers to dinner. The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge naturally accepted this invitation.

“Ladies and gentlemen, I have already requested the help of Lord Guang Ke. Lord Guang Ke has agreed that we join together and crusade against Rou Zang’s pirate group!” Tan Qiu declared at once.

The others were unsurprised.

Tan Qiu’s visit to the tavern was public, and everyone knew of it.

Since Guang Ke had accepted Tan Qiu’s invitation and entered the castle, it had been confirmed that the two had reached an agreement.

“Rou Zang’s pirate group is both savage and tyrannical, they have committed crimes against these waters time and again, now they will taste retribution.” The priest then spoke and stated that he himself would lead the templar knights in the upcoming naval battle.

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance magician then indicated her attitude, “Because of Rou Zang’s pirate group, many people have lost their lives, their property, and many other things. We cannot ignore him again, this is our best chance!”

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance’s transport fleet was ambushed, causing immense damage. Although it wasn’t Rou Zang’s pirate group who had done it, that did not hinder the erupting enmity the magician felt towards Rou Zang.

The bounty hunter, Ba Hu, candidly stood up, “I want to follow Senior Guang Ke, to uphold virtue and condemn evil. I believe firmly that this experience will be one I shall cherish for the rest of my life. Even if it will be the last thing I experience in my life.”

Ba Hu addressed Guang Ke as “senior”, because Guang Ke was also a bounty hunter.

He had traveled everywhere, and through the Bounty Hunter Guild’s bounties and information, had confirmed criminals, attacked criminals, and obtained the rewards to maintain his livelihood.

The Night Devil Hunting Group’s commander burst into laughter, “Ba Hu, you are too anxious. With Lord Guang Ke, Rou Zang is nothing to worry about. They also only have three silver levels, while we have at least double that amount!”

Tan Qiu excitedly watched everyone discussing, his heart incessantly moved.

Guang Ke’s influence was immense, and when he joined the alliance, the other powers now aspired to participate too. It formed a wonderful and distinct contrast from before.

But Tan Qiu also understood that these people were still a smidge from really joining.

If he wanted them to display their genuine strength, they needed to be heavily compensated!

If not, even if these people go to war, they won’t exert themselves too hard.

He had brought all of these powers to the battlefield. If he lost too many troops, he wouldn’t have easy days on Snake Rat Island in the future, and it was likely that other powers would encroach on it.

Thus, Tan Qiu fished out a scroll. “We welcome everyone participating in this righteous battle to look at this.”

“Recorded in here are the rules of war.”

“In summary, the stronger everyone fights in this battle, the greater military service given, and the more losses suffered, the greater spoils of war obtained.”

“We don’t know what we will obtain from Rou Zang’s pirate group.”

“Fortunately, our priest was willing to sacrifice anything to offer us a sufficient reward.”

“The rewards are also detailed in that scroll.”

The priest glanced at Tan Qiu, somewhat discontented.

Tan Qiu was still attaching the most importance to himself.

However, the priest still smiled. It was nothing to let Tan Qiu take this petty advantage. He valued what reputation was given publicity, and it was bound to be the Sheng Ming Sect on the forefront.

The leaders who believed in Sheng Ming already believed that to be the case, and it would be difficult to change the minds of those who didn’t.

The priest’s true goal was the numerous residents of Snake Rat Island, as well as the inhabitants of other islands.

By making those people deeply grateful to the Sheng Ming Church, he would extend the Sheng Ming Church’s influence greatly in this area.

The more followers, the more donations.

The priest had invested funds today so that in the future, it would increase ten or even a hundred fold.

The scroll began to be passed through everyone’s hands.

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance magician was the first to beam, she looked at the priest with heartfelt admiration.

Soon after, the Night Devil Hunting Group Commander, Ba Hu, and the others were also moved when they parsed through the detailed scroll.

Because of that scroll, Captain Hei Fei decided to join the alliance on the spot.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge quietly glanced at each other.

Both had heavy moods.

The survivors had already decided that they wouldn’t be wading into these muddy waters. Although Guang Ke could deal with Rou Zang and the alliance’s chance of winning was high, the survivors had limited time and didn’t want to be pushed into the limelight.

Keeping a low profile was more advantageous to them.

But now, the dining hall’s ambience was just right.

This time, if the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge refused to go to war, they feared that the other powers would target them.

Because previously the survivors had sold flame crystal clusters, tide snake eggs, and other resources, Tan Qiu was quite resentful of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Hei Fei had even enmity towards the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, if he saw that they were unwilling to go to war, he would likely jump out and incite others by calling the survivors secret pirate traitors, and with everyone’s help he would avenge his personal grudge.

The scroll finally reached the dragonman youngster’s hand.

He examined it superficially, his heart thinking how he should tactfully refuse and deal with the resulting situation.

This scroll was an alchemy item, it was called endless paper.

The scroll was only 30 centimeters wide. However, when a fighter imbued it with battle qi, a magician with magic power, or a priest with divine power, the contents on the paper would change.

It was like a book compressed into a single sheet of paper.

The paper changed based on the user’s spirit, and if the user wanted to flip either back or forward, it would change based on that desire. If a user wanted to go back to a specific item, it could even change to the corresponding section.

The scroll was half composed of alliance duties and half composed of entitlements.

After agreeing to join the alliance, they had to follow the priest’s commands as much as possible. Of course, unreasonable military commands could be refused.

In the latter half, were the generous rewards for going to war.

The rewards were mainly funded by the priest, and the rest by Tan Qiu.

These rewards not only consisted of gold and silver coins, but also many types of goods.

There were things the dragonman youngster was familiar with, like flame cluster crystals, tide snake eggs, soft heart stone, along with other oceanic goods with cultivation levels, like kelp, fish, and more.

The most appealing thing to him was equipment.

There was Sheng Ming Church armor, knight swords, among others. These could not be bought on the market. Although it was standard Sheng Ming Church equipment, it was made out of sturdy materials and excellent workmanship, thus it had great practical value.

The dragonman youngster also saw some art pieces.

The price of these art pieces could not be evaluated.

Apart from this, there were title deeds, house deeds, and even slave deeds.

The dragonman youngster was unsurprised by this.

Church contributions did not consist of just gold. Many followers offer up ancestral art, valuable deeds, and even family fishing boats to the church.

Items all had a distinct picture representing them, the art pieces likewise.

On the contrary, money was just listed as words and numbers.

The dragonman youngster was nonchalantly looking at it until all of a sudden, his heart sped up.

“What is that?” His heart shouted.

At that moment, he simply could not believe his eyes.

He found the Green Jade Gold Coffin!

“How is it here?”

“Is it a trap?”

The dragonman youngster pushed down his astonishment, and with all of his strength, attempted to control his expression.

He was not an actor.

He failed.

His change of color made Tan Qiu increasingly pleased, causing him to think to himself: the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps also wants to join.

When Zong Ge sensed the dragonman youngster’s change, he thought to himself: our captain is still feigning, that expression of his really looks like he is moved by the generous rewards.

“Take a good look at this.” The dragonman youngster recovered his calmness, and he handed Zong Ge the scroll, his words indicating to him to keep his mouth shut.

Zong Ge took the scroll, and when he saw the top half, he complimented Tan Qiu internally: these rules are quite fair.

But when he saw the latter half, his eyeballs too couldn’t contain themselves from popping.

“The Green Jade Gold Coffin?!”

Chapter 276: Limited Participation

The Green Jade Gold Coffin had been teleported from Mystifying Monster Island.

The survivors had spent all of their efforts to retrieve this divine artifact.

They first constructed the Deceptive Disguise Formation, and used it to wake, then deceive the tower spirit so they could find the teleportation record, leading them to Snake Rat Island.

Afterwards, with Rou Zang barring down on them, the survivors had entered Snake Rat Island.

On Snake Rat Island, they discovered a gigantic underground space and surmised that it was likely that War Merchant stashed a teleportation formation down there.

To disguise their true search target, they went to war and hunted the magic beasts in the underground. During this process, the survivors raised an army, socialized with other powers, and were very prudent throughout it.

Although they had made great progress in all other aspects, they never found the Green Jade Gold Coffin.

As time dragged on, the survivors were compelled by circumstance to risk the refinement of a space lock (base).

With it, they examined every gold magic beast territory in the underground, but they still came back empty handed.

Everyone felt quite awkward, the divine artifact was extraordinarily valuable, but if War Merchant’s concealments could not be found by currently available methods, the survivors would never find it.

On the other hand, their time was also getting scarcer.

The flaw of Mystifying Monster Island was that as time passed, sooner or later, they would be exposed.

They had to redeem themselves as soon as possible.

No one could decide, thus the dragonman youngster had to shoulder the duty of leadership and make it.

He was also hesitant.

Then by chance, Tan Qiu sent out a dinner invitation.

When he and Zong Ge attended, they unexpectedly discovered the Green Jade Gold Coffin.

He never would have thought that the Green Jade Gold Coffin would appear before him in such a way!

He carefully examined the endless paper, the Green Jade Gold Coffin’s picture was very distinct.

A gold coffin, 3 meters long and 1.8 meters wide. The surface inlaid with green jade and the coffin’s interior portraying a fresco of the gods.

After his shock dissipated, the dragonman youngster’s first thought was, “Is this a trap?”

He then shook his head internally.

He was not a greenhorn, and had been resting and reorganizing on Snake Rat Island for a while. All indicators stated that this wasn’t a trap.

At the same time, the dragonman youngster trusted the Deceptive Disguise Formation and the Anti-investigation and Prophecy Formation.

“The Green Jade Gold Coffin was dedicated to the church, the priest doesn’t know what it actually is.”

The divine artifact concealed its true self.

If one looked at the Green Jade Gold Coffin on a surface level, there was nothing to distinguish it from any other ordinary thing, it did not distribute any divine aura of any level.

Although it was made of gold and inlaid with jade in a gorgeous manner, it was not eye-catching.

Gold was valuable, but there were many other more expensive metals like secret silver, spirit gold, and more.

Even compared to the Sheng Ming Church equipment the priest offered, this Green Jade Gold Coffin was more unremarkable.

The young man pondered: if I did not know the truth, and also believed that this was just some art piece, my gaze would be drawn to more valuable things I can use.

The more the dragonman youngster thought about it, the greater understanding he gained.

“Previously, we thought that War Merchant constructed a teleportation formation in the underground.”

But after searching this place, War Merchant wouldn’t have treated this place as an escape route. At least, we didn’t discover any teleportation formations on Snake Rat Island that belong to him.”

“Therefore, at the time, Jia Sha used the alchemy tower’s teleportation formation to connect to the closest Sheng Ming Church teleportation formation coordinate to Mystifying Monster Island, that is how it ended up in the Sheng Ming Church on Snake Rat Island.”

Snake Rat Island’s Sheng Ming Church’s teleportation formation is primarily used for transportation church contributions. Sheng Ming Church infrastructure is generally robust. Not only is gold and money contributed, but also all kinds of things.

Although the Green Jade Gold Coffin had been teleported and was a divine artifact, all of the energy the teleportation consumed came from the central alchemy tower, thus, in short, none of the clergy noticed.

Why didn’t they notice?

The dragonman youngster guessed there might be two reasons.

First, it was nigh impossible that someone would dedicate a divine artifact.

It was like picking up a treasure while collecting trash, not just an ordinary treasure, but an exceptional one!

Who would think this would happen?

Compared to the other donations, the Green Jade Gold Coffin was just a beautiful and expensive looking thing. Such a type of coffin was commonly seen, and lavish burials were a popular social custom.

Maybe the clergy also thought that this coffin was unlucky, after all, it was adorned with the things of the dead.

No one knew which believer’s tomb this had been excavated from.

Second, Jia Sha was selfish.

Jia Sha likely used some method to hide the Green Jade Gold Coffin’s existence.

After all, on Mystifying Monster Island, he sent off the divine artifact first.

If this divine artifact was noticed by others, they could falsely claim the credit, an incomparably gigantic loss for Jia Sha.

“What does Commander Long Fu think about the crusade against Rou Zang’s pirate group?” When Tan Qiu saw that Long Fu and Shi Qiu hadn’t declared their positions yet after a while, he couldn’t help but urge them.

Zong Ge’s eyes tightened as he looked at the dragonman youngster.

After the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge glanced at each other, he suddenly opened his mouth, “Our Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and Rou Zang’s pirate group already have enmity.”

“Since then, all of us have always wanted to crusade against this pirate group. Unfortunately, we lacked strength and support.”

“Now with everyone as our comrades, we are very happy and also willing to join this alliance.”

“But…”

“We have more important business.”

“Our time is in short supply, and there isn’t much time before we leave Snake Rat Island.”

“Seven days. Within those seven days, our Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps can contribute our strength and participate in the naval battle against Rou Zang’s pirate group.”

Tan Qiu suddenly looked discolored.

The looks of the others also sank.

How could seven days be enough?

They were stronger than the enemy now, and although Rou Zang was savage and brutal, he was not stupid. Since Guang Ke’s appearance, coinciding with his obvious intention to join hands with the local feudal lord, he was most assuredly not going to be as rampant as before.

Finding a trace of him in the boundless ocean would always be a challenge.

They needed to gather a lot of information, and even deploy bait to facilitate an open naval battle between the alliance and Rou Zang’s pirate group.

However, the dragonman youngster’s excuse left everyone speechless.

Because they knew the survivors’ “true identity”, they were not a mercenary corps, but arms smugglers.

Since they were arms smugglers, they must have a delivery destination and date.

To their ears, the dragonman youngster’s excuse indicated to them that the delivery date was quickly approaching!

“That is very unlucky.”

“From the bottom of my heart, I had been looking forward to your two dashing figures.”

“If your group leaves this island alone at this time, will you be safe? If I was Rou Zang, I wouldn’t let part of the enemy forces get away.”

Tan Qiu, the merchant alliance magician, and the Night Devil Hunting Commander spoke in succession.

All of them did not wish to see a powerful ally like the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps leave.

The dragonman youngster stated that the timeline could not be altered, but under the persuasion of everyone, he softened his attitude.

He stated, “The Dragon Lion Mercenary will join the alliance on the frontlines for the next seven days.”

“During that time, we are also willing to dispatch troops to search for Rou Zang’s pirate group.”

“If we find them, we will hinder them as best we can so that our other allies have time to rush over and join the battle.”

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps intended to carry out the responsibility of being the vanguard, if they were the first to confront Rou Zang, they would be sure to bear the enemy’s strongest counterattacks.

Since the dragonman youngster had stated all of that, although the others were unreconciled internally, none of them retorted back.

The dragonman youngster took advantage of this to propose, “If the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps shoulders this great responsibility, I hope the daily reward will settle the bill.”

Tan Qiu and the priest negotiated with each other for a while, then agreed to this request.

Zong Ge sat on the side, his face expressionless from beginning to end.

Concerning this “delivery time limit” excuse, the survivors had already agreed upon it beforehand.

But now, the dragonman youngster’s nimble statement was far different from what was agreed upon.

All of this was due to the Green Jade Gold Coffin.

Since the Green Jade Gold Coffin was in the Sheng Ming Church’s hands, they could not pillage or steal it.

There were many reasons for this.

First, they didn’t want to provoke the Sheng Ming Church. It was the church of the Heavenly Emperor Sheng Ming, and had immense power in the Sheng Ming Empire.

Second, Guang Ke had already agreed to join Tan Qiu’s alliance. If the Dragon Lion Mercenary Group wanted to swipe the Green Jade Gold Coffin, it was almost certain they would have to do battle with Guang Ke.

Third, there was the Pirate Throne!

The sacred monument in the Pirate City ranked all of the powerful pirates.

If the survivors pillaged the Green Jade Gold Coffin, pirate behavior, it was very possible they would be ranked on the sacred monument.

If that happened, would the Deceptive Disguise Spell be able to conceal them from the pirate’s sacred monument? Even if it did deceive it, it would suffer enormous damage.

If they had to replenish the Deceptive Disguise Spell constantly, the survivors would go bankrupt.

They could not attack directly, thus all that remained was cooperation.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ advantage was that the other parties didn’t know the true worth of the Green Jade Gold Coffin. The other groups valued the Green Jade Gold Coffin based on its materials – gold and green jade.

This gave the survivors a large gap to sneak into.

When the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge returned to their ships, they informed the other survivors of the significant discovery they made at the banquet.

Cang Xu and Zi Di approved of the young man’s conduct.

The survivors did not want to fight Rou Zang’s pirate group, they just wanted to fight for seven days and take the Green Jade Gold Coffin as their reward.

Tan Qiu had accomplished his goal, he finally established an alliance of armies.

Although the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were leaving after seven days, he did not mind it.

With a gold level cultivator like Guang Ke, who would care about two silvers?

He came to Guang Ke’s door, “Lord Guang Ke.”

He had arranged Guang Ke to stay in the most luxurious room in the castle, the latter was cultivating at the moment.

He sat cross legged in the air, and calmly floated. His entire body emitted an intense white light that illuminated the entire room like the daylight.

He slowly opened his eyes, his tone tranquil, “Feudal Lord Tan Qiu, what is it?”

“The alliance has been established. Tomorrow, we will publicly sentence Lan Lu and use his head to make our vow to our troops! Of course, your Lordship’s bounty has been reported to the Hunting Guild by me. The execution is scheduled to be in the morning, I invite your Lordship to come…” Tan Qiu respectfully stated.

When Guang Ke heard that, he interrupted, “I am not interested, you can do it.”

Tan Qiu left delighted. Guang Ke letting him carry out the sentence and not participating in it meant the prestige was given to him.

“At last, this matter will be dealt with. Rou Zang, our grudges will be settled!” Tan Qiu’s eyes flashed with ire.

Tan Qiu did not know that his every movement had been detected by Mo Kong.

In the depths of the castle, in a secret underground pocket, Mo Kong was drawing a magic formation.

Purple and red magic formation lines lined the ground and walls.

The flickering magic crystal lamp on the wall cast Mo Kong’s face in shadow and uncertainty.

His hand held a recently received command.

The command had come from Rou Zang.

“The boss wants me to crash the execution and do my best to save Lan Lu?” A mountain of pressure pressed on Mo Kong’s heart.

Chapter 277: Execution Day

Tan Qiu arranged for the execution to take place in the town square.

In the square, there was a specialized execution platform set up.

This place had put to death other criminals previously.

But this was the first time it had met someone like the two headed ogre Lan Lu.

To execute him, Tan Qiu had specially ordered that the execution platform be reinforced temporarily.

Execution day.

The execution platform was surrounded by the masses.

The windows and balconies on the surrounding houses were also jam-packed with people.

As the main official on stage, Tan Qiu sat in the seat of honor, with the church priest right by his side.

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance magician, the Night Devil Hunting Group Commander, and the bounty hunter Ba Hu were also present.

As representatives of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, Commander Long Fu and Deputy Commander Shi Qi also took their seats.

“Guang Ke, Lord Guang Ke?”

“He isn’t participating, he appointed this honor to our feudal lord.”

“A true pity, he is the one my son adores the most!”

The vast majority of the masses were very disappointed when Guang Ke didn’t appear.

However, everyone quickly focused on the arrival of Lan Lu.

Personally escorted by the city guard commander and his squad, the Two Headed Pirate Group Commander, Lan Lu, staggered into everyone’s view.

The ogre magician no longer had any of his prior svelte.

His hands were clasped with enormous steel shackles and his feet dragged an iron ball half the size of a man.

He was wounded all over, and one could make out the marks of cruel punishment and torture.

His largest injury was around his neck – it was as big as a grindstone and had only lightly scarred.

One of his heads was missing.

When he fought Guang Ke, the latter had removed that head.

The two headed ogre no longer lived up to the name, for he only had one head now.

“Kill it!”

“Its really him, he killed my aunt on Thousand Bird Island. Boohoo…...”

“Put him to death, put that wretched pirate to death!”

The incessantly indignant crowd threw vegetables, stones, and other unclean things at him.

Tripleblade, Lan Zao, and the others were not important enough to sit on the main stage, thus they were dispersed throughout the crowd.

“Heavens, is he really an ogre boss? He is so much stronger than an ordinary ogre! Bai Ya exclaimed.

The big guy silently leaned against a wall.

He had Tripleblade, Mu Ban, and others by him.

Zi Di and Cang Xu took refuge in a far away tower, and used it to survey the scene from a distance.

“Ogres…I haven’t had the chance to truly study that race.” Cang Xu sighed, his gaze exposing his thirst for research.

Zi Di sighed too, “Ogres normally only have one head, and have low intelligence. As an ogre’s life level increases, it is a certain chance that they will grow a second head. The second head has very high intelligence, thus the ogre is usually able to gain the ability to cast magic.”

“When Guang Ke removed his most crucial body part, his second head, Lan Lu instantly lost his ability to cast magic, causing his battle strength to slump, its no wonder that ogre was able to be taken prisoner.”

After saying that, Zi Di looked at Cang Xu, “Without the second head, Lan Lu is nearly the same as an ordinary ogre, it isn’t worth studying him.”

Nevertheless, Cang Xu smiled and shook his head, “No, his value is still high, especially to you and I.”

“Are you talking about…his soul?” Zi Di instantly got his intention.

“Correct.” Cang Xu nodded, his eyes flashing faintly, “Although Lan Lu lost his second head, that part of his soul still exists. Fighters have few methods that can destroy souls, Guang Ke shouldn’t be an exception.”

“If we can obtain Lan Lu’s soul, it would be very good.”

“An ogre capable of growing a second head will have a dense spirit. If we can successfully manipulate that soul, then it won’t be a simple silver level ghost. Its spirituality will allow it to act independently, and will be the commander of our ghost army!”

After hearing Cang Xu’s explanation, Zi Di was moved.

During this time, when they were resting and reorganizing on Snake Rat Island, they had been frequently hunting in the underground.

Zi Di and Cang Xu had stocked up on ghosts, mainly tide snakes and smelt rats.

Unlike prior ghost summoning, their ghosts had both quality and quantity, consisting of bronze levels, iron levels, and even one or two silver levels.

Through the Ghostly Possession spell, they had temporarily housed these ghosts in their bodies.

One could say their individual battle strength had reached an unprecedented peak.

“When a ghost has a powerful spirituality, I will use less spirit when I control it.”

If Lan Lu’s soul is successfully converted, its battle strength is bound to surpass a silver level tide snake’s.

“Regretfully, we cannot do that.”

Zi Di sighed when she thought that.

Their necromancer identities could not be exposed wantonly, if they were, their situation would be even worse than that of pirates.

Most crucially, Guang Ke was resting in Tan Qiu’s castle.

As for collecting Lan Lu’s soul after the execution, that was also out of the question.

Leaving aside that as time goes on, the soul will dissipate or be received by the gods, there was a Sheng Ming Church priest here who would use a divine spell to purify the scene after the execution.

Lan Lu’s soul could not be taken by Zi Di and Cang Xu.

The city guard commander who was escorting Lan Lu onto the execution platform suddenly lifted his foot and kicked Lan Lu’s bent leg.

Caught off guard, Lan Lu kneeled down.

The city guard commander used that opportunity to push his head down.

Lan Lu, realizing that his time of death was just around the corner, struggled with all of his might.

But he didn’t have a shred of strength to struggle, and even his mournful roar was tired and feeble.

He was seriously injured and had been tortured and punished by Tan Qiu’s subordinates. To prepare for this execution, he had also been drugged, sapping both his bodily strength and battle qi.

“A futile struggle.” Tan Qiu sneered as he stood up from his seat.

With a grave face, he shouted, “Lan Lu!”

“You assaulted my waters, looted my tax collector ships, and slaughtered innocent islanders. Your crimes are unforgiveable.”

“Now, in the name of the great Emperor, the unifier of the Sheng Ming Continent, and the supreme God, Heavenly Emperor Sheng Ming, I, Feudal Lord of Snake Rat Island, Tan Qiu of the Tan Tu Clan, hereto sentence you to death!”

“Execute him!”

“Tan Qiu’s speech was direct and efficient, and after he was finished, the entire audience cheered.

“Put him to death!”

“His punishment fits his crimes, this is how all pirates should end.”

Long live Tan Qiu, long live the feudal lord!”

Tan Qiu, still solemn, slowly sat down.

But his heart bitterly laughed.

He wanted to give a lengthy speech to increase his renown, however, the leading role did not belong to him today, rather it belonged to the priest.

The execution only whetted the appetite, while the proclamation of the established alliance that would crusade against Rou Zang’s pirate group was the main course.

Impossible, for the priest was the greatest investor into this.

The one to carry out the sentence was the city guard commander.

As the people cheered, he took out his sword.

This silver level magic weapon had the magical sapphire light armor spell attached to it, and was also very sharp.

Lan Lu was a silver level, and an ogre’s body far outstripped a human one, thus ordinary headsmen axes would find it difficult to behead him. Therefore, the appropriate executioner was the city guard commander.

Without any hesitation, the city guard commander aimed at Lan Lu’s neck and swung his sharp sword down.

“Oh dear!” Mo Kong had been surveilling everything, and when he saw this, he had no choice but to act.

A moment later, a long prepared spell accurately struck the city guard commander.

A red light flashed.

A split second later, the scene before the commander changed drastically.

Lan Lu and the execution platform disappeared, replaced by a gigantic dragon head that opened its bloody mouth wide towards him.

The commander was alarmed, and promptly took a large step back, taking out his shield to protect himself as best as possible.

Mo Kong’s magic was effective, and to the others, it seemed the city guard commander suddenly stopped his blade, jumped back, then braced with his shield, like a tortoise withdrawing his head, it was both abrupt and comical.

Tan Qiu immediately stood up.

The others sitting also got up, all of them on guard.

“Magic!”

“Not only is there a fear spell, but there is also an illusion spell…composite magic!”

“Enemies!”

“Track down the caster! They have meticulously restrained their aura.”

The priest and the others rapidly communicated.

Bang bang bang…

Another change occurred.

Many explosions occurred throughout the town simultaneously, warehouses and houses were set aflame, causing thick smoke to billow out.

Space violently fluctuated, and imps chittered and shouted as they suddenly appeared everywhere in town. They attacked in all directions and wantonly destroyed everything.

Many imps threw themselves into the square, quickly killing many commoners.

The crow sank into chaos.

“A devil attack?”

“Someone used summoning magic?”

“Don’t panic, stay calm everyone!”

Tan Qiu shouted, “What are you waiting for, city guard attack!”

But a moment later, the feudal lord hesitated, he didn’t get the customary response.

He stared out again, and noticed that the city guard commander was still trapped in the illusion, he was bracing his shield and huddling his body as if he was withstanding a strong attack.

“Lord priest, lend me your help!” Tan Qiu looked at the priest.

The priest had been praying, and when it finished at that moment, he immediately used a divine spell.

The divine spell’s radiance illuminated the city guard commander’s body, dispelling the magic on him.

The city guard commander shook his head, he had completely sobered up.

“Ah, what?!” He realized the truth, and was red with shame and anger.

“You wretch!” The city guard commander lifted his sword again, and to vent his feelings, he cut down at Lan Lu.

But Lan Lu’s eyes were red, and his muscles bulged, he had actually struggled free of his chains.

He raised his head and roared and lost all reason as he brandished his fists and smashed everything around him.

The city guard commander summoned his battle qi and raised his shield as he was knocked away by Lan Lu’s fist.

“He has buffing magic on him!”

“Who cast the spell?”

“Find the caster!”

Snake Rat Island’s higher ups were incessantly enraged.

Generally, a magician will leak their aura when casting, however, Mo Kong’s three actions had been superbly restrained.

Aura only leaked the moment the magic was cast. This made it difficult for others to discover Mo Kong’s true position.

Lan Lu had received buffing magic, and his physical strength had greatly increased, his previous powerlessness swept away.

The ogre race had very strong bodies that greatly surpassed humans of the same level. He pulverized the execution platform and stormed into the crowd.

Chapter 278: Devil Ambush

Like a reckless ox, Lan Lu charged into the crowd.

He crashed into many people, tossing them into the air, the horrifying crunch of breaking bones and dying screams lingering afterwards.

“Run away.”

“Don’t step, don’t step on me!”

“Save me, save me!”

The frenzied crowd scuttled and fled, and in the chaos, trampled each other.

Space fluctuated again.

This time, it wasn’t an imp, but rather a needle demon!

The needle demons were red or dark green. They were humanoid shaped, tall, had a pair of malevolently sharp claws, and slender and nimble tails. The most dreadful thing about them were the sharp thorns growing all over their bodies, these thorns served as both their offense and defense.

The needle demons caused an even greater massacre.

These demons moved swiftly and agilely, jumping onto roofs and across walls. When they leapt into the crowd, they were bound to set off a reign of carnage.

Tan Qiu and the priest went white.

The imps were bad, but the needle demons had instantly worsened the situation further.

For a moment, the entire town sank into an abyss of suffering.

Tan Qiu immediately gave an order to the city guard commander, “Don’t tangle with Lan Lu, command the city guard and kill all of those demons!”

It was only natural that the town and its people were more important than Lan Lu.

They were Tan Qiu’s riches!

The city guard commander also knew the situation was anything but reassuring, thus he promptly abandoned Lan Lu and instead led the city guard into where the most demons had gathered.

“Everyone, the situation is urgent, act with haste! I will reward you.” Tan Qiu said to the dragonman youngster, Hei Fei, and the rest.

Everyone nodded and entered the fray.

Zong Ge rushed through the crowd in a straight line, and whatever was in his way, regardless of whether it was imp or human, was left a bloody pulp.

A dark green needle demon suddenly jumped from above, and aimed its sharp claws at Zong Ge’s eyes.

Zong Ge unsheathed his broadsword.

The broadsword slashed the air with a silver flash, bisecting the needle demon into two.

Not far off, Hei Fei climbed onto a balcony.

The balcony had a few murdered ladies on it, with imps gnawing on their flesh.

Hei Fei swung his cutlass and beheaded many of the imps.

“Night Devil Hunting Group, assemble with me!” The Night Devil Hunting Commander prioritized rallying his subordinates.

They were well coordinated as they directly crossed blades with a group of imps.

The imps quickly found they were no match and scattered away.

The Night Devil Commander sneered, although a gold tide snake had caused his hunting group disastrous casualties, the ones that remained were more elite as a result.

A needle demon trampled onto a wall, then swooped down at Ba Hu.

Ba Hu, with a war hammer in both hands, stood ready.

He heaved his war hammer and blocked the needle demon’s claw.

The needle demon’s attack was obstructed, but that only caused it to laugh sinisterly.

A moment later, another needle demon emerged from under him, its sharp needles flying directly at Ba Hu’s back.

Ba Hu was startled and mad as he urged his battle qi.

A battle skill was used, and in a split second, his defense had been greatly augmented, allowing him to firmly withstand the needle demons’ pincer attack.

“If I kill you, who knows how many bounties I’ll get?” Ba Hu sneered, his battle fervor unafraid.

Tan Qiu stood on the high platform and didn’t act. Instead, he ran his eyes across the battlefield, battle-ready.

By his side stood the Sheng Ming Church priest, as well as the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance magician.

Around them was a small squad of orderly templar knights.

The magician had just used a detection type magic, her eyes were tightly closed, and her magical aura seeped all over the place.

The priest was waiting for an opportunity and didn’t act.

The magician suddenly spoke, “Currently, I have detected 33 iron level needle demons, 124 bronze level needle demons, 47 bronze level imps, and a combined total of 368 ordinary needle and imps.”

Imps were the lowest rank of devil. Needle demons were also low ranked, but a step above the imps.

Even so, the sudden appearance of these demons had brought about the perfect ambush that ravaged Snake Rat Island.

Tan Qiu’s complexion grew more unsightly.

The longer the situation went on, the greater the damage he would suffer.

Snake Rat Island had been painstakingly built by him; a place he had spent his heart’s blood and energy to realize.

Now, he was witnessing his work being destroyed, and his heart burned with both anxiety and anger.

But he still retained his rationality: calm down! Calm down!

“What is the other party’s aim?”

“The execution ground? Saving Lan Lu?”

“Is this the vanguard assault of Rou Zang’s pirate group?”

Tan Qiu did not understand!

If he were Rou Zang, there was no way he would rescue someone he barely had a connection with like Lan Lu.

It also was unlikely he had decided to assault Snake Rat Island.

Snake Rat Island had both an army and a strong navy. There were many defensive methods here, and if Rou Zang wanted to attack this place, it was tantamount to abandoning his greatest advantage while attacking the enemy’s greatest strength. One had to suffer brain damage to do this.

“If he wanted to attack this place, wouldn’t have done so while I was building the island up? There was no reason he needed to wait until now?”

Regardless of how he thought about it, if the enemy’s intentions were unknown, Tan Qiu wouldn’t engage in the battle. This included his most secret underling, the silver female thief, he wouldn’t order her to move either.

It was a hidden trump card, one he still wanted to preserve for later.

Space once again fluctuated.

This time, three devils appeared.

These devils were sinister and fiendish, their two eyes were scarlet, their two long horns extended from their foreheads, and they had scarlet long tongues and jagged sharp teeth. From their chins grew thorny whiskers that hooked into beards.

They had purple, red, or even green skin, large tails, and had long, sawtooth machetes in their hands. Each of them was at least 1.8 meters tall, and were each a ferocious figure.

Hook devils!

There were only a few hook devils, only 5 in total, but all of them were silver level.

Tan Qiu’s and the priest’s expressions couldn’t help but change dramatically.

This was the world of the strong.

When the silver level devils appeared, their battle strength fundamentally changed everything!

The hook devils immediately attacked Hei Fei, Ba Hu, and the others, while simultaneously mustering a large amount of imps and needle demons to their side to form small battle groups.

Devils were different from demons, devils were more orderly and understood how to work as a team.

“Have you still not found the caster?” Tan Qiu couldn’t help but raise his tone as he urged the merchant alliance magician.

The merchant alliance magician’s forehead was covered with cold sweat, and her eyes were gloomy, “I can’t find them!”

“But if I were to guess who it is, this should be Mo Kong’s work.”

“He is Rou Zang’s most useful underling, and is Rou Zang’s first mate, second to only him.”

“According to intel, he has a devil bloodline. I didn’t expect that he would be this powerful!”

“But he is only a silver level, moreover, he is just a warlock. The devils present are likely the limits of his abilities.”

Tan Qiu couldn’t help but shake his head.

He had already arrived at the magician’s conjecture.

But what use was there in saying that now?

Finding Mo Kong was crucial.

“Until now, I still haven’t found a trace of Rou Zang’s pirate group.”

“We cannot find Mo Kong, who knows if there are other people we haven’t found either?”

When Tan Qiu thought that, the pressure on his mind increased greatly.

His hands and feet were tied by fear that Rou Zang would suddenly appear and ambush him.

The enemy is in the dark, while I am in the light. Thus, he had to protect the priest and magician well.

He was a battle qi cultivator and was the final line of defense for the priest and magician.

“Now, I can only hope that they are able to handle those devils.”

“I hope that the postwar damage will be less.”

Tan Qiu could only pray from the bottom of his heart.

Hei Fei, the Night Devil Commander, and the city guard commander…Tan Qiu ran his eyes over them when he involuntarily stopped to gaze at Shi Qi.

As a silver peak that was approaching gold, he naturally was expected to do more.

But as he watched from the sidelines for a moment, he noticed that the one who did the most damage was not Shi Qi, but…Long Fu!

“You won’t escape!”

The dragonman youngster pursued a needle demon.

The needle demon was coughing up blood incessantly, but it was still moving nimbly. His legs and claws were quickly ascending a wall.

The dragonman youngster pursued closely behind, he was burning with battle fervor.

A moment later, he jumped onto the wall.

Ding.

With a light echo, an explosion detonated under the soles of his feet, corresponding with his bent knees jumping, giving his body more speed as he jumped the wall blocking him.

Ding.

With another small explosion under the soles of his feet, he was able to return back over the wall he had jumped over.

After jumping between two walls several times, he leaped onto a roof.

Battle skill – Surge!

In this sudden battlefield, the dragonman youngster’s arduous training of this battle skill had produced a joyous result.

The dragonman youngster had mastered this battle skill thoroughly, and its intensity always followed his intentions, thus he was capable of accurately controlling its might every time.

Before this, he could ascend up walls and jump onto roofs, but without precise control, if it was too powerful, he would have damaged walls, and more importantly, used excessive amounts of battle qi.

The dragonman youngster used the battle skill, Surge, easily and precisely to his satisfaction.

He jumped into the air, then an explosion detonated behind him, accelerating his speed.

The needle demon couldn’t evade the pouncing dragonman from above.

The thorns the needle demon was covered in were crushed by the red dragon scales, it didn’t harm the dragonman youngster even a little.

The young man grabbed the needle demon’s head with his dragon claws.

A moment later, a large explosion banged from his palms.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist!

The needle demon’s head burst, leaving it a headless corpse.

Before, the dragonman youngster could only use this battle skill when his hands were clenched into a fist.

Now, his degree of control allowed him to use it with hands in any gesture.

After killing the needle demon, the dragonman youngster straightened his back on the roof, then swept his eyes across the battlefield.

The battlefield was in chaos, although he had eliminated all of the demons around him, in other areas, there were still many others.

Especially in the southeast, that was where two hook devils were leading a group of needle demons and imps to encircle the Night Devil Hunting Group.

This was the biggest battlefield.

The Night Devil Hunting Group was losing ground!

“Abominable, if it weren’t for the gold tide snake, we wouldn’t have so few people, and be surrounded by these demons!” The hunting group commander gnashed his teeth.

He was holding back a hook devil, however, the other silver level hook devil was attacking the front of the hunting group’s defensive line.

Hook devil machetes fluttered everywhere.

The saw teeth could easily pierce leather armor, and whenever the blades flashed by, they usually left severed limbs and splattered blood.

Several iron shield guards were already soaked in blood.

The wounds the serrated blades left caused them to bleed profusely, treatment scrolls and medicine only brought a slight relief, they couldn’t patch them permanently.

“What do we do?” The hunting group commander felt helpless and directionless when he suddenly heard an explosion.

“Who fired that?” This was the hunting group commander’s first reaction.

Chapter 279: Gold Level Devil

“Did his Lordship do a shelling over yonder? That is where the commonfolk are!” The remaining hunters were bewildered.

But a moment later, they saw a red silhouette dropping from the sky, and like an artillery shell, ferociously blasted into the heart of the group of demons.

Boom!!

Battle skill – Demolition Fist!!

The dragonman youngster’s total Bursting Explosion battle qi instantly diminished by a tenth.

The needle demon that directly met the fist exploded into dregs of flesh.

Soon after, the shockwave of the attack threw the surrounding imps into the air.

The attack cratered the ground, causing earth, gravel, and demon bits to splatter all over the place.

The demon formation was shattered, and the front was abruptly cleared.

Among the severed limbs and broken bodies, the dragonman youngster slowly stood up from his kneeling position, unclenched his fist, and turned to regard the battlefield, his dragon tail vigorously swinging to and fro.

The demons were horrified, and for a moment, none dared to encircle him as their morale plummeted.

“Commander Long Fu!” The Night Devil Commander cheered, then promptly tangled again with the enemy before them.

Upon seeing the dragonman youngster, a silver level hook devil who had originally planned to burst through the Night Devil Hunting Group’s defensive line, turned around and charged at him.

The dragonman youngster coldly snorted, and did not evade nor flee, instead he charged too.

The two collided midair.

The hook devil struck dragon scales with its serrated blade, creating a showing of sparks.

The dragonman youngster’s dragon claws tore the hook devil’s skin, then in his palm, a silver radiance erupted once more.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist!

A chariot seemed to strike the hook devil, causing it to fly through a collapsed wall and sink into a building.

The building then collapsed, causing a large amount of smoke and dust to poof out.

The smoke and dust obstructed the eyes.

The dragonman youngster was just about to press the attack, when suddenly, he sensed a magic fluctuation emanating from the ruined building.

Racial magic – High-level Teleportation!

“Where did it teleport to?!” The dragonman youngster stared, then turned his head around towards the execution platform.

“Defend!” Tan Qiu screamed as he moved forward.

A moment ago, 3 hook devils had suddenly teleported around them.

Mo Kong had been secretly following the entire battlefield.

When he saw the dragonman youngster weaving his way all over the place and putting down the largest group of demons, he had no choice but to respond.

Following his orders, the hook devils quickly teleported to the most important place, the rear lines where Tan Qiu was.

If either the priest or any magician died, it would be an immense blow.

If all of them died, the pressure on Rou Zang’s pirate group would greatly dimmish.

“Protect the magician!”

“Return to defend.”

“Protect the priest!”

The dragonman youngster, Zong Ge, Hei Fei, Ba Hu, and the other silver levels understood that this was the tipping point of the battle, and immediately withdrew back.

The Night Devil Commander and a hook devil were still tangling with each other.

The city guard commander wanted to return to help, but he was engaged with Lan Lu.

Fortunately, the priest who had been staying his hand, immediately used a divine spell.

Pure white radiance illuminated the small templar knight squad, increasing their offense and defense sharply.

The small team was in itself a battle squad, and worked together very closely, allowing them to barely hold back the three hook devils’ surprise attack.

But it wouldn’t last.

After all, the hook devils were silver levels.

In their first clash, a templar knight was sawed by a serrated blade, causing him to bleed profusely.

Racial magic – Summon Demons!

The three hook devils had noticed Long Fu, Shi Qi, and the others coming back to help, thus they used this racial magic at the same time.

Space violently fluctuated, and imps and needle demons appeared from nowhere.

The hook devils howled, and the newly summoned imps and needle demons charged at the silver warrior who had come to help.

Divine spell – Radiant Chains!

The priest coldly snorted, and raised his hands towards the sky.

Brilliant light fell from the sky, and irradiated the three hook devils, then rapidly condensed into light chains that locked around the hook devils’ hands and feet.

The hook devils did their best to struggle free, however, this resulted in delaying the tempo of their offensive.

The dragonman youngster took the lead to breakthrough the tangle, and like an artillery shell being fired, slammed into a hook devil.

Shi Qi, Hei Fei, and the others followed right behind him.

Tan Qiu let out a deep sigh of relief.

The devil’s surprise attack had mostly crumbled, and the two remaining hook devils would find it difficult to menace them.

In the depths of the castle, when Mo Kong saw this, he couldn’t help but sigh, “I should withdraw.”

He looked at the magic formation beneath his feet, his eyes flashing with pain.

Arranged in the center of the magic formation was a dark red devil heart, a pair of steel goat horns, and a lotus flower shaped pile of sulfur, these were all gold level casting materials!

Mo Kong took in a light breath, then began to activate the magic formation.

Suddenly above the town, a purple and red pentagram formation appeared.

The formation was rotating, and black smoke curled around it.

The black smoke was devoured by the formation soon after, and red light began to bubble forth from its depths.

A formidable life aura began to emanate.

The black smoke dissipated, and a tall devil appeared from nowhere.

He was 2.7 meters tall, almost as high as a three story building, and had a sturdy body.

His skin was dark red, he had pitch black demon horns towering from his head, a pair of wide bad wings grew from his back, and he had a tail that hung to the ground.

In his hand, he held a long spiked chain, and when his thick legs hit the ground, it created a soft echo, revealing how heavy his body was.

“Awoo!” He raised his head and howled as his life aura erupted like a gigantic wave.

Horn devil, gold level!

“How can this be?” The priest was shocked, “Is this really Mo Kong’s doing? He actually summoned a gold level horn devil?”

“Where is the caster?” The priest urged the female magician.

The female magician’s eyes were still closed, she had been constantly investigating, however, from beginning to end, she couldn’t make any progress.

“Damm it!” Tan Qiu went pale; the gold level devil had put great pressure on him.

“Where is Lord Guang Ke?” He unconsciously turned around to look at the castle.

But there was no sign of Guang Ke.

“It won’t be that easy to struggle free from my nightmare spell.” Mo Kong sneered, how could he leave out his most powerful opponent, Guang Ke?

“A gold level horn devil…” Cang Xu’s eyes glistened with awe.

Neither he nor Zi Di had acted.

They had the same misgivings as Tan Qiu; they also couldn’t grasp what Rou Zang was planning.

With the enemy in the dark and them in the light, if they act recklessly, they would definitely be targeted.

Magicians had a large impact, and often during war, could overturn entire battles.

Despite the heavy defenses around them, the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance magician and the priest were still raided by devils.

Many devils could teleport, thus Zi Di and Cang Xu didn’t act blindly without thinking.

“Look there, Lan Lu has nearly escaped from the public square.” Zi Di suddenly said.

In the chaotic battlefield, the commoners were in disarray while the higherups had their hands full.

Zi Di was very interested in Lan Lu’s soul, and had kept a close eye on him.

Cang Xi and Zi Di quickly glanced at each other, then quietly descended the tower.

The gold level horn devil attacked Tan Qiu, the priest, and the others.

Among them were a priest and a magician, the biggest targets!
