The stone pillars made up a stone forest, thus this was where Stone Forest Island got its name from.

“That is the island’s most important resource, those stone pillars contain Brain Pebbles.”

“If those white pebbles are of high quality, they will contain red veiny lines and look like a person’s brain.”

“Brain Pebbles are an alchemy material with many uses. The material can range from bronze level to silver level quality.” Zi Di introduced.

Before the lookouts, the survivors had used the Deep Sea Monster Fish to scout out Stone Forest Island.

A group of birds flew out, some of them were holding a rat in their feet.

It was the scout birds and scout rats.

These alchemy puppets dispersed into Stone Forest Island’s skies, those with rats first dropped them off, before then dancing in the breeze in all directions.

Many sights were subsequently transmitted to the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The survivors saw a desolated village.

Fires were still burning.

The village was already in ruins.

There were traces of the pirate’s attack everywhere.

There was not a trace of anyone.

“There must be survivors on the island. They were the ones who informed Snake Rat Island of this.” Zong Ge’s brows pursed, “We must be careful, these battlefield traces are somewhat odd.”

Zong Ge couldn’t say why it felt so odd.

It was only a feeling.

The dragonman youngster ordered the ships to slow.

The six ships circled around Stone Forest Island and cautiously observed it from a distance.

After a half hour, the Deep Sea Monster Fish finally detected human life.

The surviving villagers were hiding in a cave.

The cave was in a remote location, it was under a cliff that hung over the ocean.

The scouts couldn’t communicate and were unable to speak with the villagers.

“We need to make contact with them.” Said Cang Xu, “Merely observing won’t be enough for Tan Qiu and the others.””

Zong Ge added on, “Currently, we don’t detect any signs of Rou Zang’s pirate group, but that does not exclude a potential ambush on the island. We only need to dispatch a single person to acquire information from the villagers.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, “Which person do you recommend?”

A short while later.

Upon receiving the order, Xu Ma confronted Lan Zao and pointed a finger at him with glaring eyes, “I’m going ashore alone?!”

“Correct.” Said Lan Zao, “You will need to get more information from those villagers. Based on current observations, Rou Zang’s pirate group is not here.”

Xu Ma went white, and he anxiously replied, “But I heard that the Bath Tub can turn invisible. If by any chance they are lying in ambush, and we didn’t notice it…”

Lan Zao was solemn, “It is because of this that we dispatch you alone.”

“You cultivate the Spider Walking battle qi technique, you are agile and nimble, thus you are the most appropriate choice for this.”

Xu Ma was dumbstruck.

Lan Zao encouraged, “This is a good chance to distinguish yourself. According to our mercenary corps’ rules, after this mission is completed, you will receive a considerable contribution for it.”

To the side, Bai Ya casted an admiring gaze, “Prove yourself Senior Xu Ma, the commanders have already taken note of your abilities, this will add onto that!”

Xu Ma wanted to weep dry tears as he had no choice but to bitterly sail a boat to the island.

Using the map Lan Zao had given him, he smoothly entered the cave and went to meet with the villagers.

“Someone came!” The villagers shouted, they had already noticed the birds circling in the air and the fleeting moving around the island.

Xu Ma promptly shouted, “Don’t shoot, I am from Snake Rat Island, and under Lord Tan Qiu’s orders, have come to investigate this place!”

The villagers looked at each other, and hesitated.

As he was an iron level fighter, the village chief stood out, he gave an order to let him pass.

Xu Ma was received by the villagers.

Although it was ordinary dishes, there was wine too.

The village chief and villagers accompanying him spoke of the attack. They bitterly grumbled how brutal Rou Zang’s pirate group was and how joyful and fearful they were to escape.

Xu Ma gradually relaxed.

He figured it out.

Currently it seems that everything was normal, and that the likelihood of Rou Zang laying an ambush was very small.

Even if there was an ambush, the main force hadn’t come ashore yet, and he alone was too small of a target, unworthy of being attacked by Rou Zang’s pirate group.

The bird scouts were circling around the cave’s roof, and one was sitting on Xu Ma’s shoulder.

Some rat scouts had overtly sat down, while others scurried into the villagers’ refuge and provided the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps with first-hand intelligence.

The dragonman youngster and the others compared the information with the ocean map.

“Based on what the villagers said, after Rou Zang’s pirate group raided the island, they snatched all of the Brain Pebbles. When the Bath Tub left Stone Forest Island, it was headed north.”

“If it is in that direction, then the islands it will pass will be…” The dragonman youngster’s finger crossed the map.

Just as he said that a magic communication device suddenly flashed with purple light.

An urgent military intelligence transmission can come through.

The alliance had already been dispatched; they were pursuing the Bath Tub and requested that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps do their hardest to intercept and stop it!

The information was detailed and explained everything.

Rou Zang and Mo Kong were imprisoned, and the Bath Tub had fallen into Gu Qiao’s control. Swordsman Xi Cao had been forced by Mo Kong to risk his life to ask Snake Rat Island for help.

Who would have thought that a part of Rou Zang’s pirate group would ask their enemies for help?

But in these waters, only the alliance had the ability and motivation to deal with the Bath Tub.

Of course, Mo Kong did not hope the alliance would rescue him, he hoped to create chaos! After all, not long ago, he had caused havoc on Snake Rat Island.

The Flower Wall and Grass Head Pirates could not threaten the gold level martial monk, Gu Qiao.

After the alliance leaders discussed, they chose to believe Swordsman Xi Cao and dispatched all of their troops.

With Swordsman Xi Cao’s information, they easily found the Bath Tub.

A chase hereby unfolded.

Gu Qiao planned to escort Rou Zang back to the Great Pain Temple.

He was headed towards Stone Forest Island.

Knowing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ position, the alliance deliberately chased the Bath Tub towards Stone Forest Island.

Since the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had arrived at Stone Forest Island beforehand, due to unexpected events and deliberate action, they had become an ingenious ambush.

“If that is how things are, then who attacked Stone Forest Island?”

“Now is not the time to think about that, should we take action or not?”

“If we cower at this time, it will be too obvious, we might even be treated as spies for Rou Zang’s pirate group?”

“We can attack!” Zong Ge’s battle intent rose, “We are already prepared for war, and in peak condition.”

“Rou Zang and Gu Qiao have already clashed, both sides will be worn down.”

“Mo Kong is imprisoned.”

“On our side, we have Guang Ke, Tan Qiu, the priest, the magician, and many other strong people.”

“Our chance of winning this battle is quite high.” Zong Ge suggested that they make war.

We will be rewarded handsomely for eradicating Rou Zang’s pirate group. I can greatly alleviate our financial woes, and most of all, it isn’t that risky.” Zi Di mulled.

Cang Xu expressed his opinion, “If we get cold feet, I fear our newly established Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps will become useless. Tan Qiu and the others won’t let us off and our mercenary corps won’t be able to get a foothold in the empire. We will need to change our appearance to continue operating.”

“If we act without endangering ourselves, even if we don’t stop the Bath Tub, it will be approved. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ spotty history can perhaps be solved by Tan Qiu.”

“Then we make war.” The dragonman youngster’s gaze was as sharp as a blade, “This is a righteous battle to root out the malignant tumor that is Rou Zang and shall benefit these waters. Ah, Justice God, please look after me.”

The six ships turned around and sailed away.

When the villagers saw this, they immediately reported it.

The still drinking Xu Ma was flabbergasted: what was going on? Why were they sailing away? I’m still on the island!

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps quickly blockaded the Bath Tub’s path.

Behind the Bath Tub was the alliance’s main force.

When the martial monk, Gu Qiao, who was standing on the ship’s highest floor, saw that he had no way to escape, his face was as gloomy as water, “Feudal Lord Tan Qiu, do you really want to be my enemy? Rou Zang will be escorted away by me and be imprisoned in the temple for the rest of his life. War is dangerous, why should you risk it?”

Tan Qiu sneered wordlessly, hatred colored his entire face.

If Rou Zang didn’t die, how could he be at ease? How could he wipe away his disgrace?

Guang Ke coldly shouted, “Punishment is upholding justice. Wickedness must be thoroughly rooted out! The only punishment for Rou Zang’s sins is death.”

The enemy is weak, while I am strong. At this point, the alliance was like an arrow leaving its bow, how could words stop them?

Gu Qiao sighed deeply, then malevolence spread across his face, “If you won’t listen to advice and seek death at your own accord, then I shall help you all!”

Chapter 287: Snake Rat Naval Battle (1)

The sea breeze was slowly brushing past, the sky was azure, however, the ocean surface had an austere ambience.

A confrontation between ships was imminent.

If one was watching from above, they would see the alliance was divided into two parts.

The 38 ships led by Guang Ke, Tan Qiu, and the priest were in the southwest. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ 6 ships and the Deep Sea Monster Fish hidden on the seafloor were in the northeast.

On the other side was Rou Zang’s Bath Tub. Aside from the flagship, there were also groups belonging to Rou Zang that had 21 pirate ships in total. This fleet was being trailed by the Flower Wall Pirates. This pirate group had merged with Xi Cao’s men and ships and had 19 ships in total.

These two pirate groups were in the middle.

Rou Zang had been captured by Gu Qiao, the rest of Rou Zang’s pirate group lacked the will to fight, and Gu Qiao just wanted to return to the Sheng Ming Continent.

The Flower Wall Pirates were controlled by Mo Kong; he wanted them to attack the Bath Tub and rescue Mo Kong, Rou Zang, and the others.

Meanwhile, Tan Qiu and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wanted to encircle and annihilate Rou Zang’s pirate group.

Gu Qiao wanted to avoid conflict and was forced to negotiate.

But as a believer in the Justice God, Guang Ke couldn’t pardon Rou Zang.

Guang Ke flew into the air.

His body emitted a white radiance, and as he flew, a two meter long tail of light trailed behind him.

“That’s right, that’s Lord Guang Ke’s battle skill – Light Tail.”

“It is a flight battle skill!”

Tan Qiu and the others were inspired.

Flying battle skills were relatively rare, and were at least gold level.

A bronze level magician could fly or float, while a fighter, without equipment, needed to be gold level to be able to fly.

This was because gold level battle qi could form outside the body.

Having a flying battle skill often gave fighters the initiative.

Guang Ge flew directly above the Bath Tub.

Gu Qiao coldly snorted and gave the order to fire.

The Bath Tub was an unusual magic ship, it had devastating close quarters firepower while its long ranged battle capabilities were quite weak.

To make up for this flaw, the ship was outfitted with gunmen and archers.

Although these people complied with Gu Qiao’s command, and fired upon Guang Ke, their morale was low and some even deliberately slowed their movements.

In the air, Guang Ke aimed a long distance battle skill at the Bath Tub.

A ball of light shot into the Bath Tub, causing its hull to shake and creating a hole in a ship deck.

The Bath Tub could only take this thrashing passively.

Without options, Gu Qiao was forced to fly into the air too.

“He has a flying battle skill too!” Zi Di’s gaze tightened.

Cang Xu followed up, “The Great Pain Temple is one of the ten greatest temples on the Sheng Ming Continent, it isn’t strange that Gu Qiao has a flying battle skill.”

The two looked at each other, then decided to withdraw.

They were magicians of lowly rank, one was bronze, the other iron.

If their presence on the battlefield was too strong, it would cause Gu Qiao to kill them, a terrible fate.

A magician’s greatest fear was being ambushed by powerful fighters.

Of course, they had ways of preserving their lives. Regardless of whether it was the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge being a match for gold levels when acting together, the necromancy they controlled, or the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

But using these should be avoided if possible.

After all, they were not the protagonists of this naval battle, rather they were the higher ups of Snake Rat Island.

Tan Qiu and the priest were the ones who wanted to eradicate Rou Zang and his men the most.

No, to be more accurate, the protagonists of this naval battle were Gu Qiao and Guang Ke.

The outcome of their duel would decide the outcome of this naval battle.

This was the world of the strong.

In the air, Gu Qiao charged at Guang Ke, Guang Ke didn’t dodge away.

The two intertwined in the air, loud explosions echoed outwards from them.

Soon after, both sides turned around and charged at each other.

Gold battle qi surged through the air, but battle skills haven’t been used yet as both sides were probing each other.

Guang Ke didn’t want his fight with Gu Qiao to endanger Tan Qiu and the others; thus he decided to charge higher into the air.

Gu Qiao coldly snorted and pursued him unwaveringly.

“They’ve left.” Tan Qiu was watching from a telescope. When he saw the gold level fighters leave the battlefield, he immediately ordered, “Attack!”

The grand and mighty alliance charged at the pirate ships.

Rou Zang’s pirate ships lacked a leader, and their opinions differed.

“Should we make war?”

“The bald head said that as long as we reach the Sheng Ming Continent, he’d let us go.”

“If Tan Qiu and those guys capture us, we’ll be punished severely. We must flee!”

The pirates lacked the will to fight and sailed their ships towards the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

“Open fire!” Tan Qiu commanded.

The sailors had been prepared for a long while, and upon hearing that command, they immediately opened fire.

Bang, boom, bang…

White smoke wafted from the ships’ sides and bow.

Like a swarm of bees, several hundred artillery shells flew into the air, then descended, smashing into and around the Bath Tub.

Rou Zang’s pirate group was blown to bits.

However, these pirates were long familiar with naval battles and bombardment.

After the first round of shelling, the surviving pirates emerged from their shelters. Some were hanging sails, some were cleaning up the deck, and others were observing the situation.

Bang, boom, bang…

The pirates’ cannons were also firing.

But their primary target was not Tan Qiu and the others, rather it was the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

“Get down!!” The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had trained for this, and one after the other, hunkered down in bunkers or pressed up against the deck, both hands protecting their heads.

Hundreds of artillery shells hit the hulls, and many more pounded the ocean around, lifting white plumes of waves.

In the captain’s room, the dragonman youngster didn’t bat an eye as he observed the battlefield.

His detection equipment was not only a telescope, but also the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s reliably powerful means.

Not only that, but there were also alchemy puppet scouting birds flying and hovering in the air.

All kinds of information were constantly being compiled by him.

Reports on how damaging the bombardment was had been quickly delivered.

The first round of shelling had equally damaged the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ six ships, but the damage was minor. The most serious one was that a shell had broken through a hull, causing three casualties.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had been on their guard.

They knew they had been tasked with barring the way, and since they had fewer ships, it had been almost certain that Rou Zang’s pirate group would charge towards them.

Thus, the six ships were comparatively scattered.

The dragonman youngster didn’t act blindly or thoughtlessly, he was waiting for Zong Ge’s orders.

The dragonman youngster was unfamiliar with naval combat, but the half beastman was a versatile military man.

This was the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ first fight, so the dragonman youngster oversaw the general situation while Zong Ge was responsible for conducting the battle on the front lines.

Zong Ge’s eyes flashed fiercely, “The pirates are already panicking.”

If it was him, at that distance, he wouldn’t have given the order to open fire.

Too far a distance meant that accuracy was low.

Only when Rou Zang’s pirate group had gotten closer did Zong Ge unhurriedly give the order, “Return fire.”

The ensign waved the flag, and alchemy devices communicated it.

Flags and other signals were sent out simultaneously.

Bang, boom, bang…

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps counterattacked.

The bombardment was far more effective than the one Rou Zang’s pirate group did.

Some pirates were blown to bits, and even a pirate ship’s mast was splintered to pieces.

Zong Ge did not show delight, his expression was unperturbed as he assessed, “A passable shelling. We don’t have enough cannons, we need to get more. More crucially, we lack true gunners. The gold level cannon cannot display its real impact.”

This was all the result of bitter training.

If it had been the people from before, then perhaps the bombardment would instead make the enemy laugh heartily and grow relaxed.

“Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!” A pirate gnashed their teeth.

“They were the ones who wounded Captain Rou Zang. Otherwise, how could the captain have lost to that bald head?”

“They have a gold level cannon. Where have they hidden it this time?” Many of the pirates thought worriedly.

Last time, Rou Zang had been struck by the gold level cannon.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had treacherously hidden the cannon inside a ship and caught Rou Zang off guard.

Currently, the gold level cannon hadn’t been spotted on any of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ six ships.

During the return cannon fire, it hadn’t been exposed.

“Don’t worry about that. Our pursuers are catching up!”

“Rush through, there are only six ships.”

“Even if there is a gold level cannon, there is only one!”

“Whoever gets hit by it is the unlucky one, hehehe.”

“Charge!”

The pirates laughed, and stuck out their chests as they charged into the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps without a second thought.

“These guys…” Zong Ge clenched his teeth, he despised pirates but also recognized reality clearly.

“Although we can fight them directly, that is unnecessary.”

If we press our luck and intercept them, our side will take disastrous casualties.

“Let’s proceed according to what we decided previously.”

Zong Ge transmitted his order shortly after.

The six ships slowly moved, sailing while bombarding Rou Zang’s pirate group, yet still blocking their path ahead.

“What is going on with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?!” When Tan Qiu saw this, he immediately became gloomy.

He whispered to the city guard commander at his side, “We took the pains to chase the pirates here. But the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is letting this opportunity slip by for no reason. If this continues, how can we catch up to the pirate ships?”

The ocean was very empty and wide.

The alliance couldn’t surround them from all directions.

Nor could they hem them in.

After all, there were only so many ships. If they dispersed in a large area, their formation would be flimsy, and it would be easy for an enemy to counterattack or break through.

The pirates could sail in every direction.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps needed to cut off these people as much as possible and tangle them up in battle.

Otherwise, if they maintained their distance, the pirates would have the space to maneuver.

It can be said that there was only one chance to entangle them.

Their ambush had already failed.

The pirates seized that opportunity to adjust their course, and they skillfully manipulated their ships to pierce their way through.

But the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps also adjusted their course and blocked their path ahead.

The pirates adjusted again yet still remained blocked.

“What’s going on?”

The pirates refused to believe this abnormality, yet every time they adjusted course, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was still intercepting their path forward.

“I didn’t expect that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was so skilled at sailing!” Even though the church priest couldn’t comprehend sailing, even he could see how good they were.

Tan Qiu’s face immediately turned from gloom to happiness, “Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps! Great job, transmit the order now, it’s time we chase down this pack of bilge rats!”

Chapter 288: Snake Rat Naval Battle (2)

The group commander, Zong Ge, was still expressionless.

He had painstakingly trained these sailors, and the thing he drilled them on the most was not in shelling or weaponry but rather sailing.

Because he knew that it would be the most important fundamentals for naval combat.

Only when their movement was entirely within their element, could they hold the initiative, and go onto expand their fleet battle formations.

However, what was being accomplished today was not just the result of bitter training.

The greatest thing assisting Zong Ge was information.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish and the scouting birds allowed Zong Ge to receive intel on every directional adjustment of the pirates as they did them.

Otherwise, if they were just relying on human eyes, they would easily overlook the initial adjustments, and thus waste time and be unable to respond in time.

The second reason was the shelling.

The closer both sides got, the more accurate the shelling was.

After Zong Ge’s training, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ bombardment level had reached standards, and put sufficiently heavy pressure on the pirate ships.

During every round of bombardment, the pirates had to lie down or hide in shelters.

The third reason was the gold level cannon.

It was precisely because this cannon was concealed that Rou Zang’s flagship, the Bath Tub, could not charge in wantonly, instead it was heavily protected by layers of pirate ships.

If it abandoned its protection, and rampaged about, it would be menaced by the gold level cannon.

The Bath Tub was only a silver level magic ship, it could not withstand a gold level bombardment.

Even if they lacked a gunner, Zong Ge could still use the gold level cannon to inhibit the Bath Tub.

Under normal circumstances, the magic ship would have already charged at the forefront, and did its best to make visible its master’s brutality.

Thus, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ constant obstruction pressured it too.

Through overt and covert pressure, the momentum of Rou Zang’s pirate group’s breakout was suppressed.

Soon after, the alliance, due to Tan Qiu’s repeated orders, did their best to catch up and reduce the distance between them.

The Flower Wall Pirates somewhat separated themselves from the fray and were now at the eastern fringes. They wanted to find an opportunity to save Rou Zang, but they unexpectedly turned into the alliance’s first target.

As the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps neared Stone Forest Island again, the alliance finally caught up to Rou Zang’s pirate group and the naval battle unfolded.

“The ships are back, they’ve come back!!” Xu Ma happily jumped in the sand.

Previously, when the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps received the urgent transmission, there hadn’t been enough time to pick him up, and they sailed away in an effort to intercept the pirates.

Xu Ma had lost his head in fear, and nearly wanted to die.

The scouts couldn’t communicate, therefore, Xu Ma didn’t know the truth.

To him it seemed that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had doubtlessly discovered that the Bath Tub was lying in ambush and decided to retreat, marooning him here.

Being marooned on this island was disastrous!

There might be other pirates here too.

But if he fell into the brutal hands of Rou Zang…

“I’m putting my small life in danger!” Xu Ma was in such a frenzy that he nearly acted.

When the iron level village chief of Stone Forest Island saw how queer Xu Ma was looking, he promptly advised him.

After some handwrought persuasion, Xu Ma calmed down.

“Hold on, they are in battle! Is there another ship?” Soon after, Xu Ma saw Rou Zang’s pirate group being chased by the alliance.

The two fleets were fighting in close combat on the sea, and the ships were losing speed as they maneuvered within a small area.

“Now then, you believe what this old man has said, Lord Xu Ma?” When the village chief saw this, he found it difficult to conceal his alarm, but he also didn’t forget to continue placating Xu Ma. “This place has not been trapped by Rou Zang. Rou Zang and his men are over there.”

“Whew.” When Xu Ma recalled how he had just behaved, he felt someone embarrassed.

“We’ll go back.” The village chief was deeply worried as he urged, “If by chance they land or a stray projectile hits this place, wouldn’t it be bad luck?”

“Correct, let’s go back first!” Xu Ma repeatedly nodded, and was willing to follow good advice.

An alliance ship’s hard bow bore down at a pirate ship.

The pirate ship’s helmsman shouted out from the confusion, “Rudder fully to the left!”

The pirate ship swerved out of danger, and the hull and the bow brushed past each other.

The two ships; huss rubbed against each other in wheezing scrapes, and the forces in play pushed the pirate ship back out.

But the pirates remained standing on the tilted deck like sturdy lumber.

The level of incline, compared to how it got during rough waters, was child’s play.

The alliance’s ships were generally larger than the pirate ships, but they were slower.

The pirate ships all focused on agility and speed.

This was mainly to make maritime plundering more convenient.

How could one speak of robbery if they couldn’t catch up?

The alliance ships were mainly used for patrolling, and since the two ship types had different functions, they performed differently.

Taking advantage of the near proximity of the ships, the alliance sailors tossed their throwing hooks onto the pirate ship and affixed them.

Some brave sailors raised cutlasses and swords, then jumped across the sea to fight the pirates in close quarters.

Pirate ship decks were generally lower to the sea than the alliance ship decks, thus it was easy for the alliance sailors to land on it.

However, the pirates responded likewise.

Many pirates grabbed ropes, swung through the air with howls, then landed onto the alliance ship’s deck.

The two sides grappled bitterly with each other.

For a moment, blood flowed everywhere, and limbs flew.

Casualties began to mount quickly and violently.

The alliance sailors had better equipment that conformed to them.

The pirates were more vicious and were experts at chaotic battles. The more chaotic a situation got, the more advantageous it was for them. They often cleverly used anything on a deck, like buckets, ropes, and the masts to help them fight.

On some ships the alliance was winning steadily and surely. On other ships, the pirates strutted about the beaten and broken alliance members.

The situation was deadlocked, and for a moment, neither side could secure the advantage.

“They really are Rou Zang’s pirates, ordinary pirates wouldn’t have such a vicious nature.” Captain Hei Fei lamented. He had seen many pirates that even upon facing death’s door, continued to insolently wave their weapons and never showed a trace of fear or timidness.

“The pirates that marshalled by Rou Zang’s side are just as savage.” The merchant alliance magician’s heart shivered.

The priest was concerned, “The other side’s formation is strong, and we cannot charge through.”

The city guard commander looked at Tan Qiu, “Feudal Lord, let us go out together.”

This was the world of the strong.

The gold levels were fighting high in the sky, and couldn’t attend to matters on the ocean.

The silver levels were the key to victory or defeat.

Now was certainly an opportune moment to enter the battle.

Especially when it came to silver levels, the pirates lacked them, while the alliance had many here.

But Tan Qiu hesitated, and after thinking about it, he shook his head and refused, “Let’s wait some more.”

Although Gu Qiao had been drawn away by Guang Ke, there was still Rou Zang.

Although he had been captured, wouldn’t the pirates on board go save him?

What condition was Rou Zang in?

Has he already been rescued and is now sheltering inside the Bath Tub?

If the alliance was dispatched out, would Rou Zang rush their magician and priest?

Even if he didn’t attack the magician and priest, he could be lying in wait within the ship hold.

Could the silver levels withstand the full strength of a gold level?

Tan Qiu didn’t want to take the risk.

Regardless of whether it was him or his subordinates.

The city guard commander was from his clan, and was a trusted aide.

It would be inconvenient for the silver level thief to leave her hiding place.

Regardless of which was lost, it would greatly damage his future administration of Snake Rat Island.

“If I was a gold level, I would charge straight in.” Tan Qiu sighed to himself.

When the city guard commander saw that Tan Qiu rejected his proposal, he couldn’t help but look anxious and disappointed.

Under the current circumstances, if the feudal lord did not take the lead in this assault, others wouldn’t charge in either.

After all, the one who benefited the most from this battle was the feudal lord, followed up by the priest.

The priest had already dedicated his resources as military capital. Furthermore, he was a spellcaster, and couldn’t charge in first, there wasn’t enough protection on the battlefield.

“What’s going on?” Meanwhile in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, Tripleblade pursed his brows, “Tan Qiu still hasn’t attacked yet?”

On another ship, Cang Xu was covertly watching, and upon analyzing Tan Qiu’s misgivings, laughed grimly, “Ah, the hearts of men.”

“Although the alliance is numerous, it has many problems.” Zong Ge sighed to himself, “The number of people is secondary, only with unity can elite troops be trained. Even if there are only a few people, they can achieve an astonishing battle result.”

This was also the reason why he didn’t think the survivors had good prospects.

The survivors had natural divisions, they had too many races among them, varied origins, complicated social statuses, different religions, and substantially different life experiences.

Thanks to Zong Ge’s leadership and prior training, the Dragon Lion Mercenary was able to watch from the sidelines.

If they got close to Rou Zang’s pirate group, they could entangle themselves with the latter. If that happened, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would be bearing the greatest attacks and be the ones facing the greatest risks.

Who knew what condition Rou Zang was really in?

If he had already been freed, it would be quite terrible.

There was no other way to say it, Zong Ge’s great contribution was quite magnificent!

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps waited for the main battlefield to be decided.

If victory was in sight, they would advance.

If the battlefield was turning sour, they could also retreat easily.

They could advance or retreat, and firmly held the initiative.

At the moment, there was another group in the same situation as the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

It was the Flower Wall Pirates.

They had been trailing behind Rou Zang’s pirate group and were waiting for an opportune moment to stage a rescue.

Swordsman Xi Cao’s operation had succeeded, and the Flower Wall Pirate commander, Hua Dun, had wily slipped away, he knew he wasn’t the first target and had evaded the alliance’s spear tip force.

Naturally, Tan Qiu didn’t want to get entangled with them.

If they let Rou Zang’s pirate group get away, and let the main target break out in a clean getaway, they would be imbeciles.

Therefore, the alliance immediately ensnared Rou Zang’s pirate group.

They didn’t attack the Flower Wall Pirates off to the side.

Right now, 38 of the alliance ships were fighting 21 of Rou Zang’s pirate ships, forming the main battlefield.

In the main battlefield, murderous shouts shook the heavens, and casualties on both sides were constantly mounting.

On the outskirts of this, two fleets were sailing about.

One was the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, they were located in the north. They only had six ships, but harbored a gold level cannon. It was precisely because of that that they were able to faintly threaten the Bath Tub, denying it from charging forth.

On the other side was the Flower Wall Pirates, they had 19 ships, a far greater number. This was because this pirate group had merged with the ships and men of Xi Cao’s pirate group.

Chapter 289: Snake Rat Naval Battle (3)

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps chose to keep watching on the sidelines.

They had a good excuse; they were hiding a gold level cannon that threatened the Bath Tub and suppressed it from escaping alone.

Tan Qiu couldn’t refute it.

As time passed, the engagement between the alliance forces and Rou Zang’s pirate group grew more intense.

It was like two strongmen fighting hand to hand.

Ships were constantly piling up, and the moveable space grew smaller.

In the beginning, there was still some coordination between ships, some were charging, some were covering, others were supporting, and some tried to outflank.

Gradually, both sides could only see the enemy before them and were locked in a bitter struggle.

As the Flower Wall Pirate boss, Hua Dun had been watching without relaxing even the slightest.

·

When he saw the two fleets merging with each other, he also saw that the Bath Tub’s eastern flank had thinned out, the majority of the ships that were there had been attracted to the battle to the south.

“An opportunity! Brothers, set sail, our target is the Bath Tub, let us rescue our commander!!” Hua Dun shouted.

“Ha hu!” The pirates yelled.

The Flower Wall Pirates were already restless, and like a pack of wolves emerging from the woods, they scurried towards the Bath Tub.

Rou Zang’s pirate group was already fighting the alliance forces with all of their strength; it was difficult for them to organize a force to deal with the Flower Wall Pirates.

In fact, they didn’t want to block them.

After all, they were on the same side.

Rescuing Rou Zang was also a good choice for them.

Although Rou Zang was brutal, he had led the pirates to victory and let them eat and drink big.

Therefore, when the Wall Flower Pirates rushed in, they met little resistance along the way.

Many ships even withdrew at their own accord to form a defensive line around them.

The Wall Flower Pirates quickly approached the Bath Tub.

When Tan Qiu detected this, he anxiously transmitted an order to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, “Long Fu, Shi Qi! Intercept them now.”

Zong Ge snorted and immediately passed his refusal through the alchemy device, “We are currently concerned with stopping the Bath Tub.”

Tan Qiu worriedly replied, “The Wall Flower Pirates are Rou Zang’s subordinates. If we allow these two groups to converge, they will bring about the alliance’s downfall. You’ll spoil the ship for a penny of tar if you let the worst outcome happen, Rou Zang escaping!”

The priest interjected, “Whatever your corps wants, I promise to double it!”

Zi Di was indifferent.

The main reason they had wanted to exchange for resources previously was to retrieve the Green Jade Gold Coffin.

With their main goal accomplished, if they allowed themselves to be dispatched, the risks were utterly not worth the profit.

Doing business at a loss won’t do!!

The dragonman youngster’s eyes were tranquil, and simply didn’t reply.

Although he wanted to carry out justice, and arrest Rou Zang, as commander, he needed to put his fellows first and didn’t want to send them into excessive danger.

Finally, Cang Xu sinisterly said, “Actually, if they rescue Rou Zang, that would be a good thing. At least that way we can sound out Rou Zang’s actual condition. If you are so worried about this, why haven’t you acted yourself yet?”

Tan Qiu and the priest looked embarrassed.

They wanted to conserve their strength and make the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps go first.

But the latter didn’t submit, and thrusted back a retort.

Tan Qiu and the priest were forced to drop it.

Guang Ke flew high in the air.

The gale assaulted his senses.

Faster and fiercer than the wind was Gu Qiao’s attack.

·

Battle skill – Iron Finger First Stage!

Gu Qiao ferociously shot out finger bones from his flesh.

Pew, pew, pew.

Like bullets, the finger bones wreathed in gold battle qi shot towards Guang Ke.

Guang Ke was quick and nimble, but the finger bones were faster.

He had already probed out this attack, and when facing it, the best choice was to block it directly.

Most of the Iron Fingers hit Guang Ke, some were stopped by the light armor covering Guang Ke’s body, and some pierced the light armor, embedding themselves in Guang Ke’s skin.

Gu Qiao had lost finger bones, but he quickly regrew them.

His bloodline and the Great Pain Temple’s bone path battle qi technique had formed an ingenious pair.

If any other temples used the battle skill, Iron Finger, it would be at most a close combat finger method.

Even if it was shot out, it would simply be formed from condensed gold battle qi.

Gu Qiao was actually using his own finger bones, greatly elevating his firepower.

“A worthy member of the Great Pain Temple.” Guang Ke had experienced how troublesome Gu Qiao was.

Guang Ke found it hard to protect himself from this move.

Gu Qiao’s flying battle skill was no weaker than his, and Gu Qiao could fly just as high as Guang Ke could.

Gu Qiao deeply gazed at Guang Ke.

“Guang Ke, this guy is very good at fighting.” His iron face was grave and stern, yet his heart exclaimed in admiration.

Guang Ge’s light armor battle skill was very practical and could be thickened or thinned.

Don’t be fooled by the fact that his finger bone penetrated the light armor and caused Guang Ke to bleed, they were minor injuries.

It was Guang Ke deliberately restraining the light armor’s thickness.

It wasn’t that he couldn’t protected himself from all of them, rather it was that it was disadvantageous to do so.

His manifested battle qi wasn’t attached to him, and would be worn down by the finger bones’ gold battle qi.

Therefore, he would rather receive some minor injuries instead.

In any case, when it came to gold level fighters, such small wounds were nothing, and wouldn’t affect their battle strength in the slightest.

“Something is wrong with Rou Zang…” Gu Qiao turned around to look at the naval battle.

He had maintained a watchful eye on the main battlefield.

Upon seeing the Flower Wall Pirates charging towards the Bath Tub, he immediately wanted to fly back.

Guang Ke’s eyes were already locked onto Gu Qiao, and when he saw the latter’s inclination, he immediately flew at him.

Battle skill – War Axe!

Guang Ke waved his leg, and the image of a battle-axe chopped down.

In the previous battle, it had been used at range, and even when it touched others, it merely bushed against them.

This was the first time he had actively used it at close range.

Gu Qiao was forced to use his full strength to block and inhibit it.

Battle skill – Castle Breaking Hammer!

Guang Ge’s battle qi condensed into a gigantic hammer that ruthlessly slammed down.

Gu Qiao couldn’t maintain his guard, and when the gigantic hammer struck him, he crashed into the sea like a cannonball.

Battle skill – Attuned Divine Arrow of Light.

Guang Ke raised his hands over his head.

An enormous white ball of light condensed in his palms.

The ball of light exploded with a bang, and split into myriad arrows of white light that burned with a harsh glint as they were all fired into the seawater.

The sea was instantly penetrated, causing it to spray and splash.

The light arrows struck the sinking Gu Qiao, and for a moment, he could only take the beating, powerless to retaliate.

The ocean surface gradually churned with scarlet blood.

The battle between the gold fighters was constantly affecting the mood of many.

Upon seeing this, the pirates’ morale took a substantial drop, while the alliance forces were roused greatly.

“Good, Guang Ke has firmly suppressed Gu Qiao. We can attack!” Tan Qiu shouted, finally making a firm resolution.

Hua Dun was approaching the Bath Tub, putting a strain on Tan Qiu’s nerves.

If Hua Dun succeeded in rescuing Rou Zang, the alliance would have to face a gold level alone, a terrible situation.

Tan Qiu ordered the city guard commander to attack, while he remained on the lead ship, saying that it was to protect their casters.

A moment later, the city guard commander, the Night Devil Commander, Ba Hu, and Hei Fei acted in succession, they all jumped out of their ships and boarded the pirate ones.

They were considerably strong.

Ordinary pirates could not stop them, and they easily killed them all.

The four silver fighters did not zealously continue fighting, their target was the Bath Tub.

They jumped from ship to ship and quickly climbed onto the Bath Tub’s deck.

But they had arrived a step too late.

The Flower Wall Pirates had already rushed in and were assisting in dealing with the alliance forces.

Hua Dun was already inside the hull.

The pirates had shown him the way and easily found Rou Zang and Mo Kong.

To his surprise, Mo Kong was locked up in a prison cell, his entire body was bound, his mouth was also gagged, his forehead had a mind restraining ring on it, and his waist had a magic prohibition ring around it.

On the contrary, Rou Zang hadn’t been treated this way, he had been placed under house arrest and was even free to walk around the ship hold.

Hua Dun first freed Mo Kong, he untied the rope, took out the gag, but was unable to do anything about the mind retraining or magic prohibition rings.

“Boss!” Hua Dun went to Rou Zang.

Rou Zang didn’t respond, his eyes were closed, his face was very red, and all of his fat was shivering.

“Don’t touch him. Gu Qiao sealed the boss, it seems to be one of the Great Pain Temple’s secret skills! After their fight, the boss has always been trying to break the seal.” Mo Kong said promptly.

A subordinate dashed in to report.

“What can be done?” After obtaining the latest intelligence, Hua Dun was worried, “Gu Qiao is being suppressed by Guang Ke, and the boss needs protection, four of the alliance’s silvers are coming!”

Mo Kong coldly snorted, “Give me a minute, I can struggle free from these two alchemy items. They can’t get in for a short time.”

“You know the architecture of the Bath Tub, to enter the hold, one must enter from the top, then descend floor by floor.”

“On each floor there are mechanisms and traps, along with guards.”

“Even if the silvers charge in, they will be covered in grime and even wounded.”

Hua Dun was not convinced and was still concerned, “If someone breaches the hull and comes in, what can we do?”

Mo Kong sneered, “Impossible. The Bath Tub is a silver level ship; it can resist the attacks of silver level fighters. Unless…”

As Mo Kong spoke, he suddenly changed color, and he gazed at a rat in a corner, his voice somewhat sharp, “Is that? Damn it, that is an investigation alchemy puppet.”

“What?” Hua Dun didn’t react immediately.

“Move now!!” Mo Kong was panic-stricken, and he nearly stumbled and crawled in his haste to escape.

Hua Dun sensed the horror from Mo Kong’s actions.

“Wait for me!” He caught up at once.

“Don’t follow me, scatter…” Mo Kong screeched.

Boom!

A moment later, an explosion as loud as thunder erupted.

The massive shockwave threw Mo Kong and Hua Dun into the air.

The artillery shell seemed to pierce the Bath Tub’s hull, tore the ship’s planks to shreds, then broke into the hold.

A terrible blaze seared the impact site and quickly spread.

The Bath Tub was massive, but upon being hit with this shell, it tilted greatly and nearly turned over.

The tilted hull gradually reasserted itself on the sea, and the resulting splash created a gigantic wave six meters tall.

Many pirates on deck howled in alarm as they fell into the water.

The city guard commander, Night Hunting Commander, and the other four that had been fighting felt their ears ringing and their legs trembling.

“What’s going on?” Ba Hu still looked a little sluggish.

“Who fired that cannon?!” Don’t they know we are on board?!” The city guard commander abruptly became irate, his voice furiously cursing.

Hei Fei was pale as he pointed at the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, “Who else can it be? Who else has a gold level cannon besides them?”

Chapter 290: Snake Rat Naval Battle (4)

Early on in the battle, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps dispatched scouting birds.

While all attention on both sides was focused on the main battlefield, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had quietly released scouting tide snakes and scouting rats.

The tide snakes and rats squeezed into the pirate ships and provided the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps with even more information.

They paid the most attention to the Bath Tub.

The scouting tide snakes would have done fine, but the scouting rats looked unremarkable and easily infiltrated the ship’s top floor.

Afterwards, they journeyed down and progressively penetrated the hold.

The pirates were all busy fighting, and were very tense. Even if they glimpsed the scurrying rats, they were in no mood to pay them any heed.

If Mo Kong was in good condition, then perhaps he would have immediately noticed them. But he had been heavily locked up and by the time he noticed them, the scouting rat had already sent reliable information back to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

After obtaining information of Rou Zang’s true situation, why would Zong Ge hesitate?

Without the slightest hesitation, he left and entered the gunnery position.

This time, the gold level cannon was hidden in his ship hold.

Zong Ge had been trained in weapons and was experienced in many types.

Cannons were not an exception.

Although he wasn’t a master at it, compared to others, he was far better.

He transferred his battle qi into it and imbued it with all of his strength.

The cannon slowly ignited, the formation around it restraining its aura.

If the Bath Tub had been sailing, perhaps it might have had the chance to evade.

But the main battlefield had been scorched, and the Bath Tub was surrounded by an influx of Flower Wall Pirate ships, it was very crowded.

It was almost a stationary target.

With the addition of the scouting rat’s intelligence that indicated the target, Zong Ge decisively fired the cannon.

Gold level cannon, gold level artillery shell.

The shell pierced the Bath Tub and made its way into the hold, and with a terrible explosion, the Bath Tub was seriously damaged in an instant.

“Leaks, the hold is leaking!”

Pirates hurried over to see the enormous hole, all of them looking on foolishly.

“The ship is sinking!” They let out a despairing wail of anguish.

“Cough, cough!” Within the raging flames and black smoke, Hua Dun struggled to stand.

He spat out bloody suds, and his perplexed expression gradually faded.

“What just happened?”

“I’m still alive.”

“Damm it, that was the gold cannon!!”

“Mo Kong, you…”

Not far from him, Hua Dun saw a corpse lying on the floor.

Mo Kong was mutilated, half of his body had been blown up, blood filled the floor, and he was on his last gasps from his grave injuries.

“Undo my curse now!” Hua Dun was very anxious as he ran to Mo Kong’s side.

Mo Kong cracked open his remaining eye, and he laughed mischievously, “I’m dead, your curse will forever remain undone.”

“Wretched guy! Speak now, how can I bring you back to life?”

Mo Kong coughed up blood, then slurred his words, “No use…my death is certain.”

“Anyways, it doesn’t matter.”

“I have already signed an agreement with a devil, my soul belongs to hell, there I shall be reborn.”

“I will become a true devil, have a higher bloodline, and have greater gifts!”

Hua Dun picked Mo Kong up by the neck, unable to restrain his anger, “Then what is to be done about the curse on us?”

“It…is not a curse.”

“It is a contract.”

“Hehe, after you die, your soul too will belong to hell.”

“This is the capital I used to rely on hell, even the boss, cough cough, is not an exception…”

Hua Dun’s eyes couldn’t help but stared at his hands and feet.

Mo Kong suddenly grabbed Hua Dun’s arm tenaciously, his voice suddenly became loud, “Remember!”

“Don’t die easily.”

“Souls can grow.”

“The stronger the soul, the greater chance one can become a powerful devil when they reach hell!”

“You have to protect Rou Zang well, he cannot die. His soul is far more precious than yours!”

“When he dies, you all die.”

“Hehe, he…”

“To protect him, I will give you all a final inheritance.”

After saying that, Mo Kong’s aura vanished.

Hua Dun’s eyes were still glaring, his eyes rigidly gazing at Mo Kong’s corpse.

This was his nightmare.

It always controlled his shadow.

But now, he was dead.

Before dying, he had told him the truth of the curse, it was an immense inconvenience!

“Find Rou Zang.”

“He is inside the hold somewhere!”

“The gold level artillery shell explosion didn’t kill him?!”

The city guard commander, Night Devil Hunting Commander, and the other four silver fighters came in through the hole.

Startled, Hua Dun came to his senses.

Enemies had invaded, and in his moment of desperation, he unconsciously chose to believe Mo Kong.

“The boss isn’t dead yet!”

“If he dies, I will die too.”

“But I am still alive…”

“Boss!” Hua Dun urged his battle qi as he ran headlong into the raging flames and thick smoke.

The alliance’s four silvers had found Rou Zang first.

Rou Zang’s entire body was pitch-black, flames had scorched it.

His huge body was very conspicuous.

His eyes were shut tight, yet pain and anger mingled in his face.

“He isn’t dead?”

The four sensed Rou Zang’s life aura, causing them to be alarmed and furious.

“Then die!”

The four brandished their weapons, aimed them at his vitals, then ruthlessly swung.

“No!” Hua Dun rushed and bawled them to stop, but it was already too late.

But just as the weapons cut down, Rou Zang suddenly opened his eyes.

Battle skill - Meat Ocean Sweeping Wave!

Rou Zang vibrated, and a fervent spherical impact wave, with him as its center, surged out in all directions.

The four silvers were launched into the air, followed by Hua Dun soon after.

The gold level battle skill had extraordinary might, and even expanded the bombardment hole by a lot.

In the ruins, Rou Zang slowly strolled to the hole.

Surging seawater flowed past his ankles and into the hold.

The water level quickly rose to his knees.

Rou Zang didn’t care about this, he was malevolently staring at Zong Ge’s warship.

The gold level cannon’s firing had damaged the ship’s hull considerably.

“Since you sent your greeting, I’ll kill you first!” Rou Zang growled, and with fury and hate wreathed about him, he rushed out of the Bath Tub and made a beeline for Zong Ge’s warship.

“It’s Rou Zang!”

“Captain Rou Zang is freed!!”

Much of the alliance was frightened, while even more pirates cried out in joy.

Battle skill – Corporeal Raft!

Gold battle qi wrapped around Roun Zang, and although it was only a superficial layer, it was constantly circulating.

Rou Zang stepped into the water, but it didn’t reach above his ankles, instead it seemed like he was walking across a marsh as he charged.

“Open fire now!” Tan Qiu looked ashen, the worst situation had happened, Rou Zang not only freed himself, but he also had the ability to fight too!

In a hole in his ship, Zong Ge stroked the gold level cannon, imbuing it with the luster of silver battle qi, preparing it to be fired.

The gold level cannon’s mouth flashed once more, like the hope shining in Tan Qiu’s and the others’ hearts.

No one wished to deal with a gold level cultivator themselves.

Rou Zang accelerated, he was already half way there.

Suddenly, he grinned malevolently, and sped up again.

Tan Qiu and the others watched on hopefully, however, they only saw the cannon gradually getting brighter, but not enough for it to be fired.

Rou Zang suddenly jumped up, and landed on the ship.

“We’re done for!” Tan Qiu’s face withered.

The priest deeply sighed, “It seems the gold level cannon needs a lot of battle qi, Shi Qi has insufficient battle qi. He is in grave danger!”

The merchant alliance magician’s eyes narrowed even more, she couldn’t bear to see the tragedy unfolding.

It was difficult for silver levels to deal with gold levels. What’s more, Zong Ge had just fired the cannon, and depleted much of his battle qi reserves, he wasn’t in good condition.

“I shall blow you to bits!” Rou Zang’s plump and enormous body cast a deep shadow below.

He raised his palm high, then aimed it to slam it down on Zong Ge’s head.

Zong Ge put up his defenses.

A moment later, his head was severely struck by Rou Zang!

“Eh?!” Rou Zang’s sinister expression stiffened as his palm quickly passed through Zong Ge.

This was not Zong Ge, rather it was an alchemy projection.

“How did this deceive my senses?” Rou Zang found the projection device.

It was obviously quite ordinary!

Not only was Zong Ge a projection, but the gold level cannon was also one too.

“Fake? This…is a trap!!” Rou Zang realized things weren’t good, and wanted to withdraw, but it was already too late.

Boom!!

Moments later, the entire ship exploded.

The entire ship was blown to smithereens in an instant.

Countless splinters flew out in all directions.

The searing light caused those that saw it to squint.

A mushroom cloud rose from the ocean surface.

In a flash, the terrible heat vaporized the seawater into a large cloud of white steam.

Soon after, the explosion created a gigantic wave that spread from it, creating a slope of seawater formed from it.

The sluggish wave looked like a bulge as it cleansed the main battlefield, swayed the entangled ships. And affected the results of many close quarter fights.

“That was no less than ten boxes of dumbbell explosives, its might is astonishing when concentrated!” The dragonman youngster exclaimed in admiration on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ flagship,

Zong Ge, who had mysteriously vanished, was already standing by his side.

This trap had been schemed with the utmost care and was specially made for Rou Zang.

The main plotter was Cang Xu.

The primary explosive used was the exploding dumbbells.

The survivors had seized 12 boxes from Mystifying Monster Island, and most of them had been used for this.

The exploding dumbbells were commonly known as shakers, it was a type of grenade that was quickly thrown after shaking them.

Due to the space lock (base), the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps didn’t have the resources to refine any other alchemy bombs.

To detonate these exploding dumbbells, the tower spirit had personally drafted an alchemy formation for that purpose.

“Did that kill Rou Zang?” The dragonman youngster was uncertain.

Zong Ge replied, “If it were me, it would be certain death. However, this is Rou Zang, it is unconfirmed.”

After all, even if Rou Zang hadn’t been shot by the gold level artillery shell, he was definitely still within its blast zone.

But he not only got free, but he also could still fight!

Currently, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had only five ships left; they were quickly drawing close to the main battlefield.

If Rou Zang was dead, they would reinforce the alliance and destroy the remaining pirates.

If Rou Zang wasn’t dead, then…they needed to converge with the alliance forces.

This had also been suggested by Cang Xu.

In the center of the explosion, white steam suddenly dispersed.

Rou Zang was burnt black, but still standing.

“Rats!”

“I will tear you all to pieces!!”

Rou Zang’s roar suppressed the entire battlefield, both enemy and allies were silent.

Zong Ge’s eyes contracted, “Sure enough, he isn’t dead.”

“That explosion still wasn’t enough to kill him?” The merchant alliance magician was speechless.

“That monster simply won’t die!” Xu Ma let out a cold breath.

He hid on Stone Forest Island and gazed at the battlefield in the distance, witnessing all that had transpired.

Upon seeing Rou Zang’s extreme hate for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and once more charging, Xu Ma couldn’t help but be gripped by worry.

“Captain Rou Zang!”

“Amazing, with the boss, our victory is certain!”

When the pirate saw Rou Zang was like a martial god, their morale skyrocketed.

The alliance forces were wavering.

Especially when Tan Qiu saw the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was drawing Rou Zang towards his flagship. His face was ashen and wished that he could shout, “Don’t come here!”

But he didn’t shout that.

He had always advocated for the establishment of this alliance.

He was Snake Rat Island’s feudal lord, and politics demanded that he couldn’t shout that.

On the contrary, the priest wasn’t shackled by this. He immediately rushed over to the magic communication device and quickly reproached, “Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, lure Rou Zang away, don’t let him draw near the main battlefield.”

“As long as you stall him, we can win!”

“After the victory, we shall compensate your damages fivefold!”

The dragonman youngster turned a deaf ear.

Zong Ge sneered, and didn’t speak.

Cang Xu replied back, "Don’t be afraid, we will defeat that insignificant Rou Zang together. Allies, we have come to support you!”

“TM”

“What kind of support is this?! I…” The priest nearly exploded into obscene sears.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps obviously didn’t think they could fight Rou Zang alone, and the priest had to endure their refusal.

The big picture was more important.

He immediately issued the order and dispatched the four silver fighters to return to provide urgent assistance.

Chapter 291: Snake Rat Naval Battle (5)

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ five ships plunged straight into the central fighting group and rapidly drew near Tan Qiu’s flagship.

Rou Zang was following close behind, charging at the dragonman youngster’s flagship.

“He’s coming!”

“To battle!”

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge took a deep breath, stepped onto the deck, then from the left and right, started to attack Rou Zang.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist.

Battle skill – Sword Point Assault.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were ready when Rou Zang landed onto the deck.

The two battle skills struck Rou Zang.

But Rou Zang cackled and heeded them no mind.

The formidable attacks caused waves to tremble across his body fat.

Rou Zang actually absorbed the power of both battle skills!

Gold battle qi began to spring from him.

Fleshly waves began to roll, the force of the dragonman youngster’s and Zong Ge’s attacks rolled into Rou Zang’s upper arm, then into his lower arm.

Battle skill – Small Flesh Reverse Palm

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge sensed that things were bad and had already made a hurried withdrawal.

But the bowl sized palm made from gold battle qi still flew from Rou Zang’s palm.

Battle skill – Surge.

The dragonman youngster twisted his tail and ankles, turning his body sideways.

Then in that instant, explosions detonated under his feet, pushing him suddenly to the left, dodging the Small Flesh Reverse Palm.

Zong Ge rolled repeatedly, causing the Small Flesh Reverse Palm to hit the deck around him.

Boom.

With two consecutive explosions, the Small Flesh Reverse Palms smashed the deck to pieces, forming holes 2 meters in diameter in it.

Zong Ge fell down a level, while the dragonman youngster was still above deck, constantly moving and not counterattacking.

The city guard commander, Captain Hei Fei, and the four other silver levels had just returned to reinforce them.

In their eyes: Rou Zang was 3 meters tall; Zong Ge and the dragonman youngster only reached up to his chest. In their first confrontation against each other, Rou Zang had struck Zong Ge hard while the dragonman youngster was in a bad state.

Hei Fei unconsciously slowed his footsteps.

He had once had a feud with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and felt enmity towards them. Thus, he didn’t want to help them and also knew the disparity between him and a gold level fighter.

The city guard commander was the same, Zong Ge had defeated him publicly and there was friction between the two.

The other two’s movements also slowed.

Previously, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had perfectly intercepted Rou Zang’s pirate group, but they had done it too well.

They not only entangled Rou Zang’s main force, but they also got to sit on the side lines and watch the alliance and Rou Zang’s main force tear each other apart, a tremendous bargain on their end.

Upon seeing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ back luck, Ba Hu and the Night Devil Hunting Commander felt some schadenfreude. In any case, Long Fu and Shi Qi were thick skinned and tough, it was a good thing if they got beat up and consumed Rou Zang’s battle qi.

But at this time, Zi Di’s shout transmitted, “Stop Rou Zang now, protect the gold cannon!”

“What?” The cannon is on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ flagship?” The alliance’s cultivators’ faces changed.

Beforehand, they had seen personally how the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps laid their trap.

They had seen much more than Rou Zang had.

They knew before the cannon fired that many sailors had dived into the water and been collected by other ships.

Contrariwise, they hadn’t seen either Shi Qi or the gold cannon being moved.

But Rou Zang had failed to hit Shi Qi, and now Shi Qi was suddenly at Long Fu’s side, thus the alliance knew that Shi Qi had also escaped too. They only didn’t see it occurring.

Now that they heard Zi Di’s call for help, they unconsciously chose to believe it.

“We have to protect the cannon; it is the best weapon to deal with Rou Zang! Support them now!!” Tan Qiu cried out.

The four silver fighters, along with Tan Qiu, and even the priest and the female magician all rushed to provide support to the best of their abilities.

Compared to them, Rou Zang was the one who moved the fastest.

After hearing Zi Di’s words, he slapped his palms to the deck suddenly.

The deck shattered with a loud bang, and he went down a level.

Rou Zang barged into the hold like a gigantic elephant, and upon penetrating every room inside, he stamped on every cannon in there.

Some mercenaries were unable to evade and were crushed to death on the spot.

Rou Zang made his way through the entire second level and didn’t find the gold cannon.

He smashed through the damaged deck and went into the hold on the third level.

As he searched half of the hold, the silver fighters surrounded him again.

This time, it wasn’t just the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge, there were now four other silver fighters too. Outside the encirclement were Tan Qiu, the priest, and the female magician already in position.

“Attack together! His defensive battle skill must have a limit.” The city guard commander shouted.

The alliance had shared all the information on Rou Zang they had with each other.

The city guard commander took out his sword and activated its enchantment. Amor of blue light covered his body as he swung at Rou Zang.

Zong Ge waved his broadsword and attacked from another direction.

The dragonman youngster climbed onto the cabin wall, and with an explosion, jumped into the air, then attacked Rou Zang’s head from above.

The Night Devil Hunting Commander, Hei Fei, and Ba Hu also launched their respective offensives simultaneously.

Rou Zang had a massive and plumpy body, he was slow and was forced to defend himself.

The attacks from all over caused his body fat to tremble and once again form a wave of flesh.

Battle skill – Small Flesh Reverse Palm!

Through this battle skill, his body absorbed incoming firepower and redirected it at his attackers.

Gold battle qi condensed in his palms, and four shot out.

The bold battle skill’s might was astonishing, and the silver levels all took evasive action.

Captain Hei Fei rolled away from direct danger but was still blown away.

The Night Devil Hunting Commander, in his moment of desperation, threw a bottle of medicine. The medicine smashed into the hold, causing a black fog to burst out, and with it concealing him, managed to dodge the Small Flesh Reverse Palm.

The city guard commander took the blow head on, causing the sapphire light armor to shatter and he to cough blood.

As he crawled back up, he saw that the dragonman youngster had been smashed into the hull by an exploding palm, embedding the latter into it.

But the dragonman shouted, cracked his back and waist, and pulled himself out. Then as if that didn’t happen, he charged at Rou Zang again while ordering, “Continue the assault, don’t give him a chance to catch his breath!”

“He is a dragonman after all.” The city guard commander was envious of the dragonman youngster’s defense.

Speed was not his strong suit.

He didn’t dare to advance and instead shot his crossbow from a distance.

The other silver fighters for a moment also chose to attack from afar.

Although silver battle qi couldn’t form outside the body, the silver fighters could make up for this immense disadvantage through all kinds of equipment.

For a moment, the dragonman youngster was the main one in the fight, while the others played it by ear, primarily attacking with ranged weapons and only getting in close when the opportunity seldom came.

For every second Rou Zang was being attacked, his body’s fleshy waves were ceaselessly rolling, with no end to them in sight.

Outside of the encirclement, the merchant alliance magician finally cast blindness magic.

Rou Zang was a gold level, and wasn’t completely blinded, rather his vision became muddled as if he was seeing the world through a cloud.

The priest frequently used healing divine spells. Divine light dropped from the sky onto the silver fighters, quickly healing their injuries.

Rou Zang growled as his internal battle qi wildly circulated, he wasn’t evading or fleeing.

“He wants to use a battle skill, stop him!!” Tan Qiu shouted from behind.

For a moment, everyone attacked more often.

Rou Zang was still standing proudly after fifteen seconds.

He took a cold breath, then his internal battle qi suddenly exploded.

His body quickly swelled, especially his belly, which seemed to inflate like a balloon, and became massive.

He had already been a tall person that reached 3 meters in height. Now with the battle skill empowering him, he grew to a giant of 6 meters.

Thus, his legs were thicker than a mammoth, his shoulders busted out into the deck, and his head towered above the sides of the ship, allowing him to see the ocean and the sky.

The ship seemed to grow crowded and to Rou Zang, it suddenly became a canoe.

Battle skill – Flesh Mountain Gigantification!

The alliance’s cultivators rained down their offensive, but the fleshly waves they caused to Rou Zang were small.

After giantizing himself, Rou Zang’s defense became stronger.

But Rou Zang was not in a rush to deal with the alliance’s cultivators, instead his arms and legs exerted themselves and destroyed the ship around them.

He snapped a mast like a piece of bamboo, his shoulder smashed a boat house, then he ran through the ship, completely destroying two decks.

Finally, Rou Zang arrived at the aft and pushed with both arms.

The miserable hull could not withstand the force; it snapped into two and sank into the sea.

The silver levels were in a tight situation, and one after the other, they fled, with some jumping onto nearby ships, and others using pieces of the hull to remain above water.

The merchant alliance magician unleashed a magic offensive, but Rou Zang’s gold battle qi protected him.

Rou Zang didn’t press the attack, rather he rushed towards another of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships.

“Everyone retreat, abandon ship!” Zong Ge yelled.

The mercenaries promptly jumped off and abandoned the defense entirely.

Rou Zang had a battle skill that allowed him to walk on water, and he rammed into the hull.

Soon after, he wantonly destroyed the ship, gold battle qi bubbled out, and in less than 30 seconds, the ship was sinking.

The alliance’s silver level cultivators wildly attacked Rou Zang.

But Rou Zang paid them scant regard and hit another of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships.

“He wants to find the gold cannon!” Tan Qiu was anxious, “We must stop him!”

Indeed, the gold cannon was the greatest threat to Rou Zang.

The alliance’s cultivators did their utmost to intercept him but were never able to hinder him.

Rou Zang was no simple gold level cultivator, for now, he could not be stopped!

All kinds of attacks fell onto him, yet he did not seem to feel anything, the fleshy waves across his skin on the contrary fostered his offensive.

However, despite tearing apart all of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships, he never found the gold level cannon.

“Where is the cannon?” Tan Qiu and the others were flabbergasted.

“Don’t tell me they have a gold level storage device?”

“It’s possible, after all they are arms smugglers.”

The priest and the merchant alliance magician secretly communicated to each other.

Rou Zang bellowed angrily. He noticed he had been played with, and despite destroying all these ships and suffering so many attacks, he didn’t even find a silver cannon, let alone a gold cannon.

Chapter 292: Snake Rat Naval Battle (6)

Rou Zang resentfully roared.

When the merchant alliance magician saw that, she couldn’t help but praise the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ stratagem.

Although the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had expanded greatly, they hadn’t been established for long, and just like Snake Rat Island, they too had low numbers.

Normally, regardless of whether it is pirate groups, navies, and so on, equipment was upgraded linearly. Take cannons for example. There were ordinary cannons, bronze level cannons, iron level cannons, and silver level ones.

But the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was special.

Their arsenal was composed almost entirely of ordinary cannons, they had neither bronze, iron, or silver level cannons, and yet they had a gold level cannon!

During this battle, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps utilized this cannon to its peak potential.

Crippling the Bath Tub was already a great merit.

Soon after, they had used false information about it to lure Rou Zang into a detonating ship.

Even if that ship and its ordinary cannons were lost, they didn’t cost much.

By contrast, Rou Zang endured innumerable attacks, wasted a lot of battle qi, and greatly diminished his combat abilities in exchange.

For a business point of view, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had earned a bloody profit!

Rou Zang’s face was covered in anger, and after he looked around at the battlefield, he didn’t care about the sinking Bath Tub, rather he threw himself at Tan Qiu’s flagship.

“He is heading towards us!” Tan Qiu grinded his jaw, his eyes embittered, yet timid. A moment later, his courage roused, and he straightened his back as he stepped forth to do battle.

Rou Zang’s target this time was the merchant alliance magician and the templar priest.

Both were silver level casters.

The merchant alliance magician immediately cast magic and left the flagship.

Only the priest was left on the flagship, with Tan Qiu protecting him.

Rou Zang charged onto the flagship’s deck, rather than striking its hull.

Although Tan Qiu’s flagship was not a magic ship, it was made of materials far better than ordinary ones. Rou Zang had used up too much battle qi already, thus he was beginning to cut down on his usage.

In fact, if his flagship sank, what would he use instead?

There were many alliance ships, thus the priest could jump onto another when the time came.

Only by killing the silver level cultivators could the outcome of this naval battle be changed.

Centered around the flagship, everyone once again encircled Rou Zang, yet still couldn’t stop Rou Zang’s violent advance.

The priest was forced to use a divine spell to flee to another ship.

Magic and divine spells really were far more convenient to use than battle qi.

Rou Zang kept the pursuit up, and the silver level fighters did their utmost to intercept him, forming a moving carnage that brought calamity wherever it landed.

Ships capsized, and if the pirates or sailors were caught in the moving carnage, they would almost all die, leaving few survivors.

For more than a dozen minutes, the priest had several near death experiences.

The city guard commander was seriously injured and couldn’t keep fighting.

The merchant alliance magician, Zi Di, and Cang Xu drained their magic power, with the latter two’s nearly dry.

Their magic was ineffective against the gold level target, but it slowed Rou Zang’s steps, a necessary contribution.

“Hu hu…” Rou Zang gasped for air, he was gradually looking fatigued.

He still looked vicious, but his body had a slight shudder that was visible to others.

Rou Zang was beating back Zong Ge when Captain Hei Fei flew at him from behind.

The silver level cutlass swung down, this time cutting skin!

Hei Fei was pleasantly surprised that his hit had landed and immediately retreated back to avoid Rou Zang’s fist.

“The monster is finally bleeding…”

When everyone saw the wound on Rou Zang’s back spilling blood, they were both startled and delighted.

But a moment later, the wound was covered by flesh and the cut immediately stopped bleeding.

The alliance’s cultivators were still in high spirits though.

Since the fight had begun, Rou Zang had been an unstoppable force that dominated the battlefield. Although this scrap was small, it gave everyone hope that they’d be victorious.

The dragonman youngster shot in like an artillery shell, then after punching Rou Zang, he shot back out.

Following his lead, everyone began to attack from all sides again.

Hei Fei’s cutlass waited for its chance to slash, and the Night Hunting Group commander whistled, dispatching a dozen iron level bats to fly around Rou Zang.

The iron level bats’ magic beast claws were poisonous, and the constantly accumulating poison taxed Rou Zang further.

Rou Zang’s injury led the Night Hunting Group commander to see an opportunity.

Zong Ge was peppering arrows from far off with two crossbows.

At this stage in the fight, his old arm wound had flared up again, and due to the incessant bleeding, he was forced to forestall his assault and accept a divine treatment spell.

The merchant alliance magician drank some medicine, racing against time to restore her magic power.

The dragonman youngster’s Demolition Fist was beginning to scorch Rou Zang’s flesh black.

Hei Fei’s cutlass was often cutting once or twice.

The iron level bats were gradually slain by Rou Zang; however they had scratched and bit him many times, the wounds left behind were a distinct dark purple.

In a short time, Zong Ge had fired 80 alchemy arrows at Rou Zang, causing burns, frost damage, acid burns, and all other sorts of injuries.

With the medicine, the merchant alliance magician recovered half her magic power, then after using two attack spells, it was exhausted once again.

Tan Qiu’s chest was covered in blood.

It was his blood.

During a critical moment, he had to be the obstruction that gave the priest time.

The priest was panting, and his head was soaked in sweat. Not only was he tired, but he was also scared.

During this fight against Rou Zang, he was the alliance’s most important figure.

As long as the other silver kept fighting Rou Zang, they would be seriously hurt. Not everyone had the dragonman youngster’s dragon scales.

It was precisely because the priest had used several treatment spells in succession that maintained everyone’s battle strength that they were able to ensure they had the superiority in numbers.

Without the priest, the alliance’s silver fighters would have been dragged down by their injuries, and would in no way be as vigorous and lively as they were now.

“Cough, cough.”

As Rou Zang repelled the dragonman youngster, he suddenly coughed out blood, then began to kneel to the ground, exhausted.

Rou Zang’s visible gold battle qi also looked like a sputtering candle, appearing and disappearing.

“He’s about to die!” Tan Qiu shouted spiritedly.

“Just a bit more!” Hei Fei laughed nastily.

“Rou Zang, time for you to lose your head!” the priest solemnly looked onwards.

Everyone beleaguered him again.

The dragonman youngster charged his front, Hei Fei attacked his back, Ba Hu struck his left, and Zong Ge shot arrows at Zong Ge’s right arm.

“You want to kill me? All of you will go to hell!!” Rou Zang exploded in anger, he suddenly stood up and straightened his belly.

He waved his two palms, then beat his belly like an army drum.

Ding!

The belly vibrated greatly, and with a terrible echo, it seemed to smash into everyone’s heart.

The dragonman youngster and the others suddenly grew sluggish, and soon after, were pushed back by the clamorous soundwaves.

Battle skill – Chaotic Skin Drum!

Ding, ding, ding…

The giant Rou Zang pounded his belly several times.

The wild drumbeat created translucent soundwaves.

With a violent and ferocious attitude, the soundwaves swept across the battlefield!

Chucks flew away from the deck under Rou Zang, and as the sound ripped across the hull, it began to crack.

The soundwave caused Zong Ge to stagger, and he retreated, deathly pale.

Hei Fei knelt to the ground, covering his eardrums and dropping his cutlass in pain.

The dragonman youngster’s face went red, all of his muscles were affected by the drumbeat, the constant pulsing and trembling causing him to lose control over his muscles.

As the clamor spread, pirates and sailors fighting each other rolled their eyes and sprayed blood from their mouths, dying on the spot.

Ba Hu also fell to the ground.

He tenaciously grabbed his chest clothes, a pained expression on his face and veins popping on his forehead as he struggled on the edge of death.

The widespread sound attack even disturbed the priest and merchant alliance magician.

A figure appeared.

It was Ni.

The silver level thief, the trump card Tan Qiu had strenuously concealed, had her cover blown by the soundwaves.

Rou Zang howled in laughter, then suddenly jumped at the priest.

The priest was stooped over, unable to move.

Tan Qiu hesitated, then summoned all of his courage and attacked Rou Zang.

But Rou Zang ignored him, and with two palms, crushed the priest into a pool of blood.

The priest died!

“No!” Tan Qiu bellowed, his sword was buried deep in Rou Zang’s flesh, nearly up to the hilt.

Rou Zang kicked Tan Qiu away, then due to a lack of strength, kneeled again.

A moment later, Tan Qiu, Ni, the Night Devil Hunting Commander, and the others surrounded him and attacked together.
