Rou Zang found it difficult to turn about, his back against them all.

Battle skill – Thorn in the Flesh!

In his moment of crisis, gold battle qi was stimulated once again, and formed into fine needles that suddenly ejected from his back.

Like a downpour, the attack covered his enemies.

The gold battle qi needles pierced the skin, and immediately dug into the silver fighters’ flesh.

The fighters’ flesh suddenly changed into crystals that formed a fleshly thorn.

As long as it moved a little, the fleshly thorns would cause immense pain. More crucially, the fleshly thorns were constantly growing, and if ignored, would pierce a body’s internal organs. If it reached this step, light injuries would become serious, and even be fatal.

Zong Ge, Hei Fei, and the Night Devil Hunting Group commander groaned dimly, then urged their internal silver battle qi to suppress the fleshy thorns.

The dragonman youngster was also hit by this battle skill, and although his body was covered in dragon scales, even he couldn’t completely block the gold battle qi.

“Ah…” Ba Hu screamed before he died.

Chaotic Skin Drum had already brought him to death’s door, and he was unable to protect himself from Thorn in the Flesh that followed shortly after.

At first, Thorn in the Flesh didn’t feel like anything, but after several breaths, he started to scream.

Then a few seconds later, Ba Hu’s mournful scream quieted to a final wail.

The wail quickly diminished to nothing.

He too was dead.

“Finally, an opportunity!” The dragonman youngster’s killing intent did not drop but rose instead.

Finally, he had found an opportunity to use Life Scorching Dragon Breath.

“Protect me!” The dragonman youngster stood still.

With a draconic chant, the aura of a racial magic quickly emitted.

Zong Ge hurried to the young man’s side immediately after, shooting a crossbow while roaring at the other fighters, “Entangle Rou Zang!”

The silver fighters all knew of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ military accomplishments, and knew that the Life Scorching Dragon Breath had once forced Rou Zang to retreat.

Chapter 293: Snake Rat Naval Battle (7)

If Rou Zang broke out, the other silver fighters wouldn’t be able to keep obstructing him.

But he was naturally disposed to cruelty, and in this perilous situation, his only thought was to kill the dragonman youngster!

He beat everyone back and charged at the dragonman youngster.

“Hmph! I had enough time.” As the dragonman youngster faced down the powerful pressure of Rou Zang’s charge, he did not bat even an eyelid, and his eyes glistened with a fierce light.

He had been cultivating the Life Scorching Dragon Breath racial magic too.

Compared to before, he could now cast it half a second faster.

That half second was enough to decide the outcome of this battle!

The Life Scorching Dragon Breath retrained Rou Zang immensely, and as long as he sprayed it at him, victory was likely.

But just as he was on the verge of success, something struck the dragonman youngster out of the blue.

The dragonman youngster couldn’t move while casting, so Zong Ge threw away his hand crossbow, heaved his broadsword, and raised it up, undaunted by the danger.

Bang!

The deck the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were standing on shattered into smithereens, casting both into the sea.

A giant jet of water rose from a hole in the ship.

The ship first floated with the stream of water, then because of the water rushing into the hold, quickly began to sink.

On the sinking deck and water pouring through a hole, a person stood behind Rou Zang.

Shockingly, it was Gu Qiao!

Gu Qiao was as withered as firewood, his face was ashen, and his left arm snapped at the root, he was seriously wounded.

Everyone turned pale with fright, and for a split second, morale plummeted.

But a moment later, Guang Ke followed right behind him.

“Sorry!” His face looked regretful.

He always had the upper hand against Gu Qiao.

But despite risking his arm, Gu Qiao swooped down and saved Rou Zang’s life.

“Ever since you betrayed the temple, I vowed that I would bring you back to it in chains and make up for my criminal mistakes then.” Gu Qiao faced the entire allied forces, and said behind Ruo Zang, “Therefore, you cannot die! Rou Zang, stand up.”

Rou Zang coldly snorted, and got up with a sway, “Even if I die, I won’t go back.”

He glanced at Gu Qiao’s broken arm, then fiercely gazed at his powerful enemy, “In this situation, isn’t there only one move we can do?”

Gu Qiao, “Did you unravel the seals I engraved into your bones? Right. Your battle skill is the best at transferring injuries elsewhere; did you use that shell to open the seal?”

“Senior brother, you speak too much. Kill them!” Rou Zang growled, then suddenly as if all of his bones were removed, his six meter tall body turned into a pure wave of flesh that instantly buried Gu Qiao.

Gu Qiao’s and Rou Zang’s shouts disseminated from the fatty flesh.

Gold battle qi surged tempestuously, and the fat quickly morphed into an approximately four meter tall giant.

The giant’s face looked like a mix of Gu Qiao’s and Rou Zang’s features.

Although the giant was tall, it also had a thick stature. Besides being covered in fat, the most eye-catching thing about it was that pitch-black bones covered his body’s vitals.

The Great Pain Temple secret skill – Bone and Flesh Unification!

“Guang Ke, embrace death!” The giant’s voice sounded like both Rou Zang and Gu Qiao were speaking together.

Bang!

The giant rushed towards Guang Ke, he was much faster than Rou Zang had been.

Both arms were intact, making up for Gu Qiao’s grievous injuries.

Rou Zan had nearly exhausted his gold battle qi, but Gu Qiao’s battle qi reserves were still plentiful.

Guang Ke flew into the empty sky once again.

The giant laughed maliciously, but didn’t follow.

He turned around to kill Tan Qiu, the dragonman youngster, and the others.

The imposing enemy was quite fearsome, thus the silver fighters dispersed and fled.

Guang Ke dived back down.

The two fought fiercely.

The giant stood in place, and although the sea water below him depressed greatly, it quickly smoothed back.

Guang Ke was flung back into the air.

“Kill them all!”

“Kill all the rest!”

“Ah Guang Ke, if you have the skills, come stop me, hahaha.”

The giant crazily howled, then with inexhaustible killing intent, he pursued the silver cultivators.

The alliance’s silver cultivators continued to flee and jumped into ships.

Although the giant was powerful, his immense build and heavy body meant that speed and mobility was his weak point.

For a moment, the bone and flesh giant couldn’t reach the silver fighters and could only vent his anger on other enemies.

The alliance’s sailors were ants he crushed into rivers of blood.

“Boss, we are on your side!” Pirates were also enveloped by his attack, and turned to heaps of bone and flesh too.

A ship the giant devastated sank.

Guang Ke was furious, he used all of his might in an attempt to stop and entangle the giant.

But the giant used his iron bone armor to defend himself and also used battle skills to heal himself.

Battle skill – Fleshy Sprout!

Healing battle skills were even rarer than flying battle skills.

Were it not for that, the clergy wouldn’t have been so welcomed in present times.

Compared to fighters, magicians and clergy using divine spells had the best results when healing.

But now, the alliance’s priest was dead, while Rou Zang had revealed a restorative skill.

“The bone and flesh giant is far stronger than Rou Zang. Regardless of whether it is speed, strength, or defense…”

“Retreat now!”

The entire battle group was thrown into chaos by the bone and flesh giant.

Both the alliance and the pirates strived to be the first to withdraw.

The bone and flesh giant was mad, he killed even his own men!

Tan Qiu was deathly pale.

His flagship had already sunk.

The bone and flesh giant had caused disastrous damage to the alliance!

“How awful.” Xu Ma had been a spectator on Stone Forest Island.

He was now very glad that he had been left behind here.

Otherwise, he might have died in this naval battle.

To Xu Ma’s side, the Stone Forest Island villagers were also very tense.

The outcome of this naval battle would determine their fate.

Xu Ma noticed that the village chief was more nervous than he. He clapped the latter’s shoulder and comforted him like how he was, “We must have faith in the feudal lord! The alliance is certain to prevail!”

The village chief blurted out, “Of, of course, the alliance shall prevail!”

Xu Ma grinded his teeth, “Rou Zang will be annihilated!”

“Right! Right! Rou Zang will die!” The village chief clenched his jaw with a fierce expression.

Most of the Bath Tub was already underwater.

“Go now!”

“The Bath Tub is hopeless.”

“Hold on, these things are valuable.”

The pirates were escaping and swimming onto the surrounding pirate ships. There were also some pirate ships plundering the Bath Tub’s ship house.

Some pirates were obsessed with Mo Kong’s inheritance, and dove into the submerged hold to see Mo Kong’s remains.

A pirate was incessantly excited, “The first mate was silver level, his inheritance will surely be bountiful and help me prosper!”

In the dusky seawater, as he gradually approached Mo Kong, he suddenly spurted out blood.

In a few seconds, all of the pirate’s blood was extracted and converged around Mo Kong’s remains.

Before the pirate died, he had made a horrifying discovery, there had been desiccated corpses floating around Mo Kong.

They too had been drained of their blood.

Now he looked just like them.

The desiccated corpses around Mo Kong’s remains weren’t worth mentioning, the truly strange thing was the blood.

Most of the blood had been forcefully extracted from pirates. The large ball revolved around Mo Kong’s remains, it depicted the center of a magic formation.

If one had a wider field of view, they would see that in the depths of the seater under the battlefield, there was blood wriggling through it, forming a gigantic magic formation.

“Desist!” Guang Ke attacked with all of strength and used one battle skill after the other.

Nevertheless, the bone and flesh giant’s rough skin and thick flesh resisted Guang Ke’s battle skills and continued to surround everyone around it.

In this duel between the two gold levels, the dragonman youngster and the other silver fighters had to helplessly sit on the sidelines and do their best to save their crews.

Guang Ke really was a powerful warrior, and even the bone and flesh giant was not his match.

But Guang Ke couldn’t stop the bone and flesh giant from continuing his slaughter.

Guang Ke felt sincere sorrow and fury, he was a special person, one who had come from the lowest levels of society and had extensive love for them, as well as not having much charitability for many nobles.

“Guang Ke! You can’t stop us.”

“Stop, let us go. Otherwise, I will kill everyone, hehehe.”

The bone and flesh giant’s threat was very childish, but it was very effective.

If it were someone else, they’d have brushed it aside.

But Guang Ke…

“Lord Guang Ke, we can’t let them go! If you let them go, they will continue to do evil.” Tan Qiu worriedly exclaimed.

Guang Ke sank into silence; he had already stopped attacking.

The bone and flesh giant chuckled and sneered, “Rou Zang will be returned to the Great Pain Temple as my prisoner and will accept the punishment he deserves. He won’t return to the seas ever again.”

Guang Ke lifted his head, his face solemn and stirring, “Retribution is upholding justice!”

“If I cower now, would I not let down those that sacrificed themselves in this naval battle?”

“Oh Justice God, my God! Please let me borrow your strength again.”

Divine spell - Domain of the Light of Contrition.

The divine spell’s radiance suppressed everyone present, whether it was enemy or ally, thick pitch-black fog emerged from them all, hindering their actions greatly.

The bone and flesh giant was in the worst state.

His entire body emitted strong black smoke, and an incorporeal mountain seemed to slow his movements.

Tan Qiu, the dragonman youngster, and the others weren’t spared either.

The Domain of the Light of Contrition did not distinguish between friend and foe.

“Rou Zang, Gu Qiao, because you massacred the small and weak, and committed innumerable sins, the Domain of the Light of Contrition strikes you the worst.”

“You shan’t think of murdering ever again.”

“Justice has arrived, and injustices must be redressed!”

Guang Ke strictly shouted as his entire body radiated light.

“Abominable!” The bone and flesh giant fumed with rage, and was just about to keep fighting.

But moments later, something strange happened.

From bottom to top, the ocean under the battlefield suddenly began to glow a blackish red.

A devilish aura violently rose.

Soon after, an enormous pentagram shaped magic formation slowly opened in the seawater.

Guang Ge immediately turned pale.

“The magic formation’s center is in the Bath Tub!” The merchant alliance magician exclaimed. Without the magic formation masking its aura, she detected it far quicker than the one on Snake Rat Island.

Guang Ke instantly flew towards the Bath Tub.

When the bone and flesh giant saw this, he urged his gold battle qi, and he too flew into the sky in an attempt to stop Guang Ke.

The two’s flying battle skills were nearly equal, but Guang Ke was far smaller than the bone and flesh giant, which meant he was far nimbler.

Guang Ke didn’t zealously fight on, and after he shook off the bone and flesh giant, he crashed into the Bath Tub.

At this time, only the tip of the Bath Tub was still above water.

“A step too slow!” Guang Ke broke through the magic formation’s energy barrier, and in the center of the formation, he saw Mo Kong’s corpse finish melting, treated as a casting material.

Chapter 294: Snake Rat Naval Battle (8)

The gigantic magic formation successfully activated.

The dark red light in the seawater formed into a pillar of light that pointed towards the sky.

The tremendous magic might radiated in an area over four thousand meters in diameter.

Fear spell!

The magic Mo Kong was best at when he lived.

He sacrificed himself, including his bloodline, his skin, his flesh, his magic power, and everything else as casting materials for a single spell!

“Is this the parting gift you were talking about before!? Damn it!” The Flower Wall Pirate commander, Hua Dun, had a flash of remembrance a moment before being affected by the Fear spell.

If living beings were struck by a simple fear spell, for a short time, they would lose all calmness, reason, and reflection, and run around howling in fright.

But beforehand, Guang Ke had used his divine spell - Domain of the Light of Contrition.

The combination of the divine spell and magic caused everyone in the battlefield to immediately be affected even worse.

For a moment, fights between the alliance’s soldiers and the pirates nearly ceased.

Everywhere, people were falling to the deck.

Gunfire went silent, and shouts of fighting stopped too.

Clang, clang…

Many people no longer had the strength to move, and their weapons fell to the deck.

Soldiers and pirates could no longer fight, pain and remorse filled their faces.

Some were kneeling, some crying with covered faces, and some put their hands together in prayer, their hearts praying to the gods.

“I am guilty, I am truly guilty!”

“I want to repent, my God, this lowly believer prays for your understanding!”

“How am I still alive, I harmed others as I live, why don’t I just die?”

Unexpectedly, someone who couldn’t endure the condemnation of their innermost being committed suicide on the spot!

Compared to what happened on Snake Rat Island, this time, the combination of the Domain of the Light of Contrition and the Fear spell produced a far more frightening result.

Firstly, Guang Ke’s divine spell was far stronger than last time.

Secondly, the Fear spell was also far more powerful than the one cast on Snake Rat Island. Because this time, Mo Kong sacrificed himself as the main casting material. Last time, he only used his eyeball as a casting material.

“I am a disgrace to my clan, I am only interested in saving myself, and once, in order to survive, I swore allegiance to pirates…” Tan Qiu couldn’t move his sword as tears streamed down both cheeks.

Hei Fei was deathly pale, he held his head and shouted, “Should die, I should die!”

He was thinking of all the homes he destroyed, all the beastmen slaves he had captured.

“I knew you had families and friends, lives that were ruined because of me.”

“Yet I sold you all off for money to provide for my life of pleasure.”

“I am no human, I am a devil!”

The survivors weren’t in good condition either.

Bai Ya slammed his head into a ship plank, weeping bitter tears, “It was I who killed Jia Sha, boohoo, I killed him.”

“I am a commoner, yet I offended my elders and killed a priest.”

“I am guilty, I should die!”

Lan Zao’s heart and soul were filled with regret and self-blame, he even lifted his cutlass to his own neck.

He raised his head to the sky, “Little brother, I harmed you.”

“It was I who killed you.”

“I am your older brother, but as your older brother, I killed you to survive!”

“I’m too ashamed to face our mother, and even more ashamed towards you.”

“Everything was my mistake, all of it was my wrongs!!”

Gradually, his arm pressed the blade into his skin, and a bloody trace began to faintly appear around his neck.

Zi Di was gasping for breath.

Her body was filled with thick black smoke.

“I am a guilty woman.”

“I conspired to murder my fiancé, and had an affair with another man!”

“In my search for power, I secretly cultivated necromancy!”

“For money, I manufactured a shipwreck.”

“How can I still have the face to remain alive?”

Zong Ge grinded his teeth, weakness and anxiety manifesting in his usually firm features.

“Father.”

“I failed to live up to your expectations.”

“Right now, I am in endless straits and keep company with necromancers.”

“I did not get to the Wilderness Continent in a timely manner, I am still rushing across the seas for the sake of redemption.”

Cang Xu held a bottle of medicine unsteadily.

His other hand covered his heart, and his face was in knots, full of self-blame.

“I became a necromancer!”

“After it all, I became a living dead man, all for the purpose of my selfish desires.”

“Now, even my closest flesh and blood, my son, has reported me to the authorities.”

“I became a fugitive and can only float about in exile.”

“Perhaps, I should end this senseless life. I should go back and turn myself in.”

The Domain of the Light of Contrition and the Fear spell not only enveloped the battlefield, but continued to radiate outwards.

Everyone on Stone Forest Island was also affected.

Black smoke also rose from Xu Ma.

He lowered his head, tears of remorse dripping, “I am guilty.”

“I stole many times, too many to count.”

“I am timid, of small guts, I don’t dare to steal from the rich and strong. I stole from the poor many times and added insult to the injury that was their lives.”

“I once had companions but got cold feet when I met with a wolf group and betrayed them. I lived, but they didn’t!

“I am also guilty!!” Suddenly, the village chief to Xu Ma could be heard.

Xu Ma turned to look at him, his tearful eyes suddenly wide open.

He was shocked to discover that the black smoke around the old village chief was denser than his.

The village chief proclaimed through tears and snot, “It was I who encouraged the villagers.”

“Rou Zang’s pirate group never attacked Stone Forest Island, everyone was done by our hands.”

“It was all to hide that chunk of blood brain pebble.”

“Ordinarily, the highest quality brain pebbles get is the silver level. Blood brain pebbles are very rare, it is the brain pebble inside the brain pebble, an uncommon gold level material.”

“If the feudal lord discovered it, he would surely have confiscated the blood brain pebble.”

“We wanted to secretly keep it and sell it behind the feudal lord’s back, then use the money to migrate the entire village away from Stone Forest Island!”

“The taxes here are too heavy.”

Upon hearing the village chief’s confession, Xu Ma was stunned, “So that’s what happened!”

Hua Dun felt his armor grow exponentially heavier.

He also was in the Fear spell and the Domain of the Light of Contrition, all of his killing intent was gone.

He too emitted black smoke, for as a pirate, he naturally had done many crimes.

“I am bad!”

I was insatiably greedy, I became a pirate of my own accord, I killed, looted, and committed all imaginable crimes.”

“To satisfy my incomparably ugly cravings, I defiled many pure young men.”

“I am scared of dying, and due to Mo Kong’s curse, I have to slave away for him.”

When he thought of Mo Kong, Hua Dun was unable to understand something.

He knew that the Fear spell should be the ‘gift’ Mo Kong mentioned before he died.

But didn’t Mo Kong know that this magic couldn’t distinguish between friend and foe?

The alliance was thrown into chaos, but the pirates were in the same boat!

Boom!

The bone and flesh giant punched Guang Ke into the air.

Guang Ke crashed through three ships before steadying himself and flying back into the air.

The bone and flesh giant was already chasing after him.

The two dueled with each other through the air, through ships, and even in the ocean.

Unlike before, Guang Ke was now actually losing.

“What is going on?” Hua Dun, along with the other silver level cultivators able to withstand the divine spell and magic, were observing the battlefield, noting this fact with indefinite bewilderment.

Hua Dun and the others felt very puzzled.

Guang Ke was unconstrained by the Domain of the Light of Contrition, only the Fear spell was affecting him.

While the bone and flesh giant wasn’t affected by the Fear spell, only by the surging black smoke from the Domain of the Light of Contrition.

“Rou Zang, you have an excellent underling.” The voice from the bone and flesh giant was Gu Qiao this time.

Rou Zang coldly snorted, “Mo Kong was a crafty guy, I made a deal with him, therefore his magic doesn’t affect me.”

Rou Zang wasn’t affected by the Fear spell, thus the bone and flesh giant he had combined with Gu Qiao to make wasn’t affected either.

The bone and flesh giant spoke with Gu Qiao’s voice again, “Rou Zang, what do you believe justice is?”

“Eh?” Rou Zang was puzzled.

“This is the Justice Church’s divine spell, the Domain of the Light of Contrition, it is very renowned. Through my temple’s research, it was discovered that its true effect is that it stimulates a target’s feelings of repentance and guides their religious conversion.”

“However, it is standardized in how it stimulates the target’s feelings of repentance.”

“This standard is how the target understands justice in their heart.”

“Which things are just, and which things are wicked. If they have done more wicked things, then the target will feel more repentant.”

“Are you saying…” Rou Zang trailed off, then suddenly realized what Gu Qiao was proposing.

Guang Ke’s face slightly changed, and he intensified his attacks.

As the bone and flesh giant fought Guang Ke, Gu Qiao instructed Rou Zang, “Therefore, you understand what justice is.”

Rou Zang realized it, and told himself, “The most just thing in this world is power! The small and weak are sinful, and whatever the truly strong do is right, that is the meaning of justice!!”

He said this many times over.

Every time he said that, some of the black smoke on the bone and flesh giant dispelled, allowing the bone and flesh giant to become nimbler.

Rou Zang and Gu Qiao were both from the Great Pain Temple, and combined, they were stronger than the sum of their parts.

Guang Ke was affected by the Fear spell, while the Bone and Flesh giant was still in the Domain of the Light of Contrition.

As Rou Zang altered his definition of justice, the Domain of the Light of Contrition’s effect on the bone and flesh giant waned.

The bone and flesh giant’s battle strength continued to rise, and was beginning to firmly suppress Guang Ke.

Guang Ke was gradually wounded.

The situation was getting worse for the alliance.

“What’s going on?” Xu Ma gazed from Stone Forest Island, he couldn’t hear the conversation between Gu Qiao and Rou Zang, but he could still see how the war situation was changing.

“Even someone like Guang Ke isn’t Rou Zang’s opponent!”

“Not good…”

“It’s mainly because Rou Zang is too shameless, he was actually able to combine with another gold level with that strange battle skill!”

“Should I withdraw or not?”

Xu Ma’s eyes twinkled.

He had just joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and his sense of belonging to them was quite weak.

Upon seeing the situation was worsening, he didn’t want to fall into the hands of the pirates and already wanted to flee.

“Village chief, surely you have a boat on hand?” Xu Ma grabbed the village chief of Stone Forest Island.

The village chief replied while weeping bitter tears, “Yes, I have a boat.”

“That’s good. What are we waiting for, prepare it!” Xu Ma exclaimed.

Chapter 295: Snake Rat Naval Battle (9)

The main battlefield.

The dragonman youngster clenched his teeth, struggling until his shaky body finally stood up.

At this very moment, he was affected by both the Fear spell and the Domain of the Light of Contrition.

“Under my leadership, we killed Jia Sha.”

“I also imprisoned Zhen Jin, a noble!”

“I am guilty, guilty…”

The dragonman youngster heart was heavy, and he found it difficult to look around.

He saw the paralyzed Tan Qiu, the listless Cang Xu, the struggling Zong Ge, and many others besides themselves because of the guilt.

“This can’t continue.”

“If it does, we will lose!”

“I must do something!” The responsibility of leadership urged the dragonman youngster onwards.

Right now, Gu Qiao’s teachings to Rou Zang were ringing in his mind.

“Justice…”

“What is justice?”

Then a lighting bolt seemed to streak across his heart.

The dragonman youngster was on the Sapphire, and he immediately turned around and entered the captain’s room.

The Sapphire had an alchemy device capable of amplifying one’s voice.

The dragonman youngster’s voice was quickly transmitted from the captain’s room. “We all have a heart that seeks justice!”

The dragonman youngster’s voice spread throughout the entire battlefield.

“It is because of this that we feel remorse and are repenting right now.”

“We are all guilty!”

But it is precisely because of our sins that we repent and weep sorrowful tears. We can then understand more the importance of pursuing justice, no?

“We should be guilty!”

“Who hasn’t done wrong? Who can guarantee that throughout their life, they never erred?”

“Furthermore, there are many times that things were beyond our control, or we were compelled by circumstance.”

“We have committed crimes for the sake of survival.”

“And this isn’t wrong!”

“Survival, survival!”

“Only by continuing to live can we have the chance to admit our mistakes, to redeem ourselves, to feel remorse, and to set back onto the path of righteousness.”

“Without life, how can we defend justice?”

These words caused Tan Qiu to stop weeping, and his eyes were once again bright.

He looked towards the dragonman youngster, “You’re right, you’re really right! If I didn’t give into Rou Zang, how could today have even happened?”

Most of the smoke around Bai Ya faded away, and he once again had the strength to stop his head from mutilating itself against the plank, “His Lordship is right, we were forced to kill Jia Sha.”

In the tears, Lan Zao saw the young captain smiling and reaching out a hand.

“My Lord!”

Lan Zao reached for the hand, and in that moment, it seemed he had obtained redemption.

Not only them, but much of the black smoke also faded away from Zi Di, Cang Xu, Hei Fei, and the others, and they recovered much of their battle strength.

Only Guang Ke pursed his brows.

He felt the dragonman youngster’s words were very problematic, but they also seemed to make sense.

As for what the problem was or why they were reasonable, Guang Ke did not have the time to think about such things during a pitched battle.

Then, the dragonman youngster’s voice could be heard again.

“Everyone please believe my words!”

“I am a believer of the great Justice God!”

“I understand justice more than an ordinary man!”

“In the Justice God’s creed, there is a saying - Justice without strength is useless, and strength without justice is tyranny!”

“Rou Zang doesn’t have justice!”

“Fellow companions, comrades-in-arms, defeating the wicked Rou Zang is the righteous choice, and the best way for us to repent.”

“Stand up, and let us join hands together to eradicate a treacherous villain and carry out justice!”

“You speak true! Rou Zang must pay with his life!” Tan Qiu straightened himself for battle, most of the black smoke around him had thinned out.

Rou Zang and Gu Qiao had morphed into a bone and flesh giant of massive build. Tan Qiu barely needed to aim to hit it with a crossbow.

Tan Qiu was a lord, a feudal lord even, so of course the arrows he fired were alchemical ones.

The bone and flesh giant paid scant regard to the alchemy arrows, his target was still Guang Ke. As long as he killed the alliance’s sole gold fighter, the others were nothing to worry about.

But soon after, more alchemy arrows hit him.

Zong Ge, Zi Di, and Cang Xu were encircling him.

The dragonman youngster used Surge, and like an artillery shell, his two fists hit the bone and flesh giant’s back.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist!

The bone and flesh giant was staggered by his blow, and the gold battle qi protecting him violently shuddered.

The bone and flesh giant was forced to punch from the side.

The arm swept across.

The dragonman youngster was unable to avoid it completely, and the edge of the fist scratched him, smashing him into a ship aft house.

The dragonman youngster had dragon scales protecting him, but he had still been beaten bloody.

At that moment, the bone and flesh giant used the battle skill, Thorn in the Flesh, and like a tempest, condensed gold battle qi needles fired out in all directions.

The cultivators around him scattered and took cover.

The dragonman youngster shook his head, and stood up in the ruins of the ship aft house, he then noticed someone in front of him, the Night Devil Hunting Group commander.

The hunting commander had protected him from Thorn in the Flesh.

“Commander Long Fu, your speech was excellent! Let’s fight side by side!” The Night Devil Hunting Commander shouted.

“That’s right.” Captain Hei Fei rushed over, two blades in hand.

Once, he and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had feuded.

However, with a powerful enemy in front of them, and also receiving the dragonman youngster’s grace, he had abandoned this prejudice and now fought with all of his strength.

Everyone besieged the bone and flesh giant, their offensive continuous, all of them attacking again and again.

Guang Ke took on the greatest pressure at the front.

For a moment, the situation was deadlocked, both sides equally matched.

On a ship to the side of Stone Forest Island.

The village chief sighed ruefully, “Who was the one on the speaker? Their words really reached the bottom of our hearts!”

Xu Ma immediately replied, “That was our commander, Lord Long Fu.”

The village chief looked faintly queer, “Survival comes first, we don’t have a choice for survival’s sake.”

“Tan Qiu’s taxes are too heavy, far too heavy!”

“Living in his territory is too painful, too hard.”

“Therefore, we will hide the blood brain pebble so we can survive.”

Xu Ma promptly replied, “I can understand, I can understand that everyone’s lives are not easy.”

“Therefore, I’m sorry!” The old village chief began to emit a dangerous aura.

Xu Ma stepped back and waved his hands, “Villagers, don’t be impulsive, I am not your enemy!”

The village chief, along with many villagers were surrounding him, “But you know our secret. We are evading taxes, a serious offense. As long as we kill you, we can flee to some faraway place.”

“Tan Qiu is too busy managing his own affairs, regardless of how this battle ends, he won’t be able to pursue us.”

“By eliminating you, no one else will know about the blood brain pebble.”

“By selling it, our entire village will have a better future!”

“Kill!”

After the village chief said that he ordered the villagers to kill Xu Ma.

Xu Ma was only a bronze level, how could he dare to fight these people in combat, instead he crashed through a window and rolled onto the deck.

“What bad luck, what truly terrible luck.”

“Commander Long Fu, you brought calamity upon me!”

Xu Ma was actually quite nimble, and with hands and feet, he climbed the mast in a flurry.

The villagers on the deck did not give up the pursuit.

Cang Xu tore open a healing scroll.

A milky white wave of healing soon flowed out of it, and like a chain, it wrapped around the dragonman youngster, Zong Ge, Tan Qiu, and the others.

Their injuries soon faded away or lessened somewhat.

Zi Di was responsible for using fireball spell scrolls to help Guang Ke impede the bone and flesh giant.

These magic scrolls were from War Merchant’s storehouse.

Right now, Cang Xu’ and Zi Di’s magic power were nearly dry, but their spirits were still good, and using these scrolls were their best option.

The bone and flesh giant was scarred again and again, and it was gasping for breath.

He was already on a downward turn.

“Those damn guys…” Rou Zang gnashed his teeth.

He couldn’t escape anymore.

The ocean was boundless, and he no longer had a ship to cross it.

The Bath Tub had already sunk.

And his gold battle qi was also about to run out.

Originally, he had the advantage, but because of one person’s speech, the scales of victory were slowly slanting to another.

If this trend continued, the bone and flesh giant would no longer be able to fight.

“It is time.” The dragonman youngster detected an opportunity, and once again, stood in place and chanted draconic words.

Racial magic – Life Scorching Dragon Breath.

“I’ll kill you!” The bone and flesh giant yelled, it was Rou Zang’s voice this time.

He loathed the dragonman youngster much more than Guang Ke.

When the dragonman youngster saw the bone and flesh giant pouncing at him, he ceased the racial magic.

He coughed out blood, resisting the failed casting backfire as he extricated himself from the flesh and bone giant.

Guang Ke followed close behind.

Battle skill – Sacred Light Sword.

Divine spell – Just Punishment.

Gold battle qi bubbled out and condensed into a three meter long sword.

The battle skill and divine spell combined, coating the giant sword in a silver-gold radiance.

The silver-gold sword swung down, cutting through the bone and flesh giant’s back, and cleaving it in half.

The bone and flesh giant miserably howled, and gold battle qi scattered, separating Gu Qiao and Rou Zang back into two people.

Gu Qiao looked like a worn out sack, and by the time he hit the deck, he was already unconscious and delirious.

It was no wonder that in the latter stages of the fight that the bone and flesh giant only spoke with Rou Zang’s voice.

Guang Ke had severed Gu Qiao’s arm, the latter losing consciousness was not odd at all.

As for Rou Zang, he was still awake.

After falling into the sea, he immediately climbed onto a skiff drifting in the sea.

With the silver-gold sword of light in hand, Guang Ke floated above Rou Zang.

Rou Zang glowered at him but didn’t attack.

His battle qi was spent and was at the mercy of his foes.

“Lord Guang Ke, spare his life!” At this time, Tan Qiu unexpectedly exclaimed for leniency.

“Capture him alive, make Rou Zang tell us where his treasure is.” The merchant alliance magician added on, “There is also the bounty, Rou Zang will be worth more alive than dead!”

Right now, Rou Zang was Guang Ke’s prisoner.

Soon after, the alliance forces also imprisoned the still unconscious Gu Qiao.

The strongest forces on the pirate’s side were defeated, removing all suspense from this conflict.

The alliance was victorious, and the vast majority of the pirates had completely lost their will to fight, laying down their weapons and surrendering, Hua Dun among them.

A minority of the pirates stubbornly refused to admit defeat or were slaughtered by infuriated alliance forces as they asserted their victory.

“We are victorious!” Joyous shouts rose across the battlefield.

The entirety of the alliance looked adoringly at Guang Ke.

Guang Ke was the alliance’s strongest power, and the greatest hero during this battle.

Without him, the alliance would have been routed and be forced to nurse their grievances, there was no other possibility.

Guang Ke looked towards the dragonman youngster.

He had complex feelings about the youngster he had recently converted.

If he was the battle’s greatest hero, then the most pivotal person was doubtlessly the dragonman youngster.

It was the dragonman youngster’s speech that had roused the silver level cultivators’ battle strength. Without the silver levels helping Guang Ke, he wouldn’t have defeated the bone and flesh giant.

Guang Ke now recalled the dragonman youngster’s pivotal declaration, and the more he pondered it, the more he felt there was something amiss about it.

But he couldn’t vocalize what was amiss about it.

“Ah save me, save me!” Beyond the celebratory ambience, Xu Ma was still wildly jumping around the ship, using all of his strength to run away.

“The villagers were crowded into a large group, and whichever mast Xu Ma jumped to, they would rush over to surround it from below. They used every kind of farm tools or slipshod bows and arrows to jab and shoot at Xu Ma.

“Save me, save me!” Xu Ma begged for help with a husky voice.

Chapter 296: Standing Tall

Several days later.

White seabirds flew over Snake Rat Island’s port.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ flag fluttered above twelve ships.

Sailors and porters were buzzing with activity.

Water was filled, rum was barreled, vegetables were bundled, fresh meat, bread, cheese, and other supplies were packed and transported onto these ships.

The hulls of these ships were filled with innumerable and distinct scars from blades and cannon fire.

Speaking of which, all of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ six ships were sunk during the battle.

Upon discussion after the victory, Tan Qi and the dragonman youngster decided to not salvage those ships.

Because those ships had all been destroyed by Rou Zang, even if they were salvaged, they’d need to be overhauled too.

After calculating the salvage and repair cost, it was determined to not be cost effective and that it was better to just buy new ships.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had done exceptional, and had an outstanding battle record, thus Tan Qiu gave them 12 captured ships that were in relatively good condition.

Of course, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ gains from this conflict were far more than just these 12 ships.

In the flagship’s captain room, Guang Ke and the dragonman youngster were discussing the Justice God’s doctrine.

The dragonman youngster said, “Defending and carrying out justice should be the pursuit of every living being.”

“Isn’t that right?”

Guang Ke nodded, “That’s right.”

The dragonman youngster then said, “Then, how can we pursue justice? We need to have a usable body. Or in other words, we need to preserve life.”

“Is that not also right?”

Guang Ke hesitated and didn’t reply.

The dragonman youngster smiled, “Is it also impossible for the undead to pursue justice?”

As he said this, the young man was gazing out a window and saw the ghost girl, Zi Di, on the ship, carefully checking the goods being transported on board.

When undead were mentioned, Guang Ke solemnly replied, “The undead are all wicked, and the opposite of righteous.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, “Therefore, the prerequisite is survival. Without life, there is nothing, then how will one be able to uphold justice?”

Guang Ke slowly shook his head, “Those words are a little biased. In fact, to defend and carry out justice, we have to have a sacrificial mindset.”

“Many times, justice is carried out even at the cost of one’s life!”

Guang Ke’s eyes looked both divine and very sincere.

This was the philosophy he wholeheartedly approved of.

The dragonman youngster smiled, “Right.”

“Lord Guang Ke speaks true.”

“It is because we weren’t afraid to sacrifice our lives that we were able to defeat Rou Zang and Gu Qiao together.”

“Therefore, the prerequisite of sacrifice is that we still need to be alive. Only by retaining our life can we be able to sacrifice ourselves, no?”

Guang Ke was dumbstruck.

The dragonman youngster had circled back to his own point.

“That is also correct.” Guang Ke wrinkled his brows and carefully pondered but didn’t find any point he could refute.

Then at that moment, there was a voice at the door.

It was a sailor bringing a report, he said that Feudal Lord Tan Qiu was coming to the port to send off the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

“Good, let Zong Ge hold a reception, I will join them in a little bit.” The dragonman youngster ordered.

Guang Ke’s brows creased deeply, “Your philosophy puts life ahead of justice, and makes it a prerequisite for justice.”

“If that is the case, then survival is more important that pursuing justice.”

“Does that mean that for the sake of survival, one can abandon all morality and principles, and commit crimes?”

“Won’t your philosophy cause evil to flourish?”

The dragonman youngster shook his head respectfully, “Lord Guang Ke.”

“There are people who have done wrong.”

“We are all sinful!”

“It is because we bear sins that we repent and pursue righteousness.”

“Is it not precisely our church’s duty to lead all living beings onto this path?”

“Everyone should pursue righteousness, and have the power to pursue it! This includes both ordinary people and criminals.”

“Righteousness and sin are like light and dark, it is because light is beautiful that darkness can express its repulsiveness. Conversely, it is because of the darkness that light can show its value.”

“Allow believers to cleanse themselves of their sins, and become emissaries of justice, is that not what we pursue?”

Guang Ke was dumbstruck.

He sensed that the dragonman youngster’s philosophy had already formed a closed loop.

It could be explained circularly.

It had an impeccable feeling.

Guang Ke sighed, “With regards to my God’s doctrine, I am not an expert.”

“I am very sorry that I have other urgent affairs.”

“Otherwise, I would travel and sail with you so we could discuss our doctrine.”

The dragonman youngster also expressed his disappointment.

In actuality, his heart had greatly relaxed.

Much of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ affairs could not be exposed to the light. After leaving Snake Rat Island, they needed to go to Two Eyes Island and use Wisteria Merchant Alliance channels to contact the Bai Zhen clan elder.

This matter was very important, and if Guang Ke was by their side, it would be very inconvenient for the survivors.

“It is getting late, it is time to see you off.” Guang Ke stood up to leave, “If there is an opportunity, I hope you can travel to my God’s cathedral, the archbishop there is well versed in the field of theology.”

“If I get the chance, I will go there.” The dragonman youngster nodded his agreement.

The speech he gave on the battlefield not only changed others, but it also changed him too.

Before, he too felt the weight of his sins.

Now, through his own enlightenment, he had swept away the haze in his heart, and had now had an entirely different spirit and mindset.

Guang Ke and the dragonman youngster left the captain’s room and went to the port to meet with Tan Qiu.

Tan Qiu was beaming.

He had finally eliminated his greatest heart demon, and after defeating Rou Zang, the greatest shadow over his life had crumbled.

Rou Zang and Gu Qiao were captured alive, layered heavily with seals, and stringently watched.

Rou Zang’s mouth was very stiff, even after cruel torture, he wouldn’t reveal where he had hidden his treasure.

Rou Zang’s pirate group had rampaged in these seas for many years, there was no way the wealth he stashed was small.

Tan Qiu looked forward to what happened next greatly.

He hung Rou Zang on the punishment scaffold for all to see.

This would strengthen his prestige and renown to the maximum.

Originally, the church priest who had funded it was the leader.

But the priest died in battle.

Tan Qiu replaced him as the biggest winner.

As for Gu Qiao, Tan Qiu imprisoned him and didn’t treat him like Rou Zang.

After all, Gu Qiao was not a pirate and was from the Great Pain Temple.

The latter power was very robust. Tan Qiu was in the middle of finding someone to connect him with them, and he planned to exchange Gu Qiao for sufficient construction supplies for the island.

Gu Qiao was a gold level fighter, a valuable person the Great Pain Temple wouldn’t abandon.

“The ocean is vast, I do not know when we will see each other again.” At the time before parting, Tan Qiu expressed his desire for them to meet again.

“The memory of when we fought side by side and defeated Rou Zang’s pirate group proves the glory of our fellowship.”

“Snake Rat Island will forever welcome your corps!”

The dragonman youngster expressed that if given the chance, he would continue their collaboration together.

A short while later, the twelve ships sailed off and left the port.

After he saw the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps gradually disappear over the horizon, Tan Qiu let out a breath.

During the naval battle against Rou Zang, everyone had seen the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ substantial strength.

To be honest, when the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps berthed at Snake Rat Island, they had been a weight on Tan Qiu’s heart.

The captain’s room.

Zi Di reported the outcome of the conflict to the dragonman youngster.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps lost six ships and gained twelve ships of better quality postwar.

80 people were killed, 137 were crippled, and to compensate them, 49 gold, 82 silver, and 63 copper was spent.

But after the war, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps recruited a thousand people!

Without enough people, they wouldn’t be able to operate 12 ships.

It didn’t cost a penny to recruit this many people.

Many of them were pirates that had surrendered.

Tan Qiu wasn’t comfortable with releasing these war criminals into his territory, but the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was leaving that, their situation was different.

On the seas, there weren’t many differences between mercenary groups and pirate groups.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps hadn’t lost any members of its backbone or core.

This is because during the naval battle, the dragonman youngster, Zong Ge, and the others issued proper commands and didn’t carry out cannon fodder tactics. Especially when facing Rou Zang, they had given the order to abandon ship and withdrew willingly.

The distinguished gold level cannon remained intact and wasn’t damaged.

“We have spent 9 fireball scrolls, 27 healing scrolls, 344 alchemy arrows, 241 artillery shells, and damaged 25 alchemy crossbows.”

“We used 10 boxes of shakers.”

“One Pearl Bubble.”

Both had been used to make the trap, by using an illusion of Zong Ge and the gold level cannon, they had successfully lured Rou Zang into it.

“Before the fight, we obtained a set of standard templar knight equipment.”

“We also obtained a gold level material – the blood brain pebble.”

“We also obtained the Bath Tub’s gold level alchemy device – the Magic Medicine Bathtub.”

“Apart from this, there is also a batch of magic medicine recipes.”

“We obtained the organization method for the Drunken Shrimp Mud Spear Army from Tan Qiu.”

After the naval battle ended, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps returned for the dead tired Xu Ma.

They also arrested the Stone Forest Islanders along the way.

The blood brain pebble was given over to Tan Qiu.

In order to obtain the right to deal with Rou Zang and Gu Qiao with his own hands, Tan Qiu had given up many riches.

The blood brain pebble was the first, the magic medicine bathtub was the second, and the Drunken Shrimp Mud Spear Army organization method was the third.

When the medicine bathtub was brought up, Zi Di inevitably elaborated on it more, “This gold level item is very useful, it can refine large batches of magic medicine and will be of great help to the entire group!”

As Zi Di was giving her report to the young captain, Zong Ge was busy writing in his training manual.

He was no longer limited to one ship, now he had expanded to three.

“Kuai Tui’s battle squad is the key.”

“There are too many new mercenaries, they need much more training.”

“As we sail, they can practice steering the ships, controlling the sails, and other such activities.”

“Military discipline is important! It must be reshaped and emphasized.”

“When we reach Two Eyes Island, I should be able to cure my arm injury.”

In the latter stages of the naval battle, Zong Ge’s arm injury was a burden that forced him to rely mainly on shooting from afar, which made him feel terrible.

Bai Ya’s head was bandaged.

In his free time, he sat above deck and admired the vast seascape.

He reminisced to himself the letter he wrote to his sweetheart.

Dear Miss Xi Qiu:

Although the naval battle is done, I still see it replaying before me.

My ears still ring with the echoes of gunfire.

I still have nightmares.

I dreamed that I died in battle, and still dream about Priest Jia Sha.

But I no longer panic like before.

Whenever I recall the captain’s words, I can sense a warm feeling rising from my heart.

I am very glad that I could meet our captain!

I think that in some ways, he is greater than even Lord Guang Ke.

It was he who lifted me up, allowed me to confront my sins, then go and seek and more radiant and beautiful tomorrow.

It was he who let me know that I am walking on the correct path!

Therefore, I stand tall.

Not after we redeem ourselves, but in the process of redemption.

Chapter 297: Two Eyes Island

The sunset glow looked like glittering fireworks that filled the meeting between sky and sea.

The tranquil ocean surface reflected the multicolored sunset that seemed to burn as gorgeously as fire.

Despite several days of sailing, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ 12 ships still held their sails high, with both shouts and horns booming from their vessels.

Zong Ge stood on the foremost mast, and with rapt attention, he sized up how training was going on every ship.

“Yesterday, two pirates were executed, it had a good effect.”

After leaving Snake Rat Island, Zong Ge conducted training.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps took in many pirates from Rou Zang’s pirate group, including from the Thin Grass Pirates and the Flower Wall Pirates.

These pirates were of decent quality, and in Zong Ge’s assessment, they weren’t too bad.

After all, they were either part of Rou Zang’s main group or his auxiliary groups.

Those that could dominate a small stretch of sea had to be capable. These pirates were those people, the victors of battle and competitions of cruelty.

They could be said to be the elite.

Their battle quality was good, but their obedience was lacking.

It was considerably challenging to force these unyielding pirates to follow the rules to the letter.

Zong Ge had served in the imperial military for many years and loathed these kinds of people the most. Yesterday, he had beheaded two slacking troublemakers, slightly shocking the newly recruited pirates.

“There are still too few of our own people.”

“Most of this group are new recruits.”

Zong Ge was an outstanding military officer, and he clearly understood the systemic problems present in the group.

The main reason was that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had expanded too quickly. Creating an unstable foundation.

Having a high level of battle strength was not a problem.

With the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge surpassing normal silver level cultivators, they could intimidate others.

But on the lower levels, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was quite weak.

The only iron level cultivators they could currently trust were Cang Xu, Tripleblade, and Kuai Tui.

They had managed to recruit 7 iron level pirates.

There were even more bronze level pirates, 22 currently.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Group only had 12 bronze levels originally, including Lan Zao and Zi Di (iron level life level and bronze level magician).

There were basically twice as many bronze levels as iron levels.

Currently, the survivors had inadequate control over the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ lower ranks.

Before expanding their forces, Zong Ge had foreseen the consequences of doing so, yet he was still the greatest advocate form expanding the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ranks.

“There are unquestionably more advantages than detriments for expanding our forces.”

“We only need time and training, as well as battlefield screening to be able to inspect and promote the exceptional, allowing our entire troop to become more elite and battle worthy.

To Zong Ge, it seemed that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had many deficiencies.

For example, their ships were ordinary and had low ceilings, they had a serious lack of cannons, and their ratio of sailors was irrational.

“A fleet has appeared ahead!”

“It is Two Eyes Island’s flag.”

The lookout on the main mast suddenly warned.

Zong Ge was unsurprised.

Before the lookout had noticed, he had already been informed of it through the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s detection equipment.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish’s detection range was far greater than a lookout’s.

“We aren’t far from Two Eyes Island.”

“That should be Two Eyes Island’s patrol fleet!”

“Hahaha, we are finally reaching land.”

A surge of joyous laughter rolled from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ 12 ships.

But Zong Ge’s expression nevertheless sank.

He knew the other party was certainly Two Eyes island’s patrol fleet, but before their identity had been thoroughly verified, the reaction of these people was too undisciplined.

“All personnel, be on your guard. Gunners and marksmen squads to your positions…” Zong Ge quickly transmitted a series of orders to the lower ranks.

The ships’ sailors were startled, and one by one, they got up and moved.

Thus, when the Two Eyes Island patrol fleet contacted the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, they saw the 12 ships on their guard, with guns exposed.

The patrol fleet’s captain promptly transmitted his identity clearly, “Don’t be nervous, I am the Two Eyes Island’s patrol commander, ‘One Eye’. You are the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps? During the naval battle of Snake Rat Island, when Rou Zang’s pirate group was utterly exterminated, you performed outstanding military service. I know you!”

“We are already within Two Eyes Island’s waters, it is very safe, don’t be so tense!”

As One Eye advised the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to relax, he was in fact, very nervous.

He only had 8 ships in his fleet, regardless of whether it was numbers or cultivators, both were lacking compared to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

“One Eye, a silver level fighter.”

“Only 8 ships…it should only be a portion of the defense fleet.”

“One bronze level magic ship.”

“I gauge about 200 cannons.”

Zong Ge quickly swept his eyes over them, and with the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s gathered intelligence, he assessed the other party’s battle strength.

In terms of island size, Two Eyes Island and Snake Rat Island were both considered medium sized islands.

But in terms of development, Two Eyes Island far surpassed Snake Rat Island.

It had a good location, sandwiched between the Magic Power Shipping Route and the Masked Shipping Route, thus trade here was flourishing.

On the sea, after both fleets communicated their respective identities, Zong Ge ordered, “Lift defensive measures.”

One Eye let out a breath of relief and let his subordinates proceed with their patrol while his bronze level magic ship led the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to the harbor.

By the time the fleet arrived there, the sun had completely set.

In the dusky light, Two Eyes was illuminated brightly.

Under the curtain of night, Two Eyes Island was one half tall and one half low. The shorter island was dark, with only sporadic lights on it. While the towering island was lit up splendidly and filled with the clamor of people.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ 12 ships officially berthed at port.

One Eye departed to continue his duty of patrolling.

Ship planks were put down.

A port official smiled and walked onto the flagship.

“Commander Long Fu, honored to meet you. Your military glory and outstanding service preceded you by several days and has already disseminated throughout Two Eyes Island. Two Eyes Island welcomes your group’s arrival.” The official’s attitude was very cordial.

The destruction of Rou Zang’s pirate group was great news to the surrounding imperial islands.

With the Pirate Throne’s appearance and increasing rampancy of pirates, hearing about the infamous Rou Zang’s pirate group being wiped out at this turbulent time was like sweet rain after a long drought.

“We mainly relied on Lord Guang Ke’s strength. My group was just lucky to fish up some merits.” Long Fu laughed, “Thanks to Lord Guang Ke’s leadership, I have become a believer in the Justice Church, and henceforth, bathe in my God’s radiance.”

Upon hearing that the Long Fu and Guang Ke had a close relationship, the official became more fervent.

After a quick chat, the official collected the anchorage fee.

For a week, it would cost 15 silver for small sized ships, 30 silver for medium sized ships, and 50 silver for large sized ships.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had 12 ships. The flagship and Zong Ge’s warship were medium sized vessels, while the rest were small sized vessels. Thus, the anchorage expense was 210 silver, or 2 gold and 10 silver.

It was a little more expensive than Snake Rat Island.

The official explained, “Lord Long Fu, I will explain the anchorage fee.”

“Normally, the anchorage fee would be 10 silver for small ships, 25 silver for medium ships, and 45 silver for large ships. The price is basically the same in all imperial ports.”

“But because the island is rising and falling, trade is frequent, and there are many people visiting, supply is scarce at the port, thus we are forced to charge additional fees.”

Long Fu nodded, indicating his understanding.

The official smiled, “Then, how long will your group be stopping here?”

The dragonman youngster had been prepared for this, and didn’t answer, instead he first asked, “When is the large auction being held?”

The official reacted instantly, “According to customary practice, it will be in roughly 8 days. My Lord, besides the ascending island’s auction, the descending island’s clearance sale usually continues for half a month.”

The dragonman youngster took the middle road, “Then we’ll stay for 10 days for now.”

“Does the island have a good tavern?”

The official recommended 5 taverns, 8 inns, and provided a detailed presentation on where there were nice girls and where there were mermaid maids.

The dragonman youngster knew the official had misunderstood him, but didn’t interrupt him.

The official finally said, “After finding out your group was coming, the Feudal Lord was very happy, and invited you, Commander Long Fu, to have an audience with him tomorrow morning.”

“I am honored to pay my respects to Lord Hun Tong.” The dragonman youngster said as a result.

The official left satisfied, he was not returning empty-handed.

Before leaving, Zi Di stepped forth and secretly gave him a pouch of silver coins.

Zi Di came back, her face like a smile yet not, “My Lord, are you not going to gather information at those taverns? The official detailed where there were many beautiful girls.”

The dragonman youngster shook his head, “Today, I and the sailors will rest on the ships.”

“Do you need to go now?”

Zi Di nodded, “The earlier I get news and contact the Bai Zhen clan elder, the better right?”

“Please be safe!” The dragonman youngster reminded.

This was also part of their formulated plan.

Speaking of which, it had been a long time since they left Mystifying Monster Island.

They were delayed a lot due to their search for the Green Jade Gold Coffin on Snake Rat Island.

The longer time passed, the worse things got for the survivors.

Despite the fact they hadn’t been exposed yet, the empire had dispatched a legend level strength squad to assault Mystifying Monster Island.

There was an additional factor, War Merchant was a massive arms dealer.

Although he had concealed and cut off Mystifying Monster Island from the rest of the world, his trade with the outside world had always been immense.

Trafficking munitions was incredibly profitable, and the sales of which constantly supported War Merchant’s alchemy research.

Now that War Merchant was dead, the arms market would certainly have a large shortfall of goods. All powers that purchased War Merchant’s munitions would definitely be looking for War Merchant.

Therefore, as more time passed, the greater the survivors’ weak points.

They had to prepare well as soon as possible.

If they waited until they became wanted criminals to redeem themselves, the leeway to do so would be too small.

Zi Di left the ship and made landfall with Cang Xu.

Some sailors on board began to cause trouble.

“Why aren’t we allowed to disembark?”

“Yea, we’ve already arrived at Two Eyes Island!”

“Let us go, I want to drink, I want some girls!!”

The sailors were very discontented.

If they were still sailing, it would have been alright, but now that they were at port, they still weren’t allowed off the ship.

Wasn’t this the same as placing fine delicacies in front of a starving person?

Lan Zao was expressionless, “This is both the commander’s and the first mate’s order. None of us are leaving the ship.”

“Then why was Xu Ma allowed to get off?” Someone exclaimed.

Lan Zao coldly snorted, “He is a thief, and responsible for making discreet inquiries for information. Are you one?”

“That’s an excuse.”

“A lie!”

“Don’t think we don’t know that Xu Ma has given you many gifts. You guys are simply in this together.”

A group of sailors were stirred up and surrounded Lan Zao.

Lan Zao bleakly stroked his blade’s handle.

Of the people around him, there were three bronze levels, all of them from Rou Zang’s pirate group.

If a fight broke out, Lan Zao would be in a worrying situation against the three of them.

Chapter 298: Magic Messaging Unit

The sailors looked malevolent, and their faces were ominously glinting as they grew closer to Lan Zao on all sides, squeezing him in.

In that desperate moment, a short figure burst into the hold.

“What are you guys up to, surrounding him?”

“Do you want to court death?!”

Upon sensing the iron level aura, cold water seemed to splash into everyone’s hearts.

“Ah, its Lord Di Bi.”

Everyone was startled.

“We weren’t doing anything.”

“This is a misunderstanding, my Lord. We just wanted an explanation.”

“The ships have entered port, why aren’t we allowed to disembark?”

The dangerous ambience vanished like smoke.

Di Bi was Tripleblade’s pseudonym.

Tripleblade was an iron level and Zong Ge’s trusted aide, thus his intimidation ability was greater than Lan Zao’s.

Tripleblade’s keen gaze swept across everyone.

Wherever his gaze went, the eyes of pirates looked away, none dared to look at him face to face.

Tripleblade was once a mercenary commander, and had much prestige, things Lan Zao lacked. Although Lan Zao had worked on the Hog’s Kiss for a long time, his highest position was being an assault captain, that’s all.

He had been the survivors’ boatswain for a time.

Tripleblade coldly snorted, “You aren’t aware of why you aren’t allowed off the ship?”

“You are pirates, prisoners of war!”

“You are my group’s spoils of war; it was because Lord Shi Qi advocated for expanding our forces that we took you in.”

“You think that by joining our Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps that you can wantonly act like you used to?”

“It is because you are undisciplined and impulsive that Lord Shi Qi has taken the trouble to train you. For now, you will remain on this ship to recuperate.”

“You will be clear on your status and situation!”

The hold was in absolute silence.

Tripleblade finally looked at Lan Zao.

Lan Zao sternly said, “Yesterday, Lord Shi Qi beheaded two people, this what happens to those that don’t comply with orders.”

“If you don’t believe me, then don’t hesitate to try me!”

The sailors reluctantly smiled, “How would we dare?”

“Lord Di Bi has provided us guidance.”

“We will leave at once, at once.”

One still unreconciled asked, “Then when can we disembark?”

Tripleblade glared at the person who asked.

The sailors dispersed in confusion.

Now only Lan Zao and Tripleblade were left in the hold.

“Many thanks.” Lan Zao sincerely expressed.

Tripleblade shook his head, “You don’t need to be courteous with me.”

There was no need for such politeness between those that had survived Mystifying Monster Island together.

Lan Zao shook his head too, “If you didn’t arrive at the right time, such a wonderful outcome wouldn’t have happened.

“Although they wouldn’t kill you, they’d still teach you a lesson and likely injure you.” Tripleblade sighed when he said that, “I heard that Two Eyes Island’s market is well developed, there are many things we can buy. You must improve your strength as much as possible.”

“As you say!” Lan Zao was in favor of that.

His bronze level strength couldn’t suppress the situation.

To deal with these arrogant and obstinate pirates directly, one had to have greater strength.

However, Lan Zao’s bloodline and aptitude shackled him.

If nothing unexpected happened, his current strength would be his limit for the rest of his life.

Under these circumstances, he could only use external power like excellent equipment to increase his strength.

Lan Zao’s status was higher than most bronze levels.

But without the strength to defend his position, sooner or later, the other bronze fighters would dig at him.

This was the world of the strong.

After making his rounds, Tripleblade reported the situation to Zong Ge.

Zong Ge was the one who ordered Tripleblade to do this.

Tripleblade had outstanding strength among the iron levels. Were it not so, it was impossible for him to be a mercenary commander for so many years.

Before a riot could form, Tripleblade had successfully pacified things.

Zong Ge didn’t punish the troublesome sailors severely.

Although the pirate captives could fight, they were not excellent troops.

Their obedience was lacking, and they were hard to train.

“During naval battles, because space between ships is narrow, they often fight by themselves or in small groups.”

“Therefore, individual bravery is very important.”

“If a large-scale land battle happens, where particular formations and coordination is needed, these pirates will be a pile of rabble.”

Zong Ge was constantly thinking about the Wilderness Continent.

That was his stage.

On that stage, a well trained and obedient army was strength that could be relied on.

“At present, I have to use these reluctant pirates.”

“If Kuai Tui and the others can be trained into a battle squad, when I arrive on the Wilderness Continent, they can be used to establish an army and rapidly open up things.”

Zong Ge calculated.

After leaving Snake Rat Island, he now had his own ship.

Zong Ge pondered: whether he could secretly train Kuai Tui and the others on his ship?

If he did that, how much would be exposed?

If things were exposed, how would he and the entire Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps be jeopardized?

Zi Di was traveling through a corridor.

She wasn’t alone, as Cang Xu was by her side.

The two magicians were together to guarantee their safety.

Although their highest cultivation was the iron level, their necromancy spells could affect gold levels.

This was the advantage of a new path.

If they had used necromancy during the naval battle, the difficulty of victory would have been halved at least.

What made them more at ease was the defensive magic on them.

The Anti-investigation and Prophecy Spell and Deceptive Disguise Spell combined could counter legend level detection!

This was the power of a divine artifact.

Therefore, as they walked through Two Eyes Island, they didn’t provoke any suspicious attention.

“This is the place.” After a short while, Zi Di and Cang Xu came to a halt.

They were in front of a store open for business.

The signboard had the words – Wisteria store.

But Zi Di’s brows knitted.

For the most part, the stores she had walked past were bustling. There were streams of people in them and deals galore, however, the Wisteria store before them was barren, barely a person was at the door.

Not only that, but the store was also located in a remote area.

Zi Di and Cang Xu entered the store.

The girl president discovered that the store had barely any merchandise, and that there was only one product there.

On the contrary, the sales clerk was very enthused to see two people and promptly welcomed them in.

Before he could ask who they were, Zi Di took out a magic decree.

When the sales clerk saw that decree, his eyes immediately widened and he stammered, “Wisteria secret order?”

The sales clerk immediately took in the two people before him.

Zi Di and Cang Xu both wore hooded cloaks.

To the sales clerk, they both seemed to be ordinary looking middle aged men.

The sales clerk became deferential, “Two Lords, please come this way.”

After he walked behind the sales counter, he took out a palm sized alchemy device.

Zi Di put the wisteria secret order into the alchemy device’s slot.

The secret order fit snugly into the slot.

After a few seconds, the secret order glowed with purple light.

The purple light condensed into a vine.

This was how the Wisteria Merchant Alliance validated one’s identity.

The purple vine of light climbed into the air and formed seven vines.

“Level seven elders!” Upon seeing this, the sales clerk couldn’t help but rub his eyes, his face filling with shock.

Zi Di replied, “Who is the manager here? Call them in.”

The sales clerk gasped, then bowed and fawned, “Yes, yes!”

A jiffy later.

The store’s backyard.

The Wisteria Merchant Alliance manager bowed to Zi Di and Cang Xu, “Lord Elders, this humble one is Yan Yi, the manager stationed here.”

The Wisteria Merchant Alliance was once enormous, second only to humanity’s six great merchant alliances, naturally it had a complete organizational structure.

The merchant alliance had 10 levels of authority.

Level 10 was the president, level 9 was the vice president, level 8 senior elder, level 7 elder…level 3 store manager.

The two middle aged men who had suddenly arrived were of a much higher level compared to the store manager.

The wisteria secret order Zi Di and Cang Xu had taken out was the newest one.

Secret orders were a type of alchemy device, its core was a sequence of magic words. By knowing the written content and sequence, one could make a secret order.

Zi Di could make level 10 decrees as president, but that wasn’t necessary.

Level 7 was sufficient to spur on the store manager here.

“Where is the magic messaging unit? I need to pass on a message.” Zi Di asked bluntly.

“This way please.” The store manager, Yan Yi, led the way at once.

Magic messaging units were standard equipment for every Wisteria Merchant Alliance store.

It was a pillar-shaped alchemy device that could transmit messages over long distances.

Of course, the distance that it could reach was finite.

But through many magic messaging units, one could spread a message over a long range.

The message Zi Di transmitted from this magic messaging unit would be passed onto the nearest magic messaging unit, then to the next magic messaging unit, and then to the next.

By being passed through many magic messaging units, the message would eventually reach the Sheng Ming Continent and be delivered to the Bai Zhen clan elder.

Upon reaching the magic messaging unit, Zi Di took out a slate, the slate had a long paragraph of writing engraved into it. The words were densely packed, and all mixed up, it was clear that it needed a special cipher to deconstruct it for one to gain the true message hidden in it.

Zi Di inspected it three times, then after verifying it, she solemnly put the slate into the device’s recess.

“I won’t be able to reverse this once it’s done.” Zi Di said to herself, “Perhaps with the Pearl Bubbles, we can live incognito?”

The hesitation only lasted for a split second.

The Pearl Bubbles would be exhausted one day.

In fact, the amount of divine force inside the Mermaid Fairy Tale was always decreasing.

Zi Di used her spirit to control the pillar and began to imbue it with magic power.

The magic device activated and emitted a yellow light.

The light condensed on the slate, and the slate’s words began to move up and turn into blue light that flowed into the pillar.

The pillar’s yellow light soon turned blue, then the blue light suddenly ejected out and disappeared without a trace.

“Message transmission…successful! Now then, time to wait for a reply.” Zi Di coughed, then pushed open the secret room’s door.

A few minutes later.

Zi Di and Cang Xu returned to the back of the shop.

The store manager Yan Yi was standing to the side; he looked somewhat tense.

Cang Xu was drinking water.

Zi Di browsed the ledger, her complexion aloof.

There were many incorrect entries.

Zi Di coughed, her finger pointing at something, “Tell me, what happened to this account?”

Yan Yi stammered out an explanation.

After a few sentences, Cang Xu knew he was hearing lies.

Zi Di asked a few questions, and Yan Yi answered with a brow beaded with sweat.

“How much does the store still have in reserve?” Zi Di finally asked.

Not long after, Zi Di and Cang Xu left the store.

They had a small leather suitcase in their hands, inside there was 300 gold coins.

As a level 7 elder, Zi Di had the right to utilize any store reserves.

After leaving a tenth, Zi Di withdrew the rest of the reserve.

As for the store manager, Yan Yi, Zi Di did not fire him.

From an examination of the accounts, Yan Yi had obviously been embezzling store sales.

He also lacked business acumen.

But according to merchant alliance records, at the time, the Wisteria Merchant Alliance had carefully considered who to assign as store manager here.

Yan Yi and the feudal lord here, Hun Tong, were both members of the Duo Mu Clan.

They had a faint bloodline relationship with each other.

Now was the time to hire more staff, and Zi Di did not plan for Yan Yi to be doing the interviews.

Chapter 299: Coral Cage Tree

Xu Ma arrived at a tavern bar counter.

He was following orders to gather intelligence in Two Eyes Island discreetly.

“Give me a cup of pine wind beer!” Xu Ma took out a few silver coins and set them on the counter.

Liquor was served after payment was received, and very soon, a large cup of beer was put in front of him.

Xu Ma immersed himself in his drink.

Glug, glug, glug.

After a few sips, the cup was empty.

Pine wind beer had a cold and fresh texture, and Xu Ma sighed contently after burping.

He was very poor.

But this time, he had gotten funds from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps for his mission.

With the coin, he first chose to reward himself with a drink.

He hadn’t had this type of beer for a very long time.

“Another cup!” Xu Ma took out some more mission funds.

This time, after the liquor cup was served to him, Xu Ma called to him, “I heard you have the latest news here.”

The waiter glanced at him, “We do some information peddling.”

Xu Ma smiled, “I want information on Two Eyes Island. As much as possible, and as detailed as possible.”

The waiter was expressionless, “Customer, this depends on how much you want to pay. With enough money, I can tell you what color the feudal lord is wearing tonight.”

“I am not interested in what old men like to wear!” Xu Ma waved his hand, his face disdainful.

The waiter took out a list of items, “This is the pricing list, you can look at it in the corner. The same information can have different prices depending on how specific and detailed you want it.”

The bar counter was crowded and had the best lighting.

Xu Ma took the list and liquor to the corner, then looked through the pricing list.

“This is how much?” Xu Ma was somewhat begrudging, “I might as well buy some popular news, the leftover coin is enough for me to have two more cups.”

Xu Ma had received a huge scare during the battle against Rou Zang’s pirate group.

He decided that he would relax and appreciate himself here nicely.

After purchasing the information, Xu Ma drank his beer while gazing at the tavern’s female dancers and pretty waitresses.

A thin person decided to sit in front of him.

“I heard you wanted information?”

“We can exchange some.”

For a moment, Xu Ma didn’t react, he was very red, and his eyes were drifting, “What do you mean?”

“You wanted information on Two Eyes Island, I am well acquainted with it.” The thin person thumbed towards themselves, “In exchange, I want information on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

Xu Ma immediately sobered up a bit, and his face sank, “You’ve got the wrong person!”

“That’s right. Our younger brother saw you disembarking from their ship.” The thin person said.

“I’m no traitor!” Xu Ma’s attitude was very firm.

“The price can be discussed.” The thin person laughed.

Xu Ma coldly snorted.

The thin person began to raise the price.

Truth be told, Xu Ma was gradually being enticed.

But when he thought of offending the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge, his heart trembled in fear.

“Stop talking!” Xu Ma was cold, “I am not interested at all!”

When the thin person saw Xu Ma’s apprehension, he urged one last time, “Our trade will be kept strictly confidential, no third party will know you sold information to us.”

Xu Ma didn’t reply, he just waved his hand in disagreement.

The thin person’s smile vanished, and after looking deeply at Xu Ma, they walked away without another word.

After a half hour, Xu Ma, smelling like alcohol, left the tavern.

On a sparse road, where the light was dim on the footpath, he was blocked on all sides.

“What are you doing?!” Xu Ma was irritated.

Three people were blocking him, all of them bronze level.

Xu Ma was no match for them.

A dagger was ruthlessly jabbed into his belly.

The brazen attacker pulled off their mask and laughed.

It was the thin person from the tavern!

“We meet again, friend.”

The thin person grabbed Xu Ma’s hand and placed it on the dagger, then told him, “Hold onto this dagger, don’t pull it out. If you do, you will surely hemorrhage blood. Without healing methods, it’ll only take a few minutes for you to bleed to death.”

Xu Ma painfully grasped the dagger.

“Now, tell me everything you know about the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.” Said the thin person, “If you won’t talk, then you’ll die.”

Xu Ma shuddered from head to toe, fear and hate flooded body and mind.

With the threat of death looming, he was forced to expose what he knew about the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

“That’s all?” The thin person was dissatisfied.

“I only have a small role; I haven’t been a member for long!” Xu Ma said at once, “Don’t kill me, in the future when I go up in the ranks, I can tell you much more.”
